set --------------------------------------------------------------
// f: -1 at the bottom of the glass, 1 at the top. The sky colours are fixed in every light set (palette.json).
function bxSkyPx(f, u, v) {
  const edge = Math.sin(u * 0.07) * 0.06;
  if (bxSet === 'night') {
    if (hash2(Math.floor(u * 1.5) + 7, Math.floor(v * 1.5) + 3) > 0.965) return C.GL0;   // a few stars
    return f + edge > 0.25 ? C.NSK1 : C.NSK0;
  }
  if (bxSet === 'sun') return f + edge > 0 ? C.SS1 : C.SS0;
  if (bxSet === 'sat') return f + edge > 0.35 ? C.SKY1 : C.EV1;
  return f + edge > 0.1 ? C.SKY1 : C.SKY0;
}

// ---- floor, board, walls -------------------------------------------------------------------------------
function shBxFloor(face, u, v) {
  const B = BOXR.board;
  if (u >= B.x - 2 && u < B.x + B.w * TILE + 2 && v >= B.y - 2 && v < B.y + B.h * TILE + 2) return -1;   // the board and its kerb
  return cardFloorTex(u, v);
}
function bxBrick(u, z, seed) {                        // broad blue masonry cards with quiet overlap seams
  const row = Math.floor(z / 16), uu = (u + (row & 1) * 16 + seed) % 32;
  return z - row * 16 < 0.45 || uu < 0.45 ? C.PL0 : C.PL1;
}
let bxWall = 'L';
function shBxWall(face, u, z) {
  const R = BOXR;
  shFlat = false; shFlag = FL_ROOM; shLvl = bxWall === 'L' ? -1 : 0;   // the left wall is turned from the light, as in the office
  for (const g of R.gaps) {
    const w = g.window;
    if (!w || g.wall !== bxWall) continue;
    const du = u - w.u, dz = z - w.z, d = Math.sqrt(du * du + dz * dz);
    if (d < w.r) { shFlat = true; return bxSkyPx(dz / w.r, u, z); }
    if (d < w.r + 1.6) { shLvl = 0; return dz > 0 ? C.WD3 : C.WD2; }   // the round frame
    if (d < w.r + 2.6) return C.BK0;                                  // a ring of headers round it
  }
  if (z >= R.room.wallH - 3) { shLvl = -1; return z >= R.room.wallH - 1 ? C.WD0 : C.WD1; }   // the wall plate the roof sits on
  return bxBrick(u, z, bxWall === 'L' ? 3 : 0);
}
function bxEdge(on, c) { return on ? C.PL0 : c; }
// The model's cut ends at the open sides: the knee wall and the roof slab above it, in one section.
let bxEndX0 = 0;
function shBxEnd(face, u, v) {
  const R = BOXR, x = u + bxEndX0, z = v, W = R.room.wallH, inner = W + BX_PITCH * x, outer = inner + 5;
  shFlag = 0;
  const top = R.room.roofTop;
  if (x < 0 && z < W) return bxEdge(x < -WALL_T + 0.8 || z < 0.8, face === F_Y ? C.PL2 : C.PL1);
  if (x >= -WALL_T && z >= (x < 0 ? W : inner) && z < outer && z <= top) {   // the slab: from its underside (or the wall top) up
    const edge = z < inner + 0.8 || z >= outer - 0.8 || z > top - 0.8 || x < -WALL_T + 0.8;
    return bxEdge(edge, face === F_Y ? C.PL2 : C.PL1);
  }
  return -1;
}

// ---- the roof slopes -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Plane z = wallH + pitch * v rising from wall L (v = x, u = y) or wall R (v = y, u = x), shown beyond the hip
// line x = y. Unlike the box faces it isn't axis-aligned, so each pixel solves the plane for its world point.
function faceSlope(wall, u0, u1, v0, v1, sh, hip, wallH, pitch, resolution, writePixel) {      // hip false: draw both sides of x = y (a lone piece, for the atlas)
  const S = resolution || 1, write = writePixel || emit;
  const H = wallH === undefined ? BOXR.room.wallH : wallH, P = pitch === undefined ? BX_PITCH : pitch;
  let pa = 1e9, pb = -1e9, qa = 1e9, qb = -1e9;
  for (const [u, v] of [[u0, v0], [u0, v1], [u1, v0], [u1, v1]]) {
    const x = wall === 'L' ? v : u, y = wall === 'L' ? u : v, z = H + P * v;
    const sx = (x - y + tOX) * S, sy = ((x + y) * 0.5 - z + tOY) * S;
    if (sx < pa) pa = sx; if (sx > pb) pb = sx; if (sy < qa) qa = sy; if (sy > qb) qb = sy;
  }
  pa = Math.max(0, Math.floor(pa) - S); pb = Math.min(tW * S - 1, Math.ceil(pb) + S);
  qa = Math.max(0, Math.floor(qa) - S); qb = Math.min(tH * S - 1, Math.ceil(qb) + S);
  for (let py = qa; py <= qb; py++) for (let px = pa; px <= pb; px++) {
    const A = px / S - tOX + 0.5 / S, Bv = py / S - tOY;
    let x, y;
    if (wall === 'L') { x = (Bv + H + A / 2) / (1 - P); y = x - A; } else { y = (Bv + H - A / 2) / (1 - P); x = y + A; }
    const u = wall === 'L' ? y : x, v = wall === 'L' ? x : y;
    if (u < u0 || u >= u1 || v < v0 || v >= v1) continue;
    if (hip !== false && (wall === 'L' ? y < x : x <= y)) continue;    // the other slope's side of the hip
    shLvl = 0;
    const c = sh(F_SLOPE, u, v);
    if (c < 0) continue;
    write(px, py, x, y, H + P * v, F_SLOPE, c);
  }
}
function bxSkylightPx(s, lu, lv) {
  const W = s.u1 - s.u0, L = s.v1 - s.v0, F = 1.6;
  shLvl = 0;
  if (lu < F || lu >= W - F || lv < F || lv >= L - F) return lu < 0.8 || lu >= W - 0.8 || lv < 0.8 || lv >= L - 0.8 ? C.WD1 : lv < F ? C.WD4 : C.WD3;
  if (s.open) {                                     // summer: the sash propped open at the top, a stay holding it
    if (lv >= L - 3.4) return C.WD3;
    const su = W * 0.18 + (lv - F) / (L - 3.4 - F) * W * 0.12;
    if (Math.abs(lu - su) < 0.6 && lv > F + 1) return C.DEEP;
    shFlat = true;
    return bxSkyPx(lv / L * 2 - 1, s.u0 + lu, lv);
  }
  for (let k = 1; k < s.panes[0]; k++) if (Math.abs(lu - W * k / s.panes[0]) < 0.8) return C.WD3;
  for (let k = 1; k < s.panes[1]; k++) if (Math.abs(lv - L * k / s.panes[1]) < 0.8) return C.WD3;
  shFlat = true;
  const d = bxGlassDressing(s, lu, lv, W, L);       // snow, rain (35_boxprops)
  return d >= 0 ? d : bxSkyPx(lv / L * 2 - 1, s.u0 + lu, lv);
}
let bxSlopeWall = 'L';
function shBxSlope(face, u, v) {
  const R = BOXR, wall = bxSlopeWall;
  shFlag = FL_ROOM; shFlat = false;
  const x = wall === 'L' ? v : u, y = wall === 'L' ? u : v;
  if (Math.abs(x - y) < 1.6) return Math.abs(x - y) < 0.7 ? C.WD2 : C.WD1;               // the hip rafter
  for (const r of R.rafters[wall]) if (u >= r && u < r + R.rafters.w) return u - r < 0.8 || u - r >= R.rafters.w - 0.8 ? C.WD1 : C.WD2;
  for (const s of R.skylights) if (s.wall === wall && u >= s.u0 && u < s.u1 && v >= s.v0 && v < s.v1) return bxSkylightPx(s, u - s.u0, v - s.v0);
  if (v >= R.room.roofRun - 0.8) return C.WD1;                                             // the cut edge
  if (v < 0.8) return C.WD1;                                                              // where the slope meets the wall plate
  // Boards in the roof's shade. The slope is steep to the eye: a pixel spans 1 wu up it across and 2 down,
  // so a seam needs 2 wu to draw as a solid 2:1 line rather than dots.
  shLvl = -1;
  const k = Math.floor(v / 12);
  return v - k * 12 < 0.8 ? C.WD1 : C.WD3;
}
function shBxCap(face, u, v) { shFlag = 0; return face === F_TOP ? C.BG0 : face === F_Y ? C.PL2 : C.PL1; }
function shBxBeam(face, u, v) { shFlag = FL_ROOM; return face === F_TOP ? C.WD3 : face === F_Y ? C.WD2 : C.WD1; }

// ---- the box stacks ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
let bxCur = null, bxCurI = 0;
function shBxBox(face, u, v) {
  const b = bxCur, alongX = b.wall === 'R' || (b.wall === 'F' && b.x1 - b.x0 >= b.y1 - b.y0);
  const len = alongX ? b.x1 - b.x0 : b.y1 - b.y0, dep = alongX ? b.y1 - b.y0 : b.x1 - b.x0;
  shFlag = FL_ROOM;
  if (face === F_TOP) {
    const a = alongX ? u : v, c = alongX ? v : u;                     // along the box, and across it
    const above = bxAbove[bxCurI];
    if (above >= 0) {                                                 // a contact shadow round the box resting on this one
      const q = BOXR.boxes[above], x = b.x0 + u, y = b.y0 + v;
      if (x > q.x0 - 1.2 && x < q.x1 + 1.2 && y > q.y0 - 1.2 && y < q.y1 + 1.2) shLvl = -1;
    }
    const cm = dep / 2 + (b.nudge ? (a - len / 2) * 0.25 : 0);       // the seam; askew on a box that's been knocked round
    if ((b.string & 2) && Math.abs(c - cm) < 0.6) return C.PAPSH;
    if ((b.string & 1) && Math.abs(a - len / 2) < 0.6) return C.PAPSH;
    if (Math.abs(c - cm) < 0.6) return C.KRAFT0;
    if (Math.abs(c - cm) < 2.2 && a > 1 && a < len - 1) return C.KRAFT;   // tape over the seam
    return C.KRAFT2;
  }
  const front = alongX ? F_Y : F_X, h = b.z1 - b.z0;
  if (face === F_X) shLvl = -1;
  if (v >= h - 2.5 && v < h - 1.7) return C.KRAFT0;                   // the flap crease round the top
  if (face === front) {
    if (b.label) {
      const lu = b.label[0], lz = b.label[1];
      if (u >= lu && u < lu + 6 && v >= lz && v < lz + 4) return u < lu + 1 || u >= lu + 5 || v < lz + 1 || v >= lz + 3 ? C.PAPSH : C.CREAM;
    }
    if (b.paw && pawShape(u, v, b.paw[0] + 2, b.paw[1] + 2, 2.4)) return (bxCurI & 1) ? C.RD1 : C.BL1;
    if ((b.string & 1) && Math.abs(u - len / 2) < 0.6) return C.PAPSH;
  } else if ((b.string & 2) && Math.abs(u - dep / 2) < 0.6) return C.PAPSH;
  return C.KRAFT;
}
function shBxFlap(face, u, v) { shFlag = FL_ROOM; if (face === F_X) shLvl = -1; return face === F_TOP ? C.KRAFT0 : C.KRAFT; }
function bxFlap(b) {                                    // 1: the back flap stands up; 2: the front one hangs down; 3, 4: the ends stand up
  const L = b.wall === 'L', u0 = L ? b.y0 : b.x0, u1 = L ? b.y1 : b.x1, v0 = L ? b.x0 : b.y0, v1 = L ? b.x1 : b.y1;
  const fh = Math.ceil((v1 - v0) / 2), z = b.z1;
  const q = b.flap === 1 ? [u0 + 1, u1 - 1, v0, v0 + 1, z, z + fh] : b.flap === 2 ? [u0 + 1, u1 - 1, v1, v1 + 1, z - fh, z]
    : b.flap === 3 ? [u0, u0 + 1, v0 + 1, v1 - 1, z, z + fh] : [u1 - 1, u1, v0 + 1, v1 - 1, z, z + fh];
  const w = b.wall === 'F' ? 'R' : b.wall;
  const bx = w === 'L' ? [q[2], q[0], q[4], q[3], q[1], q[5]] : [q[0], q[2], q[4], q[1], q[3], q[5]];
  box(bx[0], bx[1], bx[2], bx[3], bx[4], bx[5], shBxFlap, C.WD1, 3);
}

// ---- the bake ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
function bakeBoxRoom() {
  const R = BOXR, P = R.room, B = R.board, X0 = B.x, Y0 = B.y, X1 = X0 + B.w * TILE, Y1 = Y0 + B.h * TILE;
  targetStatic();
  bakeBackdrop();
  bakeBase();
  backdropBaseContour();
  bxBuildAO(R);
  aoFn = bxBakeLight;                                              // contact shadows, less the lamps' pools
  // floorboards, then the stone board and its brass-capped kerb (2 wu high, so only its own edge touches a tile)
  shFlat = false; shBias = 0; shSunMax = 2; shFlag = FL_FLOOR | FL_ROOM;
  faceTop(0, 0, P.w, P.d, 0, shBxFloor, C.WD0, 0);
  shSunMax = 1; shBoard = 128;                                    // the board takes the board light (20_palette)
  faceTop(X0, Y0, X1, Y1, 0, shStone, C.FT0, 0);
  shSunMax = 2; shFlag = FL_ROOM;
  box(X0 - 2, Y0 - 2, 0, X0, Y1 + 2, 2, shKerbTop, C.WD0, 3);
  box(X0 - 2, Y0 - 2, 0, X1 + 2, Y0, 2, shKerbTop, C.WD0, 3);
  box(X0 - 2, Y1, 0, X1 + 2, Y1 + 2, 2, shKerbTop, C.WD0, 3);
  box(X1, Y0 - 2, 0, X1 + 2, Y1 + 2, 2, shKerbTop, C.WD0, 3);
  shBoard = 0;
  // brick knee walls, then the roof band above them, and the cut ends at the open sides
  shSunMax = 0;
  bxWall = 'L'; faceX(0, P.d, 0, P.wallH, 0, shBxWall, C.WD0, 0);
  bxWall = 'R'; faceY(0, P.w, 0, P.wallH, 0, shBxWall, C.WD0, 0);
  bxSlopeWall = 'L'; faceSlope('L', 0, P.d, 0, P.roofRun, shBxSlope);
  bxSlopeWall = 'R'; faceSlope('R', 0, P.w, 0, P.roofRun, shBxSlope);
  shFlat = false;
  const cap = 5 / BX_PITCH;                                       // the roof slab's top, cut level at roofTop
  faceTop(P.roofRun - cap, P.roofRun - cap, P.roofRun, P.d, P.roofTop, shBxCap, C.PL0, 1);
  faceTop(P.roofRun, P.roofRun - cap, P.w, P.roofRun, P.roofTop, shBxCap, C.PL0, 1);
  bxEndX0 = -WALL_T; faceY(-WALL_T, P.roofRun, 0, P.roofTop + 1, P.d, shBxEnd, C.PL0, 0);
  faceX(-WALL_T, P.roofRun, 0, P.roofTop + 1, P.w, shBxEnd, C.PL0, 0);
  // cross beams under the cut roof
  for (const b of R.beams) {
    if (b.axis === 'y') box(b.at - 2, b.from, b.z - 2, b.at + 2, b.to, b.z + 2, shBxBeam, C.WD1, 7);
    else box(b.from, b.at - 2, b.z - 2, b.to, b.at + 2, b.z + 2, shBxBeam, C.WD1, 7);
  }
  // the stacks and the loose boxes in front
  shSunMax = 1; shFlag = FL_ROOM;
  for (let i = 0; i < R.boxes.length; i++) {
    bxCur = R.boxes[i]; bxCurI = i;
    const b = bxCur;
    box(b.x0, b.y0, b.z0, b.x1, b.y1, b.z1, shBxBox, C.WD1, 3);
    if (b.flap) bxFlap(b);
  }
  // the props on the margins, the season's dressing, then the hanging lamps under their beams
  bxBakeProps(R);
  bxBakeDressing(R);
  R.lamps.forEach((l, i) => bxLamp(l, i < R.light.lamps, R.beams[l.beam].z));
  aoFn = null;
  shSunMax = 2; shFlat = false; shFlag = 0; shBias = 0; shLvl = 0;
}

// ---- each frame ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// The game's board (a daily puzzle) in painter's order, then the dust in the light, the napping cats, the season's
// moving bits, the string lights' twinkle and the game's sparkles and hearts.
function drawBoxDynamic() {
  nItems = 0;
  lampFn = bxLamps.length ? bxLampAdd : null;
  if (typeof GAME_MODE !== 'undefined' && GAME_MODE && gm.L) { gPrepareSrc(); addBoardItems(G_SRC); }
  if (typeof GAME_MODE !== 'undefined' && GAME_MODE && gm.shift && nsui.ending > 0) {
    addItem(IT_SORTER, WS.sorter.x + WS.sorter.y, 0);
    addItem(IT_CAT, WS.pm.x + WS.pm.y, 0);
  }
  sortItems();
  shBoard = 128;                                                    // the board's pieces and cats take the board light,
  if (BOXR.light.sun) lampFn = null;                                // and lamp light only at night (by day the sun lights them)
  for (let k = 0; k < nItems; k++) {
    if (drawItemHook) drawItemHook(ITEM_ID[k], false);
    drawItem(ITEM_ID[k]);
    if (drawItemHook) drawItemHook(ITEM_ID[k], true);
  }
  shBoard = 0;
  lampFn = bxLamps.length ? bxLampAdd : null;
  drawBoxDust(loopT);
  drawBoxNaps(loopT);
  drawBoxDressing(loopT);
  if (BOXR.light.string) drawBoxString(loopT);
  drawParticles();
  lampFn = null; shSunMax = 2;
}
// The collection, napping on the stacks: breathing every 3 s; now and then an ear (every 12 s) or the tail tip
// (every 16 s) flicks, each cat at its own phase. All three divide the 48 s loop.
function drawBoxNaps(t) {
  const N = BOXR.naps;
  shSunMax = 1; shFlag = 0; shBias = 0; shFlat = false;
  for (let k = 0; k < N.length; k++) {
    const n = N[k], tt = t + n.phase / 60;
    const frame = (fract(tt / 3) < 0.4 ? 1 : 0) | (fract(tt / 12) < 0.03 ? 2 : 0) | (fract((tt + 5) / 16) < 0.025 ? 4 : 0);
    buildNapSprite(n.cat, NAP_POSE_IDS[n.pose], frame, n.top * 7 + k);
    blitSprite(bxNapAt[k * 3], bxNapAt[k * 3 + 1], bxNapAt[k * 3 + 2], n.wall === 'L' ? n.dir === 'U' : n.dir === 'R', true);   // the head leads the way it lies
  }
}
// String lights: a bulb every 6 wu on a dark flex, each glowing in one of three colours and now and then
// dipping for a moment; at night and at sunset the pixels round each bulb pick up a level.
function drawBoxString(t) {
  const B = BOXR.bulbs, GLOW = [C.GL0, C.GL1, C.GL2], DIM = [C.BR2, C.RD2, C.BL2], glow = bxSet === 'night' || bxSet === 'sun';
  shSunMax = 0; shFlag = 0; shBias = 4;
  for (let k = 0; k < B.length; k++) {
    const b = B[k], q = k + 1 < B.length ? B[k + 1] : null;
    shFlat = false;
    if (q && Math.abs(q[0] - b[0]) + Math.abs(q[1] - b[1]) <= 8) {   // the flex to the next bulb on the same run
      for (let s = 1; s < 4; s++) { const f = s / 4; dot3(b[0] + (q[0] - b[0]) * f, b[1] + (q[1] - b[1]) * f, b[2] + (q[2] - b[2]) * f + 1 - Math.abs(f - 0.5) * 2 * 0.6, C.DEEP, 0); }
    }
    const dip = fract(t / 4 + hash2(k, 5)) < 0.06;
    shFlat = true;
    dot3(b[0], b[1], b[2], dip ? DIM[b[3]] : GLOW[b[3]], 0);
    if (glow && !dip) {
      const px = Math.round(b[0] - b[1]) + tOX, py = Math.round((b[0] + b[1]) * 0.5 - b[2]) + tOY;
      for (const [ox, oy] of [[1, 0], [-1, 0], [0, 1], [0, -1]]) {
        const x = px + ox, y = py + oy;
        if (x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < tW && y < tH && tLvl[y * tW + x] < 4) tLvl[y * tW + x]++;
      }
    }
  }
  shFlat = false; shBias = 0;
}
// Dust: motes seeded where the light is (the sun's shafts by day, under the lamps at night), drifting on the
// 48 s loop and drawn only while they're in it.
const BX_NDUST = 64, BX_DUST = new Float32Array(BX_NDUST * 6);
let bxNDust = 0;
function bxInitDust(room) {
  rngSeed(room.inputs.seed ^ 0x5eed);
  bxNDust = 0;
  const P = room.room;
  for (let guard = 0; bxNDust < BX_NDUST && guard < 20000; guard++) {
    const x = P.stackD + rnd() * (P.w - P.stackD), y = P.stackD + rnd() * (P.d - P.stackD), z = 3 + rnd() * (bxVZ - 6);
    if (!bxDustLit(x, y, z)) continue;
    const o = bxNDust * 6;
    BX_DUST[o] = x; BX_DUST[o + 1] = y; BX_DUST[o + 2] = z; BX_DUST[o + 3] = rnd() * TAU; BX_DUST[o + 4] = rnd() * TAU; BX_DUST[o + 5] = rnd();
    bxNDust++;
  }
}
function bxDustLit(x, y, z) { return bxSun(x, y, z, F_TOP) >= 0.6 || bxLampAdd(x, y, z, 1, 2) >= 1; }
function drawBoxDust(t) {
  shSunMax = 0; shFlag = 0; shFlat = true; shBias = 6;
  for (let k = 0; k < bxNDust; k++) {
    const o = k * 6;
    const breeze = BOXR.dressing.openSkylight ? 1 : 0;              // summer: the open skylight's breeze stirs it
    const x = BX_DUST[o] + Math.sin(t / 24 * TAU + BX_DUST[o + 3]) * 4 + breeze * Math.sin(t / 6 * TAU + BX_DUST[o + 4]) * 7;
    const y = BX_DUST[o + 1] + Math.cos(t / 16 * TAU + BX_DUST[o + 4]) * 3 + breeze * Math.cos(t / 8 * TAU + BX_DUST[o + 3]) * 5;
    const z = BX_DUST[o + 2] + Math.sin(t / 48 * TAU + BX_DUST[o + 5] * TAU) * 5;
    if (!bxDustLit(x, y, z)) continue;
    if (fract(t / 3 + BX_DUST[o + 5]) < 0.18) continue;           // a gentle twinkle (3 s)
    dot3(x, y, z, C.GL0, 0);
  }
  shFlat = false; shBias = 0;
}

// Point the frame at the whole room, LW x LH logical px (tests in Node, which have no app layer).
function __boxTestView(w, h) { LW = w; LH = h; viewX = Math.round(VIEW_CX - w / 2); viewY = Math.round(VIEW_CY - h / 2); }

// ---- review: every tile core still shows its own stone ---------------------------------------------------------
// The same rule as __so.coverage: no baked pixel other than the floor may sit in the 12 x 6 px core of a tile.
// Returns the offending tiles as [i, j, what] (what: 'flag' if not floor, 'deep' if floor from elsewhere).
function bxCheckCores() {
  const R = BOXR, B = R.board, out = [];
  for (let j = 0; j < B.h; j++) for (let i = 0; i < B.w; i++) {
    const cx = B.x + 8 + i * TILE, cy = B.y + 8 + j * TILE;
    const sx = Math.round(cx - cy) + OX, sy = Math.round((cx + cy) / 2) + OY;
    let bad = null;
    for (let y = sy - 3; y < sy + 3 && !bad; y++) for (let x = sx - 6; x < sx + 6 && !bad; x++) {
      const k = y * WW + x;
      if (!(sFlg[k] & FL_FLOOR)) bad = 'flag';
      else if (Math.abs(sWZ[k]) > 1 || sWX[k] < (B.x + i * TILE) * 4 - 4 || sWX[k] > (B.x + i * TILE + TILE) * 4 + 4 ||
        sWY[k] < (B.y + j * TILE) * 4 - 4 || sWY[k] > (B.y + j * TILE + TILE) * 4 + 4) bad = 'deep';
    }
    if (bad) out.push([i, j, bad]);
  }
  return out;
}
// One room interface. Layout records are made only on activation; frame dispatch and board origins
// allocate nothing. The old entry points remain adapters for the atlas and review tools.
// Swift: RoomRenderer protocol; RoomLayout and immutable scene data are separate from drawing.
function roomLayout(data) {
  if (!data) return {
    extents: [SCN.room.w, SCN.room.d, SCN.room.h], base: [-BASE_PAD,-BASE_PAD,SCN.room.w+BASE_PAD,SCN.room.d+BASE_PAD,BASE_H],
    layer: [WW,OFFICE_LAYER.WH,OFFICE_LAYER.OX,OFFICE_LAYER.OY], viewCentre: [OFFICE_LAYER.OX+24,OFFICE_LAYER.OY+49],
    sortingFloor: {x:GX0,y:GY0,w:GRID_W,h:GRID_H}
  };
  const b=data.bounds, h=b.sy1-b.sy0+24, ox=Math.round(WW/2-(b.sx0+b.sx1)/2), oy=12-b.sy0;
  if(h>WH_MAX || b.sx1-b.sx0+24>WW) throw new RangeError('Room exceeds world layer');
  return {extents:[data.room.w,data.room.d,data.room.roofTop],base:[-BASE_PAD,-BASE_PAD,data.room.w+BASE_PAD,data.room.d+BASE_PAD,BASE_H],
    layer:[WW,h,ox,oy],viewCentre:[ox+Math.round((b.sx0+b.sx1)/2),oy+Math.round((b.sy0+b.sy1)/2)],sortingFloor:data.board,backdrop:data.backdrop || null};
}
function roomNoPost() {}
function bakeOfficeRoom() { bakeStatic(); bakeOutside(); initDust(); }
const ROOMS = {
  office: {id:'office',layout:roomLayout,activate:activateOffice,sun:sunAmt,lightSet:()=>null,backdrop:()=>null,bake:bakeOfficeRoom,draw:drawOfficeDynamic,post:postOfficePasses},
  boxroom: {id:'boxroom',layout:roomLayout,activate:activateBoxRoom,sun:bxSun,lightSet:r=>r.light.set,backdrop:r=>r,bake:bakeBoxRoom,draw:drawBoxDynamic,post:roomNoPost},
  depot: {id:'depot',layout:roomLayout,activate:activateDepot,sun:depotSun,lightSet:r=>r.light.set,backdrop:r=>r,bake:bakeDepot,draw:drawDepotDynamic,post:postDepotLife},
  train: {id:'train',layout:roomLayout,activate:activateTrain,sun:trainSun,lightSet:r=>r.light.set,backdrop:r=>r,bake:bakeTrain,draw:drawTrain,post:roomNoPost},
  cityhall: {id:'cityhall',layout:roomLayout,activate:activateCityHall,sun:citySun,lightSet:r=>r.light.set,backdrop:()=>null,bake:bakeCityHall,draw:drawCityHall,post:postCityHall},
  parcel: {id:'parcel',layout:roomLayout,activate:activateParcel,sun:parcelSun,lightSet:r=>r.light.set,backdrop:()=>null,bake:bakeParcel,draw:drawParcel,post:roomNoPost},
  christmas: {id:'christmas',layout:roomLayout,activate:activateChristmas,sun:christmasSun,lightSet:r=>r.light.set,backdrop:()=>null,bake:bakeChristmas,draw:drawChristmas,post:roomNoPost}
};
let ACTIVE_ROOM=ROOMS.office, ROOM_DATA=null, ROOM_LAYOUT=roomLayout(null);
function useRoom(id, data) {
  const next=ROOMS[id]; if(!next) throw new RangeError('Unknown room: '+id);
  const layout=next.layout(data);
  ACTIVE_ROOM=next; ROOM_DATA=data || null; ROOM_LAYOUT=layout;
  DEPR=null;
  // Common coordinates are set before the room prepares its own cached light and drawing data.
  [ROOM_X,ROOM_Y,ROOM_H]=layout.extents;
  BASE_X1=layout.base[2];BASE_Y1=layout.base[3];
  WH=layout.layer[1];OX=layout.layer[2];OY=layout.layer[3];
  VIEW_CX=layout.viewCentre[0];VIEW_CY=layout.viewCentre[1];
  sunFn=next.sun; useLightSet(next.lightSet(data));
  useBackdrop(next.backdrop(data)); next.activate(data);
  aoFn=null;lampFn=null;shBoard=0;shFlat=false;shBias=0;shSunMax=2;
  targetStatic();
  paperPrepareRoomNaps(id==='boxroom'?data:null);
}
function useOffice() { useRoom('office',null); }
function useBoxRoom(room) { useRoom('boxroom',room); }
function roomBoardX(B) { return ROOM_LAYOUT.sortingFloor.x/TILE + (ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='boxroom'?0:B.roomX); }
function roomBoardY(B) { return ROOM_LAYOUT.sortingFloor.y/TILE + (ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='boxroom'?0:B.roomY); }
// ============================================================================
// 35 BOX ROOM PROPS AND DRESSING: the things on the floor margins (an old post trolley, a coat stand with a
// post-blue cap, a mailbag slumped against a stack, an armchair with a knitted blanket, a gramophone, a rag rug)
// and each season's dressing in its fixed slot (sim/18_boxroom): seedlings in egg boxes and a jam jar of
// flowers, a fan and a cat stretched out flat, a pumpkin and blankets on the stacks, a tree of boxes with
// bunting, snow, rain or leaves on the skylights, an open skylight in summer, and a bucket catching the drip
// on wet days. Everything stays inside the box the generator reserved for it, so the coverage rule holds.
// Baked with the room (bxBakeProps, bxBakeDressing); the moving bits each frame (drawBoxDressing).
// ============================================================================
// A prop's own frame: a runs across its front, b from its back (0) to its front, z up. dir is the way it faces.
function bxFrame(P) { const across = P.dir === 'R' || P.dir === 'L'; return { P, A: across ? P.y1 - P.y0 : P.x1 - P.x0, B: across ? P.x1 - P.x0 : P.y1 - P.y0 }; }
function bxAt(F, a, b) {
  const P = F.P;
  return P.dir === 'R' ? [P.x0 + b, P.y0 + a] : P.dir === 'L' ? [P.x1 - b, P.y0 + a] : P.dir === 'D' ? [P.x0 + a, P.y0 + b] : [P.x0 + a, P.y1 - b];
}
function bxPBox(F, a0, a1, b0, b1, z0, z1, sh, dk, olm) {
  const p = bxAt(F, a0, b0), q = bxAt(F, a1, b1);
  box(Math.min(p[0], q[0]), Math.min(p[1], q[1]), z0, Math.max(p[0], q[0]), Math.max(p[1], q[1]), z1, sh, dk, olm === undefined ? 3 : olm);
}
function bxPCyl(F, a, b, r, z0, z1, sh, dk, olb) { const p = bxAt(F, a, b); cylinder(p[0], p[1], r, z0, z1, sh, dk, olb); }
function bxPDisc(F, a, b, r, z, sh, dk) { const p = bxAt(F, a, b); disc(p[0], p[1], r, z, sh, dk, false); }
const bxFaces = (top, fy, fx) => (face) => (face === F_TOP ? top : face === F_Y ? fy : fx);    // a flat colour per face
const bxRingSh = (light, mid, dark) => (face, th, h) => (face === F_X ? dark : th > 1.0 ? light : mid);   // cylinders: lit on the left

// ---- the props ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
function bxTrolley(P) {                               // a flat bed on castors, red frame, a handle at one end, a parcel aboard
  const F = bxFrame(P), long = F.B >= F.A;          // the trolley's length runs along its longer side
  const L = long ? F.B : F.A, W = long ? F.A : F.B;
  const at = (s, t) => (long ? [t, s] : [s, t]);    // (along the length, across) -> (a, b)
  const pb = (s0, s1, t0, t1, z0, z1, sh) => { const p = at(s0, t0), q = at(s1, t1); bxPBox(F, Math.min(p[0], q[0]), Math.max(p[0], q[0]), Math.min(p[1], q[1]), Math.max(p[1], q[1]), z0, z1, sh, C.OUT); };
  const red = bxFaces(C.RD2, C.RD1, C.RD0), wheel = bxRingSh(C.FT0, C.DEEP, C.OUT);
  for (const s of [2.5, L - 2.5]) for (const t of [2.2, W - 2.2]) { const p = at(s, t); bxPCyl(F, p[0], p[1], 1.3, 0, 2.6, wheel, C.OUT, true); }
  pb(0.5, L - 0.5, 0.8, 2, 2.6, 4, red); pb(0.5, L - 0.5, W - 2, W - 0.8, 2.6, 4, red);
  pb(0.5, L - 0.5, 0.5, W - 0.5, 4, 5.4, bxFaces(C.WD3, C.WD2, C.WD1));
  pb(0.6, 1.8, 0.8, 2, 5.4, 15, red); pb(0.6, 1.8, W - 2, W - 0.8, 5.4, 15, red);    // the handle's uprights
  pb(0.4, 2, 0.6, W - 0.6, 14.4, 16, bxFaces(C.DEEP, C.RD1, C.RD0));                   // and its grip
  pb(L - 9.5, L - 2, 2, W - 2, 5.4, 9, (face, u, v) => (face === F_TOP ? (Math.abs(u - 3.7) < 0.6 ? C.KRAFT0 : C.KRAFT2) : face === F_Y ? C.KRAFT : C.KRAFT0));
}
function bxCoatStand(P) {
  const cx = (P.x0 + P.x1) / 2, cy = (P.y0 + P.y1) / 2, F = bxFrame(P);
  const wood = bxRingSh(C.WD3, C.WD2, C.WD1);
  cylinder(cx, cy, 3.4, 0, 1.4, wood, C.WD0, true);
  cylinder(cx, cy, 0.9, 1.4, 40, wood, C.WD0, false);
  cylinder(cx, cy, 1.4, 40, 42, wood, C.WD0, false);
  cylinder(cx, cy, 0.8, 42, 43.6, wood, C.WD0, false);
  for (const [dx, dy] of [[1.8, 0], [-1.8, 0], [0, 1.8], [0, -1.8]]) dot3(cx + dx, cy + dy, 38.5, C.BR2, 0);   // brass pegs
  const f = bxAt(F, F.A / 2, F.B / 2 + 1.6), c = [f[0], f[1]];                                        // the cap, on the front peg
  cylinder(c[0], c[1], 2.2, 34, 36.2, (face, th, h) => (h > 1.6 ? C.BL2 : face === F_X ? C.BL0 : C.BL1), C.OUT, false);
  const peak = bxAt(F, F.A / 2, Math.min(F.B - 0.4, F.B / 2 + 3.3));
  disc(peak[0], peak[1], 1.4, 34, () => C.BL0, C.OUT, false);
  dot3(peak[0], peak[1], 35.4, C.BR3, 0);                                                             // the brass badge
  const scarf = bxAt(F, F.A / 2 - 1.8, F.B / 2);                                                      // a scarf down the side
  for (let z = 22; z < 38; z++) dot3(scarf[0], scarf[1], z, (z >> 1) & 1 ? C.RD2 : C.CREAM, 0);
}
function bxMailbag(P) {                               // hessian, slumped back against the stack it leans on
  const R = BOXR.room, cx = (P.x0 + P.x1) / 2, cy = (P.y0 + P.y1) / 2, intoX = P.x0 === R.stackD, rmax = Math.min(P.x1 - P.x0, P.y1 - P.y0) / 2 - 0.4;
  const sack = bxRingSh(C.KRAFT, C.KRAFT0, C.WD1);
  for (let z = 0; z < 8; z++) {
    const f = z / 8, r = rmax * (z < 4 ? 1 - 0.04 * z : 0.84 - 0.13 * (z - 4)), lean = f * f * (rmax - r + 1.2);
    cylinder(intoX ? cx - lean : cx, intoX ? cy : cy - lean, Math.max(1.6, r), z, z + 1, sack, C.WD0, z === 0);
  }
  const nx = intoX ? cx - rmax + 1.6 : cx, ny = intoX ? cy : cy - rmax + 1.6;
  cylinder(nx, ny, 1.5, 8, 8.8, () => C.WD0, C.OUT, false);                                           // the drawstring
  cylinder(nx, ny, 1.9, 8.8, 9.8, sack, C.WD0, false);
  dot3(intoX ? cx + 1.5 : cx + 2.8, intoX ? cy + 2.8 : cy + 1.5, 5, C.BR2, 0);                       // a brass tag
}
function bxKnit(face, u, v) {                         // a cream paper throw with broad vermilion bands
  const band = Math.floor(v / 5) % 4;
  if (band === 1) return C.RD2;
  return face === F_TOP ? C.CREAM : C.PAPSH;
}
function bxArmchair(P) {                              // green velvet, a knitted blanket over the back and one arm
  const F = bxFrame(P), A = F.A, B = F.B;
  const velvet = bxFaces(C.PT2, C.PT1, C.PT0), leg = bxFaces(C.WD1, C.WD1, C.WD0);
  for (const a of [1, A - 2.5]) for (const b of [1, B - 2.5]) bxPBox(F, a, a + 1.5, b, b + 1.5, 0, 2, leg, C.WD0);
  bxPBox(F, 2, A - 2, 2, B - 1, 2, 7, velvet, C.PT0);
  bxPBox(F, 3.2, A - 3.2, 5, B - 1.4, 7, 9, bxFaces(C.PT3, C.PT2, C.PT1), C.PT0);                      // the seat cushion
  bxPBox(F, 0.5, 3.5, 2, B - 0.6, 2, 11, velvet, C.PT0);
  bxPBox(F, A - 3.5, A - 0.5, 2, B - 0.6, 2, 11, velvet, C.PT0);
  bxPBox(F, 0.5, A - 0.5, 0.5, 5, 2, 17.4, velvet, C.PT0);                                             // the back
  bxPBox(F, 3, A * 0.62, 0.6, 5.6, 9, 18, bxKnit, C.WD1);                                               // the blanket over it
  bxPBox(F, A - 3.9, A - 0.2, B * 0.42, B * 0.75, 6, 11.6, bxKnit, C.WD1);                              // and over the arm
}
function bxGramophone(P) {                            // a wooden cabinet, a record, a brass tulip horn
  const F = bxFrame(P), A = F.A, B = F.B;
  bxPBox(F, 1, A - 1, 1, B - 1, 0, 7, (face, u, v) => (face === F_TOP ? C.WD3 : v < 1.2 ? C.WD1 : face === F_Y ? C.WD2 : C.WD1), C.WD0);
  const rec = bxAt(F, A * 0.58, B * 0.6);
  disc(rec[0], rec[1], 3.5, 7.2, (face, dx, dy) => { const r = Math.hypot(dx, dy); return r < 0.9 ? C.RD2 : r > 2.6 && r < 3.0 ? C.OUT : C.DEEP; }, C.OUT, false);
  for (let k = 0; k <= 6; k++) { const p = bxAt(F, A - 2 - k * 0.45, B - 2 - k * 0.55); dot3(p[0], p[1], 8, C.BR2, 0); }   // the tone arm
  const brass = bxRingSh(C.BR3, C.BR2, C.BR1);
  const steps = [[0.7, 7, 9, 2.5, 3], [1.2, 9, 10, 3, 4], [1.8, 10, 11.5, 3.6, 5], [2.6, 11.5, 13, 4.2, 5.8], [3.4, 13, 14.5, 4.8, 6.4], [4.1, 14.5, 16, 5.2, 6.8], [4.7, 16, 17, 5.5, 7]];
  for (const [r, z0, z1, a, b] of steps) bxPCyl(F, a, b, r, z0, z1, brass, C.BR0, false);
  bxPDisc(F, 5.5, 7, 4.0, 17, () => C.DEEP, C.BR0);                                                     // the horn's mouth
}
function bxRug(P) {                                   // a woven rag rug: uneven stripes across it, fringes at the ends
  const W = P.x1 - P.x0, D = P.y1 - P.y0, long = W >= D, L = long ? W : D;
  const BANDS = [C.KRAFT2, C.RD1, C.CREAM, C.KRAFT];
  shFlag = FL_ROOM; shSunMax = 2; shBias = 2;
  faceTop(P.x0, P.y0, P.x1, P.y1, 0.6, (face, u, v) => {
    const s = long ? u : v, t = long ? v : u, w = long ? D : W;
    if (s < 1.5 || s >= L - 1.5) return (Math.floor(t) & 1) && t > 0.6 && t < w - 0.6 ? C.PAPSH : -1;   // the fringe
    if (t < 0.8 || t >= w - 0.8) return C.WD1;                                                        // the selvedge
    const k = Math.floor((s - 1.5) / 5.5) % BANDS.length;
    return BANDS[k];
  }, C.WD1, 0);
  shBias = 0;
}
function bxBucket(it) {                               // galvanised, a little water, its handle up
  const cx = (it.x0 + it.x1) / 2, cy = (it.y0 + it.y1) / 2;
  cylinder(cx, cy, 3.5, 0, 6, (face, th, h) => (h > 5.2 ? C.FT2 : face === F_X ? C.PL0 : (Math.floor(h) === 2 ? C.PL0 : C.PL1)), C.FT0, true);
  disc(cx, cy, 3.0, 5.4, (face, dx, dy) => (Math.hypot(dx - 0.8, dy - 0.8) < 0.9 ? C.BL4 : C.BL2), C.BL1, false);
  for (let k = 0; k <= 8; k++) { const a = Math.PI * k / 8; dot3(cx + Math.cos(a) * 3.4, cy - Math.cos(a) * 0.2, 6 + Math.sin(a) * 1.8, C.FT0, 0); }
}

// ---- the seasonal items ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
function bxJamJar(it) {                               // glass, water, three stems, flowers
  const cx = (it.x0 + it.x1) / 2, cy = (it.y0 + it.y1) / 2, z = it.z0;
  cylinder(cx, cy, 1.7, z, z + 4.6, (face, th, h) => (h < 2.8 ? C.BL3 : th > 1.2 ? C.WHITE : C.PL2), C.PL0, true);
  const F = [[-0.7, 0.2, C.RD3], [0.6, -0.4, C.SUN0], [0.1, 0.8, C.BL3]];
  for (const [dx, dy, c] of F) {
    for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) dot3(cx + dx * k / 4, cy + dy * k / 4, z + 4.6 + k, C.PT1, 0);
    dot3(cx + dx, cy + dy, z + 8.8, c, 0); dot3(cx + dx + 0.6, cy + dy - 0.6, z + 8.8, c, 0); dot3(cx + dx, cy + dy, z + 9.6, c, 0);
  }
  dot3(cx + 0.8, cy + 0.2, z + 6, C.PT2, 0);
}
function bxSeedlings(it) {                            // a grey egg box, a sprout in each cup
  const L = it.x1 - it.x0 >= it.y1 - it.y0, n = 6, z = it.z0;
  shFlag = FL_ROOM;
  box(it.x0, it.y0, z, it.x1, it.y1, z + 2.4, (face, u, v) => {
    if (face !== F_TOP) return face === F_Y ? C.PAPSH : C.FT0;
    const s = L ? u : v, t = L ? v : u, cs = (L ? it.x1 - it.x0 : it.y1 - it.y0) / n, ct = (L ? it.y1 - it.y0 : it.x1 - it.x0) / 2;
    const ds = s % cs - cs / 2, dt = t % ct - ct / 2;
    return ds * ds + dt * dt < 1.6 ? C.FT0 : C.PL1;
  }, C.FT0, 3);
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = 0; j < 2; j++) {
    const along = (i + 0.5) / n, across = (j + 0.5) / 2, x = L ? it.x0 + along * (it.x1 - it.x0) : it.x0 + across * (it.x1 - it.x0), y = L ? it.y0 + across * (it.y1 - it.y0) : it.y0 + along * (it.y1 - it.y0);
    const h = 2 + Math.floor(hash2(i * 3 + j, 9) * 2.5);
    for (let k = 0; k < h; k++) dot3(x, y, z + 2.4 + k, C.PT2, 0);
    dot3(x - 0.6, y + 0.6, z + 2.4 + h, C.PT3, 0); dot3(x + 0.6, y - 0.6, z + 2.4 + h, C.PT3, 0);
  }
}
function bxPumpkin(it) {                              // ribbed, a stalk and a curl of vine
  const cx = (it.x0 + it.x1) / 2, cy = (it.y0 + it.y1) / 2;
  const prof = [2.8, 3.9, 4.4, 4.5, 4.3, 3.8, 2.6];
  prof.forEach((r, z) => cylinder(cx, cy, r, z, z + 1, (face, th, h) => {
    const rib = Math.floor(fract(th / (TAU / 10)) * 10) < 2;
    return z >= 5 && th > 0.8 ? C.EV1 : rib ? C.SS0 : face === F_X ? C.SS0 : C.EV0;
  }, C.CTD, z === 0));
  cylinder(cx, cy, 0.7, 7, 8.4, () => C.WD1, C.WD0, false);
  dot3(cx + 1, cy - 0.5, 7.6, C.PT2, 0); dot3(cx + 1.6, cy - 0.2, 7.2, C.PT2, 0);
}
function bxBoxTree(it) {                              // boxes stacked into a tree, paw-print baubles, a star on top
  const cx = (it.x0 + it.x1) / 2, cy = (it.y0 + it.y1) / 2, W = Math.min(it.x1 - it.x0, it.y1 - it.y0);
  const tiers = [[W, 0, 9], [W * 0.72, 9, 17], [W * 0.46, 17, 24], [W * 0.24, 24, 27.6]];
  shFlag = FL_ROOM;
  tiers.forEach(([w, z0, z1], k) => {
    const h = w / 2;
    box(cx - h, cy - h, z0, cx + h, cy + h, z1, (face, u, v) => {
      if (face === F_TOP) return Math.abs(u - h) < 0.6 ? C.KRAFT0 : C.KRAFT2;
      if (hash2(Math.floor(u / 3) + k * 5, Math.floor(v / 3) + 11) > 0.86 && v > 1 && v < z1 - z0 - 2) return [C.RD2, C.BL2, C.SUN0][(Math.floor(u / 3) + k) % 3];
      return face === F_Y ? C.KRAFT : C.KRAFT0;
    }, C.WD1, 3);
  });
  for (const [dx, dz] of [[0, 28.6], [0, 29.8], [0.7, 29.2], [-0.7, 29.2]]) dot3(cx + dx * 0.5, cy - dx * 0.5, dz, C.BR3, 0);
}
function bxFan(it) {                                  // a pedestal fan facing the board; its blades turn (drawBoxDressing)
  const cx = (it.x0 + it.x1) / 2, cy = (it.y0 + it.y1) / 2, metal = bxRingSh(C.PL2, C.PL1, C.PL0);
  cylinder(cx, cy, 3.2, 0, 1.2, metal, C.FT0, true);
  cylinder(cx, cy, 0.6, 1.2, 11, metal, C.FT0, false);
  cylinder(cx, cy, 1.4, 11, 14, metal, C.FT0, false);
  bxFanPlane(it, (face, u, v) => {                     // the guard: a ring and four spokes
    const r = Math.hypot(u - 4.2, v - 4.2);
    if (r > 4.2) return -1;
    if (r > 3.4 || r < 0.9 || Math.abs(u - 4.2) < 0.4 || Math.abs(v - 4.2) < 0.4) return r < 0.9 ? C.PL0 : C.PL2;
    return -1;
  }, 0.8);
}
// The fan's vertical plane (8.4 wu across, centred at z 13), facing along its dir: shader(face, u, v) with u across
// and v up; front: how far in front of the stand.
function bxFanPlane(it, sh, front) {
  const cx = (it.x0 + it.x1) / 2, cy = (it.y0 + it.y1) / 2, d = it.dir || 'R';
  if (d === 'R' || d === 'L') faceX(cy - 4.2, cy + 4.2, 8.8, 17.2, cx + (d === 'R' ? front : -front), sh, C.FT0, 0);
  else faceY(cx - 4.2, cx + 4.2, 8.8, 17.2, cy + (d === 'D' ? front : -front), sh, C.FT0, 0);
}
// Blankets: a knitted throw over the top of a box and down its front.
function bxBlanket(b) {
  shFlag = FL_ROOM;
  const L = b.wall === 'L', h = b.z1 - b.z0;
  faceTop(b.x0 + 0.4, b.y0 + 0.4, b.x1 - 0.4, b.y1 - 0.4, b.z1 + 0.4, bxKnit, C.WD1, 0);
  if (L) faceX(b.y0 + 1, b.y1 - 1, b.z1 - h * 0.6, b.z1 + 0.4, b.x1 + 0.4, bxKnit, C.WD1, 6);
  else faceY(b.x0 + 1, b.x1 - 1, b.z1 - h * 0.6, b.z1 + 0.4, b.y1 + 0.4, bxKnit, C.WD1, 6);
}
// Bunting along the roof band's top edges: triangles in three colours.
function bxBunting(R) {
  const P = R.room, COL = [C.RD2, C.BL2, C.CREAM];
  shFlag = FL_ROOM; shSunMax = 0;
  for (const f of R.flags) {
    const tri = (face, u, v) => (Math.abs(u - 2) <= v * 0.5 ? COL[f[3]] : -1);
    if (f[0] === P.roofRun) faceX(f[1] - 2, f[1] + 2, f[2] - 4, f[2], f[0] + 0.5, tri, C.OUT, 0);
    else faceY(f[0] - 2, f[0] + 2, f[2] - 4, f[2], f[1] + 0.5, tri, C.OUT, 0);
    dot3(f[0], f[1], f[2] + 0.4, C.DEEP, 0);
  }
}

// ---- baking -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
const BX_PROP = { trolley: bxTrolley, coatStand: bxCoatStand, mailbag: bxMailbag, armchair: bxArmchair, gramophone: bxGramophone, rug: bxRug };
const BX_ITEM = { jamJar: bxJamJar, seedlings: bxSeedlings, pumpkin: bxPumpkin, boxTree: bxBoxTree, fan: bxFan, bucket: bxBucket };
function bxBakeProps(R) {
  for (const p of R.props) { shFlat = false; shFlag = FL_ROOM; shSunMax = 1; shBias = 0; BX_PROP[p.kind](p); }
  shFlat = false; shBias = 0; shSunMax = 2;
}
function bxBakeDressing(R) {
  for (const it of R.items) {
    shFlat = false; shFlag = FL_ROOM; shSunMax = 1; shBias = 0;
    if (BX_ITEM[it.kind]) BX_ITEM[it.kind](Object.assign({ dir: bxFaceBoard(it) }, it));
  }
  for (const i of R.dressing.blankets) { shSunMax = 1; bxBlanket(R.boxes[i]); }
  if (R.dressing.bunting) bxBunting(R);
  shFlat = false; shBias = 0; shSunMax = 2;
}
function bxFaceBoard(it) {                            // the way an item turns to face the board
  const B = BOXR.board, cx = B.x + B.w * TILE / 2 - (it.x0 + it.x1) / 2, cy = B.y + B.h * TILE / 2 - (it.y0 + it.y1) / 2;
  return Math.abs(cx) >= Math.abs(cy) ? (cx >= 0 ? 'R' : 'L') : (cy >= 0 ? 'D' : 'U');
}

// ---- the skylights' dressing, in the glass (34_boxroom's skylight shader asks here) -----------------------------
// Returns a colour for a pixel of glass at (lu across, lv up the slope) in a skylight W x L, or -1 for the plain sky.
function bxGlassDressing(s, lu, lv, W, L) {
  const d = BOXR.dressing.skylights;
  if (d === 'snow') {                                 // lying in the lower part of the glass, and a few flakes
    const edge = L * 0.3 + Math.sin(lu * 0.25) * 1.2;
    if (lv < edge) return lv > edge - 1 ? C.PAPSH : C.WHITE;
    if (hash2(Math.floor(lu * 1.3) + 17, Math.floor(lv * 1.3)) > 0.96) return C.WHITE;
  } else if (d === 'rain') {                          // streaks running down the glass
    const k = Math.floor((lu + lv * 0.25) / 3.2);
    if (Math.abs(((lu + lv * 0.25) % 3.2) - 1.6) < 0.5 && hash2(k, Math.floor(lv / 5)) > 0.45) return C.PL2;
  }
  return -1;
}

// ---- each frame: the moving bits ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
function drawBoxDressing(t) {
  const R = BOXR;
  for (const it of R.items) {
    if (it.kind === 'fan') bxFanBlades(Object.assign({ dir: bxFaceBoard(it) }, it), t);
    else if (it.kind === 'sleepingCat') bxFlatCat(it, t);
    else if (it.kind === 'bucket') bxDrip(it, t);
  }
  if (R.dressing.skylights === 'rain') bxRainDrops(t);
  else if (R.dressing.skylights === 'leaves') bxLeaves(t);
}
function bxFanBlades(it, t) {                         // three blades turning once every 0.4 s
  shFlat = false; shFlag = 0; shSunMax = 1; shBias = 1;
  const a0 = t * TAU / 0.4;
  bxFanPlane(it, (face, u, v) => {
    const du = u - 4.2, dv = v - 4.2, r = Math.hypot(du, dv);
    if (r > 3.3 || r < 1.0) return -1;
    const a = fract((Math.atan2(dv, du) - a0) / (TAU / 3));
    return a < 0.22 ? C.BL2 : a < 0.3 ? C.BL1 : -1;
  }, 0.4);
  shBias = 0;
}
// A cat stretched out flat in the summer heat: one of the collection (36_coats), breathing every 3 s.
function bxFlatCat(it, t) {
  const along = it.x1 - it.x0 >= it.y1 - it.y0;     // head towards the screen-left end of its slot
  buildNapSprite(it.cat || 'greyTabby', 'flat', fract(t / 3) < 0.4 ? 1 : 0, it.x0 * 7 + it.y0);
  shSunMax = 1; shFlag = 0; shBias = 0;
  blitSprite((it.x0 + it.x1) / 2, (it.y0 + it.y1) / 2, 0, along, true);
}
// Rainy days: a drop forms under the roof above the bucket, falls, and rings the water; every 2.4 s.
function bxDrip(it, t) {
  const cx = (it.x0 + it.x1) / 2, cy = (it.y0 + it.y1) / 2, top = BOXROOM.ceilAt(cx, cy) - 1, ph = fract(t / 2.4);
  shFlat = true; shFlag = 0; shBias = 3;
  if (ph < 0.25) dot3(cx, cy, top, C.BL3, 0);                                  // forming
  else if (ph < 0.55) dot3(cx, cy, top - (top - 6) * ((ph - 0.25) / 0.3) ** 2, C.BL4, 0);   // falling
  else if (ph < 0.75) { const r = 0.8 + (ph - 0.55) * 8; for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) dot3(cx + Math.cos(k * TAU / 8) * r, cy + Math.sin(k * TAU / 8) * r, 5.6, C.BL4, 0); }
  shFlat = false; shBias = 0;
}
// A point on a skylight's glass, a hair inside the room: (lu across, lv up the slope).
function bxGlassPoint(s, lu, lv) {
  const v = s.v0 + lv, u = s.u0 + lu, z = BOXR.room.wallH + v * BX_PITCH;
  return s.wall === 'L' ? [v + 0.3, u, z - 0.2] : [u, v + 0.3, z - 0.2];
}
function bxRainDrops(t) {                             // a few bright drops running down each skylight
  shFlat = true; shFlag = 0; shBias = 8;
  BOXR.skylights.forEach((s, i) => {
    const W = s.u1 - s.u0, L = s.v1 - s.v0;
    for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
      const lu = 2 + hash2(i * 9 + k, 1) * (W - 4), lv = L - 2 - fract(t / (1.2 + k * 0.3) + hash2(k, i)) * (L - 4);
      const p = bxGlassPoint(s, lu, lv); dot3(p[0], p[1], p[2], C.WHITE, 0);
    }
  });
  shFlat = false; shBias = 0;
}
function bxLeaves(t) {                                // autumn leaves tumbling past the glass
  const COL = [C.RD2, C.EV0, C.BR2, C.SS0];
  shFlat = false; shFlag = 0; shBias = 8; shSunMax = 0;
  BOXR.skylights.forEach((s, i) => {
    const W = s.u1 - s.u0, L = s.v1 - s.v0;
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
      const f = fract(t / (3 + k * 1.3) + hash2(i, k * 7));
      const lu = 1.5 + f * (W - 3), lv = L * (0.3 + 0.5 * hash2(k, i + 3)) + Math.sin(f * TAU * 2 + k) * 2.5;
      if (lv < 1.6 || lv > L - 1.6) continue;
      const p = bxGlassPoint(s, lu, lv), q = bxGlassPoint(s, lu + 0.9, lv + 0.9);
      dot3(p[0], p[1], p[2], COL[(i + k) % 4], 0); dot3(q[0], q[1], q[2], COL[(i + k + 1) % 4], 0);
    }
  });
  shBias = 0;
}
// ============================================================================
// 36 COATS: the collection's cats (src/data/coats.json): their napping poses, drawn with region letters
// (o body, O its light back, d its shaded underside, h head, H its light top, t tail, p paws, w chin and chest,
// e/z eyes open/closed, n nose, i inner ear), and each coat's colours and pattern: solid, tabby stripes,
// calico patches, tortoiseshell mottling or Siamese points. A pattern only reads the pixel's letter and place
// in its template (and a seed, so two calicos differ), so a port builds the same sprites.
// Side views facing screen-left, mirrored for screen-right; each pose breathes on frame 1.
// ============================================================================
const COATS = COATS_DATA.coats, COAT_IDS = Object.keys(COATS);
const NAP_POSES = {
  // curled up, the tail wrapped along the front
  curl: { rows: [
    '.h.h........',
    '.hhhhOooo...',
    'hHzHhOOOood.',
    'hwHhooddood.',
    '.wwpooooood.',
    '.ttttttttt..',
  ], breath: [7, 0], ear: [3, 0], tail: [[1, 5], [0, 4]] },
  // a loaf facing the room: both ears up, eyes shut, paws tucked, the tail round one side
  loaf: { rows: [
    '.h.....h..',
    '.hh...hh..',
    '.hHHHHHh..',
    'hhzhhhzhh.',
    'hhhwnwhhh.',
    '.oOwwwOoo.',
    '.ooopoooot',
    '..dddddtt.',
  ], breath: [2, 5], ear: [7, 0], tail: [[9, 6], [9, 5]] },
  // stretched out flat on its side, legs out
  flat: { rows: [
    '.h.h............',
    'hhhhh...........',
    'hzhHoOOOOOOOOO..',
    'nwhooooooooooot.',
    '.wwooddddddddtt.',
    '..pp.p....pp....',
  ], breath: [8, 1], ear: [3, 0], tail: [[14, 4], [15, 3]] },
};
const NAP_POSE_IDS = Object.keys(NAP_POSES);
const COAT_STRIPABLE = 'oOhHt';
// The palette colour of one template pixel in a coat (EMPTY for '.').
function coatPixel(coat, ch, x, y, seed) {
  if (ch === '.') return EMPTY;
  const K = COATS[coat] || COATS.greyTabby;
  const base = (k) => C[K[k]];
  let c;
  switch (ch) {
    case 'h': c = K.h ? base('h') : base('o'); break;
    case 'H': c = K.H ? base('H') : base('O'); break;
    case 't': case 'p': c = K[ch] ? base(ch) : base('o'); break;
    default: c = base(ch);
  }
  switch (K.pattern) {
    case 'tabby':                                      // slanting stripes on the body, a forehead M, rings on the tail
      if (COAT_STRIPABLE.includes(ch)) {
        if (ch === 't' ? x % 2 === 0 : ch === 'h' || ch === 'H' ? y <= 2 && x % 2 === 1 : (x + (y >> 1)) % 3 === 0) c = base('s');
      }
      break;
    case 'calico': {                                   // white with big patches of orange and black
      if (!'oOdhHt'.includes(ch)) break;
      const v = hash2(Math.floor(x / 3) + seed * 13, Math.floor(y / 2) + seed * 7);
      const dark = ch === 'd', light = ch === 'O' || ch === 'H';
      if (v < 0.34) c = dark ? C.CO0 : C[K.patches[0]];
      else if (v < 0.58) c = dark ? C.OUT : light ? C.COOL1 : C[K.patches[1]];
      break;
    }
    case 'tortie': {                                   // black mottled with rust and apricot, in little clumps
      if (!'oOdhHt'.includes(ch)) break;
      const v = hash2(Math.floor(x / 2) + seed * 5, y + seed * 3), light = ch === 'O' || ch === 'H';
      if (v < 0.42) c = ch === 'd' ? C.CO0 : light ? C[K.mottle[1]] : C[K.mottle[0]];
      break;
    }
    case 'points':                                     // Siamese: face, ears, tail and paws dark
      if (K.points[ch]) c = C[K.points[ch]];
      break;
  }
  return c;
}
// Build a napping cat into the sprite buffer (anchored at its feet, as every sprite is), outlined.
// frame bits: 1 breathing in (the back rises), 2 an ear flicked down, 4 the tail tip flicked up.
function buildNapSprite(coat, pose, frame, seed) {
  const P = NAP_POSES[pose] || NAP_POSES.curl, rows = P.rows, w = rows[0].length, x0 = AX - (w >> 1), y0 = AY - rows.length + 1;
  sprClear();
  for (let y = 0; y < rows.length; y++) for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
    const c = coatPixel(coat, rows[y][x], x, y, seed | 0);
    if (c !== EMPTY) sp(x0 + x, y0 + y, c);
  }
  if (frame & 1) sp(x0 + P.breath[0], y0 + P.breath[1] - 1, coatPixel(coat, 'O', P.breath[0], 0, seed | 0));   // breathing in: the back rises
  if (frame & 2) sp(x0 + P.ear[0], y0 + P.ear[1], EMPTY);                                                       // an ear flicks
  if (frame & 4) {                                                                                              // the tail tip flicks up
    const [a, b] = P.tail, c = coatPixel(coat, 't', a[0], a[1], seed | 0);
    sp(x0 + a[0], y0 + a[1], EMPTY); sp(x0 + b[0], y0 + b[1], c);
  }
  outlineSprite();
}
// Review and tests: a coat's sprite in a scratch cell (the atlas's), at neutral light or lit: { idx, lvl, w, h }.
function coatCell(coat, pose, frame, seed, lit) {
  atlasCell();
  buildNapSprite(coat, pose, frame, seed);
  atlasBlit(8, 8, 0, false);
  const idx = new Uint8Array(AC * AC), lvl = new Uint8Array(AC * AC);
  for (let i = 0; i < AC * AC; i++) { idx[i] = aIdx[i]; lvl[i] = aIdx[i] === 255 ? 2 : lit ? 3 : 2; }
  return { idx, lvl, w: AC, h: AC };
}
// The board's own cats in the same pose, for comparison: ginger and blue, curled.
function boardCatCell(coatConst, lit) {
  atlasCell();
  KEY = coatKeyOf(coatConst); sprClear(); tpl(CAT_CURL, AX - 6, AY - 5); KEY = KEY_S; outlineSprite();
  atlasBlit(8, 8, 0, false);
  const idx = new Uint8Array(AC * AC), lvl = new Uint8Array(AC * AC);
  for (let i = 0; i < AC * AC; i++) { idx[i] = aIdx[i]; lvl[i] = aIdx[i] === 255 ? 2 : lit ? 3 : 2; }
  return { idx, lvl, w: AC, h: AC };
}
// ============================================================================
// 37 BOX ROOM ATLAS: every Box Room piece for the S atlas (33_atlas), drawn by the room's own shaders at neutral
// light: the boxes in each size facing either wall, with their variants (knocked askew, each open flap, a label, a
// paw print, string, a blanket); a brick gap with and without its round window; a skylight by day, at night,
// open, snowed on and rained on; the roof's boards, rafters and cross beams; lamps off and on and the string
// lights' bulbs; every prop facing each way; the seasonal things; the bunting, a raindrop, a leaf; and the
// collection's napping cats in every coat and pose. Each piece is drawn with its own origin at the atlas cell's
// reference point, so the anchor is the pixel of that origin: the min corner (x0, y0, z0) of its box in the room
// data (boxes, props, items), the bottom back corner of a wall panel, the low back corner of a skylight on its
// slope, the bottom centre of a lamp's shade, a napping cat's feet.
// ============================================================================
function bxAtlasRoom() {                              // a stand-in room for the shaders: nothing in the way
  return { room: { w: 200, d: 200, wallH: 48, stackD: 8, roofRun: 40, roofTop: 108 }, board: { x: 1000, y: 1000, w: 1, h: 1 },
    boxes: [], columns: [], gaps: [], rafters: { L: [], R: [], w: 3 }, skylights: [], props: [], items: [], naps: [], flags: [], bulbs: [],
    dressing: { skylights: null, blankets: [], bunting: false, openSkylight: false }, light: { set: 'fri', sun: false, lamps: 0, string: false } };
}
function boxAtlasSprites() {
  const S = [], spr = (name, direction, mirroredFor, fps, loop, frames) => S.push({ name, direction, mirroredFor, fps, loop, frames });
  const fr = (tag, draw) => ({ tag, draw });
  // run a draw with a stand-in room (and the room's light set, for the night sky), then put things back
  const inRoom = (tweak, draw) => () => {
    const keep = { R: BOXR, set: bxSet, above: bxAbove };
    BOXR = bxAtlasRoom(); if (tweak) tweak(BOXR); bxSet = BOXR.light.set; bxAbove = new Int16Array([-1]);
    try { draw(); } finally { BOXR = keep.R; bxSet = keep.set; bxAbove = keep.above; shFlat = false; shBias = 0; }
  };
  // ---- boxes: each size, facing the left wall (front +x) or the right (front +y) -----------------------------
  CFG_BOXES().forEach((sz, k) => {
    for (const wall of ['L', 'R']) {
      const mk = (o) => Object.assign({ x0: 8, y0: 8, z0: 0, x1: 8 + (wall === 'L' ? sz.dep : sz.len), y1: 8 + (wall === 'L' ? sz.len : sz.dep), z1: sz.h,
        size: k, wall, col: -1, lvl: 0, nudge: false, flap: 0, string: 0, label: null, paw: null }, o);
      const draw = (o, blanket) => inRoom(null, () => {
        const b = mk(o); bxCur = b; bxCurI = 0; shFlag = FL_ROOM; shSunMax = 1;
        box(b.x0, b.y0, b.z0, b.x1, b.y1, b.z1, shBxBox, C.WD1, 3);
        if (b.flap) bxFlap(b);
        if (blanket) bxBlanket(b);
      });
      spr('box_' + sz.name + '_' + wall, wall === 'L' ? 'E' : 'S', 'none', 0, false, [
        fr('plain', draw({})), fr('rotated', draw({ nudge: true })), fr('flap_back', draw({ flap: 1 })), fr('flap_front', draw({ flap: 2 })),
        fr('flap_end', draw({ flap: 3 })), fr('label', draw({ label: [2, 2] })), fr('paw', draw({ paw: [2, 2] })), fr('string', draw({ string: 3 })),
        fr('blanket', draw({}, true))]);
    }
  });
  // ---- the knee walls: a brick gap, with and without its round window ---------------------------------------
  for (const wall of ['L', 'R']) {
    const panel = (win, set) => inRoom((R) => { R.light.set = set || 'fri'; if (win) R.gaps = [{ wall, u0: 8, u1: 32, window: { u: 20, z: 30, r: 7 } }]; }, () => {
      bxWall = wall; shFlag = FL_ROOM; shSunMax = 0;
      if (wall === 'L') faceX(8, 32, 0, 48, 8, shBxWall, C.WD0, 0); else faceY(8, 32, 0, 48, 8, shBxWall, C.WD0, 0);
    });
    spr('brick_gap_' + wall, wall === 'L' ? 'E' : 'S', 'none', 0, false, [fr('brick', panel(false)), fr('window', panel(true)), fr('window_night', panel(true, 'night'))]);
  }
  // ---- the roof: a skylight in each slope, its boards, rafters and the cross beams ---------------------------------
  // On its slope, lowered so its low back corner sits at the reference point (z 0 at v = 8).
  const onSlope = (wall, u0, u1, v0, v1, sh) => { const keep = BOXR.room.wallH; BOXR.room.wallH = -BX_PITCH * v0; faceSlope(wall, u0, u1, v0, v1, sh, false); BOXR.room.wallH = keep; };
  for (const wall of ['L', 'R']) {
    const sky = (set, dress, open) => inRoom((R) => { R.light.set = set; R.dressing.skylights = dress || null; }, () => {
      const s = { wall, u0: 8, u1: 34, v0: 8, v1: 32, panes: [2, 2], open: !!open };
      BOXR.skylights = [s]; shFlag = FL_ROOM; shSunMax = 0;
      onSlope(wall, s.u0, s.u1, s.v0, s.v1, (face, u, v) => bxSkylightPx(s, u - s.u0, v - s.v0));
    });
    spr('skylight_' + wall, wall === 'L' ? 'E' : 'S', 'none', 0, false, [fr('day', sky('fri')), fr('night', sky('night')), fr('sunset', sky('sun')),
      fr('open', sky('fri', null, true)), fr('snow', sky('fri', 'snow')), fr('rain', sky('fri', 'rain'))]);
    spr('roof_' + wall, wall === 'L' ? 'E' : 'S', 'none', 0, false, [
      fr('boards', inRoom(null, () => { bxSlopeWall = wall; shSunMax = 0; onSlope(wall, 8, 32, 8, 32, (face, u, v) => { shLvl = -1; const k = Math.floor(v / 6); return v - k * 6 < 2 ? C.WD1 : hash2(Math.floor((u + k * 13) / 23), k) < 0.25 ? C.WD1 : C.WD2; }); })),
      fr('rafter', inRoom(null, () => { shSunMax = 0; onSlope(wall, 8, 11, 8, 48, (face, u, v) => (u - 8 < 0.8 || u - 8 >= 2.2 ? C.OUT : C.DEEP)); }))]);
  }
  spr('beam', 'none', 'none', 0, false, [
    fr('along_y', inRoom(null, () => { shSunMax = 0; box(8, 8, 0, 12, 40, 4, shBxBeam, C.OUT, 7); })),
    fr('along_x', inRoom(null, () => { shSunMax = 0; box(8, 8, 0, 40, 12, 4, shBxBeam, C.OUT, 7); }))]);
  // ---- lamps and string lights -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  spr('lamp', 'none', 'none', 0, false, [false, true].map((on) => fr(on ? 'on' : 'off', inRoom(null, () => bxLamp({ x: 8, y: 8, z0: 0, z1: 8, r: 5 }, on, 14)))));
  spr('string_bulb', 'none', 'none', 0, false, [[C.GL0, 'warm'], [C.GL1, 'rose'], [C.GL2, 'cool'], [C.BR2, 'warm_dip'], [C.RD2, 'rose_dip'], [C.BL2, 'cool_dip']]
    .map(([c, t]) => fr(t, () => { shFlat = true; dot3(8, 8, 0, c, 0); shFlat = false; })));
  // ---- props, each facing the four ways (mailbag: slumped against either wall's stacks) --------------------------
  const PROPS = { trolley: [12, 22, 16], coatStand: [8, 8, 44], armchair: [18, 18, 18], gramophone: [12, 12, 18] };   // across, depth, height
  for (const kind of Object.keys(PROPS)) {
    for (const dir of ['R', 'D', 'L', 'U']) {
      const [A, B, H] = PROPS[kind], along = dir === 'R' || dir === 'L';
      const P = { kind, x0: 8, y0: 8, z0: 0, x1: 8 + (along ? B : A), y1: 8 + (along ? A : B), z1: H, dir };
      spr(kind + '_' + { R: 'E', D: 'S', L: 'W', U: 'N' }[dir], { R: 'E', D: 'S', L: 'W', U: 'N' }[dir], 'none', 0, false,
        [fr('rest', inRoom(null, () => { shFlag = FL_ROOM; shSunMax = 1; BX_PROP[kind](P); }))]);
    }
  }
  for (const wall of ['L', 'R']) {
    const P = { kind: 'mailbag', x0: 8, y0: 8, z0: 0, x1: 8 + (wall === 'L' ? 10 : 12), y1: 8 + (wall === 'L' ? 12 : 10), z1: 10, dir: wall === 'L' ? 'R' : 'D' };
    spr('mailbag_' + wall, wall === 'L' ? 'E' : 'S', 'none', 0, false, [fr('rest', inRoom((R) => { R.room.stackD = 8; }, () => {
      shFlag = FL_ROOM; shSunMax = 1;
      if (wall === 'R') { P.x0 = 9; P.x1 = 21; }            // touches y = stackD only, so it slumps towards the right wall
      bxMailbag(P);
    }))]);
  }
  spr('rug', 'none', 'none', 0, false, [[26, 16, 'along_x'], [16, 26, 'along_y']].map(([w, d, t]) => fr(t, inRoom(null, () => bxRug({ x0: 8, y0: 8, x1: 8 + w, y1: 8 + d })))));
  // ---- the seasons: everything in its slot, the fan facing four ways with its blades turning ------------------------
  const item = (kind, w, d, h, dir) => ({ kind, x0: 8, y0: 8, z0: 0, x1: 8 + w, y1: 8 + d, z1: h, dir: dir || 'R' });
  spr('jam_jar', 'none', 'none', 0, false, [fr('rest', inRoom(null, () => { shFlag = FL_ROOM; shSunMax = 1; bxJamJar(item('jamJar', 4, 4, 10)); }))]);
  spr('seedlings', 'none', 'none', 0, false, [[12, 6, 'along_x'], [6, 12, 'along_y']].map(([w, d, t]) => fr(t, inRoom(null, () => { shSunMax = 1; bxSeedlings(item('seedlings', w, d, 6)); }))));
  spr('pumpkin', 'none', 'none', 0, false, [fr('rest', inRoom(null, () => { shFlag = FL_ROOM; shSunMax = 1; bxPumpkin(item('pumpkin', 10, 10, 8)); }))]);
  spr('box_tree', 'none', 'none', 0, false, [fr('rest', inRoom(null, () => { shSunMax = 1; bxBoxTree(item('boxTree', 14, 14, 30)); }))]);
  spr('bucket', 'none', 'none', 0, false, [fr('rest', inRoom(null, () => { shFlag = FL_ROOM; shSunMax = 1; bxBucket(item('bucket', 8, 8, 8)); }))]);
  spr('drip', 'none', 'none', 10, false, [fr('drop', () => { shFlat = true; dot3(8, 8, 0, C.BL4, 0); shFlat = false; }),
    ...[0.8, 1.6].map((r, k) => fr('ring' + k, () => { shFlat = true; for (let q = 0; q < 8; q++) dot3(8 + Math.cos(q * TAU / 8) * r, 8 + Math.sin(q * TAU / 8) * r, 0, C.BL4, 0); shFlat = false; }))]);
  for (const dir of ['R', 'D', 'L', 'U']) {
    const it = item('fan', 10, 8, 18, dir), d = { R: 'E', D: 'S', L: 'W', U: 'N' }[dir];
    spr('fan_' + d, d, 'none', 0, false, [fr('rest', inRoom(null, () => { shFlag = FL_ROOM; shSunMax = 1; bxFan(it); }))]);
    spr('fan_blades_' + d, d, 'none', 15, true, [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((k) => fr('turn' + k, inRoom(null, () => bxFanBlades(it, k * 0.4 / 18)))));
  }
  spr('bunting', 'none', 'none', 0, false, [C.RD2, C.BL2, C.CREAM].map((c, k) => fr(['red', 'blue', 'cream'][k], () => {
    shFlag = FL_ROOM; shSunMax = 0;
    faceX(8, 12, 0, 4, 8, (face, u, v) => (Math.abs(u - 2) <= v * 0.5 ? c : -1), C.OUT, 0);
  })));
  spr('raindrop', 'none', 'none', 0, false, [fr('drop', () => { shFlat = true; dot3(8, 8, 0, C.WHITE, 0); shFlat = false; })]);
  spr('leaf', 'none', 'none', 0, false, [[C.RD2, C.EV0], [C.EV0, C.BR2], [C.BR2, C.SS0], [C.SS0, C.RD2]].map(([a, b], k) => fr('colour' + k, () => {
    dot3(8, 8, 0, a, 0); dot3(8.9, 8.9, 0.9, b, 0);
  })));
  // ---- the collection: every coat in every napping pose, resting, breathing, an ear and the tail flicking ------------
  for (const coat of COAT_IDS) for (const pose of NAP_POSE_IDS) {
    spr('nap_' + coat + '_' + pose, 'S', 'E', 10, true, [[0, 'rest'], [1, 'breath'], [2, 'ear'], [4, 'tail']].map(([f, t]) => fr(t, () => {
      buildNapSprite(coat, pose, f, 0); atlasBlit(8, 8, 0, false);
    })));
  }
  return S;
}
function CFG_BOXES() { return BOXROOM.CFG.boxes; }
// ============================================================================
// 38 BACKDROP: cached indexed sky and skyline layers. Empty depth only; the room keeps all its pixels.
// The strip repeats in x; each layer has a camera factor and an optional speed (logical px/second).
// No buffers or building data are allocated while sampling. Swift/Metal: indexed textures behind the room.
// ============================================================================
let BD = null;
let backdropTreatment = null;                              // null uses the room profile; review/query can override it
function useBackdrop(room) {
  const profile=room && room.backdrop && BACKDROP.CFG[room.backdrop.id];
  BD = room ? { scene: BACKDROP.build(room.inputs ? room.inputs.seed : 202, profile), set: room.light.set, cache: null, base: new Int16Array(WW).fill(-1), elapsed: 0 } : null;
  if (BD && BD.scene.cutaway) BD.cutaway = { ...BD.scene.cutaway, ...BD.scene.cutaway.lights[BD.set] };
  if (BD && BD.scene.openings) BD.openings=true;
}
const bdMod = (n, d) => ((n % d) + d) % d;
function bakeBackdropLayers(scene, set, height) {
  const W = scene.width, H = Math.max(512, Math.ceil(height)), top = -384, skyH = 512;
  const index = name => { if (C[name] === undefined || C[name] >= 128) throw new Error('bad backdrop colour ' + name); return C[name]; };
  const sky = new Uint16Array(W * skyH), colours = scene.sky && scene.sky.colours[set];
  if (scene.sky && !colours) throw new Error('bad backdrop light set ' + set);
  // Encode a fixed palette colour through the existing light LUT. The sky must not be tinted a second
  // time (DU1 at sunset, SUN0 in evening). This needs no extra pixel flag or palette entry.
  const fixed = name => {
    const target = index(name), lut = LIGHT_LUTS[set];
    for (const level of [2, 3, 1, 4, 0]) {
      if (lut[level * PAL_N + target] === target) return target | (level << 8);
      for (let c = 0; c < NPAL; c++) if (lut[level * PAL_N + c] === target) return c | (level << 8);
    }
    throw new Error('sky colour unavailable in light set: ' + name);
  };
  const skyColours = colours && colours.map(fixed), starColours = scene.sky && scene.sky.stars && scene.sky.stars.colours.map(fixed), clear = index(scene.clear) | 512;
  for (let y = 0; y < skyH; y++) for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) {
    let c = clear;
    if (scene.sky) {
      const sy = y + top, stops = scene.sky.stops, i = sy < stops[1] ? 0 : 1;
      const t = clamp((sy - stops[i]) / (stops[i + 1] - stops[i]), 0, 1);
      const edge = 0.5 + Math.sin(x * 0.015) * 0.025;
      c = skyColours[t > edge ? i + 1 : i];                // three broad sheets with gently cut edges
      const stars = scene.sky.stars;
      if (set === 'night' && sy < scene.horizon - 30 && stars && hash2(x + scene.seed, sy) < stars.density)
        c = starColours[hash2(sy, x + scene.seed) < 0.15 ? 1 : 0];
    }
    sky[y * W + x] = c;
  }
  const layers = scene.layers.map(l => {
    if(l.shapes)return bakeBackdropShapeLayer(scene,l,set,H);
    let pad = 64;
    for (const b of l.buildings) pad = Math.max(pad, -b.top + (b.chimney ? b.chimney.h : 0));
    const idx = new Uint8Array(W * H).fill(255), lvl = new Uint8Array(W * H).fill(l.level);
    const light = l.lights && l.lights[set] || {};
    const c = {}; for (const name of Object.keys(l.colours)) c[name] = index(light[name] || l.colours[name]);
    const y0 = scene.horizon + l.offset - pad;
    const put = (x, y, v) => { if (x >= 0 && x < W && y >= 0 && y < H) idx[y * W + x] = v; };
    for (const b of l.buildings) {
      const eave = b.eaves + pad;
      for (let u = 0; u < b.w; u++) {
        const roofY = b.roof === 'gable' ? b.top + Math.floor(Math.abs(u - (b.w - 1) / 2) * 0.5) : b.top + Math.floor(Math.max(0, Math.abs(u - (b.w - 1) / 2) - b.w * 0.22) * 0.65);
        for (let y = Math.max(0, roofY + pad); y < H; y++) {
          let colour = y < eave ? (u < b.w / 2 ? c.roof : c.face) : c.wall;
          if (scene.snow && y < roofY + pad + 2) colour = C.SKY1;
          put(b.x + u, y, colour);
        }
      }
      if (b.chimney) for (let y = b.top + pad - b.chimney.h; y < eave; y++) for (let u = 0; u < 3; u++) put(b.x + b.chimney.x + u, y, c.roof);
      for (let repeat = 0; repeat < H; repeat += 128) for (const win of b.windows) {
        const colour = (set === 'night' || set === 'sun') && win.lit ? c.lit : c.window;
        for (let y = 0; y < 4; y++) for (let x = 0; x < 2; x++) put(b.x + win.x + x, eave + repeat + win.y + y, colour);
      }
    }
    return { idx, lvl, y0, parallax: l.parallax, scroll: l.scroll };
  });
  const result={ width: W, height: H, sky, skyTop: top, skyH, skyAbove: skyColours ? skyColours[0] : clear, layers, scene };
  if(layers.some(l=>l.motions && l.motions.length)){result.hasMotion=true;result.motionFrame=-1;}
  return result;
}
// x/y relative to the room's centre at rest. Camera offsets are supplied separately so each layer can drift.
function sampleBackdrop(cache, x, y, seconds, cameraX, cameraY) {
  cameraX = cameraX || 0; cameraY = cameraY || 0;
  if(cache.hasMotion)prepareBackdropMotion(cache,Math.round(seconds*60));
  const W = cache.width, sx = bdMod(Math.floor(x + cameraX * 0.3), W), skyY = Math.floor(y + cameraY * 0.3) - cache.skyTop, sy = clamp(skyY, 0, cache.skyH - 1);
  let value = skyY < 0 ? cache.skyAbove : cache.sky[sy * W + sx];
  for (let k = 0; k < cache.layers.length; k++) {
    const l = cache.layers[k], ly = Math.floor(y + cameraY * l.parallax) - l.y0;
    if (ly < 0 && !l.motions) continue;
    const lx = bdMod(Math.floor(x + cameraX * l.parallax + seconds * l.scroll), W);
    const i = Math.min(ly, cache.height - 1) * W + lx;
    if (ly>=0 && l.idx[i] !== 255) value = l.idx[i] | (l.lvl[i] << 8);
    if(l.motions)for(let m=0;m<l.motions.length;m++){
      const v=sampleBackdropMotion(l.motions[m],Math.floor(x+cameraX*l.parallax),Math.floor(y+cameraY*l.parallax));
      if(v>=0)value=v;
    }
  }
  return value;
}
// Record only the wooden base, before the room is baked over it. It provides an exact attachment contour.
function backdropBaseContour() {
  if (!BD) return;
  if (BD.frame) BD.frame.cache = null;
  BD.base.fill(-1);
  for (let x = 0; x < WW; x++) for (let y = WH - 1; y >= 0; y--) if (sDep[y * WW + x] !== DEPTH_EMPTY) { BD.base[x] = y; break; }
}
function sampleCutaway(wx, wy) {
  if (!BD || wx < 0 || wx >= WW || BD.base[wx] < 0 || wy <= BD.base[wx]) return -1;
  const p = BD.cutaway, corner = OX + ROOM_X - ROOM_Y, left = wx < corner;
  const u = Math.abs(wx - corner), v = Math.floor(wy - (OY + (ROOM_X + ROOM_Y) / 2 + BASE_H) + u * 0.5);
  let c = C[left ? p.left : p.right];
  // Large masonry-paper courses follow the two existing section faces.
  if (bdMod(v, 12) === 0 || bdMod(u + (Math.floor(v / 12) & 1) * 14, 28) === 0) c = C[left ? p.mortar : p.rightMortar];
  const wu = bdMod(u, 38), wv = bdMod(v, 46);
  if (v > 12 && wu >= 14 && wu < 22 && wv >= 16 && wv < 31) {
    c = C[p.window];
    if (wu === 14 || wu === 18 || wv === 16 || wv === 23 || wv === 30) c = C[left ? p.left : p.right];
    const lit = (BD.set === 'night' || BD.set === 'sun') && hash2(Math.floor(u / 38), Math.floor(v / 46) + (left ? 11 : 71)) < 0.22;
    if (lit && wu !== 18 && wv !== 23 && wu > 14 && wv > 16 && wv < 30) c = C[p.lit];
  }
  return c | (p.level << 8);
}
function composeBackdrop() {
  if (!BD) return;
  if(ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='train')return trainComposeBackdrop();
  if (!BD.cache || BD.cache.height < LH + 128) BD.cache = bakeBackdropLayers(BD.scene, BD.set, LH + 128);
  const cache = BD.cache, cx = viewX + LW / 2 - VIEW_CX, cy = viewY + LH / 2 - VIEW_CY;
  const n = LW * LH, treatment = backdropTreatment || BD.scene.treatment;
  let seconds = 0;
  for (let k = 0; k < cache.layers.length; k++) if (cache.layers[k].scroll !== 0) { seconds = BD.elapsed; break; }
  if (!BD.frame || BD.frame.idx.length < n) BD.frame = { idx: new Uint8Array(n), lvl: new Uint8Array(n) };
  const f = BD.frame;
  if(BD.openings && (!f.opening || f.opening.length<n))f.opening=new Uint8Array(n);
  // The town is still while cats animate. Cache its composed pixels, not just its source layers.
  // A moving profile refreshes on time changes; pan, zoom, resize, room bake and treatment invalidate too.
  if (f.cache !== cache || f.w !== LW || f.h !== LH || f.x !== viewX || f.y !== viewY || f.cx !== cx || f.cy !== cy || f.treatment !== treatment || f.seconds !== seconds) {
    for (let y = 0; y < LH; y++) for (let x = 0; x < LW; x++) {
      const i = y * LW + x;
      let value = treatment === 'cutaway' ? sampleCutaway(x + viewX, y + viewY) : -1;
      if (value < 0) value = sampleBackdrop(cache, x - LW / 2, y - LH / 2, seconds, cx, cy);
      f.idx[i] = value & 127; f.lvl[i] = value >> 8;
      if(f.opening){const wx=x+viewX,wy=y+viewY;f.opening[i]=wx>=0 && wx<WW && wy>=0 && wy<WH && (sFlg[wy*WW+wx]&FL_GLASS)?1:0;}
    }
    f.cache = cache; f.w = LW; f.h = LH; f.x = viewX; f.y = viewY; f.cx = cx; f.cy = cy; f.treatment = treatment; f.seconds = seconds;
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if (fDep[i] === DEPTH_EMPTY || (f.opening && f.opening[i])) { fIdx[i] = f.idx[i]; fLvl[i] = f.lvl[i]; }
  if(cache.hasMotion)composeBackdropMotion(cache,cx,cy,WS.frame/60);
}
function backdropEdgeCSS() {
  if(ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='train')return grayscale?BG_GREY_CSS:BG_CSS;
  if (!BD) return grayscale ? BG_GREY_CSS : BG_CSS;
  const v = sampleBackdrop(BD.cache, 0, -LH / 2, 0), T = grayscale ? GRAY32 : RES32, c = T[(v >> 8) * PAL_N + (v & 127)];
  if (BD.edgeColour !== c) {
    BD.edgeColour = c;
    BD.edgeCSS = 'rgb(' + (c & 255) + ',' + ((c >>> 8) & 255) + ',' + ((c >>> 16) & 255) + ')';
  }
  return BD.edgeCSS;
}
// Small reusable primitives extend the existing indexed layer bake. Town building generation and
// its cached composition are unchanged. Shapes and motion records are data, suitable for Codable.
function backdropShapes(shapes,W,H,top,colours) {
  const idx=new Uint8Array(W*H).fill(255);
  const put=(x,y,c)=>{y-=top;if(x>=0 && x<W && y>=0 && y<H)idx[y*W+x]=c;};
  for(const s of shapes) {
    const c=colours[s.colour];if(c===undefined)throw new Error('bad shape colour '+s.colour);
    if(s.kind==='rect' || s.kind==='cobbles') {
      for(let y=Math.max(top,s.y);y<Math.min(top+H,s.y+s.h);y++)for(let x=Math.max(0,s.x);x<Math.min(W,s.x+s.w);x++) {
        let colour=c;
        if(s.kind==='cobbles') {
          const u=Math.floor(y+x*.5),v=Math.floor(y-x*.5),row=Math.floor(v/s.tile[1]);
          const a=bdMod(u+(row&1)*(s.tile[0]/2),s.tile[0]),b=bdMod(v,s.tile[1]);
          // Larger paving cards give the yard a quiet field; no isolated glitter or grit.
          if((a===0 && (Math.floor(u/s.tile[0])&1)===0) || (b===0 && (row&1)===0))colour=colours[s.joint];
        }
        put(x,y,colour);
      }
    } else if(s.kind==='line') {
      for(let k=1;k<s.points.length;k++) {
        const a=s.points[k-1],b=s.points[k],n=Math.max(Math.abs(a[0]-b[0]),Math.abs(a[1]-b[1]));
        for(let j=0;j<=n;j++)put(Math.round(a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*(n?j/n:0)),Math.round(a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*(n?j/n:0)),c);
      }
    } else {
      const pts=s.points,lo=Math.max(top,Math.min(...pts.map(p=>p[1]))),hi=Math.min(top+H,Math.max(...pts.map(p=>p[1])));
      for(let y=lo;y<hi;y++) {
        const hits=[];for(let k=0;k<pts.length;k++){const a=pts[k],b=pts[(k+1)%pts.length];if((a[1]<=y && b[1]>y)||(b[1]<=y && a[1]>y))hits.push(a[0]+(y-a[1])*(b[0]-a[0])/(b[1]-a[1]));}
        hits.sort((a,b)=>a-b);for(let k=1;k<hits.length;k+=2)for(let x=Math.max(0,Math.ceil(hits[k-1]));x<Math.min(W,hits[k]);x++)put(x,y,c);
      }
    }
  }
  return idx;
}
function bakeBackdropShapeLayer(scene,l,set,H) {
  const colours={},over=l.lights && l.lights[set] || {};
  for(const role of Object.keys(l.colours)) {
    const name=over[role] || l.colours[role];if(C[name]===undefined)throw new Error('bad backdrop colour '+name);colours[role]=C[name];
  }
  let top=-64;
  for(const s of l.shapes)top=Math.min(top,s.points?Math.min(...s.points.map(p=>p[1])):s.y);
  const base=scene.horizon+l.offset,idx=backdropShapes(l.shapes,scene.width,H,top,colours),lvl=new Uint8Array(idx.length).fill(l.level);
  const motions=(l.motions || []).map(m=>({data:m,state:new Float64Array(4),base,level:l.level,colour:colours[m.colour],
    idx:m.shapes?backdropShapes(m.shapes,m.w,m.h,-m.h,colours):null}));
  return {idx,lvl,y0:base+top,parallax:l.parallax,scroll:l.scroll,motions};
}
function prepareBackdropMotion(cache,frame) {
  frame=((frame%2880)+2880)%2880;if(cache.motionFrame===frame)return;
  for(let k=0;k<cache.layers.length;k++) {
    const a=cache.layers[k].motions;if(!a)continue;
    for(let n=0;n<a.length;n++)BACKDROP.motionAt(a[n].data,frame,a[n].state);
  }
  cache.motionFrame=frame;
}
function backdropMistContains(d,u,v) {                 // shared by ordinary sampling and the depot's cached mask
  const edge=3+Math.floor(2*Math.sin(u/d.w*TAU)+Math.sin(u/d.w*TAU*3));
  const density=Math.min(v-edge,d.h-v-1)/d.h*d.density/8;
  return density>.07;                                  // broad paper mist, with no isolated dither pixels
}
function sampleBackdropMotion(m,x,y) {
  const d=m.data,s=m.state;if(!s[2])return -1;
  if(d.kind==='train') {
    const u=x-s[0],v=y-m.base-s[1]+d.h;
    if(u<0 || u>=d.w || v<0 || v>=d.h)return -1;
    const c=m.idx[v*d.w+u];return c===255?-1:c|(m.level<<8);
  }
  const u=bdMod(x-s[0],d.w),v=y-m.base-s[1];
  if(v<0 || v>=d.h)return -1;
  return backdropMistContains(d,u,v)?m.colour|(m.level<<8):-1;
}
// Static backdrop pixels remain cached. Only the small train rectangle and mist bands are resampled
// as time advances, through all layers in order, so the near wall can hide a train naturally.
function composeBackdropMotion(cache,cx,cy,seconds) {
  prepareBackdropMotion(cache,Math.round(seconds*60));
  const opening=BD.frame.opening;
  for(let k=0;k<cache.layers.length;k++) {
    const l=cache.layers[k];if(!l.motions)continue;
    for(let j=0;j<l.motions.length;j++) {
      const m=l.motions[j],d=m.data,s=m.state;if(!s[2])continue;
      const train=d.kind==='train',x0=train?Math.max(0,Math.floor(s[0]-cx*l.parallax+LW/2)-1):0;
      const x1=train?Math.min(LW,Math.ceil(s[0]+d.w-cx*l.parallax+LW/2)+1):LW;
      const y0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(m.base+s[1]-(train?d.h:0)-cy*l.parallax+LH/2)-1);
      const y1=Math.min(LH,Math.ceil(m.base+s[1]+(train?0:d.h)-cy*l.parallax+LH/2)+1);
      for(let y=y0;y<y1;y++)for(let x=x0;x<x1;x++) {
        const i=y*LW+x;if(fDep[i]!==DEPTH_EMPTY && !(opening && opening[i]))continue;
        const value=sampleBackdrop(cache,x-LW/2,y-LH/2,seconds,cx,cy);fIdx[i]=value&127;fLvl[i]=value>>8;
      }
    }
  }
}
// The handmade goods-shed shell. Indexed faces, depth and the static bake are shared with the office.
// World geometry, sunlight and the furniture shadow solids come from DEPOT data.
let DEPR=null, depotShell=null, depotWall='L', depotRoofWall='L';
let depotSunIndex=null;
let depotTreatment=null;                        // null uses the approved data choice; query may still compare A/B
function activateDepot(room) {
  BOXR=null; DEPR=room;
  depotShell=room;depotSunIndex=DEPOT.compileSun(room);
  depotBuildContacts(room);
  initDepotDust();
  depotLifeInit(room);
}
function depotSun(x,y,z,face) {
  if(face===F_X || x<=0.25 || x>DEPR.room.w || y<0 || y>DEPR.room.d || z<-.1) return 0;
  return DEPOT.sunAmt(depotShell,x,y,z,depotSunIndex);
}
function depotLampAdd(x,y,z,px,py) {
  let add=0;
  for(let k=0;k<DEPR.lamps.length;k++) {
    const l=DEPR.lamps[k]; if(!l.on || z>l.z)continue;
    // A downward cone: a broad pool on the floor, narrowing towards the shade.
    const radius=l.pool*Math.max(.2,(l.z-z)/l.z), d=Math.hypot(x-l.x,y-l.y);
    if(d>=radius)continue;
    const n=(1-d/radius)*2.5+.5;
    add=Math.max(add,Math.min(2,Math.floor(n)));
  }
  return add;
}
function depotBakeLight(x,y,z,face,px,py) {
  const board=!!shBoard, contact=board?0:depotContact(x,y,z,face,px,py);
  return contact-Math.max(0,Math.min(board || (shFlag&FL_FLOOR)?1:2,depotLampAdd(x,y,z,px,py))-sunAdd);
}
function depotFrameLight(x,y,z,px,py,spriteSun) { return Math.max(0,depotLampAdd(x,y,z,px,py)-(spriteSun===undefined?sunAdd:spriteSun)); }

// Broad kraft floor cards. Walkways and the working line remain separate, quiet paper layers.
function shDepotFloor(face,x,y) {
  const B=DEPR.board, line=DEPR.workingLine;
  if(x>=B.x-2 && x<B.x+B.w*TILE+2 && y>=B.y-2 && y<B.y+B.h*TILE+2)return -1;
  const worn=(x>29 && x<53 && y>22 && y<157) || (y>23 && y<39 && x>28 && x<205);
  const paint=((Math.abs(x-line[0])<.65 || Math.abs(x-line[2])<.65) && y>=line[1] && y<=line[3]) ||
    ((Math.abs(y-line[1])<.65 || Math.abs(y-line[3])<.65) && x>=line[0] && x<=line[2]);
  if(paint)return C.CREAM;
  const card=cardFloorTex(x,y);
  return card===C.WD1?card:worn?C.WD3:C.WD2;
}
function depotBrick(u,z,seed) {
  const row=Math.floor(z/8), v=z-row*8, q=bdMod(u+(row&1)*8+seed,16);
  return v<.45 || q<.45?C.BK0:C.BK1;
}
function depotArch(w,u,z,inset) {
  if(u<w.u0+inset || u>=w.u1-inset || z<w.z0+inset)return false;
  return z<=w.springZ || Math.hypot(u-(w.u0+w.u1)/2,z-w.springZ)<w.radius-inset;
}
function shDepotWall(face,u,z) {
  shFlag=FL_ROOM;shFlat=false;shLvl=depotWall==='L'?-1:0;
  if(depotWall==='L')for(const w of DEPR.windows) {
    if(depotArch(w,u,z,0))return -1;
    // Radial header bricks around the round head, and alternating long/short jamb bricks.
    const du=u-(w.u0+w.u1)/2,dz=z-w.springZ,r=Math.hypot(du,dz);
    if(dz>=0 && r<w.radius+3 && r>=w.radius) {
      const a=Math.atan2(dz,du), joint=fract(a*(w.radius+1)/4);
      return joint<.08?C.PL0:r>w.radius+2.2?C.PL2:C.PL1;
    }
    if(z>w.z0-2 && z<w.springZ && (Math.abs(u-w.u0)<2 || Math.abs(u-w.u1)<2))return bxBrick(u,z,13);
  }
  if(depotWall==='R') {
    const o=DEPR.openings[0];if(u>=o.u0 && u<o.u1 && z<o.z1)return -1;
    if(u>=o.u0-3 && u<o.u1+3 && z>=o.z1 && z<o.z1+5) return z<o.z1+1?C.WD1:bxBrick(u,z,7);
  }
  if(z<4)return z<1?C.WD1:C.PL0;
  if(z>=DEPR.room.wallH-3)return z>=DEPR.room.wallH-1?C.WD2:C.WD0;
  return bxBrick(u,z,depotWall==='L'?11:23);
}
let depotWindow=null;
function shDepotGlass(face,u,z) {
  const w=depotWindow;u+=w.u0;z+=w.z0;
  if(!depotArch(w,u,z,1.5))return -1;
  shFlag=FL_ROOM|FL_GLASS;shFlat=false;shLvl=-1;
  const du=u-(w.u0+w.u1)/2,dz=z-w.springZ;
  let frame=u<w.u0+2.5 || u>w.u1-2.5 || z<w.z0+2.5 || (dz>0 && Math.hypot(du,dz)>w.radius-2.5);
  for(const m of w.mullions)if(Math.abs(u-m)<.75)frame=true;
  for(const rail of w.rails)if(Math.abs(z-rail)<1)frame=true;
  // Thin radial iron bars divide the arched fanlight.
  if(dz>1 && (Math.abs(du-dz)<.6 || Math.abs(du+dz)<.6))frame=true;
  if(frame){shFlag=FL_ROOM;return C.DEEP;}
  shFlat=true;
  const high=clamp((z-w.z0)/(w.z1-w.z0),0,1);
  return high>.76?C.DW0:high<.18?C.MS1:C.DW1;
}
function shDepotReveal(face,u,v) { shFlat=false;shFlag=FL_ROOM;shLvl=face===F_X?-1:0;return v<1?C.PL0:C.PL1; }
function shDepotSill(face,u,v) { shFlat=false;shLvl=face===F_TOP?0:-1;return face===F_TOP?C.FT1:C.PL0; }
function depotWindows() {
  for(const w of DEPR.windows) {
    depotWindow=w;shSunMax=0;
    faceX(w.u0,w.u1,w.z0,w.z1,-w.depth,shDepotGlass,C.DEEP,0);
    shFlat=false;shFlag=FL_ROOM;
    // Deep jambs, brick sill and the intrados of the arch; glass sits four wu behind the wall.
    faceY(-w.depth,0,w.z0,w.springZ,w.u0,shDepotReveal,C.BK0,0);
    faceY(-w.depth,0,w.z0,w.springZ,w.u1,shDepotReveal,C.BK0,0);
    box(-w.depth,w.u0-1,w.z0-2,2,w.u1+1,w.z0,shDepotSill,C.BK0,3);
    for(let u=w.u0;u<w.u1;u++) {
      const du=u+.5-(w.u0+w.u1)/2,z=w.springZ+Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,w.radius*w.radius-du*du));
      for(let x=-w.depth;x<=0;x++)dot3(x,u,z,C.BK0,-1);
    }
  }
  shFlat=false;shSunMax=2;
}
function shDepotTimber(face,u,v) { shFlag=FL_ROOM;shFlat=false;shLvl=face===F_X?-1:0;return plankTex(v,u); }
function shDepotIron(face,u,v) { shFlat=false;shLvl=0;return face===F_TOP?C.PL0:C.DEEP; }
function shDepotPier(face,u,z) { shFlat=false;shFlag=FL_ROOM;shLvl=face===F_X?-1:0;return bxBrick(u,z,37); }
function shDepotDoor(face,u,z) {
  shFlat=false;shFlag=FL_ROOM;shLvl=face===F_X?-1:0;
  if(z<3 || z>59 || (z>27 && z<30))return C.WD0;
  if(Math.abs(u-(z/7)%10)<1.2)return C.WD3;
  if(u%12<.55)return C.WD1;
  return C.WD2;
}
function shDepotOpening(face,u,z) {
  // Backdrop pixels through the open door; unlike glass, this plane cannot hide the van.
  shFlat=true;shFlag=FL_ROOM|FL_GLASS;
  return z>42?C.DW0:C.MS0;
}
function depotLoadingBay() {
  const o=DEPR.openings[0];shSunMax=0;
