  faceY(o.u0,o.u1,o.z0,o.z1,-4,shDepotOpening,C.BK0,0);shFlat=false;
  for(let i=0;i<WW*WH;i++)if((sFlg[i]&FL_GLASS) && sWY[i]===-16 && sWX[i]>=o.u0*4 && sWX[i]<o.u1*4)
    sDep[i]=DEPTH_EMPTY+1;
  faceX(-4,0,0,o.z1,o.u0,shDepotReveal,C.BK0,0);
  faceX(-4,0,0,o.z1,o.u1,shDepotReveal,C.BK0,0);
  for(const d of o.doors)box(...d,shDepotDoor,C.WD0,3);
  box(o.u0-12,1,o.z1+4,o.u1+12,3,o.z1+6,shDepotIron,C.PL0,3);
  for(const x of [o.u0-5,o.u1+5]) {
    box(x-1,2,58,x+1,4,69,shDepotIron,C.PL0,3);
    dot3(x,4,65,C.PL1,0);                       // roller axle and catch
  }
  shSunMax=2;
  box(o.u0,-5,-o.dockHeight,o.u1,0,0,shDepotPier,C.WD0,3);
  faceTop(o.u0,-5,o.u1,0,0,shDepotSill,C.FT0,1);
}

// The narrow cut roof retains a complete queen-post bay at each brick pier. The ridge glass is
// coloured sky, never a sunlight aperture: the only sun query is the east-wall windows.
function shDepotRoof(face,u,v) {
  shFlat=false;shLvl=-1;shFlag=FL_ROOM;
  const R=DEPR.roof,run=DEPR.room.roofRun, rafters=depotRoofWall==='L'?R.trussesL:R.trussesR;
  if(v<1 || v>run-1)return C.WD0;
  for(const r of rafters)if(Math.abs(u-r)<1.5)return Math.abs(u-r)<.65?C.WD2:C.WD0;
  const glass=depotRoofWall==='L'?R.glazingL:R.glazingR;
  if(v>=glass[0] && v<glass[1]) {
    if(v<glass[0]+1 || v>glass[1]-1 || u%12<.8)return C.DEEP;
    shFlat=true;return v<(glass[0]+glass[1])*.5?C.DW0:C.MS1;
  }
  return plankTex(u,v);
}
function shDepotCap(face,u,v) { shFlat=false;shLvl=0;shFlag=FL_ROOM;return face===F_TOP?C.WD3:C.WD1; }
function depotBeam(x0,y0,z0,x1,y1,z1) {
  const n=Math.ceil(Math.max(Math.abs(x1-x0),Math.abs(y1-y0),Math.abs(z1-z0))*2);
  for(let k=0;k<=n;k++) {
    const t=k/n,x=x0+(x1-x0)*t,y=y0+(y1-y0)*t,z=z0+(z1-z0)*t;
    dot3(x,y,z-1,C.WD0,-1);dot3(x,y,z,C.WD2,0);dot3(x,y,z+1,C.WD0,-1);
  }
}
function depotRoof() {
  const P=DEPR.room,R=DEPR.roof,pitch=R.pitch[0]/R.pitch[1];shSunMax=0;shFlat=false;shFlag=FL_ROOM;
  depotRoofWall='L';faceSlope('L',0,P.d,0,P.roofRun,shDepotRoof,true,P.wallH,pitch);
  depotRoofWall='R';faceSlope('R',0,P.w,0,P.roofRun,shDepotRoof,true,P.wallH,pitch);
  shFlat=false;
  // Exposed timber edges carry a one-pixel dark seam and a honey-coloured cut face.
  box(P.roofRun-1,P.roofRun,111,P.roofRun,P.d,112,shDepotCap,C.WD0,1);
  box(P.roofRun,P.roofRun-1,111,P.w,P.roofRun,112,shDepotCap,C.WD0,1);
  for(const wall of ['L','R'])for(const u of wall==='L'?R.trussesL:R.trussesR) {
    if(u<P.roofRun)continue;
    const L=wall==='L',p=(v,z)=>L?[v,u,z]:[u,v,z];
    depotBeam(...p(0,87),...p(16,111));
    depotBeam(...p(0,87),...p(16,87));
    for(const v of [6,12])depotBeam(...p(v,87),...p(v,87+v*pitch));
    depotBeam(...p(6,87),...p(12,105));
    // Iron straps at the mortices; no lettering or emblems.
    for(const v of [1,6,12,15]) { const q=p(v,87);dot3(...q,C.DEEP,0);dot3(q[0],q[1],q[2]+1,C.PL0,0); }
  }
}
function shDepotEnamel(face,u,v) { shFlat=false;shLvl=face===F_X?-1:0;return face===F_TOP?C.PT1:C.PT0; }
function depotLamps() {
  shSunMax=0;shFlag=FL_ROOM;
  for(const l of DEPR.lamps)depotLamp(l,true);
}
function depotLamp(l,on) {
    for(let z=l.z+l.height;z<=l.hangZ;z++)dot3(l.x,l.y,z,C.DEEP,0);
    cylinder(l.x,l.y,1,l.z+5,l.z+6,shDepotEnamel,C.PT0,false);
    cylinder(l.x,l.y,2.5,l.z+3,l.z+5,shDepotEnamel,C.PT0,false);
    cylinder(l.x,l.y,4,l.z+1,l.z+3,shDepotEnamel,C.PT0,false);
    cylinder(l.x,l.y,l.r,l.z,l.z+1,shDepotEnamel,C.PT0,true);
    disc(l.x,l.y,3.5,l.z-.5,on?shBxBulb:shDpCream,C.CREAM,false);shFlat=false;
}
function bakeDepot() {
  if(typeof DP_VIEW!=='undefined'&&DP_VIEW){DP_VIEW.x=Infinity;DP_VIEW.cache=null;}
  const P=DEPR.room,B=DEPR.board,x=B.x,y=B.y,X=x+B.w*TILE,Y=y+B.h*TILE;
  if(BD && BD.frame)BD.frame.cache=null;             // rebakes can close an old window or doorway pixel
  targetStatic();bakeBackdrop();
  if((depotTreatment || DEPR.baseTreatment)==='B')bakeDepotGround();else bakeBase();
  aoFn=depotBakeLight;
  shFlat=false;shBias=0;shSunMax=1;shBoard=0;shFlag=FL_ROOM|FL_FLOOR;
  faceTop(0,0,P.w,P.d,0,shDepotFloor,C.WD0,0);
  shBoard=128;shSunMax=1;
  faceTop(x,y,X,Y,0,shStone,C.FT0,0);
  shFlag=FL_ROOM;
  if(DEPR.flapKerbGaps){
    for(const side of ['L','U','D','R']){
      const alongX=side==='U'||side==='D',end=(alongX?X:Y)+2;
      const holes=DEPR.flapKerbGaps.filter(f=>f.side===side).map(f=>f.at).sort((a,b)=>a-b);
      let from=(alongX?x:y)-2;
      for(let k=0;k<=holes.length;k++){
        const to=k<holes.length?holes[k]-EDGE_PORTAL_DATA.railOpening/2:end;
        if(to>from){
          if(side==='L')box(x-2,from,0,x,to,2,shKerbTop,C.WD0,3);
          else if(side==='R')box(X,from,0,X+2,to,2,shKerbTop,C.WD0,3);
          else if(side==='U')box(from,y-2,0,to,y,2,shKerbTop,C.WD0,3);
          else box(from,Y,0,to,Y+2,2,shKerbTop,C.WD0,3);
        }
        if(k<holes.length)from=holes[k]+EDGE_PORTAL_DATA.railOpening/2;
      }
    }
  }else{
  box(x-2,y-2,0,x,Y+2,2,shKerbTop,C.WD0,3);
  box(x-2,y-2,0,X+2,y,2,shKerbTop,C.WD0,3);
  box(x-2,Y,0,X+2,Y+2,2,shKerbTop,C.WD0,3);
  box(X,y-2,0,X+2,Y+2,2,shKerbTop,C.WD0,3);
  }
  shBoard=0;shSunMax=2;
  depotWall='L';faceX(0,P.d,0,P.wallH,0,shDepotWall,C.BK0,0);
  depotWall='R';faceY(0,P.w,0,P.wallH,0,shDepotWall,C.BK0,0);
  depotWindows();depotLoadingBay();
  shFlat=false;shFlag=FL_ROOM;
  for(const u of DEPR.roof.piersL)box(0,u,0,3,u+4,P.wallH,shDepotPier,C.BK0,3);
  for(const u of DEPR.roof.piersR)box(u,0,0,u+4,3,P.wallH,shDepotPier,C.BK0,3);
  // Wall plates, masonry cut ends, and their small alternating return courses.
  box(-4,0,85,2,P.d,88,shDepotTimber,C.WD0,3);
  box(0,-4,85,P.w,2,88,shDepotTimber,C.WD0,3);
  faceY(-4,0,0,88,P.d,shDepotPier,C.BK0,3);
  faceX(-4,0,0,88,P.w,shDepotPier,C.BK0,3);
  depotRoof();bakeDepotProps();
  depotLifeBake();
  aoFn=null;lampFn=null;shFlat=false;shBias=0;shFlag=0;shBoard=0;shSunMax=2;
  if(DP_VIEW&&BD&&BD.cache)depotFlatBackdrop(BD.cache);
}

// Reuse the office's preallocated mote array. Seeded once on entry, evaluated analytically at 60 Hz.
function initDepotDust() {
  for(let k=0;k<NDUST;k++) {
    const o=k*6;
    DUST[o]=12+hash2(k,181)*200;DUST[o+1]=8+hash2(k,193)*155;DUST[o+2]=8+hash2(k,211)*48;
    DUST[o+3]=hash2(k,223)*TAU;DUST[o+4]=hash2(k,239)*TAU;DUST[o+5]=hash2(k,251);
  }
}
function depotPointInCore(x,y,z) {
  const B=DEPR.board,px=Math.round(x-y),py=Math.round((x+y)/2-z);
  for(let j=0;j<B.h;j++)for(let i=0;i<B.w;i++) {
    const cx=B.x+8+i*TILE,cy=B.y+8+j*TILE,sx=cx-cy,sy=(cx+cy)/2;
    if(px>=sx-6 && px<sx+6 && py>=sy-3 && py<sy+3)return true;
  }
  return false;
}
const DEPOT_DUST_POINT=new Float64Array(4);
function drawDepotDust(frame) {
  shFlat=true;shBias=6;shSunMax=0;shFlag=0;
  for(let k=0;k<NDUST;k++) {
    DEPOT.dustAt(DUST,k*6,frame,DEPOT_DUST_POINT);
    const x=DEPOT_DUST_POINT[0],y=DEPOT_DUST_POINT[1],z=DEPOT_DUST_POINT[2];
    if(!DEPOT_DUST_POINT[3] || depotSun(x,y,z,F_TOP)<.6 || depotPointInCore(x,y,z))continue;
    dot3(x,y,z,C.SUN1,0);
  }
  shFlat=false;shBias=0;shSunMax=2;
}
function drawDepotDynamic() {
  nItems=0;
  if(typeof GAME_MODE!=='undefined'&&GAME_MODE&&gm.L){const src=gBoardSource();if(src===G_SRC)gPrepareSrc();addBoardItems(src);}
  else {depotDemoPrepare();addBoardItems(DEPOT_SRC);}
  addItem(20,DPLIFE.driver.x+DPLIFE.driver.y,0);
  addItem(21,DPLIFE.pm.x+DPLIFE.pm.y,DPLIFE.pm.z);
  addItem(22,0,0);                         // depth-tested cached moving fixtures
  if(depotChapterFramesOn())addItem(30,254,0);
  addItem(23,DPLIFE.cargo.x+DPLIFE.cargo.y,DPLIFE.cargo.z);
  if(DPLIFE.parcel.kind!=='none')addItem(29,DPLIFE.parcel.x+DPLIFE.parcel.y,DPLIFE.parcel.z);
  for(let i=0;i<3;i++)addItem(24+i,DPLIFE.pigeons[i].x+DPLIFE.pigeons[i].y,DPLIFE.pigeons[i].z);
  sortItems();
  for(let k=0;k<nItems;k++){
    const id=ITEM_ID[k];if(drawItemHook)drawItemHook(id,false);
    shBoard=id>=IT_BOARD0?128:0;lampFn=id>=IT_BOARD0?null:depotFrameLight;
    if(id>=IT_BOARD0){drawDepotBoardItem(id);if(id===IT_BOARD0&&!GAME_MODE)drawDepotFlaps();
      else if(id===IT_BOARD0&&cpActive()&&CP.mode==='demo')drawDepotFlaps(CP.src);
    }else if(id===30)drawDepotSortingFrames();else drawDepotLifeItem(id);
    if(drawItemHook)drawItemHook(id,true);
  }
  shBoard=0;lampFn=depotFrameLight;shSunMax=2;
  if(drawItemHook)drawItemHook(27,false);
  drawDepotDust(WS.frame);depotLifeParticles();drawParticles(152,176);
  if(drawItemHook)drawItemHook(27,true);
  if(drawItemHook)drawItemHook(IT_BOARD0+BD_CAT0,false);
  drawParticles(0,152);drawParticles(176,200);
  if(drawItemHook)drawItemHook(IT_BOARD0+BD_CAT0,true);
  lampFn=null;shBoard=0;
}

// Treatment B: a finite section through the shed's own ground. The yard paving continues outside;
// brick footings sit over compacted soil and a stone drainage bed. The user chooses in stage 3.
function shDepotSection(face,u,v) {
  shFlat=false;shFlag=FL_ROOM;shLvl=face===F_X?-1:0;
  if(v>34)return C.FT0;
  if(v>=24)return depotBrick(u,v,43);
  if(v<5)return C.COOL1;
  if(v<6 || v>22)return C.WD0;
  const seam=13+Math.sin(u*.035)*.5;
  return v<seam?C.WD0:C.WD1;
}
function shDepotPaving(face,u,v) {
  shFlag=FL_ROOM;shFlat=false;shLvl=-1;
  const row=Math.floor(v/10),x=bdMod(u+(row&1)*8,16),y=bdMod(v,10);
  return x<.55 || y<.55?C.PL0:C.FT0;
}
function shDepotDrain(face,u,v) {
  shFlat=false;shLvl=0;shFlag=FL_ROOM;
  const d=Math.hypot(u-6,v-6);if(d>5)return -1;
  if(d<3)return C.OUT;
  return v<6?C.PL0:C.FT0;
}
function bakeDepotGround() {
  const b=DEPR.bases[1].bounds,P=DEPR.room;shSunMax=0;shBoard=0;shBias=0;shFlat=false;shFlag=FL_ROOM;
  box(...b,shDepotSection,C.WD0,3);
  // Paving stays in the reserved base/yard envelope and is lower than the room's floor.
  faceTop(b[0],P.d,b[3],b[4],0,shDepotPaving,C.PL0,0);
  faceTop(P.w,b[1],b[3],P.d,0,shDepotPaving,C.PL0,0);
  faceTop(b[0],b[1],0,P.d,0,shDepotPaving,C.PL0,0);
  faceTop(0,b[1],P.w,0,0,shDepotPaving,C.PL0,0);
  const yard=DEPR.exterior;
  faceTop(yard[0],yard[1],yard[2],yard[3],-6,shDepotPaving,C.PL0,0);
  faceY(202,214,-26,-14,b[4],shDepotDrain,C.PL0,0);
  shLvl=0;shSunMax=2;
}

// Review uses the same twelve-by-six core, on the bake and in composed frame coordinates.
function depotCheckCores() {
  const keep=BOXR;BOXR=DEPR;
  try { return bxCheckCores(); } finally { BOXR=keep; }
}
function depotCorePixels() {
  const B=DEPR.board,out=[];
  for(let j=0;j<B.h;j++)for(let i=0;i<B.w;i++) {
    const cx=B.x+8+i*TILE,cy=B.y+8+j*TILE,sx=cx-cy+OX-viewX,sy=(cx+cy)/2+OY-viewY;
    for(let y=sy-3;y<sy+3;y++)for(let x=sx-6;x<sx+6;x++)if(x>=0 && y>=0 && x<LW && y<LH)out.push(y*LW+x);
  }
  return out;
}
// Static depot furniture. The same anchored draw functions serve the room, neutral colour audits
// and the later atlas. Geometry stays inside the data envelopes; motion decisions belong to sim.
let dpWoodSeed=0, dpBagCoat='ginger', dpWheelR=3, dpDialR=6, dpDialAngle=-1.15;
let dpContacts=[];
let dpBagBreath=0,dpBagEar=0,dpBagTurn=false,dpRoller=0,dpStackCount=4,dpChuteShake=0;
function depotBuildContacts(room) {
  dpContacts=[];
  for(const p of room.furniture)if(p.zone==='interior')for(const b of p.occluders || p.parts)
    if(b[2]===0)dpContacts.push(b);
}
function depotContact(x,y,z,face,px,py) {
  if(face!==F_TOP || z>.5)return 0;
  let d=Math.min(x,y);
  for(let i=0;i<dpContacts.length;i++) {
    const b=dpContacts[i],dx=Math.max(b[0]-x,x-b[3],0),dy=Math.max(b[1]-y,y-b[4],0);
    d=Math.min(d,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
  }
  return d<1.1?1:0;
}
function shDpWood(face,u,v) {
  return face===F_TOP?C.WD3:face===F_X?C.WD1:C.WD2;
}
function shDpIron(face,u,v) { return face===F_TOP?C.PT1:face===F_X?C.DEEP:C.PT0; }
function shDpSteel(face,u,v) { return face===F_TOP?C.FT1:face===F_X?C.COOL1:C.FT0; }
function shDpDark() { return C.DEEP; }
function shDpBrass(face,u,v) { return face===F_TOP?C.BR3:face===F_X?C.BR0:C.BR1; }
function shDpCanvas(face,u,v) {
  return face===F_TOP?C.KRAFT2:face===F_X?C.KRAFT0:C.KRAFT;
}
function shDpCream(face,u,v) { return face===F_TOP?C.CREAM:face===F_X?C.FT0:C.FT1; }
function shDpRed(face,u,v) { return face===F_TOP?C.RD2:face===F_X?C.RD0:C.RD1; }
function dpBox(x,y,z,w,d,h,sh) {
  const edge=sh===shDpWood?C.WD1:sh===shDpCanvas?C.KRAFT0:sh===shDpRed?C.RD0:sh===shDpBrass?C.BR0:C.COOL1;
  box(x,y,z,x+w,y+d,z+h,sh,edge,3);
}
function dpRod(x0,y0,z0,x1,y1,z1,c) {
  const n=Math.ceil(Math.max(Math.abs((x1-y1)-(x0-y0)),Math.abs((x1+y1-x0-y0)*.5-z1+z0))*2);
  for(let i=0;i<=n;i++){const t=n?i/n:0;dot3(lerp(x0,x1,t),lerp(y0,y1,t),lerp(z0,z1,t),c,0);}
}
// A plane between four world points. Bake-only rasterisation, with per-pixel world depth/light.
function dpTri(a,b,c,col,face) {
  const ax=a[0]-a[1]+tOX,ay=(a[0]+a[1])*.5-a[2]+tOY,bx=b[0]-b[1]+tOX,by=(b[0]+b[1])*.5-b[2]+tOY,cx=c[0]-c[1]+tOX,cy=(c[0]+c[1])*.5-c[2]+tOY;
  const den=(by-cy)*(ax-cx)+(cx-bx)*(ay-cy);if(Math.abs(den)<.001)return;
  const x0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Math.min(ax,bx,cx))),x1=Math.min(tW-1,Math.ceil(Math.max(ax,bx,cx)));
  const y0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Math.min(ay,by,cy))),y1=Math.min(tH-1,Math.ceil(Math.max(ay,by,cy)));
  for(let y=y0;y<=y1;y++)for(let x=x0;x<=x1;x++){
    const u=((by-cy)*(x+.5-cx)+(cx-bx)*(y+.5-cy))/den,v=((cy-ay)*(x+.5-cx)+(ax-cx)*(y+.5-cy))/den,w=1-u-v;
    if(u<0 || v<0 || w<0)continue;
    shLvl=0;emit(x,y,a[0]*u+b[0]*v+c[0]*w,a[1]*u+b[1]*v+c[1]*w,a[2]*u+b[2]*v+c[2]*w,face,col);
  }
}
function dpQuad(a,b,c,d,col,face=F_TOP) { dpTri(a,b,c,col,face);dpTri(a,c,d,col,face); }
function shDpWheel(face,u,v) {
  const x=u-dpWheelR,z=v-dpWheelR,r=Math.hypot(x,z);
  if(r>dpWheelR)return -1;
  return r<1.2?C.BR1:r<dpWheelR-1?C.COOL1:C.OUT;
}
function dpWheel(x,y,z,r,wall) {
  dpWheelR=r;
  if(wall==='X')faceX(y-r,y+r,z-r,z+r,x,shDpWheel,C.OUT,0);
  else faceY(x-r,x+r,z-r,z+r,y,shDpWheel,C.OUT,0);
}
// Reuse the board's coat templates, but emit the billboard through the static world metadata too.
function depotSpriteAt(x,y,z,mirror=false) {
  const px0=Math.round(x-y)+tOX,py0=Math.round((x+y)*.5-z)+tOY;
  const native=typeof paperStaticTarget==='function'&&paperStaticTarget()?paperDepotSpriteTexture():null;
  const capture=native?null:paperCaptureSprite(x,y,z,mirror,tSun);
  const bias=shBias;shBias=2;
  if(native)paperStaticPrimitive++;
  try{
  for(let v=0;v<SHT;v++)for(let u=0;u<SW;u++) {
    let c=SPR[v*SW+u];if(c===EMPTY)continue;
    const dx=mirror?AX-u:u-AX,px=px0+dx,py=py0+v-AY;if(px<0||py<0||px>=tW||py>=tH)continue;
    const wx=x+dx*.5,wy=y-dx*.5,wz=z+AY-v;
    if(c===253)c=tSun && sunFn(wx,wy,wz,F_TOP)>.6?SPO[v*SW+u]:C.OUT;
    shLvl=0;emit(px,py,wx,wy,wz,F_TOP,c);
  }
  }finally{if(native)paperStaticPrimitive--;shBias=bias;}
  if(native)paperStaticSprite(native,x,y,z,mirror);
  paperFinishSprite(capture);
}
function depotRack() {
  dpWoodSeed=4;
  for(const x of [48,141]){dpBox(x,2,0,3,4,38,shDpWood);dpBox(x,2,1,3,15,3,shDpWood);}
  dpBox(48,2,35,96,4,3,shDpWood);dpBox(48,2,6,96,3,3,shDpWood);
  for(const h of DEPR.hooks){
    dpRod(h.x,4,35,h.x,8,35,C.COOL1);dpRod(h.x,8,35,h.x,9,32,C.BR1);dot3(h.x,9,31,C.BR3,0);
    dot3(h.x,6,36,C.OUT,0);
  }
}
let dpSackZ=0;
function shDpSack(face,th,h) {
  const z=dpSackZ+h;
  if(z<15 || th<.35)return C.KRAFT0;
  return th>1.1?C.KRAFT2:C.KRAFT;
}
function depotBag(h,pose) {
  const x=h.x,y=h.y,z=h.z,kind=pose || h.pose;dpBagCoat=h.cat;
  for(let k=0;k<17;k++) {
    const r=2.5+Math.sin((k+2)/20*Math.PI)*2.5;dpSackZ=z-21+k;
    cylinder(x,y+2,r,dpSackZ,dpSackZ+1,shDpSack,C.OUT,false);
  }
  disc(x,y+2,4.6,z-4,shDpSackMouth,C.OUT,false);
  dpRod(x-4,y+2,z-4,x-3,y,z,C.KRAFT0);dpRod(x+4,y+2,z-4,x+3,y,z,C.KRAFT2);
  const coat=h.cat==='blue'?COAT_BLUE:COAT_GINGER;
  if(kind!=='empty') {
    buildLoose(coat,'curl',0,0);
    if(kind==='head'){
      sprClear();KEY=coatKeyOf(coat);tpl(CAT_SIT_HEAD_F,AX-4,AY-6);KEY=KEY_S;outlineSprite();
    }
    if(dpBagBreath)sp(AX+1,AY-6,coat===COAT_BLUE?C.BC2:C.CT2);
    if(dpBagEar)sp(AX-5,AY-5,EMPTY);
    depotSpriteAt(x,y+2,z-5,(h.cat==='blue')!==dpBagTurn);
  }
  for(let j=0;j<=18;j++){
    const a=j/18*Math.PI;dot3(x+4.6*Math.cos(a),y+2+4.6*Math.sin(a),z-4,C.KRAFT2,0);
  }
  if(kind==='tail')for(let j=0;j<8;j++)dot3(x+3+Math.sin(j*.5),y+7.5,z-4-j,coat===COAT_BLUE?C.BC1:j%3===0?C.CT0:C.CT1,0);
  shBias=1;faceY(x-2,x+2,z-13,z-8,y+7.2,shDpTag,C.OUT,0);shBias=0;
  dpRod(x,y+7.2,z-8,x,y+5,z-5,C.KRAFT0);
}
function shDpTag(face,u,v) {
  if(v>1 && v<2 && u>1 && u<3)return C.WHITE;
  return dpBagCoat==='blue'?C.BL2:C.RD2;
}
function shDpSackMouth() { return C.OUT; }
function depotConveyor(incline) {
  const y0=incline?28:48,y1=incline?48:104;
  const height=y=>incline?64-(y-28)*2.6:12;
  for(const x of [8,22]) {
    for(const y of incline?[29,45]:[52,96])dpBox(x,y,0,2,2,Math.max(1,height(y)-2),shDpIron);
    dpQuad([x,y0,height(y0)-3],[x+2,y0,height(y0)-3],[x+2,y1,height(y1)-3],[x,y1,height(y1)-3],C.COOL1);
    dpRod(x,y0,height(y0),x,y1,height(y1),C.OUT);dpRod(x+1,y0,height(y0),x+1,y1,height(y1),C.FT1);
  }
  const count=incline?17:14;
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
    const y=y0+1+i*(y1-y0-2)/(count-1),z=height(y)-1;
    dpRod(10,y,z-1,22,y,z-1,C.COOL1);dpRod(10,y,z,22,y,z,((i+dpRoller)&3)===0?C.COOL1:C.FT1);
    dot3(9,y,z,C.BR0,0);dot3(23,y,z,C.BR0,0);
  }
  if(!incline){dpRod(8,55,3,24,96,8,C.PT0);dpBox(8,102,8,16,2,3,shDpSteel);}
}
function depotChute() {
  // Open mouth in the floor, polished brass lip and a dark well below it.
  dpBox(6,6,0,20,20,1,shDpBrass);faceTop(8,8,24,24,1.1,shDpDark,C.OUT,0);
  cylinder(16,16,1.5,2,73,shDpSteel,C.OUT,false);
  for(let i=0;i<84;i++) {
    const a=Math.PI*.5+i/84*TAU*2.2,b=Math.PI*.5+(i+1)/84*TAU*2.2,z=68-i/84*61+dpChuteShake*Math.sin(i*.4)*.5,Z=68-(i+1)/84*61+dpChuteShake*Math.sin((i+1)*.4)*.5;
    const p=[16+5*Math.cos(a),16+5*Math.sin(a),z],q=[16+11*Math.cos(a),16+11*Math.sin(a),z];
    const r=[16+11*Math.cos(b),16+11*Math.sin(b),Z],s=[16+5*Math.cos(b),16+5*Math.sin(b),Z];
    dpQuad([q[0],q[1],z-1.5],[r[0],r[1],Z-1.5],r,q,C.COOL1,F_Y);
    dpQuad(p,q,r,s,Math.sin(a)>.2?C.FT1:C.FT0);
    dpRod(...p,...s,C.COOL1);
    if(Math.cos(a)+Math.sin(a)<.35){
      dpQuad(q,r,[r[0],r[1],Z+4],[q[0],q[1],z+4],C.COOL1,F_Y);
      dpRod(q[0],q[1],z+4,r[0],r[1],Z+4,C.FT1);
    }else dpRod(...q,...r,C.FT1);
  }
  dpBox(6,4,70,20,3,4,shDpSteel);faceY(8,24,70,73,7.1,shDpDark,C.OUT,0);
}
function shDpDial(face,u,v) {
  const x=u-dpDialR,z=v-dpDialR,r=Math.hypot(x,z);
  if(r>dpDialR)return -1;
  if(r>dpDialR-1)return C.DEEP;
  const a=Math.atan2(z,x),marks=Math.abs(Math.sin(a*6));
  if(r>dpDialR-2 && marks<.3)return C.COOL1;
  const along=x*Math.cos(dpDialAngle)+z*Math.sin(dpDialAngle),cross=Math.abs(x*Math.sin(dpDialAngle)-z*Math.cos(dpDialAngle));
  if(along>-1 && along<dpDialR-2 && cross<.55)return C.BR1;
  return r<.85?C.BR1:C.CREAM;
}
function depotScale() {
  dpBox(8,112,0,20,12,3,shDpIron);dpBox(10,114,3,16,8,1,shDpSteel);
  for(let x=12;x<=24;x+=4)dpRod(x,115,4.1,x,121,4.1,C.COOL1);
  dpBox(16,114,4,4,4,24,shDpIron);dpBox(16,114,4,4,4,2,shDpIron);
  dpDialR=6;dpDialAngle=2.25;
  faceY(12,24,24,36,118,shDpDial,C.OUT,0);dpRod(17,118,10,19,118,10,C.BR1);
}

}
function shDpUrn(face,th,h) {
  if(h<1 || h>15)return C.BR0;
  if(Math.abs(th-1)<.12)return C.BR3;
  return th>.6?C.BR1:C.BR0;
}
function depotUrn() {
  const [x,y,z]=DEPR.furniture.find(p=>p.id==='tea-table').urn;
  cylinder(x,y,3,z,z+3,shDpBrass,C.OUT,true);cylinder(x,y,4.7,z+3,z+20,shDpUrn,C.OUT,true);
  disc(x,y,4.7,z+20,shDpBrass,C.OUT,true);cylinder(x,y,2,z+20,z+22,shDpBrass,C.OUT,false);
  cylinder(x,y,1,z+22,z+24,shDpDark,C.OUT,false);
  for(const side of [-1,1]){dpRod(x+side*4,y-1,z+14,x+side*5,y-1,z+14,C.BR0);dpRod(x+side*5,y-1,z+14,x+side*5,y+2,z+14,C.BR1);}
  dpBox(x-1,y+3,z+5,2,2,2,shDpBrass);dpRod(x,y+4,z+7,x,y+4,z+9,C.OUT);dpRod(x-1,y+4,z+9,x+1,y+4,z+9,C.BR1);
}
function shDpRadio(face,u,v) {
  shFlat=false;
  if(face===F_TOP)return C.WD2;
  if(face===F_X)return C.WD0;
  if(u<1 || u>7 || v<1 || v>7)return C.WD1;
  if(u<4)return C.KRAFT0;
  if(v>4 && v<6){shFlat=true;return C.GL0;}shFlat=false;
  return v<3 && (u<5 || u>6)?C.BR1:C.WD0;
}
function depotRadio() {
  const [x,y,z]=DEPR.furniture.find(p=>p.id==='tea-table').wireless;
  dpBox(x-4,y-3,z,8,6,8,shDpRadio);shFlat=false;
}
function shDpTin(face,u,v) {
  if(face!==F_TOP)return v<1?C.DEEP:C.PT0;
  if(u<1 || v<1 || u>7 || v>5)return C.PT1;
  return (u>2 && u<6 && v>2 && v<4)?C.CREAM:C.PT0;
}

}

function shDpCarrier(face,u,v) {
  if(face===F_TOP)return u<.6?C.KRAFT:C.KRAFT2;
  if(v<1 || v>6)return C.KRAFT0;
  if(face===F_Y){if(u<1 || u>15)return C.KRAFT;return (Math.floor(u)%3===0 || Math.floor(v)%3===0)?C.KRAFT2:C.DEEP;}
  return C.KRAFT;
}

function depotVan(cargo=true,closed=false) {
  // Original small post van: rounded bonnet and roof, plain panels, open cargo doors.
  shSunMax=0;
  dpBox(170,-37,4,30,32,5,shDpDark);
  for(const y of [-34,-11]){dpWheel(203,y,5,5,'X');dpWheel(169,y,5,5,'X');}
  dpBox(170,-24,9,31,17,2,shDpWood);dpBox(169,-24,9,3,18,17,shDpRed);dpBox(199,-24,9,3,18,17,shDpRed);
  dpBox(172,-25,10,27,3,17,shDpRed);
  for(let x=169;x<202;x++){
    const z=26+4*Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-((x-185.5)/17)**2));
    faceTop(x,-24,x+1,-7,z,shDpRed,C.RD0,0);
  }
  // A narrower cab, side glazing and rounded wings distinguish it from a box lorry.
  dpBox(173,-33,9,26,9,15,shDpRed);
  faceX(-32,-25,17,23,199.1,shDpSteel,C.OUT,3);
  dpRod(199.2,-28,18,199.2,-26,18,C.FT1);
  dpRod(199.2,-25,12,199.2,-25,16,C.RD0);dot3(199.2,-26,16,C.BR1,0);
  for(const x of [173,197])cylinder(x,-34,4,8,12,shDpRed,C.RD0,true);
  for(let y=-42;y<-31;y++){
    const r=Math.min(1,(y+43)/7),x=185,w=12*r,z=16+Math.sin((y+42)/12*Math.PI)*3;
    dpBox(x-w,y,8,w*2,1,z-8,shDpRed);
  }
  // Cargo in quiet hessian; floor planks and a steel threshold remain visible.
  for(const x of cargo?[176,185,194]:[]){
    dpBox(x-3,-20,11,6,8,7,shDpCanvas);dpBox(x-2,-18,18,4,4,2,shDpCanvas);
    dpRod(x-2,-17,19,x+2,-17,19,C.KRAFT0);
  }
  if(closed){
    dpBox(170,-7,10,31,1,16,shDpRed);dpRod(185.5,-5.9,10,185.5,-5.9,26,C.RD0);
    dpRod(183,-5.8,17,184,-5.8,17,C.BR2);dpRod(187,-5.8,17,188,-5.8,17,C.BR2);
  }else{
  dpQuad([169,-7,10],[173,0,10],[173,0,25],[169,-7,25],C.RD1,F_Y);
  dpQuad([201,-7,10],[197,0,10],[197,0,25],[201,-7,25],C.RD0,F_Y);
  dpRod(169,-7,25,173,0,25,C.RD2);dpRod(201,-7,25,197,0,25,C.RD1);
  dpRod(173,0,10,173,0,25,C.OUT);dpRod(197,0,10,197,0,25,C.OUT);
  }
  dpRod(172,-5,9,198,-5,9,C.FT1);dpBox(167,-5,5,36,2,2,shDpSteel);
  dot3(170,-4,8,C.RD2,0);dot3(201,-4,8,C.RD2,0);shSunMax=1;
}
function depotClock() {
  dpDialR=8;dpDialAngle=Math.PI*.55;
  faceY(176,192,68,84,2,shDpDial,C.OUT,0);
  dpRod(184,2.2,76,180,2.2,78,C.DEEP);dot3(184,2.3,76,C.BR1,0);
}
function shDpNotice(face,u,v) {
  if(u<1 || u>13 || v<1 || v>23)return C.WD1;
  if(u>2 && u<7 && v>13 && v<21){
    if(v>20)return C.BR1;return v<15?C.KRAFT0:C.CREAM;
  }
  if(u>7 && u<12 && v>3 && v<11){
    if(v>10 || v<4 || u<8 || u>11)return C.CREAM;
    if((v<8 && u>8 && u<11) || (v>7 && v<9 && Math.abs(u-9.5)>.5))return C.DEEP;
    return C.FT0;
  }
  if(pawShape(u,v,4,7,2))return C.WD0;
  return C.KRAFT;
}
function depotNotice() { faceY(210,224,32,56,2,shDpNotice,C.OUT,0); }
function depotPigeon(p,mirror) {
  sprClear();
  rect(AX-3,AY-5,6,4,C.COOL1);rect(AX-2,AY-5,4,2,C.FT0);
  rect(AX-4,AY-7,3,4,C.COOL1);sp(AX-4,AY-6,C.OUT);sp(AX-5,AY-5,C.BR1);
  run(AX-1,AY-2,4,C.DEEP);run(AX+3,AY-2,2,C.COOL1);
  sp(AX-2,AY,C.BR1);sp(AX+1,AY,C.BR1);outlineSprite();depotSpriteAt(...p,mirror);
}
function depotPropCatalog(room) {
  const entries=room.furniture.map(p=>({id:p.id,anchor:p.anchor,variants:['rest']}));
  const tea=room.furniture.find(p=>p.id==='tea-table');
  for(const [id,key] of [['urn','urn'],['wireless','wireless'],['biscuit-tin','biscuitTin']])entries.push({id,anchor:tea[key],variants:['rest']});
  for(const coat of ['ginger','blue'])entries.push({id:'bag-'+coat,anchor:[room.hooks[0].x,room.hooks[0].y,room.hooks[0].z],variants:['empty','curled','head','tail']});
  entries.push({id:'lamp',anchor:[room.lamps[0].x,room.lamps[0].y,room.lamps[0].z],variants:['on','off']});
  entries.push({id:'pigeon',anchor:room.characters.pigeons.perches[0],variants:['left','right']});return entries;
}
function drawDepotProp(id,variant) {
  shBoard=0;shFlag=FL_ROOM;shFlat=false;shLvl=0;shBias=0;shSunMax=1;
  switch(id) {
    case 'chute':depotChute();break;
    case 'conveyor-run':depotConveyor(false);break;
    case 'conveyor-incline':depotConveyor(true);break;
    case 'sack-rack':depotRack();break;
    case 'scale':depotScale();break;
    case 'tea-table':depotTeaTable();break;
    case 'urn':depotUrn();break;
    case 'wireless':depotRadio();break;
    case 'biscuit-tin':depotTin();break;
    case 'tea-stool':depotStool();break;
    case 'mug-hooks':depotMugs();break;
    case 'flat-packs':depotFlatPacks();break;
    case 'sack-pile':depotSackPile();break;
    case 'trolley':depotTrolley();break;
    case 'sack-barrow':depotBarrow();break;
    case 'carrier':depotCarrier();break;
    case 'van':depotVan();break;
    case 'clock':depotClock();break;
    case 'noticeboard':depotNotice();break;
    case 'bag-ginger':case 'bag-blue':depotBag({...DEPR.hooks[0],cat:id.slice(4)},variant);break;
    case 'pigeon':depotPigeon(DEPR.characters.pigeons.perches[0],variant==='right');break;
    case 'lamp':{
      depotLamp(DEPR.lamps[0],variant==='on');break;
    }
    default:throw new Error('Unknown depot prop '+id);
  }
  shFlat=false;shBias=0;shFlag=FL_ROOM;shSunMax=1;
}
function bakeDepotProps() {
  for(const p of DEPR.furniture)if(!['chute','clock','conveyor-run','conveyor-incline','flat-packs'].includes(p.id))drawDepotProp(p.id,'rest');
  for(const id of ['urn','wireless','biscuit-tin'])drawDepotProp(id,'rest');
  bakeDepotSortingFrames();
  // Moving bags, pigeons and machinery are cached separately; the bake contains no ghost pose.
}
// Review/export only: allocate a neutral cell around the data origin, never in the frame loop.
function depotPropCell(id,variant) {
  const p=depotPropCatalog(DEPR).find(p=>p.id===id);if(!p)throw new Error('Unknown depot prop '+id);
  const w=256,h=256,idx=new Uint8Array(w*h).fill(255),lvl=new Uint8Array(w*h).fill(2),dep=new Int16Array(w*h).fill(DEPTH_EMPTY);
  const a=p.anchor,ox=128-(a[0]-a[1]),oy=160-Math.round((a[0]+a[1])*.5-a[2]),keep=[aoFn,lampFn];
  targetCustom(idx,lvl,dep,w,h,ox,oy);aoFn=null;lampFn=null;
  drawDepotProp(id,variant || p.variants[0]);
  let x0=w,y0=h,x1=-1,y1=-1;
  for(let k=0;k<idx.length;k++)if(idx[k]!==255){idx[k]=LUT[lvl[k]*PAL_N+idx[k]];const x=k%w,y=(k/w)|0;x0=Math.min(x0,x);x1=Math.max(x1,x);y0=Math.min(y0,y);y1=Math.max(y1,y);}
  aoFn=keep[0];lampFn=keep[1];targetStatic();shSunMax=2;shFlag=0;
  return {w,h,idx,anchor:a.slice(),origin:[128,160],bounds:[x0,y0,x1,y1]};
}
// Depot actors use the same procedural sprite buffer, outlines, depth and light hooks as the
// sorter. Moving fixture variants are cached once on room entry, never rasterised each frame.
let DPLIFE=null,DEPOT_DEMO=null,DEPOT_SRC=null,DP_CACHE=[];
function depotLifeInit(room){
  DPLIFE=DEPOTWORLD.create(room);DEPOT_DEMO=DPLIFE.demo;
  const d=DEPOT_DEMO,s=makeBoardSrc(d.traces.length);s.ox=room.board.x;s.oy=room.board.y;s.w=7;s.h=5;
  for(const h of d.level.hatches)s.hatches.push({x:h.x,y:h.y,dir:GR_DIR[h.dir]});
  for(const b of d.level.baskets)s.baskets.push({x:b.x,y:b.y,coat:GR_COAT[b.coat]});
  for(const t of d.turns)s.turntables.push({x:t.x,y:t.y,dir:GR_DIR[t.dir],kind:GR_TYPE[t.type]});
  for(const p of d.level.dogs)s.dogs.push(p);
  for(const g of d.level.gates)s.gates.push({x:g.x,y:g.y,a:GR_DIR[g.exits[0]],b:GR_DIR[g.exits[1]],pose:BOARDANIM.makeGateOut()});
  s.n=d.traces.length;s.traces=d.traces;DEPOT_SRC=s;
}
function depotDemoPrepare(){
  const t=DPLIFE.frame/60,s=DEPOT_SRC;
  for(let i=0;i<s.n;i++)s.times[i]=t>=s.traces[i].rel-s.traces[i].pop&&t<DEPOT_DEMO.ends[i]?t:-1;
  for(let i=0;i<s.gates.length;i++)BOARDANIM.gatePose(DEPOT_DEMO.gates,i,t,s.gates[i].pose);
}
// Capture a sparse world-space indexed/depth cell, already shaded through the depot light model.
function depotCachedCell(draw){
  const n=WW*WH,idx=new Uint8Array(n).fill(255),lvl=new Uint8Array(n),dep=new Int16Array(n).fill(DEPTH_EMPTY);
  targetCustom(idx,lvl,dep,WW,WH,OX,OY);tSun=true;aoFn=null;lampFn=depotFrameLight;
  shFlag=0;shBoard=0;shBias=0;shFlat=false;shSunMax=1;
  paperCacheBuild=true;paperCachePiece=null;
  try{draw();}finally{paperCacheBuild=false;}
  const pixels=[];for(let i=0;i<n;i++)if(idx[i]!==255)pixels.push(i);
  const out={x:new Int16Array(pixels.length),y:new Int16Array(pixels.length),idx:new Uint8Array(pixels.length),lvl:new Uint8Array(pixels.length),dep:new Int16Array(pixels.length)};
  for(let k=0;k<pixels.length;k++){const i=pixels[k];out.x[k]=i%WW;out.y[k]=(i/WW)|0;out.idx[k]=idx[i];out.lvl[k]=lvl[i];out.dep[k]=dep[i];}
  if(paperCachePiece)out.paper=paperCachePiece;
  targetStatic();aoFn=depotBakeLight;lampFn=null;return out;
}
function depotLifeBake(){
  DP_CACHE=[];
  for(let i=0;i<DEPR.hooks.length;i++){
    const h=DEPR.hooks[i],frames=[];
    for(let k=0;k<6;k++)frames.push(depotCachedCell(()=>{
      dpBagBreath=k===2?1:0;dpBagEar=k===3?1:0;dpBagTurn=k===4||k===5;
      const sway=k===4?1:k===5?-1:0;
      depotBag({...h,x:h.x+sway*.5,y:h.y-sway*.5},k===0?'empty':h.pose);dpBagBreath=dpBagEar=0;dpBagTurn=false;
    }));DP_CACHE.push({kind:'bag',i,frames});
  }
  for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
    const l=DEPR.lamps[i],frames=[];
    for(let k=-1;k<=1;k++)frames.push(depotCachedCell(()=>depotLamp({...l,x:l.x+k*.5,y:l.y-k*.5},true)));
    DP_CACHE.push({kind:'lamp',i,frames});
  }
  DP_CACHE.push({kind:'chute',frames:[0,1].map(k=>depotCachedCell(()=>{dpChuteShake=k;depotChute();dpChuteShake=0;}))});
  const rollers=[];for(let k=0;k<4;k++)rollers.push(depotCachedCell(()=>{dpRoller=k;depotConveyor(false);depotConveyor(true);dpRoller=0;}));
  DP_CACHE.push({kind:'roller',frames:rollers});
  const scale=[];for(let k=0;k<16;k++)scale.push(depotCachedCell(()=>{dpDialR=6;dpDialAngle=2.25-k*.26;shBias=3;faceY(12,24,24,36,118,shDpDial,C.OUT,0);shBias=0;}));
  DP_CACHE.push({kind:'needle',frames:scale});
  const clock=[];for(let k=0;k<60;k++)clock.push(depotCachedCell(()=>{dpDialR=8;dpDialAngle=Math.PI*.5-k/60*TAU;faceY(176,192,68,84,2,shDpDial,C.OUT,0);const hour=2.68+Math.sin(k/60*TAU)*.28;dpRod(184,2.2,76,184+4*Math.cos(hour),2.2,76+4*Math.sin(hour),C.DEEP);dot3(184,2.3,76,C.BR1,0);}));
  DP_CACHE.push({kind:'clock',frames:clock});
  DP_CACHE.push({kind:'packs',frames:[depotCachedCell(()=>{dpStackCount=3;depotFlatPacks();dpStackCount=4;}),depotCachedCell(()=>depotFlatPacks())]});
  depotSyncBaskets(DEPOT_SRC);
}
function depotBlitCell(c){
  const paper=paperCaptureCachedPiece(c);
  for(let k=0;k<c.idx.length;k++){
    const x=c.x[k]-viewX,y=c.y[k]-viewY;if(x<0||y<0||x>=LW||y>=LH)continue;
    const i=y*LW+x;if(c.dep[k]<fDep[i])continue;fIdx[i]=c.idx[k];fLvl[i]=c.lvl[k];fDep[i]=c.dep[k];
  }
  paperFinishPiece(paper);
}
function drawDepotFixtures(){
  for(let k=0;k<DP_CACHE.length;k++){
    const c=DP_CACHE[k];let f=0;
    switch(c.kind){
      case 'bag':{const b=DPLIFE.bags[c.i];if(!b.on)continue;f=!b.cat?0:b.sway>0?4:b.sway<0?5:b.ear?3:b.breath?2:1;break;}
      case 'lamp':f=DPLIFE.machine.lamps[c.i]+1;break;
      case 'roller':f=DPLIFE.machine.roller;break;
      case 'chute':f=DPLIFE.machine.chute;break;
      case 'needle':f=DPLIFE.machine.needle;break;
      case 'clock':f=DPLIFE.machine.clock;break;
      case 'packs':f=DPLIFE.stores[1]===4?1:0;break;
    }
    depotBlitCell(c.frames[f]);
  }

}
// Four facings from two hand-placed views. The long, split dustcoat has its own silhouette.
function buildDriver(d){
  sprClear();const x=AX-7,y=AY-28+d.bob,back=d.face===2||d.face===3;
  const stride=d.walk===0?-2:d.walk===2?2:0,sway=d.walk>=0?stride/2:0;
  rect(x+4,AY-6,3,5,C.BL0);rect(x+8,AY-6,3,5,C.BL1);
  run(x+3+stride,AY,4,C.WD0);run(x+8-stride,AY,4,C.WD0);sp(x+4+stride,AY-1,C.WD1);sp(x+9-stride,AY-1,C.WD1);
  rect(x+10,y+11,3,10,C.BL0);rect(x+10,y+20,3,2,C.WD1);
  rect(x+3,y+10,8,12,C.BL1);rect(x+9,y+11,2,11,C.BL0);run(x+4,y+10,6,C.BL2);
  rect(x+2+sway,y+20,5,4,C.BL1);rect(x+8+sway,y+20,4,4,C.BL0);sp(x+3+sway,y+23,C.BL2);
  run(x+4,y+19,6,C.BL0);sp(x+8,y+19,C.BR1);
  if(!back){rect(x+5,y+10,3,6,C.CREAM);run(x+5,y+15,3,C.PAPSH);for(let j=14;j<22;j+=3)sp(x+7,y+j,C.BR3);}
  else{run(x+4,y+11,6,C.BL2);rect(x+7,y+13,1,6,C.BL0);}
  // Cream knitted scarf over the lapel, with a two-pixel fringe.
  run(x+4,y+10,6,C.CREAM);rect(x+3,y+11,2,5,C.CREAM);sp(x+3,y+13,C.PAPSH);sp(x+3,y+16,C.CREAM);
  rect(x+4,y+4,6,6,back?C.WD0:C.SK0);rect(x+9,y+5,2,5,C.WD0);
  if(!back){sp(x+4,y+6,C.OUT);sp(x+6,y+6,C.OUT);sp(x+3,y+8,C.SK0);run(x+5,y+9,3,C.SK1);}
  else{rect(x+5,y+4,4,3,C.WD1);sp(x+4,y+8,C.SK1);}
  rect(x+4,y,6,3,C.BL1);run(x+5,y,4,C.BL2);run(x+2,y+3,10,C.BL0);sp(x+5,y+2,C.BR3);
  if(!d.pencil){sp(x+10,y+4,C.BR1);sp(x+10,y+5,C.CREAM);sp(x+10,y+6,C.BR1);}
  const hy=d.pour?y-5:d.reach?y+4:d.pencil?y+17:d.hold?y+16:y+21;
  const hx=x+(d.reach?2:d.hold?0:2)+(d.walk>=0?stride:0);
  for(let j=0;j<=8;j++){const t=j/8,xx=Math.round(mixDriver(x+3,hx,t)),yy=Math.round(mixDriver(y+12,hy,t));sp(xx,yy,C.BL2);sp(xx+1,yy,C.BL1);}
  run(hx,hy+1,3,C.WD1);sp(hx,hy,C.WD2);
  if(d.pencil){sp(hx-1,hy,C.BR1);sp(hx-2,hy+1,C.BR1);sp(hx-3,hy+2,C.OUT);}
  if(d.scratch)sp(hx-1,hy+2,C.WD2);
  outlineSprite();
}
function mixDriver(a,b,t){return a+(b-a)*t;}
function buildDepotPigeon(a){
  sprClear();const bob=a.state==='peck'?(a.frame&1?2:0):a.bob;
  rect(AX-3,AY-5,6,4,C.COOL1);rect(AX-2,AY-5,4,2,C.FT0);
  rect(AX-4,AY-7+bob,3,4,C.COOL1);sp(AX-4,AY-6+bob,C.OUT);sp(AX-5,AY-5+bob,C.BR1);
  run(AX-1,AY-2,4,C.DEEP);run(AX+3,AY-2,2,C.COOL1);sp(AX-2,AY,C.BR1);sp(AX+1,AY,C.BR1);
  if(a.state==='fly'){
    const up=(a.frame&1)===0;
    for(let i=0;i<7;i++){const y=AY-4+(up?-i:i);run(AX+i-1,y,3,C.FT0);sp(AX+i+1,y,C.COOL1);}
    for(let i=0;i<4;i++)run(AX-i-1,AY-4+(up?-i:i),2,C.COOL1);
  }
  outlineSprite();
}
function buildDepotCargo(c){
  if(c.kind==='none')return;
  if(c.kind==='box'){buildCatBox(COAT_GINGER,1,1,0,0,0,0,true);if(c.tag){run(AX-2,AY-3,4,C.KRAFT2);sp(AX-1,AY-2,C.OUT);}}
  else if(c.kind==='cat'){
    buildLoose(COAT_GINGER,'curl',0,0);
    if(c.fold>0){KEY=KEY_I;tpl(c.fold<.5?HI_HALF:HI_OPEN,AX-4,AY-2);KEY=KEY_S;outlineSprite();}
  }else{
    sprClear();const height=c.kind==='empty-bag'?7-Math.round(c.fold*6):7;
    rect(AX-4,AY-height,8,height,C.KRAFT);run(AX-3,AY-height-1,6,C.KRAFT2);run(AX-3,AY-1,6,C.KRAFT0);
    for(let y=AY-height+1;y<AY;y+=2)sp(AX+2,y,C.KRAFT0);
    if(c.kind==='full-bag'){KEY=coatKeyOf(COAT_GINGER);tpl(CUB_PEEK_BLINK,AX-2,AY-11);KEY=KEY_S;}
    outlineSprite();
  }
}
function drawDepotCargo(c=DPLIFE.cargo){
  if(c.kind==='none')return;buildDepotCargo(c);
  blitClipZ=c.place==='hatch'?0:-1e9;blitSprite(c.x,c.y,c.z,false,true);blitClipZ=-1e9;
}
function drawDepotMug(){
  const m=DPLIFE.mug;if(!m.on)return;sprClear();rect(AX-1,AY-3,3,4,C.CREAM);run(AX-1,AY-3,3,C.BL1);sp(AX+2,AY-2,C.CREAM);sp(AX+2,AY-1,C.CREAM);outlineSprite();blitSprite(m.x,m.y,m.z,false,true);
  if(m.pour){shBias=2;dpRod(m.x,m.y,m.z+3,m.x,m.y,m.z+5,C.CREAM);shBias=0;}
}
function drawDepotLifeItem(id){
  shFlat=false;shBias=0;shFlag=0;shSunMax=1;
  if(id===20){const d=DPLIFE.driver;buildDriver(d);blitSprite(d.x,d.y,0,d.face===1||d.face===3,true);drawDepotMug();}
  else if(id===21){Object.assign(cat,DPLIFE.pm);drawCat();}
  else if(id===22)drawDepotFixtures();
  else if(id===23)drawDepotCargo();
  else if(id===29)drawDepotCargo(DPLIFE.parcel);
  else if(id>=24&&id<=26){const a=DPLIFE.pigeons[id-24];buildDepotPigeon(a);blitSprite(a.x,a.y,a.z,a.face===1,true);}
}
// The final 48 slots in the EXISTING 200-particle pool belong to analytic depot effects.
// Slots 152–175 are scenery, 176–199 are board delivery effects. Game bursts use slots 0–151.
const DP_PARTICLES={type:PTYPE,x:PX,y:PY,z:PZ,life:PLIFE,max:PMAXL,col:PCOL,phase:PPH,
  pale:C.MS1,mist:C.MS0,cream:C.CREAM,brass:C.BR3,heart:C.RD3,ginger:C.RD4,blue:C.BL4,stone:C.FT1};
function depotLifeParticles(){DEPOTWORLD.particles(DPLIFE,DP_PARTICLES,GAME_MODE);}
// Neutral family audit and future atlas input; no effect on the public atlas until stage 8.
function depotLifeColours(){
  const driver=new Set(),pigeon=new Set(),cargo={cat:new Set(),box:new Set(),'empty-bag':new Set(),'full-bag':new Set()},keep=shBoard;shBoard=0;
  const collect=set=>{for(let i=0;i<SPR.length;i++)if(SPR[i]!==EMPTY)set.add(SPR[i]===253?C.OUT:SPR[i]);};
  for(let f=0;f<2880;f+=6){DEPOTWORLD.step(DPLIFE,f);buildDriver(DPLIFE.driver);collect(driver);if(DPLIFE.cargo.kind!=='none'){buildDepotCargo(DPLIFE.cargo);collect(cargo[DPLIFE.cargo.kind]);}for(const a of DPLIFE.pigeons){buildDepotPigeon(a);collect(pigeon);}}
  DEPOTWORLD.step(DPLIFE,WS.frame);shBoard=keep;return {driver:driver.size,pigeon:pigeon.size,cat:cargo.cat.size,box:cargo.box.size,emptyBag:cargo['empty-bag'].size,fullBag:cargo['full-bag'].size};
}
function depotActorShadow(a,r,h){
  contactShadow(a.x,a.y,Math.round(r*1.7),2,.8);
  const dx=DEPR.sun.direction[0]/-DEPR.sun.direction[2],dy=DEPR.sun.direction[1]/-DEPR.sun.direction[2];
  viewBox(a.x-r,Math.min(a.y,a.y+dy*h)-r,a.x+dx*h+r,a.y+r,0,0);
  const norm=dx*dx+dy*dy,B=DEPR.board;
  for(let py=VB[2];py<=VB[3];py++)for(let px=VB[0];px<=VB[1];px++){
    const i=py*LW+px,k=(py+viewY)*WW+px+viewX;
    if(fDep[i]!==sDep[k]||!(sFlg[k]&FL_FLOOR)||!(sFlg[k]>>6))continue;
    const x=sWX[k]/4,y=sWY[k]/4;
    // Readable stone is reserved for the real board. Cast shadows end at its kerb.
    if(x>=B.x-2&&x<=B.x+B.w*16+2&&y>=B.y-2&&y<=B.y+B.h*16+2)continue;
    const t=((x-a.x)*dx+(y-a.y)*dy)/norm;if(t<0||t>h)continue;
    const dist=Math.hypot(x-a.x-dx*t,y-a.y-dy*t),radius=r*(1-.4*t/h);
    if(dist<radius&&bayer(px+viewX,py+viewY)<Math.min(1,radius-dist+.3))fLvl[i]=Math.max(0,fLvl[i]-(sFlg[k]>>6));
  }
}
function postDepotLife(){
  if(!DPLIFE)return;if(drawItemHook)drawItemHook(28,false);
  depotActorShadow(DPLIFE.driver,3,28);
  if(DPLIFE.pm.z===0)depotActorShadow(DPLIFE.pm,3,DPLIFE.pm.state===CS_SLEEP?4:8);
  if(DPLIFE.pigeons[1].z===0)contactShadow(DPLIFE.pigeons[1].x,DPLIFE.pigeons[1].y,4,2,.6);
  if(drawItemHook)drawItemHook(28,true);
}

function drawDepotFlaps(src=DEPOT_SRC){
  for(let i=0;i<src.baskets.length;i++){
    const b=src.baskets[i],x=src.ox+b.x*TILE+3;
    flapColour=b.coat===COAT_GINGER?C.RD2:C.BL2;flapSwing=boardCatsAt(src,b.x,0,bdFlap);
    shBias=3;faceY(x,x+10,.4,7.6,src.oy,shCatFlap,C.OUT,0);shBias=0;
  }
}
// Basket geometry and light do not move. Cache its empty/occupied art at each level's
// actual origin; the shared board still decides which state to show and depth-tests it.
let depotPieceCacheEnabled=true;
function depotSyncBaskets(src){
  src.depotBaskets=src.baskets.map(b=>[0,1].map(occupied=>depotCachedCell(()=>{
    shBoard=128;lampFn=null;drawBasket(src.ox+b.x*TILE+8,src.oy+b.y*TILE+8,b.coat,occupied);
  })));
  for(const h of src.hatches)h.depotCells=[0,1,2].map(open=>depotCachedCell(()=>{shBoard=128;lampFn=null;drawHatch(bdX(src,h.x),bdY(src,h.y),h.dir,open);}));
  for(const t of src.turntables)t.depotCells=[0,1,2].map(spin=>depotCachedCell(()=>{shBoard=128;lampFn=null;drawTurntable(bdX(src,t.x),bdY(src,t.y),t.dir,t.kind,spin,t.locked);}));
  src.depotRails=src.rails?depotCachedCell(()=>{
    shBoard=128;lampFn=null;drawBoardRails(src);
  }):null;
  aoFn=null;lampFn=null;shBoard=0;shSunMax=2;
}
function drawDepotBoardItem(id){
  const k=id-IT_BOARD0;
  if(depotPieceCacheEnabled&&k>=BD_BASKET0&&k<BD_DOG0&&boardSrc.depotBaskets){
    const j=k-BD_BASKET0,b=boardSrc.baskets[j],occupied=boardCatsAt(boardSrc,b.x,b.y,bdNapping);
    depotBlitCell(boardSrc.depotBaskets[j][occupied?1:0]);
  }else if(k===BD_FLAT&&depotPieceCacheEnabled&&boardSrc.rails&&boardSrc.depotRails){
    depotBlitCell(boardSrc.depotRails);boardSrc.rails=false;drawItem(id);boardSrc.rails=true;
  }else drawItem(id);
}
// Three low rolling frames on the clear front apron. Native paper art, shared
// sprite depth and light; nothing is baked into the board or moved under a tap.
function buildDepotSortingFrame(){sprClear();SPR.set(DEPOT_FRAME_SOURCE);}
function depotChapterFramesOn(){
  return typeof GAME_MODE!=='undefined'&&GAME_MODE&&gm.L&&!gm.custom&&gm.L.id[0]==='4'&&gm.L.id[1]==='-';
}
function bakeDepotSortingFrames(){
  if(typeof DEPR.chapterFramesOffset!=='number')return;
  for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
    buildDepotSortingFrame();depotSpriteAt(88+i*14+DEPR.chapterFramesOffset,166,0);
  }
}
function drawDepotSortingFrames(){
  const moving=cpActive()&&CP.mode==='growth';
  const offset=moving?CP.motion.offset:DEPR.chapterFramesOffset||0;
  for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
    const x=88+i*14+offset,y=166;
    contactShadow(x-4,y-4,6,3,.6);
    if(moving){buildDepotSortingFrame();blitSprite(x,y,0,false,true);}
  }
}
// Depot-only composition cache. Keep full indexed/light/depth buffers for the same renderer and
// coverage tooling; cache the still frame, then resolve only the world/motion rows. Typed arrays
// are allocated on resize. Camera steps resample a flattened backdrop texture, not every layer.
let depotFastEnabled=true,DP_VIEW=null;
function depotAllocFrame(){
  const n=LW*LH;
  DP_VIEW={idx:new Uint8Array(n),lvl:new Uint8Array(n),dep:new Int16Array(n),rgba:new Uint32Array(n),
    x:Infinity,y:Infinity,gray:null,cache:null,sky:null,skyTop:0,skyH:0,opening:new Uint8Array(n)};
  BD.frame={idx:new Uint8Array(n),lvl:new Uint8Array(n),opening:DP_VIEW.opening};
  if(!BD.cache||BD.cache.height<LH+128)BD.cache=bakeBackdropLayers(BD.scene,BD.set,LH+128);
  depotFlatBackdrop(BD.cache);
}
function depotFlatBackdrop(cache){
  const top=-Math.ceil(LH/2)-160,h=LH+320,w=cache.width,sky=new Uint16Array(w*h),front=new Int8Array(w*h).fill(-1);
  for(const l of cache.layers)if(l.parallax!==.3||l.scroll!==0)throw new Error('Depot cache needs stationary layers at parallax 0.3');
  for(let y=0;y<h;y++)for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
    const sy=y+top-cache.skyTop,i=y*w+x;
    let value=sy<0?cache.skyAbove:cache.sky[clamp(sy,0,cache.skyH-1)*w+x];
    for(let k=0;k<cache.layers.length;k++){
      const l=cache.layers[k],ly=y+top-l.y0;if(ly<0)continue;
      const p=Math.min(ly,cache.height-1)*w+x;
      if(l.idx[p]!==255){value=l.idx[p]|l.lvl[p]<<8;front[i]=k;}
    }
    sky[i]=value;
  }
  for(const l of cache.layers)for(const m of l.motions||[]){
    const d=m.data;if(d.kind==='train')continue;
    m.mask=new Uint8Array(d.w*d.h).fill(255);
    for(let y=0;y<d.h;y++)for(let x=0;x<d.w;x++){
      if(backdropMistContains(d,x,y))m.mask[y*d.w+x]=m.colour;
    }
  }
  const n=LW*LH,size=w*h,v=DP_VIEW;
  v.rawIdx=new Uint8Array(n+size+WW*WH);v.rawLvl=new Uint8Array(n+size+WW*WH);v.rawRGBA=new Uint32Array(n+size*2+WW*WH*2);
  v.rawDep=new Int16Array(n+WW*WH);v.dep=v.rawDep.subarray(0,n);v.rawDep.set(sDep.subarray(0,WW*WH),n);
  v.idx=v.rawIdx.subarray(0,n);v.lvl=v.rawLvl.subarray(0,n);v.rgba=v.rawRGBA.subarray(0,n);
  for(let i=0;i<size;i++){const c=sky[i]&127,l=sky[i]>>8;v.rawIdx[n+i]=c;v.rawLvl[n+i]=l;v.rawRGBA[n+i]=RES32[l*PAL_N+c];v.rawRGBA[n+size+i]=GRAY32[l*PAL_N+c];}
  v.rawIdx.set(sIdx.subarray(0,WW*WH),n+size);v.rawLvl.set(sLvl.subarray(0,WW*WH),n+size);
  const runs=[];
  for(let y=0;y<WH;y++){
    let x=0;
    while(x<WW){
      const i=y*WW+x;if(sDep[i]===DEPTH_EMPTY){x++;continue;}
      const start=x,glass=sFlg[i]&FL_GLASS;
      while(x<WW&&sDep[y*WW+x]!==DEPTH_EMPTY&&(sFlg[y*WW+x]&FL_GLASS)===glass)x++;
      runs.push(y,start,x,glass);
    }
  }
  v.runs=Int16Array.from(runs);
  for(let i=0;i<WW*WH;i++){
    const c=sIdx[i],at=(c>>7)*NLVL*PAL_N+sLvl[i]*PAL_N+(c&127);
    v.rawRGBA[n+size*2+i]=RES32[at];v.rawRGBA[n+size*2+WW*WH+i]=GRAY32[at];
  }
  DP_VIEW.sky=sky;DP_VIEW.front=front;DP_VIEW.skyTop=top;DP_VIEW.skyH=h;DP_VIEW.cache=cache;
}
// Each moving silhouette is a tiny indexed texture. The static front-layer mask preserves
// occlusion by nearer walls/roofs without resampling every layer for every mist pixel.
function depotFastMotion(cache,cx,cy){
  prepareBackdropMotion(cache,WS.frame);
  const v=DP_VIEW,w=cache.width,sx0=Math.floor(-LW/2+cx*.3),sy0=Math.floor(-LH/2+cy*.3);
  for(let k=0;k<cache.layers.length;k++){
    const l=cache.layers[k];if(!l.motions)continue;
    for(let j=0;j<l.motions.length;j++){
      const m=l.motions[j],d=m.data,s=m.state;if(!s[2])continue;
      const train=d.kind==='train',mask=train?m.idx:m.mask;
      const x0=train?clamp(s[0]-sx0,0,LW):0,x1=train?clamp(s[0]+d.w-sx0,0,LW):LW;
      const top=m.base+s[1]-(train?d.h:0),y0=clamp(top-sy0,0,LH),y1=clamp(top+d.h-sy0,0,LH);
      for(let y=y0;y<y1;y++){
        const source=(y+sy0-top)*d.w,frontRow=clamp(y+sy0-v.skyTop,0,v.skyH-1)*w;
        let u=bdMod(x0+sx0-s[0],d.w),fx=bdMod(x0+sx0,w);
        for(let x=x0;x<x1;x++){
          const i=y*LW+x,c=mask[source+u];
          if(c!==255&&v.front[frontRow+fx]<=k&&(fDep[i]===DEPTH_EMPTY||v.opening[i])){fIdx[i]=c;fLvl[i]=m.level;}
          if(++u===d.w)u=0;if(++fx===w)fx=0;
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
function depotFastFrame(){
  const v=DP_VIEW;if(!v)return false;
  if(!BD.cache||BD.cache.height<LH+128)BD.cache=bakeBackdropLayers(BD.scene,BD.set,LH+128);
  const cache=BD.cache;
  if(v.cache!==cache){depotFlatBackdrop(cache);v.x=Infinity;}
  const cx=viewX+LW/2-VIEW_CX,cy=viewY+LH/2-VIEW_CY;
  if(v.x!==viewX||v.y!==viewY||v.gray!==grayscale){
    const T=grayscale?GRAY32:RES32,half=NLVL*PAL_N,w=cache.width;
    const sx0=Math.floor(-LW/2+cx*.3),sy0=Math.floor(-LH/2+cy*.3)-v.skyTop;
    const n=LW*LH,sx=bdMod(sx0,w),colourOffset=grayscale?v.skyH*w:0;
    v.dep.fill(DEPTH_EMPTY);v.opening.fill(0);
    for(let y=0;y<LH;y++){
      const source=clamp(sy0+y,0,v.skyH-1)*w,row=y*LW;
      // Template and destination share a backing array, so copyWithin performs bulk copies
      // without creating subarray views in the frame loop.
      for(let x=0;x<LW;){
        const u=x===0?sx:0,len=Math.min(LW-x,w-u),p=n+source+u;
        v.rawIdx.copyWithin(row+x,p,p+len);v.rawLvl.copyWithin(row+x,p,p+len);
        v.rawRGBA.copyWithin(row+x,p+colourOffset,p+colourOffset+len);x+=len;
      }
    }
    const foreground=n+v.skyH*w,colourBase=n+v.skyH*w*2+(grayscale?WW*WH:0);
    for(let k=0;k<v.runs.length;k+=4){
      const y=v.runs[k]-viewY;if(y<0||y>=LH)continue;
      const x0=Math.max(v.runs[k+1],viewX),x1=Math.min(v.runs[k+2],viewX+LW);if(x1<=x0)continue;
      const i=y*LW+x0-viewX,p=v.runs[k]*WW+x0,len=x1-x0;
      v.rawDep.copyWithin(i,n+p,n+p+len);
      if(v.runs[k+3])v.opening.fill(1,i,i+len);
      else{
        v.rawIdx.copyWithin(i,foreground+p,foreground+p+len);v.rawLvl.copyWithin(i,foreground+p,foreground+p+len);
        v.rawRGBA.copyWithin(i,colourBase+p,colourBase+p+len);
      }
    }
    v.x=viewX;v.y=viewY;v.gray=grayscale;
  }
  fIdx.set(v.idx);fLvl.set(v.lvl);fDep.set(v.dep);img32.set(v.rgba);
  // The shared motion sampler uses the same opening mask as the ordinary compositor.
  BD.frame.opening=v.opening;
  depotFastMotion(cache,cx,cy);
  targetFrame(viewX,viewY);drawDynamic();postPasses();
  const T=grayscale?GRAY32:RES32,half=NLVL*PAL_N;
  let y0=clamp(-viewY,0,LH),y1=clamp(WH-viewY,0,LH);
  // Include all motion bands, even when the user pans far away from the room.
  for(let k=0;k<cache.layers.length;k++){
    const l=cache.layers[k];if(!l.motions)continue;
    for(let j=0;j<l.motions.length;j++){
      const m=l.motions[j],d=m.data,s=m.state;if(!s[2])continue;
      const a=m.base+s[1]-cy*l.parallax+LH/2;
      y0=Math.min(y0,clamp(Math.floor(a-(d.kind==='train'?d.h:0))-1,0,LH));
      y1=Math.max(y1,clamp(Math.ceil(a+(d.kind==='train'?0:d.h))+1,0,LH));
    }
  }
  paperVignette=true;
  for(let i=y0*LW,end=y1*LW;i<end;i++){
    if(VIG[i]&&fLvl[i]>0&&fDep[i]!==DEPTH_EMPTY)fLvl[i]--;
    const c=fIdx[i];img32[i]=T[(c>>7)*half+fLvl[i]*PAL_N+(c&127)];
  }
  offCtx.putImageData(img,0,0);paperResolveFrame();return true;
}
// Export-only adapters. The scene's own draw functions supply every pixel; origins are
// the data's world points. Large fittings get a larger scratch cell, not scaled artwork.
function depotAtlasDriverKey(d){return [d.face>=2?2:0,d.walk,d.bob,d.reach,d.hold,d.scratch,+d.pencil,+d.pour].join(',');}
function depotAtlasSprites(){
  const room=DEPOT.build(DEPOT_DATA),state=DEPOTWORLD.create(room),S=[];
  const fr=(tag,draw)=>({tag,draw});
  const at=(origin,draw)=>()=>{
    const keep={room:DEPR,sprite:depotSpriteAt,cat:{...cat},wood:dpWoodSeed,coat:dpBagCoat,wheel:dpWheelR,dial:dpDialR,angle:dpDialAngle,
      breath:dpBagBreath,ear:dpBagEar,turn:dpBagTurn,roller:dpRoller,stack:dpStackCount,chute:dpChuteShake,sack:dpSackZ};
    DEPR=room;depotSpriteAt=atlasBlit;dpWoodSeed=0;
    tOX=aOriginX-(origin[0]-origin[1]);tOY=aOriginY-Math.round((origin[0]+origin[1])*.5-origin[2]);
    try{draw();}finally{
      DEPR=keep.room;depotSpriteAt=keep.sprite;Object.assign(cat,keep.cat);dpWoodSeed=keep.wood;dpBagCoat=keep.coat;dpWheelR=keep.wheel;dpDialR=keep.dial;dpDialAngle=keep.angle;
      dpBagBreath=keep.breath;dpBagEar=keep.ear;dpBagTurn=keep.turn;dpRoller=keep.roller;dpStackCount=keep.stack;dpChuteShake=keep.chute;dpSackZ=keep.sack;
    }
  };
  const spr=(id,origin,frames,fps=0,loop=false,direction='none',mirroredFor='none',cellSize=128,light='room')=>{
    const material=id==='clock'||id==='scale'||id==='lamp';
    S.push({name:id.startsWith('gate_')?id:'depot_'+id,origin:origin.slice(),frames:frames.map(f=>({...f,draw:at(origin,material?paperPropWrap(f.draw):f.draw)})),fps,loop,direction,mirroredFor,cellSize,light});
  };
  for(const pair of ['RD','DL','LU','UR']){
    const a=GR_DIR[pair[0]],b=GR_DIR[pair[1]],frames=[];
    for(const current of [0,1]){
      frames.push(fr('exit_'+pair[current],()=>drawGate(0,0,a,b,{state:current,from:1-current,frame:2})));
      for(const k of [0,1])frames.push(fr('to_'+pair[1-current]+'_swing'+(k+1),()=>drawGate(0,0,a,b,{state:1-current,from:current,frame:k})));
    }
    spr('gate_'+pair,[8,8,0],frames,10,false,'none','none',128,'board');
  }
  for(const p of depotPropCatalog(room)){
    if(['bag-ginger','bag-blue','lamp','pigeon'].includes(p.id))continue;
    let frames=[fr('rest',()=>drawDepotProp(p.id,'rest'))],fps=0,loop=false;
    if(p.id.startsWith('conveyor-')){fps=10;loop=true;frames=[0,1,2,3].map(k=>fr('roller'+k,()=>{dpRoller=k;drawDepotProp(p.id,'rest');}));}
    if(p.id==='scale'){fps=10;frames=Array.from({length:16},(_,k)=>fr('needle'+k,()=>{depotScale();dpDialAngle=2.25-k*.26;shBias=3;faceY(12,24,24,36,118,shDpDial,C.OUT,0);}));}
    if(p.id==='clock'){fps=10;loop=true;frames=Array.from({length:60},(_,k)=>fr('hands'+k,()=>{dpDialR=8;dpDialAngle=Math.PI*.5-k/60*TAU;faceY(176,192,68,84,2,shDpDial,C.OUT,0);const h=2.68+Math.sin(k/60*TAU)*.28;dpRod(184,2.2,76,184+4*Math.cos(h),2.2,76+4*Math.sin(h),C.DEEP);dot3(184,2.3,76,C.BR1,0);}));}
    if(p.id==='flat-packs')frames=[3,4].map(k=>fr('count'+k,()=>{dpStackCount=k;depotFlatPacks();}));
    if(p.id==='chute'){fps=10;frames=[0,1].map(k=>fr(k?'rattle':'rest',()=>{dpChuteShake=k;depotChute();}));}
    spr(p.id,p.anchor,frames,fps,loop,'none','none',256);
  }
  for(const coat of ['ginger','blue']){
    const h={...room.hooks[0],cat:coat},frames=[];
    for(const kind of ['empty','curled','head','tail'])for(let k=0;k<(kind==='empty'?1:5);k++){
      frames.push(fr(kind+['_rest','_breath','_ear','_turn_right','_turn_left'][k],()=>{
        dpBagBreath=k===1?1:0;dpBagEar=k===2?1:0;dpBagTurn=k>=3;
        const sway=k===3?1:k===4?-1:0;depotBag({...h,x:h.x+sway*.5,y:h.y-sway*.5},kind);
      }));
    }
    spr('bag-'+coat,[h.x,h.y,h.z],frames,10,true);
  }
  const lamp=room.lamps[0];spr('lamp',[lamp.x,lamp.y,lamp.z],['off','on'].flatMap(mode=>[-1,0,1].map(sway=>fr(mode+'_sway'+sway,()=>depotLamp({...lamp,x:lamp.x+sway*.5,y:lamp.y-sway*.5},mode==='on')))),10,true);
  // The hand-held mug has its own origin, independent of the wall's hanging mugs.
  spr('mug',[0,0,0],[false,true].map(pour=>fr(pour?'pour':'held',()=>{
    sprClear();rect(AX-1,AY-3,3,4,C.CREAM);run(AX-1,AY-3,3,C.BL1);sp(AX+2,AY-2,C.CREAM);sp(AX+2,AY-1,C.CREAM);outlineSprite();atlasBlit(0,0,0,false);
    if(pour){shBias=2;dpRod(0,0,3,0,0,5,C.CREAM);}
  })),10);
  const birds=[];
  for(const mode of ['perch','peck','fly'])for(let f=0;f<2;f++)for(const bob of mode==='fly'?[0,1]:[f])birds.push(fr(mode+f+'_bob'+bob,()=>{buildDepotPigeon({state:mode,frame:f,bob});atlasBlit(0,0,0,false);}));
  spr('pigeon',[0,0,0],birds,10,true,'W','E');
  // Deduplicate visible art while retaining every parameter key that selects that art.
  // The native renderer can use the key directly; sample names also link to the loop data.
  for(const [face,name,dir,mirror]of [[0,'towards','S','E'],[2,'away','W','N']]){
    const frames=[],seen=new Map();
    for(let f=0;f<2880;f+=6){
      DEPOTWORLD.step(state,f);const d={...state.driver,face},key=depotAtlasDriverKey(d);buildDriver(d);
      let pixels='';for(let i=0;i<SPR.length;i++)pixels+=String.fromCharCode(SPR[i],SPR[i]===253?SPO[i]:0);
      let frame=seen.get(pixels);
      if(!frame){frame={tag:d.action+'_'+f,sample:f,poses:[],draw:()=>{buildDriver(d);atlasBlit(0,0,0,false);}};seen.set(pixels,frame);frames.push(frame);}
      if(!frame.poses.includes(key))frame.poses.push(key);
    }
    spr('driver-'+name,[0,0,0],frames,10,false,dir,mirror);
  }
  const pm=[];
  for(const mode of [CS_SLEEP,CS_STRETCH,CS_WALK,CS_SIT])for(let f=0;f<(mode===CS_WALK?4:mode===CS_SIT?6:mode===CS_SLEEP?2:1);f++){
    const p={state:mode,frame:f,head:mode===CS_SIT?Math.floor(f/2):0,flick:f&1};
    pm.push({...fr(['curl','stretch','walk','watch'][mode]+f,()=>{Object.assign(cat,p);buildCat();atlasBlit(0,0,0,false);}),poses:[[p.state,p.frame,p.head,p.flick].join(',')]});
  }
  spr('postmistress',[0,0,0],pm,10,true,'W','E');
  for(const kind of ['cat','box','empty-bag','full-bag']){
    const count=kind==='empty-bag'?7:kind==='cat'?3:kind==='box'?2:1;
    spr('cargo-'+kind,[0,0,0],Array.from({length:count},(_,k)=>fr(kind==='box'?(k?'labelled':'plain'):'pose'+k,()=>{
      buildDepotCargo({kind,fold:kind==='cat'?k/2:k/6,tag:!!k});atlasBlit(0,0,0,false);
    })),10,false);
  }
  spr('sorting-frame',[0,0,0],[fr('rest',()=>{buildDepotSortingFrame();atlasBlit(0,0,0,false);})]);
  return S;
}
// A chute and its parcel have separate material palettes, as they do in the room.
// Composite frames retain the exact box path and depth instead of recolouring either layer.
function depotAtlasAssemblies(){
  const room=DEPOT.build(DEPOT_DATA),state=DEPOTWORLD.create(room),origin=room.furniture.find(p=>p.id==='chute').anchor;
  const frames=[{tag:'empty',layers:[{sprite:'depot_chute',frame:'rest',at:[0,0,0]}]}];
  for(let k=0;k<12;k++){
    const sample=1020+k*7;DEPOTWORLD.step(state,sample);const c=state.parcel.kind==='box'?state.parcel:state.cargo;
    frames.push({tag:'box'+k,sample,layers:[{sprite:'depot_chute',frame:state.machine.chute?'rattle':'rest',at:[0,0,0]},
      {sprite:'depot_cargo-box',frame:'labelled',at:[c.x-origin[0],c.y-origin[1],c.z-origin[2]]}]});
  }
  return [{name:'depot_chute_with_box',origin,fps:10,depth:'world',frames}];
}
// Generated palette textures are presentation-only. The original logical renderer still
// supplies depths, lighting and game/review buffers; a 4x layer removes its matched sprites.
const PAPER_SCALE = 4, PAPER_CAPACITY = 256, PAPER_MISSING_CAPACITY = 1024;
let paperEnabled = false, paperCount = 0, paperReady = false, paperVignette = false;
let paperLookup = new Map(), paperSlots = null, paperAtlas = null, paperSlotSize = SW*SHT;
let paperSpriteRecords=[],paperAtlasBuilding=false,paperAtlasIdx=null,paperAtlasCls=null,paperAtlasSel=null;
let paperPieceRecords=[],paperPieceLookup=[[],[],[],[],[],[],[],[],[],[],[],[],[]],paperCacheBuild=false,paperCachePiece=null,paperVersion=0;
const paperMissingHashes=new Uint32Array(2048),paperMissingHits=new Uint32Array(2048);
const paperPieceMisses=[new Uint32Array(60),new Uint32Array(12),new Uint32Array(4),new Uint32Array(96),new Uint32Array(8),new Uint32Array(8),new Uint32Array(56),new Uint32Array(10),new Uint32Array(96),new Uint32Array(32),new Uint32Array(4),new Uint32Array(8),new Uint32Array(4)];
let paperMissingSlots=0,paperMissingOverflow=0;
let paperNapLookup=new Map(),paperNapFamilies=new Map(),paperNapIssues=[],paperDerivedHashes=new Set();
let paperBoxFadeIssues=[];
let paperTakeoffSources=[],paperFlapSources=[],paperBigSources=[];
const paperCanvas = document.createElement('canvas'), paperCtx = paperCanvas.getContext('2d');
let paperImage = null, paperRGBA = null, paperDepth = null, paperIndex = null;
let paperViewScale=PAPER_SCALE;
function paperRenderScale(){return paperAtlasBuilding?PAPER_SCALE:paperViewScale;}
function paperSetViewScale(value){
  const density=clamp(Math.floor(value)||PAPER_SCALE,1,PAPER_SCALE);
  // Keep fractional4:3 downsampling in Canvas; its horizontal tie phases depend on position.
  paperViewScale=density===3?PAPER_SCALE:density;paperReady=false;
}
// Canvas nearest-neighbour downsampling chooses the earlier texel on exact centre ties.
function paperSampleOffset(subpixel,S){return Math.ceil((subpixel+.5)*PAPER_SCALE/S)-1;}

function paperSpriteHash() {
  let h = 0x811c9dc5;
  for (let i = 0; i < SPR.length; i++) {
    h = Math.imul(h ^ SPR[i], 0x01000193);
    if (SPR[i] === 253) h = Math.imul(h ^ SPO[i], 0x01000193);
  }
  return h >>> 0;
}
function paperDepotSpriteTexture(){return paperEnabled?paperLookup.get(paperSpriteHash()):null;}
function paperDecodePixels(item,w,h) {
  let pixels;
  if(typeof item.pixels==='string'){
    const raw=atob(item.pixels);pixels=new Uint8Array(raw.length);
    for(let i=0;i<raw.length;i++)pixels[i]=raw.charCodeAt(i);
  }else pixels=Uint8Array.from(item.pixels||[]);
  if(pixels.length!==w*h*PAPER_SCALE*PAPER_SCALE)throw new Error('Invalid paper texture length: '+item.id);
  for(const c of pixels)if(c!==EMPTY){
    if(c>=NPAL)throw new Error('Paper colour outside palette: '+item.id);
  }
  return pixels;
}
function paperPieceSelector(family,frame) {
  const cue=family.match(/^hatch_(start|shadow)_([RDLU])$/);if(cue&&frame==='idle')return [11,GR_DIR[cue[2]]+(cue[1]==='shadow'?4:0)];
  const bed=family.match(/^big_basket_(ginger|blue)$/);if(bed&&['empty','compressed'].includes(frame))return [12,(bed[1]==='blue'?2:0)+(frame==='compressed'?1:0)];
  let m=family.match(/^turntable_(plain|ginger|blue)_([ESWN])$/);
  if(m){
    const kind=['plain','ginger','blue'].indexOf(m[1]),dir='ESWN'.indexOf(m[2]);
    const pose=['idle','spin1','spin2','spin3','locked'].indexOf(frame);
    if(pose>=0)return [0,(kind*4+dir)*5+pose];
  }
  m=family.match(/^hatch_([ESWN])$/);
  if(m){const pose=['closed','ajar','open'].indexOf(frame);if(pose>=0)return [1,'ESWN'.indexOf(m[1])*3+pose];}
  m=family.match(/^basket_(ginger|blue)$/);
  if(m){const pose=['empty','occupied'].indexOf(frame);if(pose>=0)return [2,(m[1]==='blue'?2:0)+pose];}
  m=family.match(/^gate_(RD|DL|LU|UR)$/);
  if(m){
    const a='RDLU'.indexOf(m[1][0]),b='RDLU'.indexOf(m[1][1]),exit=frame.match(/^exit_([RDLU])$/),swing=frame.match(/^to_([RDLU])_swing([12])$/);
    const chosen=exit?exit[1]:swing?swing[1]:'';
    if(chosen&&m[1].includes(chosen)){const state=m[1].indexOf(chosen),pose=exit?2:Number(swing[2])-1;return [3,((a*4+b)*2+state)*3+pose];}
  }
  m=family.match(/^edge_flap_(ginger|blue)_([RDLU])$/);
  if(m&&frame==='closed')return [4,(m[1]==='blue'?4:0)+'RDLU'.indexOf(m[2])];
  m=family.match(/^flap_box_(red|blue)$/);
  if(m){const pose=['open','fold1','fold2','fold3','flat'].indexOf(frame);if(pose>=0)return [7,(m[1]==='blue'?5:0)+pose];}
  m=family.match(/^edge_flap_motion_(ginger|blue)_([RDLU])$/);
  if(m){const pose=EDGE_PORTAL_DATA.doorTags.indexOf(frame);if(pose>0)return [6,((m[1]==='blue'?4:0)+'RDLU'.indexOf(m[2]))*7+pose-1];}
  m=family.match(/^edge_approach_(ginger|blue)_([RDLU])$/);
  if(m&&frame==='idle')return [5,(m[1]==='blue'?4:0)+'RDLU'.indexOf(m[2])];
  m=family.match(/^points_([RDLU])([RDLU])$/);
  if(m&&m[1]!==m[2]){
    const pair=m[1]+m[2],exit=frame.match(/^exit_([RDLU])$/),swing=frame.match(/^to_([RDLU])_swing([12])$/),selected=exit?exit[1]:swing?swing[1]:'';
    if(selected&&pair.includes(selected))return [8,(GR_DIR[m[1]]*4+GR_DIR[m[2]])*6+pair.indexOf(selected)*3+(exit?2:Number(swing[2])-1)];
  }
  m=family.match(/^tunnel_(circle|square|triangle|diamond)$/);
  if(m){const p=['idle','entry','transit','exit'].indexOf(frame.replace('_focus',''));if(p>=0)return [9,(['circle','square','triangle','diamond'].indexOf(m[1])<<3)+(p<<1)+(frame.endsWith('_focus')?1:0)];}
  if(family==='tunnel_air'&&/^puff[0-3]$/.test(frame))return [10,Number(frame.slice(4))];
  throw new Error('Unknown paper piece state: '+family+'/'+frame);
}
function paperGateSourceMasks(index,r) {
  const size=96,anchor=48,n=size*size,idx=new Uint8Array(n).fill(EMPTY),lvl=new Uint8Array(n),dep=new Int16Array(n).fill(DEPTH_EMPTY),owner=new Uint8Array(n);
  const saved={tIdx,tLvl,tDep,tW,tH,tOX,tOY,tBake,tSun,tPX,tPY,shLvl,shFlat,shFlag,shBias,shBoard,shSunMax,sunAdd,aoFn,lampFn,
    gateHX,gateHY,gateVX,gateVY,paperEnabled,gateArrow,emit,faceTop,box};
  let part=0;
  try{
    paperEnabled=false;targetCustom(idx,lvl,dep,size,size,anchor,anchor-8);
    aoFn=null;lampFn=null;shLvl=0;shFlat=false;shFlag=0;shBias=0;shBoard=0;shSunMax=0;
    emit=function(px,py,x,y,z,face,col){
      const i=py*size+px,d=Math.round((x+y+z)*4)+shBias;
      if(d>=dep[i])owner[i]=part;
      saved.emit(px,py,x,y,z,face,col);
    };
    gateArrow=function(cx,cy,dir,open){const previous=part;part=open?1:2;try{saved.gateArrow(cx,cy,dir,open);}finally{part=previous;}};
    faceTop=function(x0,y0,x1,y1,z,sh,dk,olm){const previous=part;if(sh===shGateBlade)part=3;try{saved.faceTop(x0,y0,x1,y1,z,sh,dk,olm);}finally{part=previous;}};
    box=function(x0,y0,z0,x1,y1,z1,sh,dk,olm){const previous=part;if(sh===shGatePost)part=4;try{saved.box(x0,y0,z0,x1,y1,z1,sh,dk,olm);}finally{part=previous;}};
    const q=Math.floor(index/3),state=q&1,pair=q>>1;
    drawGate(0,0,pair>>2,pair&3,{state,from:1-state,frame:index%3});
  }finally{
    ({tIdx,tLvl,tDep,tW,tH,tOX,tOY,tBake,tSun,tPX,tPY,shLvl,shFlat,shFlag,shBias,shBoard,shSunMax,sunAdd,aoFn,lampFn,
      gateHX,gateHY,gateVX,gateVY,paperEnabled,gateArrow,emit,faceTop,box}=saved);
  }
  const masks={dx:r.dx,dy:r.dy,w:r.w,h:r.h,open:new Uint8Array(r.w*r.h),closed:new Uint8Array(r.w*r.h),blade:new Uint8Array(r.w*r.h),post:new Uint8Array(r.w*r.h),
    depth:new Int16Array(r.w*r.h).fill(DEPTH_EMPTY),indices:new Uint8Array(r.w*r.h).fill(EMPTY)};
  for(let y=0;y<size;y++)for(let x=0;x<size;x++){
    const i=y*size+x,part=owner[i];if(!part)continue;
    const tx=x-anchor-r.dx,ty=y-anchor-r.dy;
    if(tx<0||ty<0||tx>=r.w||ty>=r.h){if(part<3)throw new Error('Paper gate rectangle clips a source arrow');continue;}
    const k=ty*r.w+tx;
    if(part===1)masks.open[k]=1;else if(part===2)masks.closed[k]=1;else if(part===3)masks.blade[k]=1;else masks.post[k]=1;
    masks.depth[k]=dep[i]-64;masks.indices[k]=idx[i]&127;
  }
  return masks;
}
function paperApplyGateDecals(texture,index) {
  const masks=paperGateSourceMasks(index,texture),S=PAPER_SCALE,W=texture.w*S;
  for(let y=0;y<texture.h;y++)for(let x=0;x<texture.w;x++){
    const k=y*texture.w+x,c=masks.open[k]?C.CREAM:masks.closed[k]?C.WD2:EMPTY;if(c===EMPTY)continue;
    for(let sy=0;sy<S;sy++)for(let sx=0;sx<S;sx++)texture.pixels[(y*S+sy)*W+x*S+sx]=c;
  }
  texture.arrowFree=true;texture.gateMasks=masks;
}
function paperGateMasks(family,frame) {
  const selector=paperPieceSelector(family,frame),texture=paperPieceLookup[selector[0]][selector[1]];
  return texture&&texture.gateMasks||null;
}
function paperInstallData(data) {
  if(!data||data.scale!==PAPER_SCALE||!Array.isArray(data.sprites))throw new Error('Paper textures need scale 4 and a sprite list');
  if(NPAL>128)throw new Error('Paper palette exceeds the seven-bit colour index');
  const next=new Map(),records=[],pieces=[],selectors=[new Array(60).fill(null),new Array(12).fill(null),new Array(4).fill(null),new Array(96).fill(null),new Array(8).fill(null),new Array(8).fill(null),new Array(56).fill(null),new Array(10).fill(null),new Array(96).fill(null),new Array(32).fill(null),new Array(4).fill(null),new Array(8).fill(null),new Array(4).fill(null)],takeoffs=new Array(18).fill(null),flapSources=new Array(32).fill(null),bigSources=new Array(182).fill(null);
  let slotSize=SW*SHT;
  for(const item of data.sprites){
    const r=item.rect;
    if(!r||![r.x,r.y,r.w,r.h].every(Number.isInteger)||r.w<1||r.h<1||r.x<0||r.y<0||r.x+r.w>SW||r.y+r.h>SHT)throw new Error('Invalid paper sprite rectangle: '+item.id);
    if(!Number.isInteger(item.sourceHash)||item.sourceHash<0||item.sourceHash>0xffffffff)throw new Error('Invalid paper source hash: '+item.id);
    let texture=next.get(item.sourceHash);
    if(!texture){texture={id:item.id,sourceHash:item.sourceHash,x:r.x,y:r.y,dx:r.x-AX,dy:r.y-AY,w:r.w,h:r.h,pixels:paperDecodePixels(item,r.w,r.h)};next.set(item.sourceHash,texture);}
    if(item.depth){
      if((item.flapIndex===undefined&&item.bigIndex===undefined)||!Array.isArray(item.depth)||!Array.isArray(item.faces)||item.depth.length!==r.w*r.h||item.faces.length!==r.w*r.h||item.depth.some(d=>!Number.isInteger(d)||d<-32768||d>32767)||item.faces.some(f=>!Number.isInteger(f)||f<0||f>2))throw new Error('Invalid paper cat depth: '+item.id);
      texture.depth=Int16Array.from(item.depth);texture.faces=Uint8Array.from(item.faces);
    }
    if(item.entryNative){
      if((item.flapIndex===undefined&&item.bigIndex===undefined)||!Array.isArray(item.entryNative)||item.entryNative.length!==2||item.entryNative.some((n,i)=>!Number.isInteger(n)||n<0||n>=(i?SHT:SW)*PAPER_SCALE)||!Array.isArray(item.entryMouth)||item.entryMouth.length!==2||item.entryMouth.some(n=>!Number.isFinite(n))||!Array.isArray(item.entryAperture)||item.entryAperture.length!==4||item.entryAperture.some(n=>!Number.isFinite(n)))throw new Error('Invalid paper entry anchor: '+item.id);
      const a=item.entryAperture,m=item.entryMouth;
      if(a[0]>=a[1]||a[2]>=a[3]||m[0]<a[0]||m[0]>a[1]||m[1]<a[2]||m[1]>a[3]||typeof item.entryStage!=='string')throw new Error('Entry anchor is outside its aperture: '+item.id);
      texture.entryNative=Int16Array.from(item.entryNative);texture.entryMouth=Float64Array.from(item.entryMouth);texture.entryAperture=Float64Array.from(item.entryAperture);texture.entryStage=item.entryStage;
    }
    records.push({...texture,id:item.id,direction:item.direction,mirroredFor:item.mirroredFor,fps:item.fps,loop:item.loop});
    if(item.bigIndex!==undefined){
      const k=item.bigIndex;
      if(!Number.isInteger(k)||k<0||k>=182||bigSources[k]||typeof item.source!=='string')throw new Error('Invalid big-cat source: '+item.id);
      const raw=atob(item.source),indices=Uint8Array.from(raw,ch=>ch.charCodeAt(0));
      if(indices.length!==SW*SHT||indices.some(c=>c!==EMPTY&&c>=NPAL))throw new Error('Invalid big-cat buffer: '+item.id);
      let hash=0x811c9dc5;for(const c of indices)hash=Math.imul(hash^c,0x01000193);
      if((hash>>>0)!==item.sourceHash)throw new Error('Invalid big-cat source hash: '+item.id);
      bigSources[k]=indices;
    }
    if(item.flapIndex!==undefined){
      const k=item.flapIndex;
      if(!Number.isInteger(k)||k<0||k>=32||flapSources[k]||typeof item.source!=='string')throw new Error('Invalid flap cat source: '+item.id);
      const raw=atob(item.source),indices=Uint8Array.from(raw,ch=>ch.charCodeAt(0));
      if(indices.length!==SW*SHT||indices.some(c=>c!==EMPTY&&c>=NPAL))throw new Error('Invalid flap cat buffer: '+item.id);
      flapSources[k]=indices;
    }
    if(item.takeoffIndex!==undefined){
      const k=item.takeoffIndex;
      if(!Number.isInteger(k)||k<0||k>=18||takeoffs[k]||typeof item.source!=='string')throw new Error('Invalid take-off source: '+item.id);
      const raw=atob(item.source),indices=Uint8Array.from(raw,ch=>ch.charCodeAt(0));
      if(indices.length!==SW*SHT||indices.some(c=>c!==EMPTY&&c>=NPAL))throw new Error('Invalid take-off buffer: '+item.id);
      takeoffs[k]=indices;
    }
  }
  for(const item of data.pieces||[]){
    const r=item.rect;
    if(!r||![r.dx,r.dy,r.w,r.h].every(Number.isInteger)||r.w<1||r.h<1||r.w*r.h>4096||Math.abs(r.dx)>128||Math.abs(r.dy)>128)throw new Error('Invalid paper piece rectangle: '+item.id);
    const selector=paperPieceSelector(item.family,item.frame),texture={id:item.id||item.family+'/'+item.frame,family:item.family,frame:item.frame,isPiece:true,
      x:r.dx,y:r.dy,dx:r.dx,dy:r.dy,w:r.w,h:r.h,pixels:paperDecodePixels(item,r.w,r.h)};
    if(item.arrowFree){
      if(selector[0]!==3)throw new Error('Source gate decals require a gate texture');
      paperApplyGateDecals(texture,selector[1]);
    }
    if(selector[0]>=4&&selector[0]<=7||selector[0]===12){
      if(!Array.isArray(item.depth)||!Array.isArray(item.faces)||item.depth.length!==r.w*r.h||item.faces.length!==r.w*r.h||
        item.depth.some(d=>!Number.isInteger(d)||d<-32768||d>32767)||item.faces.some(f=>!Number.isInteger(f)||f<0||f>2))throw new Error('Invalid entrance depth: '+item.id);
      texture.depth=Int16Array.from(item.depth);texture.faces=Uint8Array.from(item.faces);
      texture.logical=new Uint8Array(r.w*r.h).fill(EMPTY);
      for(let y=0;y<r.h;y++)for(let x=0;x<r.w;x++){
        let colour=EMPTY;
        for(let sy=0;sy<PAPER_SCALE;sy++)for(let sx=0;sx<PAPER_SCALE;sx++){
          const c=texture.pixels[(y*PAPER_SCALE+sy)*r.w*PAPER_SCALE+x*PAPER_SCALE+sx];
          if(c!==EMPTY)colour=c;
        }
        texture.logical[y*r.w+x]=colour;
      }
    }
    pieces.push(texture);selectors[selector[0]][selector[1]]=texture;slotSize=Math.max(slotSize,r.w*r.h);
  }
  if(selectors[12].some(Boolean)&&selectors[12].some(r=>!r))throw new Error('Large baskets require both coats and support poses');
  const napLookup=new Map();
  for(const item of data.napSources||[]){
    if(!COAT_IDS.includes(item.coat)||!NAP_POSE_IDS.includes(item.pose)||![0,1,2,4].includes(item.framebits))throw new Error('Invalid canonical nap source');
    let indices;
    if(typeof item.indices==='string'){const raw=atob(item.indices);indices=Uint8Array.from(raw,ch=>ch.charCodeAt(0));}
    else indices=Uint8Array.from(item.indices||[]);
    if(indices.length!==SW*SHT)throw new Error('Canonical nap source needs the full sprite buffer');
    const key=item.coat+'/'+item.pose;let frames=napLookup.get(key);if(!frames){frames=[];napLookup.set(key,frames);}
    frames[item.framebits]={sourceHash:item.sourceHash,indices};
  }
  if(takeoffs.some(Boolean)&&takeoffs.some(r=>!r))throw new Error('Take-off artwork requires both coats, both views and both rim layers');
  if(flapSources.some(Boolean)&&flapSources.some(r=>!r))throw new Error('Flap artwork requires both coats and both views');
  if(bigSources.some(Boolean)&&bigSources.some(r=>!r))throw new Error('Big-cat artwork requires both coats, both views and all poses');
  paperBigSources=bigSources;paperFlapSources=flapSources;paperLookup=next;paperSpriteRecords=records;paperPieceRecords=pieces;paperPieceLookup=selectors;paperTakeoffSources=takeoffs;paperAtlas=null;paperVersion++;
  const artworkOn=next.size>0||pieces.length>0;
  hatchCueInstall(pieces,selectors);
  paperNapLookup=napLookup;paperNapFamilies=new Map();paperNapIssues=[];paperDerivedHashes=new Set();
  paperBoxFadeIssues=[];paperPrepareBoxFades();
  paperMissingHits.fill(0);paperMissingSlots=0;paperMissingOverflow=0;for(const counts of paperPieceMisses)counts.fill(0);
  if(slotSize>paperSlotSize)paperSlots=null;
  paperSlotSize=slotSize;paperEnabled=artworkOn;paperBeginFrame();
  if(paperEnabled&&img)paperAllocView();
}
function paperAllocView() {
  if (!paperEnabled) return;
  const S=paperRenderScale(),w=LW*S,h=LH*S;
  if (!paperImage || paperImage.width !== w || paperImage.height !== h) {
    paperCanvas.width=w;paperCanvas.height=h;paperImage=paperCtx.createImageData(w,h);
    paperRGBA=new Uint32Array(paperImage.data.buffer);paperDepth=new Int16Array(w*h);paperIndex=new Uint8Array(w*h);
  }
  if (!paperSlots) paperSlots=paperCreateSlots(paperSlotSize,false);
}
function paperCreateSlots(size,atlas) {
  const slots=new Array(PAPER_CAPACITY);
  for(let i=0;i<PAPER_CAPACITY;i++){
    slots[i]={
      underIdx:new Uint8Array(size),underLvl:new Uint8Array(size),underDep:new Int16Array(size),
      mask:new Uint8Array(size),stripped:new Uint8Array(size),source:new Uint8Array(size),sourceDep:new Int16Array(size),light:new Uint8Array(size),
      ...(atlas?{underCls:new Uint8Array(size),underSel:new Uint8Array(size)}:{})
    };
  }
  return slots;
}
function paperBeginFrame() { paperCount=0;paperReady=false;paperVignette=false; }
function paperCaptureSprite(fx,fy,fz,mirror,sunOn) {
  if (!paperEnabled || !paperSlots || tIdx !== fIdx || paperCount === PAPER_CAPACITY) return null;
  const hash=paperSpriteHash(),texture=paperLookup.get(hash);
  if(!texture){if(!paperAtlasBuilding)paperRecordMissing(hash);return null;}
  const s=paperSlots[paperCount++];
  s.kind=0;s.w=SW;s.h=SHT;s.offsetX=-AX;s.offsetY=-AY;
  s.texture=texture;s.ax=Math.round(fx-fy)+tOX;s.ay=Math.round((fx+fy)*.5-fz)+tOY;
  s.d0=(fx+fy+fz)*4;s.fx=fx;s.fy=fy;s.fz=fz;s.mirror=mirror;s.sunOn=sunOn;
  s.sun=sunFn;s.lamp=lampFn;s.board=shBoard;s.clip=blitClipZ;s.clipSide=blitClipSide;s.clipAt=blitClipAt;s.surface=blitSurface;s.px=tPX;s.py=tPY;
  s.mask.fill(0);
  for(let y=0;y<SHT;y++)for(let x=0;x<SW;x++){
    const k=y*SW+x,px=s.ax+(mirror?AX-x:x-AX),py=s.ay+y-AY;
    if(px<0||py<0||px>=LW||py>=LH)continue;
    const dx=mirror?AX-x:x-AX,h=AY-y,depth=s.surface?s.surface.depth[(y-texture.y)*texture.w+x-texture.x]:h*4;
    const sum=s.surface?(depth/4-h)/1.5:0,wx=fx+(sum+dx)*.5,wy=fy+(sum-dx)*.5,wz=fz+h+sum*.5;
    const i=py*LW+px,dep=Math.round(s.d0+depth)+2;
    s.underIdx[k]=fIdx[i];s.underLvl[k]=fLvl[i];s.underDep[k]=fDep[i];
    if(paperAtlasBuilding){s.underCls[k]=aCls[i];s.underSel[k]=aSel[i];}
    if(SPR[k]!==EMPTY&&dep>=fDep[i]&&wz>=blitClipZ&&(blitClipSide<0||(blitClipSide&1?wy:wx)*(blitClipSide<2?1:-1)<=blitClipAt))s.mask[k]=1;
  }
  return s;
}
function paperFinishSprite(s) {
  if(!s)return;
  const r=s.texture;
  for(let y=0;y<SHT;y++)for(let x=0;x<SW;x++){
    const k=y*SW+x,dx=s.mirror?AX-x:x-AX,px=s.ax+dx,py=s.ay+y-AY;
    if(px<0||py<0||px>=LW||py>=LH)continue;
    const i=py*LW+px;
    if(s.mask[k]){s.source[k]=fIdx[i];s.light[k]=fLvl[i];continue;}
    if(x<r.x||y<r.y||x>=r.x+r.w||y>=r.y+r.h)continue;
    const depth=s.surface?s.surface.depth[(y-r.y)*r.w+x-r.x]:0;
    const sum=s.surface?(depth/4+y-AY)/1.5:0;
    const wx=s.fx+(sum+dx)*.5,wy=s.fy+(sum-dx)*.5,wz=s.fz+AY-y+sum*.5;
    let lit=0;
    if(s.sunOn&&s.sun(wx,wy,wz,F_TOP)>bayer(px+s.px,py+s.py)){
      lit=1;const nx=s.mirror?x+1:x-1;
      if(nx<0||nx>=SW||SPR[y*SW+nx]===EMPTY||SPR[y*SW+nx]===253)lit=2;
    }
    const lamp=s.lamp?s.lamp(wx,wy,wz,px+s.px,py+s.py,lit):0;
    s.light[k]=clamp(2+lit+lamp,0,4);
  }
}
function paperNapFamily(coat,pose,missing) {
  const key=coat+'/'+pose;
  if(paperNapFamilies.has(key))return paperNapFamilies.get(key);
  const sources=paperNapLookup.get(key),textures=[],allowed=new Set();
  let x=SW,y=SHT,x1=0,y1=0;
  for(const bit of [0,1,2,4]){
    const source=sources&&sources[bit],texture=source&&paperLookup.get(source.sourceHash);
    if(!texture){missing.push({coat,pose,framebits:bit,reason:'missing canonical texture/source'});return null;}
    buildNapSprite(coat,pose,bit,0);
    if(paperSpriteHash()!==source.sourceHash){missing.push({coat,pose,framebits:bit,reason:'canonical source hash does not match original builder'});return null;}
    for(let i=0;i<SPR.length;i++)if(SPR[i]!==source.indices[i]){missing.push({coat,pose,framebits:bit,reason:'canonical source indices do not match original builder'});return null;}
    textures[bit]=texture;x=Math.min(x,texture.x);y=Math.min(y,texture.y);x1=Math.max(x1,texture.x+texture.w);y1=Math.max(y1,texture.y+texture.h);
    for(const c of texture.pixels)if(c!==EMPTY)allowed.add(c);
  }
  if(!allowed.size){missing.push({coat,pose,reason:'canonical texture family has no opaque palette colours'});return null;}
  const S=PAPER_SCALE,w=x1-x,h=y1-y,frames=[];
  for(let frame=0;frame<8;frame++){
    const pixels=new Uint8Array(w*h*S*S).fill(EMPTY),base=textures[frame&1];
    for(let sy=0;sy<base.h*S;sy++)for(let sx=0;sx<base.w*S;sx++)pixels[((base.y-y)*S+sy)*w*S+(base.x-x)*S+sx]=base.pixels[sy*base.w*S+sx];
    for(const bit of [2,4])if(frame&bit){
      const feature=textures[bit],rest=sources[0].indices,changed=sources[bit].indices;
      for(let cy=y;cy<y1;cy++)for(let cx=x;cx<x1;cx++)if(rest[cy*SW+cx]!==changed[cy*SW+cx]){
        for(let sy=0;sy<S;sy++)for(let sx=0;sx<S;sx++){
          const fx=(cx-feature.x)*S+sx,fy=(cy-feature.y)*S+sy;
          pixels[((cy-y)*S+sy)*w*S+(cx-x)*S+sx]=fx<0||fy<0||fx>=feature.w*S||fy>=feature.h*S?EMPTY:feature.pixels[fy*feature.w*S+fx];
        }
      }
    }
    buildNapSprite(coat,pose,frame,0);
    frames[frame]={pixels,indices:Uint8Array.from(SPR),sourceHash:paperSpriteHash()};
  }
  const family={x,y,w,h,allowed:[...allowed],frames};paperNapFamilies.set(key,family);return family;
}
function paperPrepareNapVariants(specs) {
  const saved=Uint8Array.from(SPR),outline=Uint8Array.from(SPO),key=KEY,missing=[];
  let created=0;
  try{
    for(const spec of specs){
      const family=paperNapFamily(spec.coat,spec.pose,missing);if(!family)continue;
      const seed=spec.seed|0;
      for(let frame=0;frame<8;frame++){
        buildNapSprite(spec.coat,spec.pose,frame,seed);const hash=paperSpriteHash();
        if(paperLookup.has(hash))continue;
        const canonical=family.frames[frame],pixels=Uint8Array.from(canonical.pixels),S=PAPER_SCALE,W=family.w*S;
        // Only the procedural coat's changed source cells can recolour generated pixels.
        for(let cy=family.y;cy<family.y+family.h;cy++)for(let cx=family.x;cx<family.x+family.w;cx++){
          const i=cy*SW+cx,from=canonical.indices[i],to=SPR[i];
          if(from===to||from>=NPAL||to>=NPAL)continue;
          const desired=PALRGB[to],fromLum=lumOf(from);
          for(let sy=0;sy<S;sy++)for(let sx=0;sx<S;sx++){
            const p=((cy-family.y)*S+sy)*W+(cx-family.x)*S+sx,c=pixels[p];if(c===EMPTY)continue;
            const shift=lumOf(c)-fromLum;
            pixels[p]=nearestColour([clamp(desired[0]+shift,0,255),clamp(desired[1]+shift,0,255),clamp(desired[2]+shift,0,255)],family.allowed);
          }
        }
        const texture={id:'nap_'+spec.coat+'_'+spec.pose+'/bits'+frame+'_seed'+seed,sourceHash:hash,derived:true,
          x:family.x,y:family.y,dx:family.x-AX,dy:family.y-AY,w:family.w,h:family.h,pixels};
        paperLookup.set(hash,texture);paperDerivedHashes.add(hash);created++;
      }
    }
  }finally{SPR.set(saved);SPO.set(outline);KEY=key;}
  if(created){paperAtlas=null;paperVersion++;}
  paperNapIssues.push(...missing);return {created,missing};
}
function paperPrepareRoomNaps(room) {
  for(const hash of paperDerivedHashes)paperLookup.delete(hash);
  if(paperDerivedHashes.size){paperAtlas=null;paperVersion++;}
  paperDerivedHashes.clear();paperNapIssues=[];
  if(!paperEnabled||!room)return;
  const specs=[];
  for(let k=0;k<(room.naps||[]).length;k++){
    const n=room.naps[k];specs.push({coat:n.cat,pose:NAP_POSE_IDS[n.pose],seed:n.top*7+k});
  }
  for(const item of room.items||[])if(item.kind==='sleepingCat')specs.push({coat:item.cat||'greyTabby',pose:'flat',seed:item.x0*7+item.y0});
  paperPrepareNapVariants(specs);
}
function paperPrepareBoxFades() {
  const saved=SPR.slice(),outline=SPO.slice(),key=KEY,missing=[];let created=0;
  try{
    buildBox(4,false,false);
    const base=paperLookup.get(paperSpriteHash());
    if(!base){
      if(paperSpriteRecords.some(r=>r.id.startsWith('box_fold/')))missing.push({reason:'missing accepted full flat-box texture'});
    }else for(let step=1;step<=16;step++){
      buildBox(4,false,false);
      for(let y=0;y<SHT;y++)for(let x=0;x<SW;x++)if(bayer(x,y)<step/16)SPR[y*SW+x]=EMPTY;
      const hash=paperSpriteHash();if(paperLookup.has(hash))continue;
