      const S=PAPER_SCALE,W=base.w*S,pixels=base.pixels.slice();
      // Apply the original Bayer decision to every artwork cell, including generated
      // detail extending beyond the original silhouette within its accepted crop.
      for(let y=0;y<base.h;y++)for(let x=0;x<base.w;x++)if(bayer(base.x+x,base.y+y)<step/16){
        for(let sy=0;sy<S;sy++)pixels.fill(EMPTY,(y*S+sy)*W+x*S,(y*S+sy)*W+(x+1)*S);
      }
      paperLookup.set(hash,{...base,id:'box_fold/flat_fade'+String(step).padStart(2,'0'),sourceHash:hash,pixels,derived:true,derivation:'flat-box fade',fadeStep:step});created++;
    }
  }finally{SPR.set(saved);SPO.set(outline);KEY=key;}
  if(created){paperAtlas=null;paperVersion++;}
  paperBoxFadeIssues.push(...missing);return {created,missing};
}
function paperGateIndex(a,b,state,frame) {
  if(((a+1)&3)!==b){const previous=a;a=b;b=previous;state=1-state;}
  return ((a*4+b)*2+state)*3+frame;
}
function paperBindSourceAlias(actualHash,acceptedHash) {
  if(!Number.isInteger(actualHash)||actualHash<0||actualHash>0xffffffff)throw new Error('Invalid paper alias hash');
  const texture=paperLookup.get(acceptedHash);if(!texture)return false;
  if(paperLookup.has(actualHash))return paperLookup.get(actualHash)===texture;
  paperLookup.set(actualHash,texture);paperAtlas=null;paperVersion++;return true;
}
function paperRecordMissing(hash) {
  let slot=hash&2047;
  while(paperMissingHits[slot]){
    if(paperMissingHashes[slot]===hash){paperMissingHits[slot]++;return;}
    slot=(slot+1)&2047;
  }
  if(paperMissingSlots===PAPER_MISSING_CAPACITY){paperMissingOverflow++;return;}
  paperMissingSlots++;paperMissingHashes[slot]=hash;paperMissingHits[slot]=1;
}
function paperMissing() {
  const sprites=[],pieces=[];
  for(let i=0;i<paperMissingHits.length;i++)if(paperMissingHits[i])sprites.push({sourceHash:paperMissingHashes[i],count:paperMissingHits[i]});
  for(let type=0;type<paperPieceMisses.length;type++)for(let index=0;index<paperPieceMisses[type].length;index++)if(paperPieceMisses[type][index])pieces.push({type,index,count:paperPieceMisses[type][index]});
  sprites.sort((a,b)=>b.count-a.count||a.sourceHash-b.sourceHash);
  return {sprites,pieces,naps:paperNapIssues.slice(),fades:paperBoxFadeIssues.slice(),overflow:paperMissingOverflow};
}
function paperCapturePiece(type,index,fx,fy) {
  if(!paperEnabled)return null;
  const texture=paperPieceLookup[type][index];
  if(!texture){if(!paperAtlasBuilding&&tIdx===fIdx&&index>=0&&index<paperPieceMisses[type].length)paperPieceMisses[type][index]++;return null;}
  if(paperCacheBuild){paperCachePiece={type,index,fx,fy};return null;}
  if(!paperSlots||tIdx!==fIdx||paperCount===PAPER_CAPACITY)return null;
  const s=paperSlots[paperCount++];s.kind=1;s.texture=texture;s.w=texture.w;s.h=texture.h;
  s.offsetX=texture.dx;s.offsetY=texture.dy;s.mirror=false;s.clip=-1e9;s.clipSide=-1;s.clipAt=0;s.surface=null;s.fz=0;
  s.ax=Math.round(fx-fy)+tOX;s.ay=Math.round((fx+fy)*.5)+tOY;s.mask.fill(0);
  for(let y=0;y<s.h;y++)for(let x=0;x<s.w;x++){
    const k=y*s.w+x,px=s.ax+s.offsetX+x,py=s.ay+s.offsetY+y;
    if(px<0||py<0||px>=LW||py>=LH)continue;
    const i=py*LW+px;s.underIdx[k]=fIdx[i];s.underLvl[k]=fLvl[i];s.underDep[k]=fDep[i];
    if(paperAtlasBuilding){s.underCls[k]=aCls[i];s.underSel[k]=aSel[i];}
  }
  return s;
}
function paperFinishPiece(s) {
  if(!s)return;
  for(let y=0;y<s.h;y++)for(let x=0;x<s.w;x++){
    const k=y*s.w+x,px=s.ax+s.offsetX+x,py=s.ay+s.offsetY+y;
    if(px<0||py<0||px>=LW||py>=LH)continue;
    const i=py*LW+px;
    if(fDep[i]!==s.underDep[k]||fIdx[i]!==s.underIdx[k]||fLvl[i]!==s.underLvl[k]){
      s.mask[k]=1;s.source[k]=fIdx[i];s.sourceDep[k]=fDep[i];s.light[k]=fLvl[i];
    }
  }
}
function paperCaptureCachedPiece(cell) {
  const p=cell.paper;return p?paperCapturePiece(p.type,p.index,p.fx,p.fy):null;
}
function paperResolveFrame() {
  paperReady=false;
  const staticOn=!paperAtlasBuilding&&typeof paperStaticAvailable==='function'&&paperStaticAvailable();
  const atlasOn=paperAtlasBuilding&&typeof paperStaticAtlasHasFaces==='function'&&paperStaticAtlasHasFaces();
  if(!paperEnabled||(!paperCount&&!staticOn&&!atlasOn)||!paperImage)return;
  const S=paperRenderScale(),W=LW*S,T=grayscale?GRAY32:RES32,half=NLVL*PAL_N;
  for(let y=0;y<LH;y++)for(let x=0;x<LW;x++){
    const i=y*LW+x;
    for(let sy=0;sy<S;sy++)for(let sx=0;sx<S;sx++){
      const p=(y*S+sy)*W+x*S+sx;
      paperRGBA[p]=img32[i];paperDepth[p]=fDep[i];paperIndex[p]=fIdx[i];
      if(paperAtlasBuilding){paperAtlasIdx[p]=fIdx[i]===EMPTY?EMPTY:LUT[fLvl[i]*PAL_N+(fIdx[i]&127)];paperAtlasCls[p]=aCls[i];paperAtlasSel[p]=aSel[i];}
    }
  }
  // Strip latest first, so a covered sprite's saved underlayer can itself be stripped.
  for(let n=paperCount-1;n>=0;n--){
    const s=paperSlots[n];s.stripped.fill(0);
    for(let y=0;y<s.h;y++)for(let x=0;x<s.w;x++){
      const k=y*s.w+x;if(!s.mask[k])continue;
      const px=s.ax+(s.mirror?-s.offsetX-x:s.offsetX+x),py=s.ay+s.offsetY+y,i=py*LW+px;
      const depth=s.surface?s.texture.depth[(y-s.texture.y)*s.texture.w+x-s.texture.x]:-(s.offsetY+y)*4;
      const dep=s.kind?s.sourceDep[k]:Math.round(s.d0+depth)+2,c=s.underIdx[k];
      let l=s.underLvl[k];if(paperVignette&&VIG[i]&&l>0&&!(BD&&s.underDep[k]===DEPTH_EMPTY))l--;
      const rgba=T[(c>>7)*half+l*PAL_N+(c&127)];
      for(let sy=0;sy<S;sy++)for(let sx=0;sx<S;sx++){
        const p=(py*S+sy)*W+px*S+sx;
        if(paperDepth[p]!==dep||paperIndex[p]!==s.source[k])continue;
        s.stripped[k]=1;paperDepth[p]=s.underDep[k];paperIndex[p]=c;paperRGBA[p]=rgba;
        if(paperAtlasBuilding){paperAtlasIdx[p]=c===EMPTY?EMPTY:LUT[l*PAL_N+(c&127)];paperAtlasCls[p]=s.underCls[k];paperAtlasSel[p]=s.underSel[k];}
      }
    }
  }
  if(paperAtlasBuilding){if(atlasOn&&typeof paperStaticAtlasResolve==='function')paperStaticAtlasResolve();}
  else if(typeof paperResolveStatic==='function')paperResolveStatic();
  for(let n=0;n<paperCount;n++){
    const s=paperSlots[n],r=s.texture,w=r.w*S,h=r.h*S;
    for(let y=0;y<h;y++){
      const logicalY=r.dy+Math.floor(y/S),py=s.ay+logicalY;
      if(py<0||py>=LH||s.fz-logicalY<s.clip)continue;
      const sampleY=S===PAPER_SCALE?y:Math.floor(y/S)*PAPER_SCALE+paperSampleOffset(y%S,S);
      for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
        const sampleX=S===PAPER_SCALE?x:Math.floor(x/S)*PAPER_SCALE+(s.mirror?PAPER_SCALE-1-paperSampleOffset(S-1-x%S,S):paperSampleOffset(x%S,S));
        const c=r.pixels[sampleY*r.w*PAPER_SCALE+sampleX];if(c===EMPTY)continue;
        const logicalX=r.dx+Math.floor(x/S),px=s.ax+(s.mirror?-logicalX:logicalX);
        if(px<0||px>=LW)continue;
        const dx=s.mirror?-r.dx*S+S-1-x:r.dx*S+x;
        const p=((s.ay+r.dy)*S+y)*W+s.ax*S+dx;
        const cell=(logicalY-r.dy)*r.w+logicalX-r.dx;
        if(s.clipSide>=0){
          const sum=s.surface?(r.depth[cell]/4+logicalY)/1.5:0;
          const q=dx/S,wx=s.fx+(sum+q)*.5,wy=s.fy+(sum-q)*.5;
          if((s.clipSide&1?wy:wx)*(s.clipSide<2?1:-1)>s.clipAt)continue;
        }
        const k=(logicalY-s.offsetY)*s.w+logicalX-s.offsetX,i=py*LW+px;
        if(s.kind&&!s.mask[k])continue;
        const dep=s.kind?s.sourceDep[k]:Math.round(s.d0+(s.surface?r.depth[cell]:-logicalY*4))+2;
        if(dep<paperDepth[p])continue;
        if(dep===paperDepth[p]&&(s.mask[k]?!s.stripped[k]:paperIndex[p]!==s.underIdx[k]||paperDepth[p]!==s.underDep[k]))continue;
        let l=s.mask[k]&&fDep[i]===dep&&fIdx[i]===s.source[k]?fLvl[i]:s.light[k];
        if(!(s.mask[k]&&fDep[i]===dep&&fIdx[i]===s.source[k])&&paperVignette&&VIG[i]&&l>0)l--;
        const board=s.kind?s.source[k]&128:s.board;
        paperDepth[p]=dep;paperIndex[p]=c|board;paperRGBA[p]=T[(board>>7)*half+l*PAL_N+c];
        if(paperAtlasBuilding){paperAtlasIdx[p]=LUT[l*PAL_N+c];paperAtlasCls[p]=0;paperAtlasSel[p]=0;}
      }
    }
  }
  if(!paperAtlasBuilding){hatchCueResolve(boardSrc);paperCtx.putImageData(paperImage,0,0);}paperReady=true;
}
function paperOutputCanvas(){return paperEnabled&&paperReady?paperCanvas:offCanvas;}
function paperOutputScale(){return paperEnabled&&paperReady?paperRenderScale():1;}
function paperWithNativeView(draw){
  if(!paperEnabled||paperViewScale===PAPER_SCALE)return draw();
  const saved={paperViewScale,paperImage,paperRGBA,paperDepth,paperIndex,paperCount,paperReady,paperVignette};
  const width=paperCanvas.width,height=paperCanvas.height;
  try{paperSetViewScale(PAPER_SCALE);paperAllocView();return draw();}
  finally{
    ({paperViewScale,paperImage,paperRGBA,paperDepth,paperIndex,paperCount,paperReady,paperVignette}=saved);
    paperCanvas.width=width;paperCanvas.height=height;if(paperImage)paperCtx.putImageData(paperImage,0,0);
  }
}
// Atlas-only scratch rendering uses the same capture/compositor as live frames.
// Its targets, buffers and colour tables are restored before returning to the scene.
function paperBuildAtlasSource() {
  const saved={LW,LH,fIdx,fLvl,fDep,img,img32,VIG,RES32,GRAY32,grayscale,
    tIdx,tLvl,tDep,tW,tH,tOX,tOY,tBake,tSun,tPX,tPY,shLvl,shFlat,shFlag,shBias,shSunMax,SUN_OBJ,shBoard,sunAdd,aoFn,lampFn,rngState,
    aSize,aOriginX,aOriginY,aIdx,aLvl,aDep,aCls,aSel,atlasSprites,atlasBlit,KEY,
    blitClipZ,blitClipSide,blitClipAt,blitSurface,cbKey,cbMirror,cbBX,cbBY,ttDir,ttKind,ttSpin,ttLocked,htDir,htOpen,bkCoat,bkEmpty,bkX0,bkY0,gateHX,gateHY,gateVX,gateVY,
    bxWall,bxSlopeWall,bxCur,bxCurI,dcx,dcy,dr,dsh,ddk,dol,blobShade,shOffU,flapColour,flapSwing,
    bankCx,pW,pD,pH,pSeed,cushRed,curWin,crW,crH,tagCol,
    paperSlots,paperCount,paperReady,paperVignette,paperEnabled,paperImage,paperRGBA,paperDepth,paperIndex,paperAtlasBuilding,paperAtlasIdx,paperAtlasCls,paperAtlasSel,
    paperCacheBuild,paperCachePiece};
  const sprite=SPR.slice(),outline=SPO.slice(),catState={...cat},sorterState={...pose};
  const cells=new Map(),frames=new Map(),offsets=new Map(),hashes=new Map(),origins=new Map();let current=null,source;
  function scratch(size){
    let c=cells.get(size);
    if(!c){
      const image=offCtx.createImageData(size*PAPER_SCALE,size*PAPER_SCALE),low=offCtx.createImageData(size,size),n=size*size*PAPER_SCALE*PAPER_SCALE;
      c={image,rgba:new Uint32Array(image.data.buffer),dep:new Int16Array(n),index:new Uint8Array(n),idx:new Uint8Array(n),cls:new Uint8Array(n),sel:new Uint8Array(n),low,low32:new Uint32Array(low.data.buffer),vig:new Uint8Array(size*size)};
      cells.set(size,c);
    }
    LW=LH=size;fIdx=aIdx;fLvl=aLvl;fDep=aDep;img=c.low;img32=c.low32;VIG=c.vig;
    paperImage=c.image;paperRGBA=c.rgba;paperDepth=c.dep;paperIndex=c.index;paperAtlasIdx=c.idx;paperAtlasCls=c.cls;paperAtlasSel=c.sel;
    paperBeginFrame();
  }
  try{
    paperAtlasBuilding=true;paperEnabled=true;blitClipSide=-1;blitClipAt=0;blitSurface=null;paperCacheBuild=false;paperSlots=paperCreateSlots(paperSlotSize,true);
    RES32=BASE_TABLES.res;GRAY32=BASE_TABLES.gray;grayscale=false;
    atlasBlit=function(fx,fy,fz,mirror){
      const hash=paperSpriteHash();
      if(current){
        hashes.get(current.id).add(hash);
        if(!offsets.has(current.name))offsets.set(current.name,{x:Math.round(fx-fy)+tOX-aOriginX,y:Math.round((fx+fy)*.5-fz)+tOY-aOriginY});
      }
      const capture=paperCaptureSprite(fx,fy,fz,mirror,false);saved.atlasBlit(fx,fy,fz,mirror);paperFinishSprite(capture);
    };
    atlasSprites=function(){return saved.atlasSprites().map(s=>{const size=s.cellSize||AC;origins.set(s.name,{x:size/2,y:size===AC?ACY:size/2});return {...s,frames:s.frames.map(f=>({...f,draw:()=>{
      const id=s.name+'/'+f.tag;current={id,name:s.name};hashes.set(id,new Set());scratch(aSize);
      try{
      if(typeof paperStaticAtlasBegin==='function')paperStaticAtlasBegin();
      f.draw();
      if(typeof paperStaticAtlasFinish==='function')paperStaticAtlasFinish();
      const sourcePaper=typeof paperStaticAtlasHasFaces==='function'&&paperStaticAtlasHasFaces();
      if(!paperCount&&!sourcePaper)return;
      let x0=aSize,y0=aSize,x1=-1,y1=-1;
      for(let y=0;y<aSize;y++)for(let x=0;x<aSize;x++){
        const i=y*aSize+x,c=aIdx[i];img32[i]=c===EMPTY?0:BASE_TABLES.res[aLvl[i]*PAL_N+(c&127)];
        if(c!==EMPTY){x0=Math.min(x0,x);y0=Math.min(y0,y);x1=Math.max(x1,x);y1=Math.max(y1,y);}
      }
      if(x1<0)return;
      paperResolveFrame();
      const S=PAPER_SCALE,w=(x1-x0+1)*S,h=(y1-y0+1)*S,out={x:x0,y:y0,w:w/S,h:h/S,idx:new Uint8Array(w*h),cls:new Uint8Array(w*h),sel:new Uint8Array(w*h),sourceDecals:undefined,textureKind:paperCount?'generated':'source-paper'};
      for(let y=0;y<h;y++){
        const start=(y+y0*S)*aSize*S+x0*S;
        out.idx.set(paperAtlasIdx.subarray(start,start+w),y*w);out.cls.set(paperAtlasCls.subarray(start,start+w),y*w);out.sel.set(paperAtlasSel.subarray(start,start+w),y*w);
      }
      for(let n=0;n<paperCount;n++)if(paperSlots[n].texture.arrowFree)out.sourceDecals=['gate_open','gate_closed'];
      frames.set(id,out);
      }finally{current=null;if(typeof paperStaticAtlasEnd==='function')paperStaticAtlasEnd();}
    }}))};});};
    source=buildAtlas();
  }finally{
    ({LW,LH,fIdx,fLvl,fDep,img,img32,VIG,RES32,GRAY32,grayscale,
      tIdx,tLvl,tDep,tW,tH,tOX,tOY,tBake,tSun,tPX,tPY,shLvl,shFlat,shFlag,shBias,shSunMax,SUN_OBJ,shBoard,sunAdd,aoFn,lampFn,rngState,
      aSize,aOriginX,aOriginY,aIdx,aLvl,aDep,aCls,aSel,atlasSprites,atlasBlit,KEY,
      blitClipZ,blitClipSide,blitClipAt,blitSurface,cbKey,cbMirror,cbBX,cbBY,ttDir,ttKind,ttSpin,ttLocked,htDir,htOpen,bkCoat,bkEmpty,bkX0,bkY0,gateHX,gateHY,gateVX,gateVY,
      bxWall,bxSlopeWall,bxCur,bxCurI,dcx,dcy,dr,dsh,ddk,dol,blobShade,shOffU,flapColour,flapSwing,
      bankCx,pW,pD,pH,pSeed,cushRed,curWin,crW,crH,tagCol,
      paperSlots,paperCount,paperReady,paperVignette,paperEnabled,paperImage,paperRGBA,paperDepth,paperIndex,paperAtlasBuilding,paperAtlasIdx,paperAtlasCls,paperAtlasSel,
      paperCacheBuild,paperCachePiece}=saved);
    SPR.set(sprite);SPO.set(outline);Object.assign(cat,catState);Object.assign(pose,sorterState);
  }
  return {source,frames,offsets,hashes,origins};
}
function paperAtlasData() {
  if(paperAtlas)return paperAtlas;
  const built=paperBuildAtlasSource(),source=built.source,S=PAPER_SCALE,groups=new Map(),sprites=[],meta=new Map(),originalIds=new Set(),pack=[];
  for(const old of source.json.sprites){
    const s={...old,size:{w:old.size.w*S,h:old.size.h*S},anchor:{x:old.anchor.x*S,y:old.anchor.y*S},logicalSize:{...old.size},logicalAnchor:{...old.anchor},frames:old.frames.map(f=>{
      const id=old.name+'/'+f.tag;originalIds.add(id);const hi=built.frames.get(id),hashes=[...built.hashes.get(id)||[]];
      const frame={...f,x:0,y:0,sourceX:f.x,sourceY:f.y,textureKind:hi?hi.textureKind:'source',...(hashes.length===1?{sourceHash:hashes[0]}:hashes.length?{sourceHashes:hashes}:{}),...(hi&&hi.sourceDecals?{sourceDecals:hi.sourceDecals}:{})};
      pack.push({frame,w:old.size.w*S,h:old.size.h*S,old,sourceFrame:f,hi});return frame;
    })};sprites.push(s);meta.set(s.name,s);
  }
  const extraIds=new Set();
  for(const texture of [...paperSpriteRecords,...paperPieceRecords,...[...paperLookup.values()].filter(r=>r.derived)]){
    if(originalIds.has(texture.id)||extraIds.has(texture.id))continue;extraIds.add(texture.id);
    const slash=texture.id.indexOf('/'),name=slash<0?texture.id:texture.id.slice(0,slash),tag=slash<0?'idle0':texture.id.slice(slash+1);
    let group=groups.get(name);
    if(!group){const reference=meta.get(name);group={name:reference?name+'_runtime_variants':name,sourceFamily:name,x:Infinity,y:Infinity,x1:-Infinity,y1:-Infinity,frames:[],reference};groups.set(name,group);}
    group.x=Math.min(group.x,texture.dx);group.y=Math.min(group.y,texture.dy);
    group.x1=Math.max(group.x1,texture.dx+texture.w);group.y1=Math.max(group.y1,texture.dy+texture.h);
    group.frames.push({texture,tag});
  }
  for(const group of groups.values()){
    if(!group.meta){
      const first=group.frames[0].texture;
      group.meta={name:group.name,direction:first.direction||group.reference&&group.reference.direction||'none',mirroredFor:first.mirroredFor||group.reference&&group.reference.mirroredFor||'none',fps:0,loop:false,
        size:{w:(group.x1-group.x)*S,h:(group.y1-group.y)*S},anchor:{x:-group.x*S,y:-group.y*S},
        logicalSize:{w:group.x1-group.x,h:group.y1-group.y},logicalAnchor:{x:-group.x,y:-group.y},frames:[],
        runtimeControlled:true,sourceFamily:group.sourceFamily};
      sprites.push(group.meta);meta.set(group.name,group.meta);
    }
    const s=group.meta;
    for(const entry of group.frames){
      const r=entry.texture,frame={x:0,y:0,tag:entry.tag,sourceHash:r.sourceHash,logicalRect:r.isPiece?{dx:r.dx,dy:r.dy,w:r.w,h:r.h}:{x:r.x,y:r.y,w:r.w,h:r.h},
        textureKind:'generated',...(r.depth?{logicalDepth:Array.from(r.depth),logicalFaces:Array.from(r.faces)}:{}),sourceDecals:r.arrowFree?['gate_open','gate_closed']:undefined,
        ...(r.entryNative?{entryAnchorNative:Array.from(r.entryNative),entryMouth:Array.from(r.entryMouth),entryAperture:Array.from(r.entryAperture),entryStage:r.entryStage}:{}),
        ...(r.derivation?{derivation:r.derivation}:{}),...(r.fadeStep?{fadeStep:r.fadeStep}:{}),...(r.cueOpacity!==undefined?{presentationOpacity:r.cueOpacity,depthPlane:r.cuePlane}:{})};
      s.frames.push(frame);pack.push({frame,w:s.size.w,h:s.size.h,texture:r,sprite:s});
    }
  }
  // Shelf packing affects only physical export coordinates. Metadata frame order stays intact.
  const rows=[];let W=0,H=0;
  pack.sort((a,b)=>b.h-a.h||b.w-a.w);
  for(const entry of pack){
    if(entry.w>4096)throw new Error('Paper atlas frame exceeds export width');
    let row=null;
    for(const candidate of rows)if(candidate.x+entry.w<=4096&&candidate.h>=entry.h&&(!row||candidate.h<row.h))row=candidate;
    if(!row){row={x:0,y:H,h:entry.h};rows.push(row);H+=entry.h+S;}
    entry.frame.x=row.x;entry.frame.y=row.y;row.x+=entry.w+S;W=Math.max(W,row.x-S);
  }
  H=Math.max(1,H-S);if(H>16384)throw new Error('Paper atlas exceeds single-PNG export height');
  const idx=new Uint8Array(W*H).fill(EMPTY),cls=new Uint8Array(W*H),sel=new Uint8Array(W*H);
  for(const entry of pack){
    const f=entry.frame;
    if(entry.texture){
      const r=entry.texture,s=entry.sprite,dx=(r.dx+s.logicalAnchor.x)*S,dy=(r.dy+s.logicalAnchor.y)*S;
      for(let y=0;y<r.h*S;y++)for(let x=0;x<r.w*S;x++)idx[(f.y+dy+y)*W+f.x+dx+x]=r.pixels[y*r.w*S+x];
    }else if(entry.hi){
      const hi=entry.hi,old=entry.old,origin=built.origins.get(old.name);
      const dx=(hi.x-(origin.x-old.anchor.x))*S,dy=(hi.y-(origin.y-old.anchor.y))*S,w=hi.w*S,h=hi.h*S;
      for(let y=0;y<h;y++)for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
        const tx=dx+x,ty=dy+y;if(tx<0||ty<0||tx>=entry.w||ty>=entry.h)continue;
        const from=y*w+x,to=(f.y+ty)*W+f.x+tx;idx[to]=hi.idx[from];cls[to]=hi.cls[from];sel[to]=hi.sel[from];
      }
    }else{
      const old=entry.old,o=entry.sourceFrame;
      for(let y=0;y<old.size.h;y++)for(let x=0;x<old.size.w;x++){
        const from=(o.y+y)*source.w+o.x+x;
        for(let sy=0;sy<S;sy++)for(let sx=0;sx<S;sx++){
          const to=(f.y+y*S+sy)*W+f.x+x*S+sx;idx[to]=source.idx[from];cls[to]=source.cls[from];sel[to]=source.sel[from];
        }
      }
    }
  }
  paperAtlas={w:W,h:H,idx,cls,sel,json:{...source.json,scale:S,
    tile:{...source.json.tile,w:source.json.tile.w*S,h:source.json.tile.h*S},sprites,
    aliases:[...paperLookup].filter(entry=>entry[0]!==entry[1].sourceHash).map(entry=>({sourceHash:entry[0],textureSourceHash:entry[1].sourceHash})),
    notes:source.json.notes.concat(['All original families and state metadata are retained. Pixel sizes and anchors are at scale 4; physical frame coordinates are repacked within4096×16384. logicalSize/logicalAnchor and sourceX/sourceY retain original metadata.',
      'Entrance frames retain logicalDepth (quarter world units, -32768 empty) and logicalFaces (0 top, 1 Y, 2 X) alongside logicalRect, relative to the projected tile centre.',
      'textureKind identifies generated replacements and source artwork. Static source frames use nearest4× pixels; generated pixels have no separate selective-outline channel. Additional conditional states use separate fps0 runtimeControlled families.'])},
    paletteJson:source.paletteJson,backdropsJson:source.backdropsJson};
  return paperAtlas;
}
if(typeof PAPER_SPRITE_DATA!=='undefined'||typeof PAPER_PIECE_DATA!=='undefined'){
  const sprites=typeof PAPER_SPRITE_DATA!=='undefined'?PAPER_SPRITE_DATA:{scale:PAPER_SCALE,sprites:[]};
  const pieces=typeof PAPER_PIECE_DATA!=='undefined'?PAPER_PIECE_DATA.pieces:sprites.pieces;
  paperInstallData({scale:sprites.scale,sprites:sprites.sprites.concat(typeof CHRISTMAS_COSTUME_DATA==='undefined'?[]:CHRISTMAS_COSTUME_DATA.sprites,typeof CHRISTMAS_CHAPTER_DATA==='undefined'?[]:CHRISTMAS_CHAPTER_DATA.sprites),pieces:pieces.concat(typeof TUNNEL_PIECE_DATA==='undefined'?[]:TUNNEL_PIECE_DATA),napSources:sprites.napSources});
}
// Exact source faces at presentation resolution. Logical colour, light, depth and
// world metadata are still drawn by the original rasterisers, without alteration.
let paperStaticEnabled=true,paperStaticReview=false,paperStaticValid=false,paperStaticCapturing=false,paperStaticBackdropEdges=true;
let paperStaticIdx=null,paperStaticLvl=null,paperStaticDep=null;
let paperStaticSourceIdx=null,paperStaticSourceLvl=null,paperStaticSourceDep=null;
let paperStaticWH=0,paperStaticOX=0,paperStaticOY=0,paperStaticPrimitive=0;
const PAPER_STATIC_W=WW*PAPER_SCALE;
// Materials affect only the second, presentation draw. Prop entry points also
// serve the atlas, so its faces use the same quiet seams as the live room.
let paperPropMaterials=true,paperPropScope=0,paperStaticAtlasActive=false,paperStaticAtlasFaces=0;
let paperStaticTargetIdx=sIdx,paperStaticTargetLvl=sLvl,paperStaticTargetDep=sDep,paperStaticLogicalW=WW,paperStaticLogicalH=WH;
let paperStaticWidth=PAPER_STATIC_W,paperStaticAtlasSaved=null;
let paperStaticPX=0,paperStaticPY=0;
const paperStaticAtlasCells=new Map();
function paperStaticTarget(){return paperStaticCapturing&&tIdx===paperStaticTargetIdx&&(tBake||paperStaticAtlasActive);}
function paperPropShadow(c){const s=SH[c&127];return s===C.OUT||s===C.DEEP?c:s;}
function paperPropShader(sh,face,u,v){
  if(!paperPropMaterials||!paperPropScope)return sh(face,u,v);
  if(sh===shDisc){
    const dx=u-dr,dy=v-dr,d=Math.hypot(dx,dy);if(d>=dr)return -1;
    const c=dsh(F_TOP,dx,dy);if(c<0)return c;
    if(dol&&d>dr-.25&&(dx+dy)<.5*dr)return paperPropShadow(c);
    return c;
  }
  if(sh===shDpWheel){
    const r=Math.hypot(u-dpWheelR,v-dpWheelR);if(r>dpWheelR)return -1;
    return r<1.2?C.BR1:r<dpWheelR-.25?C.COOL1:C.DEEP;
  }
  if(sh===shDpDial){
    const x=u-dpDialR,z=v-dpDialR,r=Math.hypot(x,z);if(r>dpDialR)return -1;
    if(r>dpDialR-.25)return C.COOL1;
    if(r>dpDialR-1)return C.CREAM;
  }
  return sh(face,u,v);
}
function paperPropWrap(original){return function(){paperPropScope++;try{return original.apply(this,arguments);}finally{paperPropScope--;}};}
for(const key of Object.keys(BX_PROP))BX_PROP[key]=paperPropWrap(BX_PROP[key]);
for(const key of Object.keys(BX_ITEM))BX_ITEM[key]=paperPropWrap(BX_ITEM[key]);
depotRack=paperPropWrap(depotRack);depotBag=paperPropWrap(depotBag);depotConveyor=paperPropWrap(depotConveyor);
depotChute=paperPropWrap(depotChute);depotScale=paperPropWrap(depotScale);depotTeaTable=paperPropWrap(depotTeaTable);
depotUrn=paperPropWrap(depotUrn);depotRadio=paperPropWrap(depotRadio);depotTin=paperPropWrap(depotTin);
depotStool=paperPropWrap(depotStool);depotMugs=paperPropWrap(depotMugs);depotFlatPacks=paperPropWrap(depotFlatPacks);
depotSackPile=paperPropWrap(depotSackPile);depotTrolley=paperPropWrap(depotTrolley);depotBarrow=paperPropWrap(depotBarrow);
depotCarrier=paperPropWrap(depotCarrier);depotVan=paperPropWrap(depotVan);
depotClock=paperPropWrap(depotClock);bxFanBlades=paperPropWrap(bxFanBlades);

function paperStaticState(){
  return {shLvl,shFlat,shFlag,shBias,shSunMax,SUN_OBJ,shBoard,sunAdd,aoFn,lampFn,rngState,bayer};
}
function paperStaticRestore(s){
  ({shLvl,shFlat,shFlag,shBias,shSunMax,SUN_OBJ,shBoard,sunAdd,aoFn,lampFn,rngState,bayer}=s);
}
function paperStaticThreshold(){return .5;}
function paperStaticCopyPixel(i){
  const S=PAPER_SCALE,x=i%paperStaticLogicalW,y=Math.floor(i/paperStaticLogicalW),c=paperStaticTargetIdx[i],l=paperStaticTargetLvl[i],d=paperStaticTargetDep[i];
  for(let sy=0;sy<S;sy++)for(let sx=0;sx<S;sx++){
    const p=(y*S+sy)*paperStaticWidth+x*S+sx;
    if(d<paperStaticDep[p])continue;
    paperStaticIdx[p]=c;paperStaticLvl[p]=l;paperStaticDep[p]=d;
  }
}
function paperStaticReset(){
  paperPropStaticPointCount=0;
  paperStaticValid=false;
  paperStaticCapturing=paperStaticEnabled&&((paperEnabled&&img!==null)||paperStaticReview);
  if(!paperStaticCapturing)return;
  paperStaticTargetIdx=sIdx;paperStaticTargetLvl=sLvl;paperStaticTargetDep=sDep;
  paperStaticLogicalW=WW;paperStaticLogicalH=WH;paperStaticWidth=PAPER_STATIC_W;
  if(!paperStaticIdx){
    const n=WN*PAPER_SCALE*PAPER_SCALE;
    paperStaticIdx=new Uint8Array(n);paperStaticLvl=new Uint8Array(n);paperStaticDep=new Int16Array(n);
    paperStaticSourceIdx=new Uint8Array(WN);paperStaticSourceLvl=new Uint8Array(WN);paperStaticSourceDep=new Int16Array(WN);
  }
  paperStaticWH=WH;paperStaticOX=OX;paperStaticOY=OY;paperStaticPrimitive=0;
  paperStaticDep.fill(DEPTH_EMPTY);
  paperStaticSourceIdx.set(sIdx);paperStaticSourceLvl.set(sLvl);paperStaticSourceDep.set(sDep);
  for(let i=0;i<WW*WH;i++)paperStaticCopyPixel(i);
}
function paperStaticFinish(){
  if(!paperStaticCapturing)return;
  // Bitmap stamps and tiny direct writes keep their original scaled pixels.
  for(let i=0;i<paperStaticLogicalW*paperStaticLogicalH;i++)if(paperStaticTargetIdx[i]!==paperStaticSourceIdx[i]||paperStaticTargetLvl[i]!==paperStaticSourceLvl[i]||paperStaticTargetDep[i]!==paperStaticSourceDep[i])paperStaticCopyPixel(i);
  paperPropStaticPoints();
  paperStaticCapturing=false;paperStaticValid=true;
}
function paperStaticEmit(px,py,x,y,z,face,col){
  const i=py*paperStaticWidth+px,d=Math.round((x+y+z)*4)+shBias;
  if(d<paperStaticDep[i])return;
  let l=2+shLvl;sunAdd=0;
  if(!shFlat)l+=lightAt(x,y,z,face,px/PAPER_SCALE+paperStaticPX+tPX,py/PAPER_SCALE+paperStaticPY+tPY);
  paperStaticDep[i]=d;paperStaticIdx[i]=col|shBoard;paperStaticLvl[i]=clamp(l,0,4);
}
function paperStaticFace(face,a){
  const S=PAPER_SCALE,W=paperStaticWidth,H=paperStaticLogicalH*S;
  let pa,pb,qa,qb;
  if(face===F_TOP){
    pa=a[0]-a[3]+tOX;pb=a[2]-a[1]+tOX;
    qa=(a[0]+a[1])*.5-a[4]+tOY;qb=(a[2]+a[3])*.5-a[4]+tOY;
  }else{
    pa=(face===F_Y?a[0]-a[4]:a[4]-a[1])+tOX;
    pb=(face===F_Y?a[1]-a[4]:a[4]-a[0])+tOX;
    qa=(a[0]+a[4])*.5-a[3]+tOY;qb=(a[1]+a[4])*.5-a[2]+tOY;
  }
  pa=Math.max(0,Math.floor(pa*S)-S);pb=Math.min(W-1,Math.ceil(pb*S)+S);
  qa=Math.max(0,Math.floor(qa*S)-S);qb=Math.min(H-1,Math.ceil(qb*S)+S);
  const sh=a[5],dk=a[6],olm=a[7];
  for(let py=qa;py<=qb;py++)for(let px=pa;px<=pb;px++){
    const A=(px+.5)/S-tOX,B=py/S-tOY;
    let x,y,z,u,v;
    if(face===F_TOP){
      z=a[4];x=B+z+A*.5;y=B+z-A*.5;
      if(x<a[0]||x>=a[2]||y<a[1]||y>=a[3])continue;
      u=x-a[0];v=y-a[1];
    }else if(face===F_Y){
      x=A+a[4];y=a[4];z=(x+y)*.5-B;
      if(x<a[0]||x>=a[1]||z<a[2]||z>=a[3])continue;
      u=x-a[0];v=z-a[2];
    }else{
      x=a[4];y=x-A;z=(x+y)*.5-B;
      if(y<a[0]||y>=a[1]||z<a[2]||z>=a[3])continue;
      u=y-a[0];v=z-a[2];
    }
    shLvl=0;let c=paperPropShader(sh,face,u,v);if(c<0)continue;
    const material=paperPropMaterials&&paperPropScope,width=material?.25:1;
    const outline=face===F_TOP?(olm&1)&&(u<width||v<width):((olm&2)&&u<width)||((olm&4)&&v<width)||((olm&1)&&z>=a[3]-width);
    if(outline){c=material?paperPropShadow(c):dk;shLvl=material?0:-1;}
    paperStaticEmit(px,py,x,y,z,face,c);
  }
}
function paperStaticRot(a){
  const S=PAPER_SCALE,W=paperStaticWidth,H=paperStaticLogicalH*S,z=a[4];
  const pa=Math.max(0,Math.floor((a[0]-a[3]+tOX)*S)-S),pb=Math.min(W-1,Math.ceil((a[2]-a[1]+tOX)*S)+S);
  const qa=Math.max(0,Math.floor(((a[0]+a[1])*.5-z+tOY)*S)-S),qb=Math.min(H-1,Math.ceil(((a[2]+a[3])*.5-z+tOY)*S)+S);
  for(let py=qa;py<=qb;py++)for(let px=pa;px<=pb;px++){
    const b=py/S-tOY+z,A=((px+.5)/S-tOX)*.5,x=b+A,y=b-A,dx=x-a[5],dy=y-a[6];
    const u=dx*a[9]+dy*a[10],v=dy*a[9]-dx*a[10];
    if(u< -a[7]||u>=a[7]||v< -a[8]||v>=a[8])continue;
    shLvl=0;const c=a[11](F_TOP,u+a[7],v+a[8]);if(c>=0)paperStaticEmit(px,py,x,y,z,F_TOP,c);
  }
}
function paperStaticCylinder(cx,cy,r,z0,z1,sh,dk,olb){
  if(!paperPropMaterials||!paperPropScope)return paperStaticOriginalCylinder(cx,cy,r,z0,z1,sh,dk,olb,PAPER_SCALE,paperStaticEmit);
  const S=PAPER_SCALE,ac=cx-cy,pa=Math.max(0,(Math.floor(ac-r*1.415)-1+tOX)*S),pb=Math.min(paperStaticWidth-1,(Math.ceil(ac+r*1.415)+1+tOX)*S);
  for(let px=pa;px<=pb;px++){
    const a=(px+.5)/S-tOX,q=(a-ac)/r;if(q<=-1.414||q>=1.414)continue;
    const th=Math.acos(q/Math.SQRT2)-Math.PI/4,x=cx+r*Math.cos(th),y=cy+r*Math.sin(th);
    const face=(-Math.cos(th)+SUN_M*Math.sin(th))>0?F_Y:F_X,edge=Math.abs(q)>1.414-.25/r,zc=(x+y)*.5+tOY;
    const qa=Math.max(0,(Math.floor(zc-z1)-1)*S),qb=Math.min(paperStaticLogicalH*S-1,(Math.ceil(zc-z0)+1)*S);
    for(let py=qa;py<=qb;py++){
      const z=zc-py/S;if(z<z0||z>=z1)continue;
      shLvl=0;let c=sh(face,th,z-z0);if(c<0)continue;
      if(edge||(olb&&z<z0+.25)){c=paperPropShadow(c);shLvl=0;}
      paperStaticEmit(px,py,x,y,z,face,c);
    }
  }
}

// Review/setup scratch uses its own reusable buffers. A finally guard in39f
// restores these pointers before returning to the live scene, even on errors.
function paperStaticAtlasBegin(){
  if(paperStaticAtlasSaved)throw new Error('Nested paper source capture');
  paperStaticAtlasSaved={paperStaticCapturing,paperStaticValid,paperStaticIdx,paperStaticLvl,paperStaticDep,paperStaticSourceIdx,paperStaticSourceLvl,paperStaticSourceDep,
    paperStaticTargetIdx,paperStaticTargetLvl,paperStaticTargetDep,paperStaticLogicalW,paperStaticLogicalH,paperStaticWidth,paperStaticPrimitive,paperStaticAtlasActive,paperStaticAtlasFaces,paperPropStaticPointData,paperPropStaticPointCount};
  paperStaticAtlasActive=true;paperStaticAtlasFaces=0;paperStaticCapturing=paperStaticEnabled;paperStaticPrimitive=0;
  paperPropStaticPointData=paperPropAtlasPointData;paperPropStaticPointCount=0;
  if(!paperStaticCapturing)return;
  const key=tW+'x'+tH;let c=paperStaticAtlasCells.get(key);
  if(!c){const n=tW*tH,m=n*PAPER_SCALE*PAPER_SCALE;c={idx:new Uint8Array(m),lvl:new Uint8Array(m),dep:new Int16Array(m),sourceIdx:new Uint8Array(n),sourceLvl:new Uint8Array(n),sourceDep:new Int16Array(n)};paperStaticAtlasCells.set(key,c);}
  paperStaticTargetIdx=tIdx;paperStaticTargetLvl=tLvl;paperStaticTargetDep=tDep;
  paperStaticLogicalW=tW;paperStaticLogicalH=tH;paperStaticWidth=tW*PAPER_SCALE;
  paperStaticIdx=c.idx;paperStaticLvl=c.lvl;paperStaticDep=c.dep;
  paperStaticSourceIdx=c.sourceIdx;paperStaticSourceLvl=c.sourceLvl;paperStaticSourceDep=c.sourceDep;
  c.idx.fill(EMPTY);c.lvl.fill(2);c.dep.fill(DEPTH_EMPTY);
  c.sourceIdx.set(tIdx);c.sourceLvl.set(tLvl);c.sourceDep.set(tDep);
  for(let i=0;i<tW*tH;i++)if(tIdx[i]!==EMPTY)paperStaticCopyPixel(i);
}
function paperStaticAtlasFinish(){if(paperStaticCapturing)paperStaticFinish();}
function paperStaticAtlasHasFaces(){return paperStaticAtlasActive&&paperStaticEnabled&&paperStaticAtlasFaces>0;}
function paperStaticAtlasResolve(){
  if(!paperStaticAtlasHasFaces())return false;
  const S=PAPER_SCALE,W=LW*S;
  for(let y=0;y<LH;y++)for(let x=0;x<LW;x++){
    const i=y*LW+x;if(fIdx[i]===EMPTY&&paperCount)continue;
    for(let sy=0;sy<S;sy++)for(let sx=0;sx<S;sx++){
      const p=(y*S+sy)*W+x*S+sx;
      // A stripped generated sprite keeps its captured underlayer and replay.
      if(paperIndex[p]!==fIdx[i]||paperDepth[p]!==fDep[i])continue;
      const c=paperStaticDep[p]===DEPTH_EMPTY?EMPTY:paperStaticIdx[p],l=paperStaticLvl[p];
      paperRGBA[p]=c===EMPTY?0:BASE_TABLES.res[l*PAL_N+(c&127)];
      paperAtlasIdx[p]=c===EMPTY?EMPTY:LUT[l*PAL_N+(c&127)];paperAtlasCls[p]=c===EMPTY?0:aCls[i];paperAtlasSel[p]=c===EMPTY?0:aSel[i];
    }
  }
  return true;
}
function paperStaticAtlasEnd(){
  if(!paperStaticAtlasSaved)return;
  ({paperStaticCapturing,paperStaticValid,paperStaticIdx,paperStaticLvl,paperStaticDep,paperStaticSourceIdx,paperStaticSourceLvl,paperStaticSourceDep,
    paperStaticTargetIdx,paperStaticTargetLvl,paperStaticTargetDep,paperStaticLogicalW,paperStaticLogicalH,paperStaticWidth,paperStaticPrimitive,paperStaticAtlasActive,paperStaticAtlasFaces,paperPropStaticPointData,paperPropStaticPointCount}=paperStaticAtlasSaved);
  paperStaticAtlasSaved=null;
}
// Accepted imperative depot sprites can join a source cell before its front
// rim and label. Their original emit loop still owns all logical metadata.
function paperStaticSprite(texture,fx,fy,fz,mirror){
  if(!paperStaticTarget()||!texture)return false;
  if(paperStaticAtlasActive)paperStaticAtlasFaces++;
  const S=PAPER_SCALE,ax=Math.round(fx-fy)+tOX,ay=Math.round((fx+fy)*.5-fz)+tOY,bias=shBias,level=shLvl,sun=sunAdd;
  shBias=2;shLvl=0;
  try{
    for(let y=0;y<texture.h*S;y++)for(let x=0;x<texture.w*S;x++){
      const c=texture.pixels[y*texture.w*S+x];if(c===EMPTY)continue;
      const dx=mirror?-texture.dx*S+S-1-x:texture.dx*S+x,px=ax*S+dx,py=(ay+texture.dy)*S+y;
      if(px<0||py<0||px>=paperStaticWidth||py>=paperStaticLogicalH*S)continue;
      const logicalX=texture.dx+Math.floor(x/S),logicalY=texture.dy+Math.floor(y/S),wx=fx+(mirror?-logicalX:logicalX)*.5,wy=fy-(mirror?-logicalX:logicalX)*.5,wz=fz-logicalY;
      paperStaticEmit(px,py,wx,wy,wz,F_TOP,c);
    }
  }finally{shBias=bias;shLvl=level;sunAdd=sun;}
  return true;
}
const paperStaticOriginalTri=dpTri,paperStaticOriginalRod=dpRod;
dpTri=function(a,b,c,col,face){
  if(!paperStaticTarget())return paperStaticOriginalTri(a,b,c,col,face);
  const initial=paperStaticState();paperStaticPrimitive++;
  try{paperStaticOriginalTri(a,b,c,col,face);}finally{paperStaticPrimitive--;}
  const final=paperStaticState();if(paperStaticAtlasActive)paperStaticAtlasFaces++;
  try{
    paperStaticRestore(initial);bayer=paperStaticThreshold;
    const S=PAPER_SCALE,ax=a[0]-a[1]+tOX,ay=(a[0]+a[1])*.5-a[2]+tOY,bx=b[0]-b[1]+tOX,by=(b[0]+b[1])*.5-b[2]+tOY,cx=c[0]-c[1]+tOX,cy=(c[0]+c[1])*.5-c[2]+tOY;
    const den=(by-cy)*(ax-cx)+(cx-bx)*(ay-cy);if(Math.abs(den)<.001)return;
    const x0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Math.min(ax,bx,cx)*S)),x1=Math.min(paperStaticWidth-1,Math.ceil(Math.max(ax,bx,cx)*S));
    const y0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Math.min(ay,by,cy)*S)),y1=Math.min(paperStaticLogicalH*S-1,Math.ceil(Math.max(ay,by,cy)*S));
    for(let y=y0;y<=y1;y++)for(let x=x0;x<=x1;x++){
      const X=(x+.5)/S,Y=(y+.5)/S,u=((by-cy)*(X-cx)+(cx-bx)*(Y-cy))/den,v=((cy-ay)*(X-cx)+(ax-cx)*(Y-cy))/den,w=1-u-v;
      if(u<0||v<0||w<0)continue;shLvl=0;
      paperStaticEmit(x,y,a[0]*u+b[0]*v+c[0]*w,a[1]*u+b[1]*v+c[1]*w,a[2]*u+b[2]*v+c[2]*w,face,col);
    }
  }finally{paperStaticRestore(final);}
};
dpRod=function(x0,y0,z0,x1,y1,z1,c){
  if(!paperStaticTarget()||!paperPropMaterials||!paperPropScope)return paperStaticOriginalRod(x0,y0,z0,x1,y1,z1,c);
  const initial=paperStaticState();paperStaticPrimitive++;
  try{paperStaticOriginalRod(x0,y0,z0,x1,y1,z1,c);}finally{paperStaticPrimitive--;}
  const final=paperStaticState();if(paperStaticAtlasActive)paperStaticAtlasFaces++;
  try{
    paperStaticRestore(initial);bayer=paperStaticThreshold;
    const S=PAPER_SCALE,ax=x0-y0+tOX,ay=(x0+y0)*.5-z0+tOY,bx=x1-y1+tOX,by=(x1+y1)*.5-z1+tOY,dx=bx-ax,dy=by-ay,len=dx*dx+dy*dy;
    const pa=Math.max(0,Math.floor((Math.min(ax,bx)-.5)*S)),pb=Math.min(paperStaticWidth-1,Math.ceil((Math.max(ax,bx)+.5)*S));
    const qa=Math.max(0,Math.floor((Math.min(ay,by)-.5)*S)),qb=Math.min(paperStaticLogicalH*S-1,Math.ceil((Math.max(ay,by)+.5)*S));
    for(let py=qa;py<=qb;py++)for(let px=pa;px<=pb;px++){
      const X=(px+.5)/S,Y=(py+.5)/S,t=len?clamp(((X-ax)*dx+(Y-ay)*dy)/len,0,1):0;
      if((X-ax-t*dx)**2+(Y-ay-t*dy)**2>.25)continue;shLvl=0;
      paperStaticEmit(px,py,lerp(x0,x1,t),lerp(y0,y1,t),lerp(z0,z1,t),F_TOP,c===C.OUT?C.COOL1:c);
    }
  }finally{paperStaticRestore(final);}
};

const paperStaticOriginalEmit=emit,paperStaticOriginalBackdrop=bakeBackdrop;
emit=function(px,py,x,y,z,face,col){
  paperStaticOriginalEmit(px,py,x,y,z,face,col);
  if(!paperStaticTarget())return;
  const i=py*paperStaticLogicalW+px;
  paperStaticSourceIdx[i]=tIdx[i];paperStaticSourceLvl[i]=tLvl[i];paperStaticSourceDep[i]=tDep[i];
  if(!paperStaticPrimitive)paperStaticCopyPixel(i);
};
bakeBackdrop=function(){paperStaticOriginalBackdrop();paperStaticReset();};

function paperStaticWrapFace(original,face,x0,y0,x1,y1,z,sh,dk,olm){
  if(paperStaticAtlasActive)paperStaticAtlasFaces++;
  const initial=paperStaticState();paperStaticPrimitive++;
  try{original(x0,y0,x1,y1,z,sh,dk,olm);}finally{paperStaticPrimitive--;}
  const final=paperStaticState();
  try{paperStaticRestore(initial);bayer=paperStaticThreshold;paperStaticFace(face,[x0,y0,x1,y1,z,sh,dk,olm]);}finally{paperStaticRestore(final);}
}
const paperStaticOriginalTop=faceTop,paperStaticOriginalX=faceX,paperStaticOriginalY=faceY,paperStaticOriginalRot=faceTopRot;
faceTop=function(x0,y0,x1,y1,z,sh,dk,olm){
  if(paperPropFrameActive())return paperPropFrameFace(paperStaticOriginalTop,F_TOP,x0,y0,x1,y1,z,sh,dk,olm);
  if(!paperStaticTarget())return paperStaticOriginalTop(x0,y0,x1,y1,z,sh,dk,olm);
  paperStaticWrapFace(paperStaticOriginalTop,F_TOP,x0,y0,x1,y1,z,sh,dk,olm);
};
faceX=function(y0,y1,z0,z1,X,sh,dk,olm){
  if(paperPropFrameActive())return paperPropFrameFace(paperStaticOriginalX,F_X,y0,y1,z0,z1,X,sh,dk,olm);
  if(!paperStaticTarget())return paperStaticOriginalX(y0,y1,z0,z1,X,sh,dk,olm);
  paperStaticWrapFace(paperStaticOriginalX,F_X,y0,y1,z0,z1,X,sh,dk,olm);
};
faceY=function(x0,x1,z0,z1,Y,sh,dk,olm){
  if(paperPropFrameActive())return paperPropFrameFace(paperStaticOriginalY,F_Y,x0,x1,z0,z1,Y,sh,dk,olm);
  if(!paperStaticTarget())return paperStaticOriginalY(x0,x1,z0,z1,Y,sh,dk,olm);
  paperStaticWrapFace(paperStaticOriginalY,F_Y,x0,x1,z0,z1,Y,sh,dk,olm);
};
faceTopRot=function(x0,y0,x1,y1,z,cx,cy,hu,hv,ca,sa,sh){
  if(!paperStaticTarget())return paperStaticOriginalRot(x0,y0,x1,y1,z,cx,cy,hu,hv,ca,sa,sh);
  if(paperStaticAtlasActive)paperStaticAtlasFaces++;
  const initial=paperStaticState();paperStaticPrimitive++;
  try{paperStaticOriginalRot(x0,y0,x1,y1,z,cx,cy,hu,hv,ca,sa,sh);}finally{paperStaticPrimitive--;}
  const final=paperStaticState();
  try{paperStaticRestore(initial);bayer=paperStaticThreshold;paperStaticRot([x0,y0,x1,y1,z,cx,cy,hu,hv,ca,sa,sh]);}finally{paperStaticRestore(final);}
};

const paperStaticOriginalOffice=bakeStatic,paperStaticOriginalBox=bakeBoxRoom,paperStaticOriginalDepot=bakeDepot,paperStaticOriginalAlloc=allocView;
const paperStaticOriginalBase=bakeBase,paperStaticOriginalCylinder=cylinder,paperStaticOriginalSlope=faceSlope;
bakeBase=function(){
  if(!paperStaticTarget())return paperStaticOriginalBase();
  const initial=paperStaticState();paperStaticPrimitive++;
  try{paperStaticOriginalBase();}finally{paperStaticPrimitive--;}
  const final=paperStaticState();
  try{paperStaticRestore(initial);bayer=paperStaticThreshold;paperStaticOriginalBase(PAPER_SCALE,paperStaticEmit);}finally{paperStaticRestore(final);}
};
cylinder=function(cx,cy,r,z0,z1,sh,dk,olb){
  if(!paperStaticTarget())return paperStaticOriginalCylinder(cx,cy,r,z0,z1,sh,dk,olb);
  if(paperStaticAtlasActive)paperStaticAtlasFaces++;
  const initial=paperStaticState();paperStaticPrimitive++;
  try{paperStaticOriginalCylinder(cx,cy,r,z0,z1,sh,dk,olb);}finally{paperStaticPrimitive--;}
  const final=paperStaticState();
  try{paperStaticRestore(initial);bayer=paperStaticThreshold;paperStaticCylinder(cx,cy,r,z0,z1,sh,dk,olb);}finally{paperStaticRestore(final);}
};
faceSlope=function(wall,u0,u1,v0,v1,sh,hip,wallH,pitch){
  if(!paperStaticTarget())return paperStaticOriginalSlope(wall,u0,u1,v0,v1,sh,hip,wallH,pitch);
  if(paperStaticAtlasActive)paperStaticAtlasFaces++;
  const initial=paperStaticState();paperStaticPrimitive++;
  try{paperStaticOriginalSlope(wall,u0,u1,v0,v1,sh,hip,wallH,pitch);}finally{paperStaticPrimitive--;}
  const final=paperStaticState();
  try{paperStaticRestore(initial);bayer=paperStaticThreshold;paperStaticOriginalSlope(wall,u0,u1,v0,v1,sh,hip,wallH,pitch,PAPER_SCALE,paperStaticEmit);}finally{paperStaticRestore(final);}
};
bakeStatic=function(){paperStaticOriginalOffice();paperStaticFinish();};
bakeBoxRoom=function(){paperStaticOriginalBox();paperStaticFinish();};
bakeDepot=function(){paperStaticOriginalDepot();paperStaticFinish();};
ROOMS.boxroom.bake=bakeBoxRoom;ROOMS.depot.bake=bakeDepot;
allocView=function(){
  paperStaticOriginalAlloc();
  paperPropAlloc();
  // Boot bakes before allocating its first view. Capture once the view exists.
  if(paperEnabled&&paperStaticEnabled&&!paperStaticValid)ACTIVE_ROOM.bake();
};

function paperStaticAvailable(){
  return paperStaticEnabled&&paperStaticValid&&paperStaticWH===WH&&paperStaticOX===OX&&paperStaticOY===OY;
}
function paperResolveStatic(){
  if(!paperStaticAvailable())return false;
  const S=paperRenderScale(),W=LW*S,T=grayscale?GRAY32:RES32,half=NLVL*PAL_N;
  for(let y=0;y<LH;y++){
    const wy=y+viewY;if(wy<0||wy>=WH)continue;
    for(let x=0;x<LW;x++){
      const wx=x+viewX;if(wx<0||wx>=WW)continue;
      const i=y*LW+x,si=wy*WW+wx;
      if(sFlg[si]&FL_GLASS)continue;
      // Keep runtime lamp changes, but the paper surface has no checker vignette.
      const delta=(fIdx[i]&127)===(sIdx[si]&127)&&fDep[i]===sDep[si]?fLvl[i]-sLvl[si]+(paperVignette&&VIG[i]&&sLvl[si]>0?1:0):0;
      for(let sy=0;sy<S;sy++)for(let sx=0;sx<S;sx++){
        const p=(y*S+sy)*W+x*S+sx;
        const k=(wy*PAPER_SCALE+paperSampleOffset(sy,S))*PAPER_STATIC_W+wx*PAPER_SCALE+paperSampleOffset(sx,S);
        const source=(paperIndex[p]&127)===(sIdx[si]&127)&&paperDepth[p]===sDep[si];
        // A quarter-pixel silhouette may enter its original empty backdrop cell.
        // Both depth authorities must still be empty, so foreground draws survive.
        const edge=paperStaticBackdropEdges&&sDep[si]===DEPTH_EMPTY&&paperDepth[p]===DEPTH_EMPTY&&fDep[i]===DEPTH_EMPTY&&paperStaticDep[k]!==DEPTH_EMPTY;
        if(!source&&!edge)continue;
        const c=(paperStaticIdx[k]&127)|(paperIndex[p]&128);
        if(BD&&paperStaticDep[k]===DEPTH_EMPTY)continue;
        const l=clamp(paperStaticLvl[k]+delta,0,4);
        paperRGBA[p]=T[(c>>7)*half+l*PAL_N+(c&127)];
      }
    }
  }
  return true;
}

// Moving source fixtures keep their existing cached poses. Presentation cells
// are sampled once on room entry; the fan alone samples its small blade face.
let paperPropSources=true,paperPropCount=0,paperPropSlots=null;
const PAPER_PROP_CAPACITY=32,PAPER_PROP_CELL=16384;
function paperPropAlloc(){
  if(!paperEnabled||paperPropSlots)return;
  paperPropSlots=new Array(PAPER_PROP_CAPACITY);
  for(let n=0;n<PAPER_PROP_CAPACITY;n++)paperPropSlots[n]={
    underIdx:new Uint8Array(PAPER_PROP_CELL),underLvl:new Uint8Array(PAPER_PROP_CELL),underDep:new Int16Array(PAPER_PROP_CELL),
    source:new Uint8Array(PAPER_PROP_CELL),depth:new Int16Array(PAPER_PROP_CELL),mask:new Uint8Array(PAPER_PROP_CELL),a:new Array(8),state:{}
  };
  // One fan face can be active in a Box Room. Its native cell is setup storage.
  paperPropSlots[0].faceIdx=new Uint8Array(PAPER_PROP_CELL*16);
  paperPropSlots[0].faceLvl=new Uint8Array(PAPER_PROP_CELL*16);
  paperPropSlots[0].faceDep=new Int16Array(PAPER_PROP_CELL*16);
}
const paperPropOriginalBegin=paperBeginFrame;
paperBeginFrame=function(){paperPropOriginalBegin();if(!paperAtlasBuilding){paperPropCount=0;paperPropPointCount=0;}};
function paperPropFrameActive(){return paperPropSources&&paperStaticEnabled&&paperEnabled&&paperPropScope>0&&!paperStaticAtlasActive&&!paperAtlasBuilding&&!tBake&&tIdx===fIdx&&paperPropSlots!==null;}
function paperPropCapture(x0,y0,x1,y1){
  if(!paperPropSlots||paperPropCount===PAPER_PROP_CAPACITY)return null;
  x0=clamp(Math.floor(x0),0,LW);y0=clamp(Math.floor(y0),0,LH);x1=clamp(Math.ceil(x1),0,LW);y1=clamp(Math.ceil(y1),0,LH);
  const w=x1-x0,h=y1-y0;if(w<1||h<1||w*h>PAPER_PROP_CELL)return null;
  const s=paperPropSlots[paperPropCount++];s.x0=x0;s.y0=y0;s.w=w;s.h=h;s.idx=null;s.mask.fill(0,0,w*h);
  for(let y=0;y<h;y++)for(let x=0;x<w;x++){const k=y*w+x,i=(y+y0)*LW+x+x0;s.underIdx[k]=fIdx[i];s.underLvl[k]=fLvl[i];s.underDep[k]=fDep[i];}
  return s;
}
function paperPropFinish(s){
  for(let y=0;y<s.h;y++)for(let x=0;x<s.w;x++){
    const k=y*s.w+x,i=(y+s.y0)*LW+x+s.x0;
    if(fIdx[i]!==s.underIdx[k]||fDep[i]!==s.underDep[k]||fLvl[i]!==s.underLvl[k]){s.mask[k]=1;s.source[k]=fIdx[i];s.depth[k]=fDep[i];}
  }
}
function paperPropFrameFace(original,face,a0,a1,a2,a3,a4,sh,dk,olm){
  let x0,x1,y0,y1;
  if(face===F_TOP){x0=a0-a3+tOX;x1=a2-a1+tOX;y0=(a0+a1)*.5-a4+tOY;y1=(a2+a3)*.5-a4+tOY;}
  else{x0=(face===F_Y?a0-a4:a4-a1)+tOX;x1=(face===F_Y?a1-a4:a4-a0)+tOX;y0=(a0+a4)*.5-a3+tOY;y1=(a1+a4)*.5-a2+tOY;}
  const s=paperPropCapture(x0-1,y0-1,x1+2,y1+2);original(a0,a1,a2,a3,a4,sh,dk,olm);if(!s)return;
  paperPropFinish(s);
  if(!s.faceIdx)return; // Bounded fallback if another dressing face preceded it.
  s.idx=s.faceIdx;s.lvl=s.faceLvl;s.nativeW=s.w*PAPER_SCALE;s.tx=0;s.ty=0;
  const n=s.w*s.h*16;s.idx.fill(EMPTY,0,n);s.lvl.fill(2,0,n);s.faceDep.fill(DEPTH_EMPTY,0,n);
  s.a[0]=a0;s.a[1]=a1;s.a[2]=a2;s.a[3]=a3;s.a[4]=a4;s.a[5]=sh;s.a[6]=dk;s.a[7]=olm;
  const ox=tOX,oy=tOY,idx=paperStaticIdx,lvl=paperStaticLvl,dep=paperStaticDep,w=paperStaticWidth,h=paperStaticLogicalH,px=paperStaticPX,py=paperStaticPY;
  const saved=s.state;saved.shLvl=shLvl;saved.shFlat=shFlat;saved.shFlag=shFlag;saved.shBias=shBias;saved.shSunMax=shSunMax;saved.SUN_OBJ=SUN_OBJ;saved.shBoard=shBoard;saved.sunAdd=sunAdd;saved.aoFn=aoFn;saved.lampFn=lampFn;saved.rngState=rngState;saved.bayer=bayer;
  try{
    tOX-=s.x0;tOY-=s.y0;paperStaticPX=s.x0;paperStaticPY=s.y0;paperStaticIdx=s.idx;paperStaticLvl=s.lvl;paperStaticDep=s.faceDep;paperStaticWidth=s.nativeW;paperStaticLogicalH=s.h;bayer=paperStaticThreshold;
    paperStaticFace(face,s.a);
  }finally{tOX=ox;tOY=oy;paperStaticIdx=idx;paperStaticLvl=lvl;paperStaticDep=dep;paperStaticWidth=w;paperStaticLogicalH=h;paperStaticPX=px;paperStaticPY=py;paperStaticRestore(saved);}
}
function paperPropCachedTexture(){
  if(!paperStaticAtlasHasFaces())return null;
  let x0=tW,y0=tH,x1=-1,y1=-1;
  for(let i=0;i<tW*tH;i++)if(tIdx[i]!==EMPTY){const x=i%tW,y=Math.floor(i/tW);x0=Math.min(x0,x);y0=Math.min(y0,y);x1=Math.max(x1,x);y1=Math.max(y1,y);}
  if(x1<0)return null;const w=x1-x0+1,h=y1-y0+1,W=w*PAPER_SCALE,N=w*h*16,idx=new Uint8Array(N),lvl=new Uint8Array(N);
  for(let y=0;y<h*PAPER_SCALE;y++){const start=(y+y0*PAPER_SCALE)*paperStaticWidth+x0*PAPER_SCALE;idx.set(paperStaticIdx.subarray(start,start+W),y*W);lvl.set(paperStaticLvl.subarray(start,start+W),y*W);}
  return {x0,y0,w,h,idx,lvl,nativeW:W};
}
const paperPropOriginalCached=depotCachedCell,paperPropOriginalBlit=depotBlitCell;
depotCachedCell=function(draw){
  let source=null;
  const c=paperPropOriginalCached(()=>{
    if(!paperEnabled||!paperStaticEnabled||!paperPropSources)return draw();
    paperStaticAtlasBegin();paperPropScope++;
    try{draw();paperStaticAtlasFinish();source=paperPropCachedTexture();}finally{paperPropScope--;paperStaticAtlasEnd();}
  });
  if(source)c.paperSource=source;return c;
};
depotBlitCell=function(c){
  const r=paperPropSources&&paperStaticEnabled&&paperEnabled&&c.paperSource;
  const s=r?paperPropCapture(r.x0-viewX,r.y0-viewY,r.x0-viewX+r.w,r.y0-viewY+r.h):null;
  paperPropOriginalBlit(c);if(!s)return;
  s.idx=r.idx;s.lvl=r.lvl;s.nativeW=r.nativeW;s.tx=s.x0+viewX-r.x0;s.ty=s.y0+viewY-r.y0;paperPropFinish(s);
};
function paperPropUnder(n,s,k,x,y,sx,sy,T){
  let c=s.underIdx[k],l=s.underLvl[k],d=s.underDep[k];
  // Restore an earlier quarter-pixel fixture through a hole in a later one.
  for(let j=n-1;j>=0;j--){const q=paperPropSlots[j],qx=x-q.x0,qy=y-q.y0;if(qx<0||qy<0||qx>=q.w||qy>=q.h)continue;
    const a=qy*q.w+qx;if(!q.mask[a]||q.source[a]!==c||q.depth[a]!==d||!q.idx)continue;
    const p=((qy+q.ty)*PAPER_SCALE+sy)*q.nativeW+(qx+q.tx)*PAPER_SCALE+sx;
    if(q.idx[p]!==EMPTY)return T[(q.idx[p]>>7)*NLVL*PAL_N+q.lvl[p]*PAL_N+(q.idx[p]&127)];c=q.underIdx[a];l=q.underLvl[a];d=q.underDep[a];
  }
  const wx=x+viewX,wy=y+viewY,si=wy*WW+wx;
  if(paperStaticAvailable()&&wx>=0&&wx<WW&&wy>=0&&wy<WH&&c===sIdx[si]&&d===sDep[si]){
    const p=(wy*PAPER_SCALE+sy)*PAPER_STATIC_W+wx*PAPER_SCALE+sx;c=paperStaticIdx[p];l=clamp(paperStaticLvl[p]+l-sLvl[si],0,4);
  }
  return T[(c>>7)*NLVL*PAL_N+l*PAL_N+(c&127)];
}
function paperPropResolve(){
  if(!paperPropSources||!paperStaticEnabled||!paperEnabled||paperAtlasBuilding||!paperPropSlots)return;
  const S=paperRenderScale(),W=LW*S,T=grayscale?GRAY32:RES32;
  for(let n=0;n<paperPropCount;n++){const s=paperPropSlots[n];if(!s.idx)continue;
    for(let y=0;y<s.h;y++)for(let x=0;x<s.w;x++){
      const k=y*s.w+x;if(!s.mask[k])continue;const px=s.x0+x,py=s.y0+y;
      for(let sy=0;sy<S;sy++)for(let sx=0;sx<S;sx++){
        const p=(py*S+sy)*W+px*S+sx;if(paperIndex[p]!==s.source[k]||paperDepth[p]!==s.depth[k])continue;
        const X=paperSampleOffset(sx,S),Y=paperSampleOffset(sy,S),a=((y+s.ty)*PAPER_SCALE+Y)*s.nativeW+(x+s.tx)*PAPER_SCALE+X,c=s.idx[a];
        paperRGBA[p]=c===EMPTY?paperPropUnder(n,s,k,px,py,X,Y,T):T[(c>>7)*NLVL*PAL_N+s.lvl[a]*PAL_N+(c&127)];
      }
    }
  }
}
const paperPropOriginalResolve=paperResolveStatic;
paperResolveStatic=function(){const result=paperPropOriginalResolve();paperPropResolve();paperPropResolvePoints();return result;};

// Source points retain their exact bitmap topology and fades. A half-pixel
// material seam sits only on exposed right/bottom edges, including tiny marks.
const PAPER_PROP_POINTS=8192;
function paperPropPointStorage(){return {x:new Int16Array(PAPER_PROP_POINTS),y:new Int16Array(PAPER_PROP_POINTS),c:new Uint8Array(PAPER_PROP_POINTS),l:new Uint8Array(PAPER_PROP_POINTS),d:new Int16Array(PAPER_PROP_POINTS)};}
const paperPropPointData=paperPropPointStorage(),paperPropAtlasPointData=paperPropPointStorage();
let paperPropStaticPointData=paperPropPointStorage();
let paperPropPointCount=0,paperPropStaticPointCount=0;
function paperPropPointShadow(c){
  if(c===C.GL0)return C.BR3;if(c===C.GL1)return C.RD3;if(c===C.GL2)return C.BL3;
  return paperPropShadow(c);
}
function paperPropPointRecord(px,py){
  if(!paperPropSources||!paperStaticEnabled||!paperEnabled||px<0||py<0||px>=tW||py>=tH)return;
  const capture=paperStaticTarget();if(capture&&paperStaticPrimitive)return;
  if(!capture&&(paperAtlasBuilding||tIdx!==fIdx||tBake))return;
  const n=capture?paperPropStaticPointCount:paperPropPointCount;if(n===PAPER_PROP_POINTS)return;
  const q=capture?paperPropStaticPointData:paperPropPointData,i=py*tW+px;
  q.x[n]=px;q.y[n]=py;q.c[n]=tIdx[i];q.l[n]=tLvl[i];q.d[n]=tDep[i];
  if(capture){paperPropStaticPointCount++;if(paperStaticAtlasActive)paperStaticAtlasFaces++;}else paperPropPointCount++;
}
const paperPropOriginalDot=dot3,paperPropOriginalHeart=heartPixel,paperPropOriginalZ=drawZ;
dot3=function(x,y,z,c,l){
  const px=Math.round(x-y)+tOX,py=Math.round((x+y)*.5-z)+tOY,d=Math.round((x+y+z)*4)+shBias,i=py*tW+px;
  const owns=px>=0&&py>=0&&px<tW&&py<tH&&d>=tDep[i];paperPropOriginalDot(x,y,z,c,l);if(owns)paperPropPointRecord(px,py);
};
heartPixel=function(px,py,xyz,c){
  const owns=px>=0&&py>=0&&px<tW&&py<tH&&Math.round(xyz*4)+8>=tDep[py*tW+px];
  paperPropOriginalHeart(px,py,xyz,c);if(owns)paperPropPointRecord(px,py);
};
drawZ=function(x,y,z,big,fade){
  paperPropOriginalZ(x,y,z,big,fade);
  const g=big?Z_BIG:Z_SMALL,px0=Math.round(x-y)+tOX,py0=Math.round((x+y)*.5-z)+tOY,d=Math.round((x+y+z)*4)+8;
  for(let y=0;y<g.length;y++)for(let x=0;x<g[y].length;x++){
    if(g[y][x]!=='#')continue;const px=px0+x,py=py0+y;if(px<0||py<0||px>=tW||py>=tH)continue;
    if(fade>.55&&bayer(px+tPX,py+tPY)<(fade-.55)*4)continue;
    const i=py*tW+px;if(tIdx[i]===(C.WHITE|shBoard)&&tDep[i]===d)paperPropPointRecord(px,py);
  }
};
function paperPropStaticPoints(){
  const q=paperPropStaticPointData,W=paperStaticLogicalW,S=PAPER_SCALE;
  for(let n=0;n<paperPropStaticPointCount;n++){
    const x=q.x[n],y=q.y[n],i=y*W+x,c=q.c[n];if(paperStaticTargetIdx[i]!==c||paperStaticTargetDep[i]!==q.d[n])continue;
    const right=x===W-1||paperStaticTargetIdx[i+1]!==c,bottom=y===paperStaticLogicalH-1||paperStaticTargetIdx[i+W]!==c,edge=(c&128)|paperPropPointShadow(c&127);
    for(let sy=0;sy<S;sy++)for(let sx=0;sx<S;sx++){
      if(!(right&&sx>=2)&&!(bottom&&sy>=2))continue;const p=(y*S+sy)*paperStaticWidth+x*S+sx;
      if(paperStaticIdx[p]===c&&paperStaticDep[p]===q.d[n])paperStaticIdx[p]=edge;
    }
  }
  paperPropStaticPointCount=0;
}
function paperPropResolvePoints(){
  if(!paperPropSources||!paperStaticEnabled||!paperEnabled||paperAtlasBuilding)return;
  const q=paperPropPointData,S=paperRenderScale(),W=LW*S,T=grayscale?GRAY32:RES32;
  for(let n=0;n<paperPropPointCount;n++){
    const x=q.x[n],y=q.y[n],i=y*LW+x,c=q.c[n];if(fIdx[i]!==c||fDep[i]!==q.d[n])continue;
    const right=x===LW-1||fIdx[i+1]!==c,bottom=y===LH-1||fIdx[i+LW]!==c,edge=paperPropPointShadow(c&127);
    for(let sy=0;sy<S;sy++)for(let sx=0;sx<S;sx++){
      if(!(right&&paperSampleOffset(sx,S)>=2)&&!(bottom&&paperSampleOffset(sy,S)>=2))continue;
      const p=(y*S+sy)*W+x*S+sx;if(paperIndex[p]!==c||paperDepth[p]!==q.d[n])continue;
      paperRGBA[p]=T[(c>>7)*NLVL*PAL_N+fLvl[i]*PAL_N+edge];
    }
  }
}
// Source-driven office details at 4x. The original routines remain the only
// writers of logical colour/light/depth; these reusable captures refine paper.
let paperSceneryEnabled=true,paperSceneryCount=0,paperScenerySlots=null,paperSceneryWindowIdx=null;
let paperSceneryTreeCache=null,paperSceneryWindowDU=0,paperSceneryWindowDV=0;
const PAPER_SCENERY_CAPACITY=32,PAPER_SCENERY_CELL=4096;
function paperSceneryActive(){
  return paperSceneryEnabled&&paperEnabled&&!paperAtlasBuilding&&paperScenerySlots!==null&&tIdx===fIdx&&!tBake&&ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='office';
}
function paperSceneryAlloc(){
  if(!paperEnabled)return;
  if(!paperScenerySlots){
    paperScenerySlots=new Array(PAPER_SCENERY_CAPACITY);
    for(let n=0;n<PAPER_SCENERY_CAPACITY;n++)paperScenerySlots[n]={
      underIdx:new Uint8Array(PAPER_SCENERY_CELL),underLvl:new Uint8Array(PAPER_SCENERY_CELL),underDep:new Int16Array(PAPER_SCENERY_CELL),
      source:new Uint8Array(PAPER_SCENERY_CELL),depth:new Int16Array(PAPER_SCENERY_CELL),mask:new Uint8Array(PAPER_SCENERY_CELL),stripped:new Uint8Array(PAPER_SCENERY_CELL)
    };
  }
  if(!paperSceneryWindowIdx||paperSceneryWindowIdx.length!==LW*LH)paperSceneryWindowIdx=new Uint8Array(LW*LH).fill(EMPTY);
  if(!paperSceneryTreeCache){
    const S=PAPER_SCALE,W=OVW*S,H=OVH*S;paperSceneryTreeCache=new Uint8Array(W*H);
    for(let y=0;y<H;y++)for(let x=0;x<W;x++){
      const c=paperSceneryTreeSourceAt((x+.5)/S-.5,(y+.5)/S-.5);
      paperSceneryTreeCache[y*W+x]=c<0?EMPTY:c;
    }
  }
}
const paperSceneryOriginalAlloc=paperAllocView,paperSceneryOriginalBegin=paperBeginFrame;
paperAllocView=function(){paperSceneryOriginalAlloc();paperSceneryAlloc();};
paperBeginFrame=function(){
  paperSceneryOriginalBegin();if(paperAtlasBuilding)return;paperSceneryCount=0;
  if(paperSceneryWindowIdx)paperSceneryWindowIdx.fill(EMPTY);
};
function paperSceneryCapture(x0,y0,x1,y1){
  if(paperSceneryCount===PAPER_SCENERY_CAPACITY)return null;
  x0=clamp(x0,0,LW);y0=clamp(y0,0,LH);x1=clamp(x1,0,LW);y1=clamp(y1,0,LH);
  const w=x1-x0,h=y1-y0;if(w<1||h<1||w*h>PAPER_SCENERY_CELL)return null;
  const s=paperScenerySlots[paperSceneryCount++];s.x0=x0;s.y0=y0;s.w=w;s.h=h;s.board=shBoard;s.sun=sunFn;s.lamp=lampFn;s.sunMax=shSunMax;s.sunOn=tSun;
  s.mask.fill(0,0,w*h);s.stripped.fill(0,0,w*h);
  for(let y=0;y<h;y++)for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
    const k=y*w+x,i=(y+y0)*LW+x+x0;
    s.underIdx[k]=fIdx[i];s.underLvl[k]=fLvl[i];s.underDep[k]=fDep[i];
  }
  return s;
}
function paperSceneryFinish(s){
  if(!s)return;
  for(let y=0;y<s.h;y++)for(let x=0;x<s.w;x++){
    const k=y*s.w+x,i=(s.y0+y)*LW+s.x0+x;
    s.source[k]=fIdx[i];s.depth[k]=fDep[i];
    s.mask[k]=fIdx[i]!==s.underIdx[k]||fLvl[i]!==s.underLvl[k]||fDep[i]!==s.underDep[k]?1:0;
  }
}
const paperSceneryOriginalBlob=blob,paperSceneryOriginalTop=faceTop;
blob=function(x,y,z,rx,ry,dark){
  let s=null;
  if(paperSceneryActive()&&blobShade===0){
    const cx=Math.round(x-y)+tOX,cy=Math.round((x+y)*.5-z)+tOY,ex=Math.ceil(rx+1.3),ey=Math.ceil(ry+1.3);
    s=paperSceneryCapture(cx-ex,cy-ey,cx+ex+1,cy+ey+1);
    if(s){s.kind=0;s.cx=cx;s.cy=cy;s.fx=x;s.fy=y;s.fz=z;s.rx=rx;s.ry=ry;}
  }
  paperSceneryOriginalBlob(x,y,z,rx,ry,dark);paperSceneryFinish(s);
};
faceTop=function(x0,y0,x1,y1,z,sh,dk,olm){
  let s=null;
  if(paperSceneryActive()&&(sh===shRollers||sh===shFlatPackOff||sh===shFlatBox)){
    s=paperSceneryCapture(Math.floor(x0-y1)+tOX-1,Math.floor((x0+y0)*.5-z)+tOY-1,
                          Math.ceil(x1-y0)+tOX+2,Math.ceil((x1+y1)*.5-z)+tOY+2);
    if(s){
      s.kind=sh===shRollers?1:sh===shFlatPackOff?2:3;s.fx=x0;s.fy=y0;s.x1=x1;s.y1=y1;s.fz=z;s.ox=tOX;s.oy=tOY;
      s.roll=WS.conveyor.roll;s.pw=pW;s.pd=pD;s.off=shOffU;s.dk=dk;s.olm=olm;
    }
  }
  paperSceneryOriginalTop(x0,y0,x1,y1,z,sh,dk,olm);paperSceneryFinish(s);
};

// The same canopy clumps, trunk and light direction as treeAt. A fixed rim
// threshold and smaller clump shade variation replace its pixel checker.
function paperSceneryTreeSourceAt(uu,v){
  if(uu>=56&&uu<61&&v>72)return C.WD1;
  let best=-1,bk=0;
  for(let k=0;k<TREE_BLOBS.length;k++){
    const b=TREE_BLOBS[k],dx=(uu-b[0])/b[2],dy=(v-b[1])/b[3],d=1-Math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy);
    if(d>best){best=d;bk=k;}
  }
  if(best<.095)return -1;
  const b=TREE_BLOBS[bk],lx=(uu-b[0])/b[2],ly=(v-b[1])/b[3];
  const lite=-lx*.3-ly*.8+(hash2(uu>>1,v>>1)-.5)*.15;
  return lite>.55?C.PT3:lite>.05?C.PT2:lite>-.45?C.PT1:C.PT0;
}
function paperSceneryTreeAt(u,v){
  const uu=u-WS.treeDX,S=PAPER_SCALE,qx=(uu+.5)*S-.5,qy=(v+.5)*S-.5,x=Math.round(qx),y=Math.round(qy);
  // Runtime tree sway and parallax are integral source pixels. Fractional review
  // coordinates still use the exact source shader, rather than a snapped guess.
  if(!paperSceneryTreeCache||Math.abs(qx-x)>1e-9||Math.abs(qy-y)>1e-9)return paperSceneryTreeSourceAt(uu,v);
  if(x<0||y<0||x>=OVW*S||y>=OVH*S)return uu>=56&&uu<61&&v>72?C.WD1:-1;
  const c=paperSceneryTreeCache[y*OVW*S+x];return c===EMPTY?-1:c;
}
function paperSceneryWindowColour(wx,wy,si){
  const u=wx-paperSceneryWindowDU-OV_U0,v=wy-paperSceneryWindowDV-OV_V0,iu=Math.floor(u+.5),iv=Math.floor(v+.5);
  let c=iu>=0&&iv>=0&&iu<OVW&&iv<OVH?OUTV[iv*OVW+iu]:C.SKY0;
  const tc=paperSceneryTreeAt(u,v);if(tc>=0)c=tc;
  if(WS.pigeon.on){const pc=pigeonAt(Math.floor(wx+.5)-OV_U0,Math.floor(wy+.5)-OV_V0);if(pc>=0)c=pc;}
  const g=((wx+wy)%23+23)%23;
  if((g<1||(g>=2&&g<3))&&(sWZ[si]>>2)>46)c=c===C.WHITE?C.SKY1:C.WHITE;
  return c;
}
const paperSceneryOriginalWindows=paintWindows;
paintWindows=function(){
  paperSceneryOriginalWindows();
  if(!paperSceneryActive())return;
  paperSceneryWindowDU=Math.round(.7*(viewX-viewX0));paperSceneryWindowDV=Math.round(.7*(viewY-viewY0));
  for(let y=Math.max(0,GLASS_T-viewY);y<=Math.min(LH-1,GLASS_B-viewY);y++)for(let x=Math.max(0,GLASS_L-viewX);x<=Math.min(LW-1,GLASS_R-viewX);x++){
    const i=y*LW+x,si=(y+viewY)*WW+x+viewX;
    if((sFlg[si]&FL_GLASS)&&fDep[i]===sDep[si])paperSceneryWindowIdx[i]=fIdx[i];
  }
};
function paperSceneryUnder(s,k,px,py,sx,sy,T){
  const S=paperRenderScale(),N=PAPER_SCALE,nx=paperSampleOffset(sx,S),ny=paperSampleOffset(sy,S);
  const wx=px+viewX,wy=py+viewY,si=wy*WW+wx,half=NLVL*PAL_N;
  let c=s.underIdx[k],l=s.underLvl[k];
  if(wx>=0&&wy>=0&&wx<WW&&wy<WH&&s.underDep[k]===sDep[si]){
    if(sFlg[si]&FL_GLASS){c=paperSceneryWindowColour(wx+(nx+.5)/N-.5,wy+(ny+.5)/N-.5,si);l=2;}
    else if(paperStaticAvailable()&&c===sIdx[si]){
      const p=(wy*N+ny)*PAPER_STATIC_W+wx*N+nx;
      c=paperStaticIdx[p];l=clamp(paperStaticLvl[p]+l-sLvl[si],0,4);
    }
  }
  return T[(c>>7)*half+l*PAL_N+(c&127)];
}
function paperSceneryResolve(){
  if(!paperSceneryEnabled||!paperEnabled||paperAtlasBuilding||!paperScenerySlots||ACTIVE_ROOM.id!=='office')return;
  const S=paperRenderScale(),N=PAPER_SCALE,W=LW*S,T=grayscale?GRAY32:RES32,half=NLVL*PAL_N;
  // Refine only glass still owned by the original window draw.
  for(let y=Math.max(0,GLASS_T-viewY);y<=Math.min(LH-1,GLASS_B-viewY);y++)for(let x=Math.max(0,GLASS_L-viewX);x<=Math.min(LW-1,GLASS_R-viewX);x++){
    const i=y*LW+x,c=paperSceneryWindowIdx[i];if(c===EMPTY)continue;
    const wx=x+viewX,wy=y+viewY,si=wy*WW+wx;
    for(let sy=0;sy<S;sy++)for(let sx=0;sx<S;sx++){
      const p=(y*S+sy)*W+x*S+sx;
      if(paperDepth[p]===sDep[si]&&paperIndex[p]===c)paperRGBA[p]=T[2*PAL_N+paperSceneryWindowColour(wx+(paperSampleOffset(sx,S)+.5)/N-.5,wy+(paperSampleOffset(sy,S)+.5)/N-.5,si)];
    }
  }
  // Strip latest first, then replay in the original painter order. A later
  // foreground colour or depth never matches this capture's source authority.
  for(let n=paperSceneryCount-1;n>=0;n--){
    const s=paperScenerySlots[n];s.stripped.fill(0,0,s.w*s.h);
    for(let y=0;y<s.h;y++)for(let x=0;x<s.w;x++){
      const k=y*s.w+x;if(!s.mask[k])continue;const px=s.x0+x,py=s.y0+y;
      for(let sy=0;sy<S;sy++)for(let sx=0;sx<S;sx++){
        const p=(py*S+sy)*W+px*S+sx;
        if(paperDepth[p]!==s.depth[k]||paperIndex[p]!==s.source[k])continue;
        s.stripped[k]=1;paperDepth[p]=s.underDep[k];paperIndex[p]=s.underIdx[k];paperRGBA[p]=paperSceneryUnder(s,k,px,py,sx,sy,T);
      }
    }
  }
  for(let n=0;n<paperSceneryCount;n++){
    const s=paperScenerySlots[n];
    for(let y=0;y<s.h;y++)for(let x=0;x<s.w;x++){
      const k=y*s.w+x;if(!s.mask[k]||!s.stripped[k])continue;const px=s.x0+x,py=s.y0+y;
      for(let sy=0;sy<S;sy++)for(let sx=0;sx<S;sx++){
        const p=(py*S+sy)*W+px*S+sx;if(s.depth[k]<paperDepth[p])continue;
        const X=px+(paperSampleOffset(sx,S)+.5)/N-.5,Y=py+(paperSampleOffset(sy,S)+.5)/N-.5;
        let c,l=2,wx,wy,wz=s.fz;
        if(s.kind===0){
          const dx=X-s.cx,dy=Y-s.cy,e=dx*dx/((s.rx+.5)*(s.rx+.5))+dy*dy/((s.ry+.5)*(s.ry+.5));
          const eo=dx*dx/((s.rx+1.3)*(s.rx+1.3))+dy*dy/((s.ry+1.3)*(s.ry+1.3));if(eo>1)continue;
          c=e>1?C.PT1:dx<0?C.PT2:C.PT1;wx=s.fx+dx*.5;wy=s.fy-dx*.5;wz-=dy;
          if(s.sun(wx,wy,wz,F_TOP)>.5)l++;
        }else{
          const A=X+.5-s.ox,B=Y-s.oy+s.fz;wx=B+A*.5;wy=B-A*.5;
          if(wx<s.fx||wx>=s.x1||wy<s.fy||wy>=s.y1)continue;
          const u=wx-s.fx,v=wy-s.fy;
          if(s.kind===1){const band=((u-s.roll)%4+4)%4;c=band<1?C.PL0:band<2?C.FT1:band<3?C.FT2:C.FT1;}
          else if(s.kind===2){
            const pu=u+s.off;
            c=Math.abs(pu-s.pw*.5)<.5||Math.abs(v-s.pd*.5)<.5?C.CREAM:pu<.8||v<.8?C.KRAFT2:C.KRAFT;
          }else c=u<.8||v<.8?C.KRAFT2:C.KRAFT;
          if((s.olm&1)&&(u<1||v<1)){c=s.dk;l--;}
          if(s.sunOn&&s.sunMax>0)l+=Math.min(s.sunMax,Math.floor(s.sun(wx,wy,wz,F_TOP)*s.sunMax+.4995));
        }
        if(s.lamp)l+=s.lamp(wx,wy,wz,px+viewX,py+viewY);
        c|=s.board;paperDepth[p]=s.depth[k];paperIndex[p]=c;paperRGBA[p]=T[(c>>7)*half+clamp(l,0,4)*PAL_N+(c&127)];
      }
    }
  }
}
const paperSceneryOriginalResolve=paperResolveStatic;
paperResolveStatic=function(){const result=paperSceneryOriginalResolve();paperSceneryResolve();return result;};
// Carriage paper layers and independent window apertures. Live floor coordinates never sway.
let TRR=null,TRLIFE=null,TR_WINDOWS=null,TR_POSES=null,TR_PM=null,TR_LIGHTS=null;
let trainWall='Y',trainRackColour=0,trainAtlasWindowCutouts=false;
let trainFixturesEnabled=true;
function trainApplyFixture(kind,x,y,z){
 const t=TRAIN_FIXTURE_TEXTURES[kind];if(!trainFixturesEnabled||!t||!paperStaticTarget())return;
 const sx=(Math.round(x-y)+tOX)*4-t.anchor[0],sy=(Math.round((x+y)/2-z)+tOY)*4-t.anchor[1];
 for(let y0=0;y0<t.h;y0++)for(let x0=0;x0<t.w;x0++){
  const px=sx+x0,py=sy+y0;if(px<0||py<0||px>=paperStaticWidth||py>=paperStaticLogicalH*4)continue;
  const k=y0*t.w+x0,i=py*paperStaticWidth+px,c=t.pixels[k];
  if(c!==255&&t.depth[k]===paperStaticDep[i])paperStaticIdx[i]=c;
 }
}
function trainMaterialColour(c,u,v,kind){
 if(!paperStaticCapturing||paperStaticPrimitive)return c;
 const a=TRAIN_MATERIALS[kind],x=((Math.floor(u*4)%64)+64)%64,y=((Math.floor(v*4)%64)+64)%64,t=a[y*64+x];
 return TRAIN_MATERIAL_TINTS[kind][c*3+t];
}
function trainSun(){return 0;}
function trainBakeLight(x,y,z){
 if(shBoard||z<-.1||z>.5)return 0;
 if(x<2||y<2)return 1;
 for(const f of TRR.furniture)for(const b of f.parts)if(x>b[0]-1.5&&x<b[3]+1.5&&y>b[1]-1.5&&y<b[4]+1.5)return 1;
 return 0;
}
function trainAllocView(){
 if(!BD)return;
 const n=LW*LH;BD.frame={idx:new Uint8Array(n),lvl:new Uint8Array(n),opening:new Uint8Array(n)};
 if(!BD.cache||BD.cache.height<LH+128)BD.cache=bakeBackdropLayers(BD.scene,BD.set,LH+128);
}
function trainPrepareActors(){
 const w=WORLD.create(SCN),pools={sort:[],tea:[],watch:[],walk1:[],walk2:[]};
 for(let f=0;f<2880;f+=6){
  WORLD.step(w,f);const p=w.pose,k=w.sorter.action;
  if(k===WORLD.K.TAKE&&p.item===ITEM_NONE)pools.sort.push({...p});
  if(k===WORLD.K.TEA&&p.item===ITEM_MUG)pools.tea.push({...p});
  if(k===WORLD.K.IDLE&&p.face===FACE_S)pools.watch.push({...p});
  if(k===WORLD.K.WALK&&p.item===ITEM_NONE&&(p.face===FACE_E||p.face===FACE_W))pools['walk'+p.face].push({...p});
 }
 TR_POSES=pools;TR_PM=[0,2,1].map(head=>({state:CS_SIT,frame:0,face:0,head,flick:0}));
}
function trainPrepareLights(){
 if(TR_LIGHTS)return;TR_LIGHTS=[];
 const N=NLVL*PAL_N,sky={},warm=new Set(),red=new Set(),blue=new Set(),paint=new Set();sky[C.DU1]=C.NI0;sky[C.SS0]=C.NI1;sky[C.SS1]=C.NI2;
 for(const name of ['WD0','WD1','WD2','WD3','WD4','KRAFT0','KRAFT','KRAFT2','CREAM','PAPSH','BR0','BR1','BR2','BR3','SK0','SK1','SK2','RD0','RD1','RD2','RD3','RD4','BL0','BL1','BL2','BL3','BL4'])warm.add(C[name]);
 for(const name of ['RD0','RD1','RD2','RD3','RD4'])red.add(C[name]);for(const name of ['BL0','BL1','BL2','BL3','BL4'])blue.add(C[name]);
 for(const name of ['PT0','PT1','PT2','PT3','PC15','PC30'])paint.add(C[name]);
 for(let i=0;i<32;i++)warm.add(C['PC'+String(i).padStart(2,'0')]);
 for(let k=0;k<32;k++){
  const table=new Uint8Array(N);
  for(let i=0;i<N;i++){
   const id=i%PAL_N,level=Math.floor(i/PAL_N),indoor=warm.has(id)||paint.has(id),from=indoor?LUT[i]:LIGHT_LUTS.sun[i],to=red.has(id)||blue.has(id)||paint.has(id)?from:indoor?LUT[Math.max(0,level-1)*PAL_N+id]:sky[id]===undefined?LIGHT_LUTS.night[i]:sky[id];
   const a=PALRGB[from],b=PALRGB[to],t=k/31;
   if(paint.has(id)){table[i]=from;continue;}
   let best=0,score=Infinity;
   for(let c=0;c<NPAL;c++){const p=PALRGB[c];if(red.has(id)?!red.has(c):blue.has(id)?!blue.has(c):indoor&&(p[0]<p[1]||p[1]<p[2]))continue;const r=p[0]-a[0]*(1-t)-b[0]*t,g=p[1]-a[1]*(1-t)-b[1]*t,bl=p[2]-a[2]*(1-t)-b[2]*t,d=r*r*2+g*g*3+bl*bl;if(d<score){score=d;best=c;}}
   table[i]=best;
  }
  const stage=resolveTables(table,BOARD_LUTS.fri);stage.roomLut=table;TR_LIGHTS.push(stage);
 }
}
function activateTrain(room){
 for(const kind of ['mailbag','hanging-lamp','tea-urn','desk-lamp','parcel-stack'])if(!TRAIN_PROP_SOURCES[kind])throw new Error('Missing accepted Train prop: '+kind);
 BOXR=null;TRR=room;TRLIFE=TRAIN.create(room);trainPrepareActors();trainPrepareLights();trainAllocView();trainStep(0);
}
function trainStep(frame){
 if(!TRLIFE)return;TRAIN.step(TRLIFE,frame);WS.frame=TRLIFE.frame;
 const l=TR_LIGHTS[TRLIFE.light];RES32=l.res;GRAY32=l.gray;
}
function shTrainWood(face,u,v){
 shFlag=FL_ROOM;shFlat=false;
 const line=bdMod(v+Math.floor(u/40)*3,14),edge=.24+Math.sin(u*.11)*.09;
 const c=line<edge?C.PC14:face===F_X?C.PC19:Math.sin(u*.035+v*.017)>.3?C.PC20:C.PC25;
 return trainMaterialColour(c,u,v,'teak');
}
function shTrainFloor(face,u,v){
 shFlag=FL_ROOM|FL_FLOOR;shFlat=false;
 const row=Math.floor(v/12),join=bdMod(u+(row&1)*28,56);
 const c=bdMod(v,12)<.18||join<.18?C.PC14:(row&1)?C.PC19:C.PC20;
 return trainMaterialColour(c,u,v,'kraft');
}
function shTrainMetal(face,u,v){shFlat=false;shFlag=FL_ROOM;return trainMaterialColour(face===F_TOP?C.PL1:C.PL0,u,v,'slate');}
function shTrainCream(face,u,v){shFlat=false;shFlag=FL_ROOM;return trainMaterialColour(face===F_X?C.PAPSH:C.CREAM,u,v,'cream');}
function shTrainCushion(face,u,v){
 shFlat=false;shFlag=FL_ROOM;
 const seam=face===F_TOP&&(u<.7||v<.7||u>22.4||v>26.4);
 return trainMaterialColour(seam?C.BL2:face===F_X?C.BL0:C.BL1,u,v,'navy');
}
function shTrainBrass(){return C.BR3;}
function shTrainFascia(){shFlat=false;shFlag=FL_ROOM;return C.RD1;}
function shTrainWheel(face,u,v){
 const x=u-10,y=v-10,r=Math.hypot(x,y);if(r>10)return -1;
 shFlag=FL_ROOM;shFlat=false;
 if(r<1.4)return C.BR1;if(r<3)return C.PL1;if(r>9.3)return C.PL0;if(r>8.3)return C.PL2;
 const spoke=Math.min(Math.abs(x),Math.abs(y),Math.abs(x-y)*.7,Math.abs(x+y)*.7);
 return trainMaterialColour(spoke<.8?C.PL1:C.NI0,u,v,'slate');
}
function trainWheel(x,y,z){faceY(x-10,x+10,z-10,z+10,y,shTrainWheel,C.PL0,0);}
function shTrainRack(face,u,v){shFlag=FL_ROOM;shFlat=false;return trainMaterialColour(face===F_TOP?LITC[trainRackColour]:trainRackColour,u,v,trainRackColour===C.RD2?'vermilion':'navy');}
function shTrainWall(face,u,z){
 shFlag=FL_ROOM;shFlat=false;
 for(let i=0;i<TRR.windows.length;i++){
  const w=TRR.windows[i];if(w.wall!==trainWall||u<w.u0||u>=w.u1||z<w.z0||z>=w.z1)continue;
  if(u<w.u0+1.5||u>=w.u1-1.5||z<w.z0+1.5||z>=w.z1-1.5||Math.abs(u-(w.u0+w.u1)/2)<.65)return C.WD4;
  shFlag=FL_ROOM|FL_GLASS;shFlat=true;return trainAtlasWindowCutouts?-1:C.DU1;
 }
 if(z<3)return C.PC14;
 if(z>65)return C.CREAM;
 if(z>29&&z<31)return C.BR2;
 if(z<29)return trainMaterialColour(bdMod(u,32)<.55?C.PC14:C.PC19,u,z,'teak');
 if(bdMod(u,32)<.4)return C.BL1;
 const fibre=TRAIN_MATERIALS.slate[((Math.floor(z*4)&63)*64)+(Math.floor(u*4)&63)];
 return paperStaticCapturing&&!paperStaticPrimitive&&fibre===0?C.PL0:C.BL2;
}
function shTrainBow(face,u,z){
 const h=68+14*Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-Math.pow((u-68)/68,2)));
 if(z+66>h||z+66<h-2)return -1;
 shFlat=false;shFlag=FL_ROOM;return C.WD4;
}
function trainCubbies(x,coat){
 const c=coat==='ginger'?C.RD2:C.BL1;
 trainRackColour=c;
 shFlag=FL_ROOM;box(x,3,2,x+64,5,28,()=>SH[c],C.WD0,0);
 for(let i=0;i<=8;i++)box(x+i*8,3,2,x+i*8+.7,10,29,shTrainRack,C.WD1,0);
 for(let j=0;j<=3;j++)box(x,3,2+j*9,x+64,10,3+j*9,shTrainRack,C.WD1,0);
 box(x-1,2,1,x+.5,12,30,shTrainWood,C.WD1,0);box(x+63.5,2,1,x+65,12,30,shTrainWood,C.WD1,0);
 box(x-1,2,0,x+65,12,2,shTrainWood,C.WD1,0);
 box(x-1,2,28,x+65,12,30,shTrainWood,C.WD1,0);
 for(let i=0;i<8;i++)for(let j=0;j<3;j++){
  const a=x+1.3+i*8,z=4+j*9,k=(i*7+j*3+(coat==='blue'?1:0))%5;
  if(k===4)continue;
  if(k<2){
   box(a,6,z,a+5.5,9.5,z+1.2,shTrainCream,C.PAPSH,0);
   if(k===0)box(a+.4,6,z+1.4,a+5.2,9,z+2.3,shTrainCream,C.PAPSH,0);
  }else{
   box(a,7,z,a+5.3,8,z+4.5,shTrainCream,C.PAPSH,0);
   dpTri([a,8.1,z+4.3],[a+2.65,8.1,z+2],[a+5.3,8.1,z+4.3],C.PAPSH,F_Y);
  }
 }
 trainApplyFixture(coat==='ginger'?'red-cubbies':'blue-cubbies',x+32,7,0);
}
function buildTrainProp(kind,frame=1){sprClear();const a=TRAIN_PROP_SOURCES[kind];if(a)SPR.set(a[Math.min(a.length-1,frame)]);}

function trainWindowFrames(wall){
 for(const w of TRR.windows){
  if(wall&&w.wall!==wall)continue;
  if(w.wall==='Y'){
   for(const z of [w.z0+1,w.z1-1.5])box(w.u0+.5,0,z,w.u1-.5,.5,z+.5,shTrainMetal,C.PL0,0);
   for(const u of [w.u0+.5,w.u1-1.5])box(u,0,w.z0+1,u+1,.5,w.z1-1,shTrainMetal,C.PL0,0);
   box(w.u0-1,0,w.z0-1,w.u1+1,1.5,w.z0+.5,shTrainWood,C.WD1,0);box(w.u0-1,0,w.z1-.5,w.u1+1,1.5,w.z1+1,shTrainWood,C.WD1,0);
   box(w.u0-1,0,w.z0,w.u0+.5,1.5,w.z1,shTrainWood,C.WD1,0);box(w.u1-.5,0,w.z0,w.u1+1,1.5,w.z1,shTrainWood,C.WD1,0);
   box(w.u0+1.5,0,w.z0+1.3,w.u1-1.5,.2,w.z0+1.7,shTrainCream,C.PAPSH,0);
   const u=(w.u0+w.u1)/2;box(u-.5,.2,w.z0+2,u+.5,1,w.z0+3.5,shTrainBrass,C.BR0,0);
  }else{
   for(const z of [w.z0+1,w.z1-1.5])box(0,w.u0+.5,z,.5,w.u1-.5,z+.5,shTrainMetal,C.PL0,0);
   for(const u of [w.u0+.5,w.u1-1.5])box(0,u,w.z0+1,.5,u+1,w.z1-1,shTrainMetal,C.PL0,0);
   box(0,w.u0-1,w.z0-1,1.5,w.u1+1,w.z0+.5,shTrainWood,C.WD1,0);box(0,w.u0-1,w.z1-.5,1.5,w.u1+1,w.z1+1,shTrainWood,C.WD1,0);
   box(0,w.u0-1,w.z0,1.5,w.u0+.5,w.z1,shTrainWood,C.WD1,0);box(0,w.u1-.5,w.z0,1.5,w.u1+1,w.z1,shTrainWood,C.WD1,0);
   box(0,w.u0+1.5,w.z0+1.3,.2,w.u1-1.5,w.z0+1.7,shTrainCream,C.PAPSH,0);
   const u=(w.u0+w.u1)/2;box(.2,u-.5,w.z0+2,1,u+.5,w.z0+3.5,shTrainBrass,C.BR0,0);
  }
 }
}

 for(const y of [18,138]){
  box(-22,y-2,-33,278,y+2,-32,shTrainMetal,C.PL0,0);box(-22,y-.7,-32,278,y+.7,-31,shTrainMetal,C.PL0,0);
 }
}

}
function bakeTrain(){
 targetStatic();bakeBackdrop();shBoard=0;shBias=0;shFlat=false;shSunMax=0;aoFn=trainBakeLight;lampFn=null;
 const B=TRR.board;trainTrack();trainUnderframe();trainDeck();
 shBoard=128;faceTop(B.x,B.y,B.x+B.w*16,B.y+B.h*16,0,shStone,C.FT0,0);shBoard=0;
 box(B.x-2,B.y-2,0,B.x+B.w*16+2,B.y,.75,shKerbTop,C.WD1,0);box(B.x-2,B.y,0,B.x,B.y+B.h*16+2,.75,shKerbTop,C.WD1,0);
 box(B.x,B.y+B.h*16,0,B.x+B.w*16+2,B.y+B.h*16+2,.75,shKerbTop,C.WD1,0);box(B.x+B.w*16,B.y,0,B.x+B.w*16+2,B.y+B.h*16,.75,shKerbTop,C.WD1,0);
 trainLongWall();trainEndWall();trainRoofBow();trainRoofRear();
 trainProps();trainPersonalDetails();
 const x=[],y=[],u=[],v=[];for(let i=0;i<WW*WH;i++)if(sFlg[i]&FL_GLASS){x.push(i%WW);y.push((i/WW)|0);u.push((sWX[i]+sWY[i])*.375);v.push(BD.scene.horizon+35-(sWZ[i]*.25-34)*3);}
 TR_WINDOWS={x:Int16Array.from(x),y:Int16Array.from(y),u:Float32Array.from(u),v:Float32Array.from(v)};
 aoFn=null;shFlag=0;shFlat=false;shBoard=0;shSunMax=2;paperStaticFinish();
}
function trainComposeBackdrop(){
 const cache=BD.cache,f=BD.frame;f.opening.fill(0);
 const cx=viewX+LW/2-VIEW_CX,t=TRLIFE.frame/60;
 for(let k=0;k<TR_WINDOWS.x.length;k++){
  const x=TR_WINDOWS.x[k]-viewX,y=TR_WINDOWS.y[k]-viewY;if(x<0||y<0||x>=LW||y>=LH)continue;
  const i=y*LW+x,value=sampleBackdrop(cache,TR_WINDOWS.u[k],TR_WINDOWS.v[k],t,cx*.25,0);let c=value&127;
  if(c===C.RD2&&TRLIFE.signal)c=C.PT2;
  if(c===C.GL0&&TRLIFE.light<6)c=C.WD1;
  fIdx[i]=c;fLvl[i]=value>>8;f.idx[i]=c;f.lvl[i]=fLvl[i];f.opening[i]=1;
 }
}
function trainActors(){
 const s=TRLIFE.sorter,list=TR_POSES[s.action==='walk'?'walk'+s.face:s.action],p=list[Math.floor((cpActive()&&CP.train&&CP.mode==='exit'?CP.frame:TRLIFE.frame)/6)%list.length];contactShadow(s.x,s.y,3,4,.45);Object.assign(pose,p);buildSorter();blitSprite(s.x,s.y,0,p.face===FACE_E||p.face===FACE_N,true);
 const a=TR_PM[TRLIFE.pm.head+1];Object.assign(cat,a);cat.x=TRLIFE.pm.x;cat.y=TRLIFE.pm.y;cat.z=TRLIFE.pm.z;drawCat();
 if(cpActive()&&CP.train&&CP.mode==='exit')tcSatchel();
 if(s.action==='watch')trainWatch(s.x-3,s.y+2,10,s.pose);
}
function drawTrain(){
 nItems=0;const src=gBoardSource();if(src===G_SRC)gPrepareSrc();addBoardItems(src);sortItems();
 for(let k=0;k<nItems;k++){const id=ITEM_ID[k];if(drawItemHook)drawItemHook(id,false);shBoard=128;drawBoardItem(id-IT_BOARD0);if(drawItemHook)drawItemHook(id,true);}
 shBoard=0;shSunMax=0;
 if(drawItemHook)drawItemHook(40,false);
 for(let i=0;i<TRR.bags.length;i++){const p=TRR.bags[i];buildTrainProp('mailbag',TRLIFE.bags[i]+1);blitSprite(p.x,p.y,p.z,false,true);}
 for(let i=0;i<TRR.lamps.length;i++){
  const p=TRR.lamps[i],s=TRLIFE.lamps[i];
  buildTrainProp('hanging-lamp',s+1);blitSprite(p.x,p.y,p.z,false,true);
 }
 trainActors();if(drawItemHook)drawItemHook(40,true);shBoard=0;shSunMax=2;drawParticles(0,PMAX);
}
