function trainAtlasCatalog(){
 return [
  ['long-wall',[128,0,0],trainLongWall,512],['end-wall',[0,68,0],trainEndWall,256],
  ['roof-rear',[128,7,74],trainRoofRear,512],['roof-bow',[-2,68,76],trainRoofBow,256],
  ['deck',[128,68,0],trainDeck,512],['underframe',[128,78,-18],trainUnderframe,512],
  ['wheel',[56,138,-22],()=>trainWheel(56,138,-22),128],['track-base',[128,68,-36],trainTrack,512],
  ['red-cubbies',[80,7,0],()=>trainCubbies(48,'ginger'),128],['blue-cubbies',[158,7,0],()=>trainCubbies(126,'blue'),128],
  ['tea-shelf',[22,17,0],trainTeaShelf,128],['desk',[236,28,0],trainDesk,128],['window-seat',[24,88,0],trainSeat,128],
  ['hook',[34,12,46],()=>trainBagHook(34),128],['lamp-bracket',[140,20,74],()=>trainLampBracket(140),128]
 ];
}
function trainAtlasSprites(){
 const r=TRAIN.build(),sprites=[];
 function frame(tag,origin,draw){return {tag,draw:()=>{
  const room=TRR,wall=trainWall,colour=trainRackColour,cutouts=trainAtlasWindowCutouts;
  TRR=r;trainAtlasWindowCutouts=true;tOX=aOriginX-(origin[0]-origin[1]);tOY=aOriginY-Math.round((origin[0]+origin[1])/2-origin[2]);
  try{draw();}finally{TRR=room;trainWall=wall;trainRackColour=colour;trainAtlasWindowCutouts=cutouts;}
 }};}
 for(const [id,origin,draw,cellSize]of trainAtlasCatalog())sprites.push({name:'train_'+id,origin,cellSize,direction:'none',mirroredFor:'none',fps:0,loop:false,frames:[frame('rest',origin,draw)]});
 for(const d of trainDetailCatalog())sprites.push({name:'train_'+d.id,origin:d.origin,cellSize:128,direction:'none',mirroredFor:'none',fps:0,loop:false,frames:[frame('rest',d.origin,d.draw)]});
 for(const [kind,source]of Object.entries(TRAIN_PROP_SOURCES)){
  const tags=source.length===3?['left','rest','right']:['rest'];
  sprites.push({name:'train_'+kind,origin:[0,0,0],direction:'none',mirroredFor:'none',fps:0,loop:false,frames:tags.map((tag,k)=>frame(tag,[0,0,0],()=>{buildTrainProp(kind,k);atlasBlit(0,0,0,false);} ))});
 }
 sprites.push({name:'train_pocket-watch',origin:[0,0,0],direction:'none',mirroredFor:'none',fps:0,loop:false,frames:[0,1,2,3].map(k=>frame('check'+k,[0,0,0],()=>trainWatch(0,0,0,k)))});
 return sprites;
}
function trainAtlasAssemblies(){
 const r=TRAIN.build(),layers=trainAtlasCatalog().filter(p=>p[0]!=='wheel'&&p[0]!=='hook'&&p[0]!=='lamp-bracket').map(p=>({family:'train_'+p[0],origin:p[1]}));
 for(const d of trainDetailCatalog())layers.push({family:'train_'+d.id,origin:d.origin});
 for(const p of r.bags)layers.push({family:'train_hook',origin:[p.x,12,46]},{family:'train_mailbag',origin:[p.x,p.y,p.z],pose:'bagSway'});
 for(const p of r.lamps)layers.push({family:'train_lamp-bracket',origin:[p.x,20,74]},{family:'train_hanging-lamp',origin:[p.x,p.y,p.z],pose:'lampSway'});
 layers.push({family:'train_tea-urn',origin:[20,16,14]},{family:'train_desk-lamp',origin:[232,22,15]},{family:'train_parcel-stack',origin:[238,35,15]});
 trainPrepareLights();
 return {room:'train',worldUnitsPerTile:16,loopFrames:2880,board:r.board,windows:r.windows,layers,
  sway:{bagPeriodFrames:240,lampPeriodFrames:320,poseOrder:['left','rest','right'],nativeFrameRate:60,actorPoseFrameRate:10,watchHoldFrames:60},
  light:{boardSet:'fri',stageCurve:'round((1-cos(frame/2880*2*pi))*15.5)',stageLuts:TR_LIGHTS.map(s=>Array.from(s.roomLut)),boardLut:Array.from(BOARD_LUTS.fri)},
  actorReuse:{sorter:'office_sorter',postmistress:'accepted tuxedo Postmistress states; head left/front/right'},
  notes:['Windows are transparent in exported wall panels; landscape is composed independently through the recorded apertures.','Poses are runtime controlled by TRAIN.step. Nothing moves the live board. Coupling uses prepared Chapter views; the live floor remains continuous.']};
}
// Original generated paper faces mounted on native planes. No billboard drift.
let trainDetailKind='',trainDetailW=1,trainDetailH=1;
function shTrainDetail(face,u,v){
 shFlat=false;shFlag=FL_ROOM;
 const t=TRAIN_DETAIL_TEXTURES[trainDetailKind],U=face===F_X?1-u/trainDetailW:u/trainDetailW,V=face===F_TOP?v/trainDetailH:1-v/trainDetailH;
 const x=Math.max(0,Math.min(t.w-1,Math.floor(U*t.w))),y=Math.max(0,Math.min(t.h-1,Math.floor(V*t.h))),c=t.pixels[y*t.w+x];
 return c===255?-1:c;
}
function trainDetailPanel(kind,x,y,z,w,h,face){
 trainDetailKind=kind;trainDetailW=w;trainDetailH=h;
 if(face===F_X)faceX(y,y+w,z,z+h,x,shTrainDetail,C.WD0,0);
 else if(face===F_Y)faceY(x,x+w,z,z+h,y,shTrainDetail,C.WD0,0);
 else faceTop(x,y,x+w,y+h,z,shTrainDetail,C.WD0,0);
}

 return band?C.BR1:face===F_TOP?C.PT1:C.PT0;
}

// The same finished point function supplies runtime and the native sprite atlas.
function pointsAtlasSprites(){
 const sprites=[];
 for(let a=0;a<4;a++)for(let b=0;b<4;b++)if(a!==b){
  const pair='RDLU'[a]+'RDLU'[b],frames=[];
  const add=(tag,state,frame)=>frames.push({tag,poses:[[a,b,state,frame].join(',')],draw:()=>{
   tOX=aOriginX;tOY=aOriginY-8;drawPoints(0,0,a,b,{state,from:1-state,frame});
  }});
  for(const current of [0,1]){
   add('exit_'+pair[current],current,2);
   for(const frame of [0,1])add('to_'+pair[1-current]+'_swing'+(frame+1),1-current,frame);
  }
  sprites.push({name:'points_'+pair,origin:[8,8,0],direction:'none',mirroredFor:'none',fps:10,loop:false,cellSize:128,light:'board',frames});
 }
 return sprites;
}
function pointsAtlasContract(){
 return {worldUnitsPerTile:16,planeZ:0,drawBiasQuarterUnits:1,logicalSelector:'shared gates[a,b,state]',initialState:0,framesPerSwitch:3,poseRate:10,
  framePoseKey:'a,b,state,frame; directions R,D,L,U=0,1,2,3; frame0/1 intermediate,2 rest',
  directionCue:'Both arrows fixed to their exits; selected arrow larger and cream, inactive dark; centre turns without obscuring arrows.',
  familyRule:'Train uses points for every ordered pair; other rooms use points for opposite pairs and retain accepted perpendicular gates.'};
}

 box(-9,-1,-1,-gap,1,2,shTrainMetal,C.PL0,0);box(gap,-1,-1,9,1,2,shTrainMetal,C.PL0,0);
 box(-gap-1,-3,-2,-gap,3,4,shTrainMetal,C.PL0,0);box(gap,-3,-2,gap+1,3,4,shTrainMetal,C.PL0,0);
 for(const x of[-11,11]){box(x-.5,-4,3,x+.5,4,3.5,shTrainBrass,C.BR0,0);}
 if(frame>=2){dpRod(-3,3,-1,0,4,-4,C.BR1);dpRod(0,4,-4,3,3,-1,C.BR1);}

}

function trainChapterAtlasSprites(){
 function sprite(name,tags,draw){return {name:'train_station-'+name,origin:[0,0,0],cellSize:128,direction:'none',mirroredFor:'none',fps:0,loop:false,frames:tags.map((tag,k)=>({tag,draw:()=>{tOX=aOriginX;tOY=aOriginY;shBoard=0;draw(k);}}))};}

// Prepared snapshots are used only in modal establishing shots. The live board
// keeps its native sprite/depth path. All images are allocated on the button event.
function tcArtCanvases(){
 if(TC.art)return;TC.art=[];
 for(const r of TRAIN_CHAPTER_ART.frames){
  const colours=[];const bytes=atob(r.pixels);
  for(const gray of[false,true]){const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=r.w*4;canvas.height=r.h*4;
   const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d'),im=ctx.createImageData(canvas.width,canvas.height),a=new Uint32Array(im.data.buffer);
   for(let i=0;i<a.length;i++){const c=bytes.charCodeAt(i);if(c!==255)a[i]=gray?GRAY32[2*PAL_N+c]:PAL32[c];}
   ctx.putImageData(im,0,0);colours.push(canvas);
  }TC.art.push({record:r,colour:colours[0],gray:colours[1]});
 }
}
function tcPrepareStation(){
 tcArtCanvases();
 if(!TC.stageSrc){TC.stageSrc=makeBoardSrc(0);TC.stageSrc.ox=TRAIN_DATA.board.x;TC.stageSrc.oy=TRAIN_DATA.board.y;TC.stageSrc.w=10;TC.stageSrc.h=4;}
 const wasGray=grayscale,camX=WS.camX,camY=WS.camY;grayscale=false;TC.preparing=true;WS.camX=WS.camY=0;
 try{renderFrame();}finally{grayscale=wasGray;TC.preparing=false;WS.camX=camX;WS.camY=camY;}
 const S=paperOutputScale(),source=paperEnabled&&paperReady?paperImage:img,b=TRR.bounds;
 const x0=b.sx0-8,y0=b.sy0-8,w=b.sx1-b.sx0+16,h=b.sy1-b.sy0+16;
 const W=w*S,H=h*S,canvas=document.createElement('canvas'),grey=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=grey.width=W;canvas.height=grey.height=H;
 const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d'),gc=grey.getContext('2d'),im=ctx.createImageData(W,H),gi=gc.createImageData(W,H);
 const sx=(x0+OX-viewX)*S,sy=(y0+OY-viewY)*S;TC.captureClipped=sx<0||sy<0||sx+W>source.width||sy+H>source.height;
 for(let y=0;y<H;y++)for(let x=0;x<W;x++){
  const xx=x+sx,yy=y+sy;if(xx<0||yy<0||xx>=source.width||yy>=source.height)continue;
  const logical=Math.floor(yy/S)*LW+Math.floor(xx/S);if((fIdx[logical]&127)===C.BG0)continue;
  const i=(y*W+x)*4,j=(yy*source.width+xx)*4;im.data.set(source.data.subarray(j,j+4),i);
  const v=Math.round(source.data[j]*.299+source.data[j+1]*.587+source.data[j+2]*.114);gi.data[i]=gi.data[i+1]=gi.data[i+2]=v;gi.data[i+3]=source.data[j+3];
 }
 ctx.putImageData(im,0,0);gc.putImageData(gi,0,0);TC.carriage=canvas;TC.carriageGrey=grey;TC.cropX=x0;TC.cropY=y0;TC.width=w;TC.height=h;
 // One mutable fit result, sampled without arrays/objects in the animation loop.
 TC.layout={left:0,top:0,right:0,bottom:0,scale:1,x:0,y:0};
}
function tcLayout(){
 const o=TC.layout,m=TRAINCHAPTER.station(TC.stage,CP.frame,TC.motion),two=TC.stage>1;
 const side=devW>devH&&window.innerHeight<=520,cardW=side?Math.ceil(CP.card.offsetWidth*devDpr)+24*devDpr:0,cardH=side?0:Math.ceil(CP.card.offsetHeight*devDpr)+24*devDpr;
 const width=TC.width+(two?272+(TC.stage===2?96:0):0),height=TC.height+(two?136+(TC.stage===2?48:0):0);
 const k=Math.min((devW-cardW-32*devDpr)/width,(devH-cardH-32*devDpr)/height);
 o.scale=Math.max(.05,k);o.x=cardW+16*devDpr+(devW-cardW-32*devDpr-width*o.scale)/2;o.y=16*devDpr+(devH-cardH-32*devDpr-height*o.scale)/2;
 o.left=o.x;o.top=o.y;o.right=o.x+width*o.scale;o.bottom=o.y+height*o.scale;return o;
}
function tcPresent(){
 const b=tcLayout(),m=TC.motion,k=b.scale,canvas=grayscale?TC.carriageGrey:TC.carriage;
 sctx.fillStyle=grayscale?BG_GREY_CSS:BG_CSS;sctx.fillRect(0,0,devW,devH);sctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
 sctx.drawImage(canvas,b.x,b.y,TC.width*k,TC.height*k);
 if(TC.stage>1){const d=272+m.approach+m.bump;sctx.drawImage(canvas,b.x+d*k,b.y+d*.5*k,TC.width*k,TC.height*k);}
 tcDrawArt(4,48,156,-36,b);tcDrawArt(5,14,148,-36,b);
 if(TC.stage>1){
  // Show the foreground buffers below the deck. The centre drawbar is hidden
  // by the nearer carriage; painting it last would cover its cat and seat.
  const x=TRR.growth.coupling[0],y=TRR.room.d+4,z=TRR.growth.coupling[2];
  if(TC.stage===2&&m.phase<2){
   tcDrawArt(0,x,y,z,b,-1);tcDrawArt(0,x+m.approach,y,z,b,1);
  }else tcDrawArt(m.joint,x,y,z,b);
 }
}
function tcDrawArt(index,x,y,z,b,half=0){
 const a=TC.art[index],r=a.record,px=x-y-TC.cropX,py=(x+y)/2-z-TC.cropY;
 const cut=Math.max(0,Math.min(r.w,-r.dx)),left=half>0?cut:0,w=half<0?cut:half>0?r.w-cut:r.w;
 sctx.drawImage(grayscale?a.gray:a.colour,left*4,0,w*4,r.h*4,b.x+(px+r.dx+left)*b.scale,b.y+(py+r.dy)*b.scale,w*b.scale,r.h*b.scale);
}

function tcSatchel(){
 const s=TC.satchel;buildTrainProp('mailbag',1);blitSprite(s.x,s.y,s.z,false,true);
}
// City Hall: native paper planes, separately functioning board, reused accepted actors.
let CITYR=null,CITYLIFE=null,CITY_POOLS=null,CITY_DRIVER=null,CITY_PM=null;
let cityFaceKind='',cityFaceW=1,cityFaceH=1,cityWallAxis=0,cityColour=0,cityWindowW=40,cityWindowH=35;
function cityMaterial(c,u,v,kind='cream'){
 if(!paperStaticCapturing||paperStaticPrimitive)return c;
 const key=kind==='teak'||kind==='kraft'?'wood':kind==='slate'?'iron':kind==='ochre'?'brass':null;
 if(!key)return c;
 const t=CITYHALL_MATERIALS[key],x=bdMod(Math.floor(u*3),t.w),y=bdMod(Math.floor(v*3),t.h);
 return t.tints[c*3+t.tones[y*t.w+x]];
}
function shCityWood(face,u,v){shFlag=FL_ROOM;shFlat=false;return cityMaterial(face===F_X?C.WD2:face===F_TOP?C.WD4:C.WD3,u,v,'teak');}
function shCityIron(face,u,v){shFlag=FL_ROOM;shFlat=false;return cityMaterial(face===F_X?C.PT0:face===F_TOP?C.PT2:C.PT1,u,v,'slate');}
function shCityCream(face,u,v){shFlag=FL_ROOM;shFlat=false;return cityMaterial(face===F_X?C.PAPSH:C.CREAM,u,v,'cream');}
function shCityBrass(face,u,v){shFlag=FL_ROOM;shFlat=false;return cityMaterial(face===F_X?C.BR0:face===F_TOP?C.BR3:C.BR2,u,v,'ochre');}
function shCityFloor(face,u,v){
 shFlag=FL_ROOM|FL_FLOOR;shFlat=false;
 const row=Math.floor(v/12),join=bdMod(u+(row&1)*26,52);return cityMaterial(bdMod(v,12)<.18||join<.18?C.WD1:(row&1)?C.PC19:C.PC20,u,v,'kraft');
}
function shCityMarble(face,u,v){
 shFlag=FL_ROOM|FL_FLOOR;shFlat=false;const x=bdMod(u,16),y=bdMod(v,16),tile=Math.floor(u/16)+Math.floor(v/16)*3;
 if(x<.23||y<.23)return C.PAPSH;
 const t=CITYHALL_FACES['material-marble'],p=t.pixels[bdMod(Math.floor((v+tile*7)*3),t.h)*t.w+bdMod(Math.floor((u+tile*3)*3),t.w)];
 return p===255?C.CREAM:p;
}
function shCityFace(face,u,v){
 shFlag=FL_ROOM;shFlat=false;const t=CITYHALL_FACES[cityFaceKind];if(!t)return C.CREAM;
 const U=face===F_X?1-u/cityFaceW:u/cityFaceW,V=face===F_TOP?v/cityFaceH:1-v/cityFaceH;
 const x=clamp(Math.floor(U*t.w),0,t.w-1),y=clamp(Math.floor(V*t.h),0,t.h-1),c=t.pixels[y*t.w+x];return c===255?-1:c;
}
function cityFace(kind,x,y,z,w,h,face){cityFaceKind=kind;cityFaceW=w;cityFaceH=h;
 if(face===F_X)faceX(y,y+w,z,z+h,x,shCityFace,C.WD0,0);else if(face===F_Y)faceY(x,x+w,z,z+h,y,shCityFace,C.WD0,0);else faceTop(x,y,x+w,y+h,z,shCityFace,C.WD0,0);
}
function citySun(){return 0;}
function cityHallPrepareActors(){
 if(CITY_POOLS)return;
 const s=DEPOTWORLD.create(DEPOT.build()),pose=f=>{DEPOTWORLD.step(s,f);return {...s.driver};};
 // These original source hashes all have approved paper textures. Never mix task snapshots into a gait.
 CITY_POOLS={walk:[pose(90),pose(108),pose(90),pose(96)],read:[pose(396)],check:[pose(0)],sort:[pose(714)],wave:[pose(294)]};
 CITY_DRIVER={...s.driver};CITY_PM={};
}
function activateCityHall(r){
 BOXR=null;CITYR=r;CITYLIFE=CITYHALL.create(r);cityHallPrepareActors();
 const l=resolveTables(LIGHT_LUTS[r.light.finalScenerySet||'fri'],BOARD_LUTS[r.light.boardSet]);RES32=l.res;GRAY32=l.gray;cityHallStep(0);
}
function cityHallStep(f){if(CITYLIFE){CITYHALL.step(CITYLIFE,f);WS.frame=CITYLIFE.frame;}}
function cityHallBase(){box(-6,-6,-9,CITYR.room.w+6,CITYR.room.d+6,-1,shCityWood,C.WD1,0);box(-5,-5,-1,CITYR.room.w+5,CITYR.room.d+5,0,shCityBrass,C.BR0,0);}
function cityHallDeck(){faceTop(0,0,CITYR.room.w,CITYR.room.d,.05,shCityFloor,C.WD1,0);}
function cityHallBoard(){
 const b=CITYR.board;shBoard=128;faceTop(b.x,b.y,b.x+b.w*16,b.y+b.h*16,.1,shCityMarble,C.PAPSH,0);shBoard=0;
 box(b.x-1.7,b.y-1.7,0,b.x+b.w*16+1.7,b.y,.45,shCityBrass,C.BR0,0);box(b.x-1.7,b.y,0,b.x,b.y+b.h*16+1.7,.45,shCityBrass,C.BR0,0);
 box(b.x,b.y+b.h*16,0,b.x+b.w*16+1.7,b.y+b.h*16+1.7,.45,shCityBrass,C.BR0,0);box(b.x+b.w*16,b.y,0,b.x+b.w*16+1.7,b.y+b.h*16,.45,shCityBrass,C.BR0,0);
}
function shCityWall(face,u,z){
 shFlag=FL_ROOM;shFlat=false;
 if(z<3)return C.WD1;if(z<39)return cityMaterial(bdMod(u,28)<.5?C.WD1:face===F_X?C.WD2:C.WD3,u,z,'teak');
 if(z<42)return C.BR2;return cityMaterial(z>108?C.BG1:C.CREAM,u,z,'cream');
}
function shCityWindow(face,u,v){
 shFlag=FL_ROOM;shFlat=false;const mid=cityWindowW/2,top=cityWindowH-14+14*Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-Math.pow((u-mid)/mid,2)));
 if(v>top)return -1;
 if(u<1.5||u>cityWindowW-1.5||v<1.5||v>top-1.5||Math.abs(u-mid)<.5||Math.abs(v-22)<.45||Math.abs(v-33)<.45)return C.CREAM;
 if(v<9)return C.BL3;
 if(Math.abs(u-v*.2-7)<.9||Math.abs(u-v*.2-19)<.45)return C.SKY1;
 return v>24?C.SKY0:C.BL4;
}
function cityHallWall(axis){
 if(axis===F_Y)faceY(0,CITYR.room.w,0,112,0,shCityWall,C.WD1,0);else faceX(0,CITYR.room.d,0,112,0,shCityWall,C.WD1,0);
 for(const w of CITYR.windows){if((axis===F_Y?'Y':'X')!==w.wall)continue;cityWindowW=w.u1-w.u0;cityWindowH=w.z1-w.z0;

  else{faceX(w.u0,w.u1,w.z0,w.z1,9,shCityWindow,C.PAPSH,0);box(1,w.u0-1,w.z0-2,10,w.u1+1,w.z0,shCityCream,C.PAPSH,0);}
 }
 // Warm suspended card lamps and framed postal notices sit above the apron.

 }
}
function cityHallColumns(){

 }
}
function shCityRosette(face,u,v){const x=u-3,y=v-3,r=x*x+y*y;shFlag=FL_ROOM;shFlat=false;if(r>8)return -1;return r>5?C.BR1:Math.abs(x)+Math.abs(y)<1.5?C.CREAM:C.BR3;}
function shCitySpandrel(face,u,v){
 shFlag=FL_ROOM;shFlat=false;const top=14,dx=Math.min(u,56-u),curve=14-14*Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-Math.pow((dx-28)/28,2)));
 if(v<top-curve-.7)return -1;return cityMaterial(v>12?C.PT0:C.PT1,u,v,'slate');
}

}

// Eight matte-card facets and quiet collars; drawn only while baking static scenery.
function cityHallTubeSegment(x0,y0,z0,x1,y1,z1,r,glass=false){
 const dx=x1-x0,dy=y1-y0,dz=z1-z0,n=Math.hypot(dx,dy,dz);if(n<.001)return;
 const ux=dx/n,uy=dy/n,uz=dz/n,side=Math.hypot(ux,uy),vx=side?-uy/side:1,vy=side?ux/side:0,vz=0,wx=uy*vz-uz*vy,wy=uz*vx-ux*vz,wz=ux*vy-uy*vx;
 for(let k=0;k<8;k++){
  if(glass&&(k===0||k===7||k===3||k===4))continue;
  const a=k*Math.PI/4,b=(k+1)*Math.PI/4,ca=Math.cos(a)*r,sa=Math.sin(a)*r,cb=Math.cos(b)*r,sb=Math.sin(b)*r;
  const p=[x0+vx*ca+wx*sa,y0+vy*ca+wy*sa,z0+vz*ca+wz*sa],q=[x1+vx*ca+wx*sa,y1+vy*ca+wy*sa,z1+vz*ca+wz*sa],v=[x1+vx*cb+wx*sb,y1+vy*cb+wy*sb,z1+vz*cb+wz*sb],t=[x0+vx*cb+wx*sb,y0+vy*cb+wy*sb,z0+vz*cb+wz*sb];
  dpQuad(p,q,v,t,glass?(k<4?C.SKY0:C.BL3):(k===1||k===2?C.BR2:k===5||k===6?C.CD1:C.CD0),F_Y);
 }
}
function cityHallPipe(x0,y0,z0,x1,y1,z1,r=2){
 cityHallTubeSegment(x0,y0,z0,x1,y1,z1,r);
 const n=Math.hypot(x1-x0,y1-y0,z1-z0);for(let t=12;t<n-3;t+=24){const v=t/n,d=.65/n;cityHallTubeSegment(x0+(x1-x0)*(v-d),y0+(y1-y0)*(v-d),z0+(z1-z0)*(v-d),x0+(x1-x0)*(v+d),y0+(y1-y0)*(v+d),z0+(z1-z0)*(v+d),r+.35);}
}
function cityHallPipes(){
 for(const p of CITYR.pipes){const a=p.points[0],b=p.points[1],c=p.points[2],r=p.radius-.4,bend=4;
  cityHallPipe(...a,b[0],b[1],b[2]-bend,r);
  const alongX=c[0]!==b[0],vx=alongX?1:0,vy=alongX?0:1;
  let px=b[0],py=b[1],pz=b[2]-bend;
  for(let k=1;k<=8;k++){const t=k/8*Math.PI/2,x=b[0]+vx*bend*(1-Math.cos(t)),y=b[1]+vy*bend*(1-Math.cos(t)),z=b[2]-bend+bend*Math.sin(t);cityHallTubeSegment(px,py,pz,x,y,z,r);px=x;py=y;pz=z;}
  if(p.inspection){const [lo,hi]=p.inspection;
   cityHallPipe(px,py,pz,alongX?lo:px,alongX?py:lo,pz,r);
   cityHallTubeSegment(alongX?lo:px,alongX?py:lo,pz,alongX?hi:px,alongX?py:hi,pz,r,true);
   cityHallPipe(alongX?hi:px,alongX?py:hi,pz,...c,r);
   for(const v of[lo,hi])cityHallTubeSegment(alongX?v-.7:px,alongX?py:v-.7,pz,alongX?v+.7:px,alongX?py:v+.7,pz,r+.6);
  }else cityHallPipe(px,py,pz,...c,r);
 }
 // Two forward-facing station hoods beneath the working line.
 for(const x of[110,140]){cityHallPipe(x,24,35,x,33,35,3);cityHallTubeSegment(x,32,35,x,38,32,4.2);cityHallMouth(x,38.5,32,4.2);}
 cityHallPipe(24,32,36,26,32,36,3);cityHallTubeSegment(26,32,36,26,38,34,4.2);cityHallMouth(26,38.5,34,4.2);
}
function shCityMouth(face,u,v){const r=cityMouthR,x=u-r,z=v-r,d=x*x+z*z;shFlag=FL_ROOM;shFlat=false;if(d>r*r)return -1;return d>(r-.8)*(r-.8)?C.BR3:d>(r-1.3)*(r-1.3)?C.BR0:C.PC02;}
let cityMouthR=4;
function cityHallMouth(x,y,z,r){cityMouthR=r;faceY(x-r,x+r,z-r,z+r,y,shCityMouth,C.BR0,0);}

 for(let row=0;row<=3;row++)box(x,y,z+3+row*(h-3)/3,x+w,y+12,z+4+row*(h-3)/3,shCityBank,C.WD0,0);
 box(x-1,y-1,z+h,x+w+1,y+14,z+h+2,shCityWood,C.WD1,0);
 for(let col=0;col<6;col++)for(let row=0;row<3;row++){if((col+row)%4===3)continue;const a=x+1.2+col*w/6,b=z+4+row*(h-3)/3;

  box(a,y+5,b,a+w/6-2,y+10,b+1.3,shCityCream,C.PAPSH,0);if((col+row)%3===0)box(a+.3,y+5,b+1.6,a+w/6-2.4,y+9.5,b+2.6,shCityCream,C.PAPSH,0);
 }
}

function cityHallPalm(f){
 const x=f.anchor[0],y=f.anchor[1];cylinder(x,y,7,0,12,shCityIron,C.PT0,0);cylinder(x,y,7.6,11,14,shCityBrass,C.BR0,0);disc(x,y,6.4,14,()=>C.WD0,C.WD0,false);cityHallPipe(x,y,13,x,y,40,1.1);
 for(let leaf=0;leaf<8;leaf++){
  const a=leaf/8*Math.PI*2,c=Math.cos(a),s=Math.sin(a),len=leaf%2?21:25;
  for(let k=0;k<9;k++){
   const t=k/9,t1=(k+1)/9,d=t*len,e=t1*len,z=39+Math.sin(t*Math.PI)*14-t*t*4,z1=39+Math.sin(t1*Math.PI)*14-t1*t1*4;
   const w=Math.sin(t*Math.PI)*3.4+.35,w1=Math.sin(t1*Math.PI)*3.4+.2,px=x+c*d,py=y+s*d,qx=x+c*e,qy=y+s*e;
   dpTri([px-s*w,py+c*w,z],[qx-s*w1,qy+c*w1,z1],[qx+s*w1,qy-c*w1,z1],leaf%2?C.PT1:C.PT2,F_TOP);
   dpTri([px-s*w,py+c*w,z],[qx+s*w1,qy-c*w1,z1],[px+s*w,py-c*w,z],leaf%2?C.PT2:C.PT3,F_TOP);
  }
 }
 cityFace('crest',x-4.2,y+7.2,3,8.4,7,F_Y);
}

function cityHallFurniture(f){
 if(f.kind==='sorting-table')cityHallTable(f);
 else if(f.kind==='tube-bank'){cityHallBank(156,5,0,52,15,C.BL1);cityHallBank(156,5,16,52,13,C.RD2);}
 else if(f.kind==='supervisor-desk')cityHallDesk(f);
 else if(f.kind==='trolley')cityHallTrolley(f);
 else if(f.kind==='palm')cityHallPalm(f);
 else if(f.kind==='bench')cityHallBench(f);
 else if(f.kind==='stairs')cityHallStairs();
 else if(f.kind==='clock'){cityFace('clock',116,22,66,32,32,F_Y);cityFace('crest',126,22,99,12,7,F_Y);}
 else if(f.kind==='map'){box(21,114,28,23.8,158,52,shCityWood,C.WD1,0);cityFace('map',24,116,30,40,20,F_X);}
 else if(f.kind==='station'){box(8,22,0,40,48,6,shCityWood,C.WD1,0);box(10,24,6,38,44,30,shCityWood,C.WD1,0);box(8,22,30,40,48,32,shCityWood,C.WD1,0);cityFace('drawer',11,44.2,9,26,14,F_Y);cityFace('crest',40.2,26,10,16,12,F_X);}
}
function cityHallStaticCatalog(){
 const a=[{id:'base',origin:[112,96,-1],size:512,draw:cityHallBase},{id:'deck',origin:[112,96,0],size:512,draw:cityHallDeck},{id:'marble-floor',origin:[128,112,0],size:256,draw:cityHallBoard},
 {id:'wall-y',origin:[112,0,56],size:512,draw:()=>cityHallWall(F_Y)},{id:'wall-x',origin:[0,96,56],size:512,draw:()=>cityHallWall(F_X)},
 {id:'columns',origin:[112,96,52],size:512,draw:cityHallColumns},{id:'gallery-y',origin:[112,10,55],size:512,draw:()=>cityHallGallery(F_Y)},{id:'gallery-x',origin:[10,96,55],size:512,draw:()=>cityHallGallery(F_X)},

}
function bakeCityHall(){
 targetStatic();bakeBackdrop();shBoard=0;shBias=0;shFlat=false;shSunMax=0;aoFn=null;lampFn=null;
 paperPropScope++;try{for(const p of cityHallStaticCatalog())p.draw();}finally{paperPropScope--;}shBoard=0;shBias=0;shFlat=false;shSunMax=2;shFlag=0;paperStaticFinish();

function cityHallHands(pose){
 const x=132,y=22.3,z=82,a=Math.PI/2-pose/12*Math.PI*2;dpRod(x,y,z,x+Math.cos(a)*11,y,z+Math.sin(a)*11,C.BL0);dpRod(x,y+.2,z,x-7,y+.2,z+4,C.BL0);dot3(x,y+.4,z,C.BR2,0);
}
function shCityGauge(face,u,v){const x=u-4,y=v-4,r=x*x+y*y;shFlag=FL_ROOM;shFlat=false;if(r>16)return -1;return r>12?C.BR2:C.CREAM;}
function cityHallGauge(x,y,z,pose){faceY(x-4,x+4,z-4,z+4,y,shCityGauge,C.BR0,0);const a=2.4-pose*.85;dpRod(x,y+.2,z,x+Math.cos(a)*2.7,y+.2,z+Math.sin(a)*2.7,C.BL0);}
let cityPuffPose=0;
function shCityPuff(face,u,v){
 shFlag=FL_ROOM;shFlat=false;const q=cityPuffPose,x=u-7,z=v-q*1.4,r=1.2+q*.5;
 const a=(x+.7*q)*(x+.7*q)/(r*r)+(z-3-q)*(z-3-q)/(r*r*1.4),b=(x-1.2-q*.5)*(x-1.2-q*.5)/(r*r*.7)+(z-5-q*1.2)*(z-5-q*1.2)/(r*r),c=(x+2+q*.7)*(x+2+q*.7)/(r*r*.5)+(z-7-q)*(z-7-q)/(r*r*.8);
 return a<1||b<1||c<1?(z<4?C.CR1:C.CREAM):-1;
}
function cityHallPuff(p){if(!p.visible)return;cityPuffPose=p.pose;faceY(p.x-7,p.x+7,p.z,p.z+17,p.y,shCityPuff,C.PAPSH,0);}
function cityHallCapsule(c){
 if(!c.visible)return;const x=c.x,y=c.y,z=c.z;
 if(c.vertical){box(x-1.4,y-3,z-1.4,x+1.4,y+3,z+1.4,shCityWood,C.WD1,0);box(x-1.5,y-.4,z-1.5,x+1.5,y+.4,z+1.5,shCityCream,C.PAPSH,0);}
 else{box(x-3,y-1.4,z-1.4,x+3,y+1.4,z+1.4,shCityWood,C.WD1,0);box(x-.4,y-1.5,z-1.5,x+.4,y+1.5,z+1.5,shCityCream,C.PAPSH,0);}
}
function cityHallActors(){
 for(const a of CITYLIFE.workers){const list=CITY_POOLS[a.action],p=list[a.frame];Object.assign(CITY_DRIVER,p);CITY_DRIVER.face=a.face;buildDriver(CITY_DRIVER);blitSprite(a.x,a.y,a.z,CITYHALL.staffMirror(a.face),true);}
 const pm=CITYLIFE.pm;
 if(pm.bat>=0){cityHallBuildBat(pm.bat);blitSprite(pm.x,pm.y,pm.z,false,true);}else{
  cat.x=pm.x;cat.y=pm.y;cat.z=pm.z;cat.state=pm.state;cat.frame=pm.frame;cat.face=pm.face;cat.head=pm.head;cat.flick=pm.flick;drawCat();
 }
 const b=CITYLIFE.pigeon;buildDepotPigeon(b);blitSprite(b.x,b.y,b.z,b.face===1,true);
}
function cityHallBuildBat(pose){KEY=KEY_S;SPR.set(CITYHALL_FIDELITY_SOURCES[pose]);}
function drawCityHall(){
 nItems=0;const src=gBoardSource();if(src===G_SRC)gPrepareSrc();addBoardItems(src);sortItems();for(let k=0;k<nItems;k++){const id=ITEM_ID[k];if(drawItemHook)drawItemHook(id,false);shBoard=128;drawBoardItem(id-IT_BOARD0);if(drawItemHook)drawItemHook(id,true);}
 shBoard=0;shSunMax=0;if(drawItemHook)drawItemHook(40,false);cityHallActors();if(cpActive()&&CP.hall)drawHallChapter();paperPropScope++;
 try{cityHallHands(CITYLIFE.clock);for(let i=0;i<3;i++){cityHallGauge(i===0?24:80+i*30,i===0?44.4:30, i===0?24:29,CITYLIFE.pressure[i]);cityHallPuff(CITYLIFE.puffs[i]);}for(const c of CITYLIFE.capsules)cityHallCapsule(c);}finally{paperPropScope--;}
 if(drawItemHook)drawItemHook(40,true);shBoard=0;shSunMax=2;drawParticles(0,PMAX);
}
function postCityHall(){
 if(!CITYLIFE)return;
 contactShadow(CITYLIFE.workers[0].x,CITYLIFE.workers[0].y,5,2,.4);
 // Slow roof-light bands drift only across exposed wooden apron; fixed board light is protected.
 for(let y=0;y<LH;y++)for(let x=0;x<LW;x++){
  const i=y*LW+x,k=(y+viewY)*WW+x+viewX;if(fDep[i]!==sDep[k]||!(sFlg[k]&FL_FLOOR)||sWZ[k]>2)continue;
  const wx=sWX[k]/4,wy=sWY[k]/4,b=CITYR.board;
  if(wx>=b.x-2&&wx<=b.x+b.w*16+2&&wy>=b.y-2&&wy<=b.y+b.h*16+2)continue;
  if(bdMod(wx+wy*.35+CITYLIFE.lightX*2,58)<5+CITYLIFE.lightY)fLvl[i]=Math.min(4,fLvl[i]+1);
 }
}

// Chapter-only paper runners. Native geometry is shared with64 appended atlas poses.
let hallRunnerAxis=0;
function shHallRunner(face,u,v){
 shFlag=FL_ROOM;shFlat=false;
 const across=bdMod(hallRunnerAxis?u:v,16),along=hallRunnerAxis?v:u;
 if(across<1||across>15)return C.PT0;
 if(across<2||across>14)return C.BR2;
 const weave=bdMod(Math.floor(u*4)+Math.floor(v*4),7);
 return weave===0?C.PT1:bdMod(along,16)<.5?C.PT0:C.PT2;
}
function cityHallRunner(axis,pose,x=0,y=0){
 const length=axis?76:92,u=pose/32,roll=length*u,r=1.5+Math.sqrt(u)*2.4;
 hallRunnerAxis=axis;
 if(axis)faceTop(x,y+roll,x+16,y+length,.35,shHallRunner,C.PT0,0);
 else faceTop(x+roll,y,x+length,y+16,.35,shHallRunner,C.PT0,0);
 // Small layered half-round roll, with exposed cream card edges and green cloth.
 for(let k=0;k<8;k++){
  const d=(k-3.5)*r/4,h=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,r*r-d*d));
  if(axis)box(x,y+roll+d,r-h+.4,x+16,y+roll+d+r/4,r+h+.4,shHallRunner,C.PT0,0);
  else box(x+roll+d,y,r-h+.4,x+roll+d+r/4,y+16,r+h+.4,shHallRunner,C.PT0,0);
 }
 // Woven pulling strap runs alongside the cleared, inactive floor.
 if(axis){dpRod(x+1,y+roll,2,x-10,y+roll+10,11,C.BR1);}
 else dpRod(x+roll,y+1,2,x+roll+10,y-10,11,C.BR1);
}

 }else if(CP.mode==='exit'){

 }

function drawTunnelWallAccents(src){
 if(!src||!src.tunnels.length)return;
 let seen=0;const board=shBoard,bias=shBias,sun=shSunMax;shBoard=0;shSunMax=0;shBias=2;paperPropScope++;
 try{for(const t of src.tunnels){

 }}finally{paperPropScope--;shBoard=board;shBias=bias;shSunMax=sun;}
}

function parcelStaticCatalog(){

 {id:'correspondence',origin:[2,94,53],size:128,draw:()=>parcelFace('correspondence',2.2,82,42,24,22,F_X)},{id:'coats',origin:[2,121,50],size:128,draw:()=>parcelFace('coats',2.2,111,34,21,31,F_X)},
 {id:'warm-lamp',origin:[27,139,68],size:256,draw:()=>parcelLamp(27,139,68,true)},{id:'conveyor-lamp',origin:[83,30,70],size:256,draw:()=>parcelLamp(83,30,70,false)}];
 for(const f of PARR.furniture){let draw;if(f.kind==='rack'||f.kind==='rack-left')draw=()=>parcelRack(f);else if(f.kind==='bench')draw=()=>parcelBench(f);else if(f.kind==='tea')draw=()=>parcelTea(f);else if(f.kind==='floor-scale')draw=()=>parcelScaleBase(f);else if(f.kind==='box-stack')draw=()=>parcelStack(f);else if(f.kind==='conveyor')draw=()=>parcelConveyor(f);else if(f.kind==='trolley')draw=()=>parcelTrolley(53,61);else if(f.kind==='packing-supplies')draw=parcelPackingSupplies;else continue;out.push({id:f.kind==='conveyor'?'conveyor-frame':f.kind==='floor-scale'?'floor-scale-base':f.id,origin:f.anchor,size:256,draw});}return out;
}
function bakeParcel(){targetStatic();bakeBackdrop();shBoard=0;shBias=0;shFlat=false;shSunMax=0;aoFn=parcelBakeLight;lampFn=null;paperPropScope++;try{for(const p of parcelStaticCatalog())p.draw();}finally{paperPropScope--;}aoFn=null;shBoard=0;shBias=0;shSunMax=2;shFlag=0;paperStaticFinish();}
// Sparse world/depth cells plus native4x faces, captured only on room activation.
function parcelCacheCell(draw){
 const n=WW*WH,idx=new Uint8Array(n).fill(EMPTY),lvl=new Uint8Array(n),dep=new Int16Array(n).fill(DEPTH_EMPTY),state=paperStaticState();targetCustom(idx,lvl,dep,WW,WH,OX,OY);tSun=false;aoFn=null;lampFn=parcelLampAdd;shFlag=0;shBoard=0;shBias=0;shFlat=false;shSunMax=0;
 let paperSource=null;paperCacheBuild=true;paperCachePiece=null;paperStaticAtlasBegin();paperPropScope++;
 try{draw();paperStaticAtlasFinish();paperSource=paperPropCachedTexture();}finally{paperPropScope--;paperStaticAtlasEnd();paperCacheBuild=false;}
 let count=0;for(let i=0;i<n;i++)if(idx[i]!==EMPTY)count++;const out={x:new Int16Array(count),y:new Int16Array(count),idx:new Uint8Array(count),lvl:new Uint8Array(count),dep:new Int16Array(count),paperSource};
 for(let i=0,k=0;i<n;i++)if(idx[i]!==EMPTY){out.x[k]=i%WW;out.y[k]=(i/WW)|0;out.idx[k]=idx[i];out.lvl[k]=lvl[i];out.dep[k++]=dep[i];}targetStatic();paperStaticRestore(state);return out;
}

function parcelBlit(c,dx=0,dy=0,dz=0){
 const sx=Math.round(dx-dy),sy=Math.round((dx+dy)*.5-dz),depth=Math.round((dx+dy+dz)*4),r=c.paperSource,s=r?paperPropCapture(r.x0+sx-viewX,r.y0+sy-viewY,r.x0+sx-viewX+r.w,r.y0+sy-viewY+r.h):null;
 for(let k=0;k<c.idx.length;k++){const x=c.x[k]+sx-viewX,y=c.y[k]+sy-viewY;if(x<0||y<0||x>=LW||y>=LH)continue;const i=y*LW+x,d=c.dep[k]+depth;if(d<fDep[i])continue;fIdx[i]=c.idx[k];fLvl[i]=c.lvl[k];fDep[i]=d;}
 if(s){s.idx=r.idx;s.lvl=r.lvl;s.nativeW=r.nativeW;s.tx=s.x0+viewX-r.x0-sx;s.ty=s.y0+viewY-r.y0-sy;paperPropFinish(s);}
}
// Additive handler pictures keep the accepted settled carry and gait unchanged.
function buildParcelLift(stage,back){
 Object.assign(PARCEL_DRIVER,PARCEL_POOLS.read[0]);PARCEL_DRIVER.face=back?2:0;PARCEL_DRIVER.bob=stage===2?1:2;buildDriver(PARCEL_DRIVER);
 const bend=stage===2?1:2,hy=AY-(stage===0?14:15),left=AX-10,right=AX-7;
 for(let j=0;j<=8;j++){const t=j/8,x=Math.round(mixDriver(AX+3,right,t)),y=Math.round(mixDriver(AY-18+bend,hy,t));sp(x,y,C.BL2);sp(x+1,y,C.BL1);}
 for(let j=0;j<=8;j++){const t=j/8,x=Math.round(mixDriver(AX-3,left,t)),y=Math.round(mixDriver(AY-18+bend,hy,t));sp(x,y,C.BL2);sp(x+1,y,C.BL1);}
 rect(left-1,hy,3,2,C.WD1);rect(right-1,hy,3,2,C.WD1);outlineSprite();
}
function buildParcelBaseHandler(a){
 if(a.handling>=0&&a.handling<3)buildParcelLift(a.handling,a.face>=2);
 else{const list=a.handling===3?PARCEL_POOLS.carry:PARCEL_POOLS[a.action];Object.assign(PARCEL_DRIVER,list[a.handling===3?0:a.frame]);PARCEL_DRIVER.face=a.face;buildDriver(PARCEL_DRIVER);}
}
function parcelRearWork(a){return a.face>=2&&(a.handling>=0||a.action==='carry'||a.action==='carry-walk'||a.action==='push'||a.action==='read'||a.action==='sort');}
function buildParcelHandler(a){
 buildParcelBaseHandler(a);if(!parcelRearWork(a))return;
 PARCEL_TURN_SPR.set(SPR);PARCEL_TURN_SPO.set(SPO);const headEnd=a.handling>=0&&a.handling<3?22:20;
 for(let y=0;y<SHT;y++)for(let x=0;x<SW;x++){const from=2*AX-x,i=y*SW+x,j=y<headEnd&&x>=AX-6&&x<=AX+6?i:from>=0&&from<SW?y*SW+from:-1;SPR[i]=j<0?EMPTY:PARCEL_TURN_SPR[j];SPO[i]=j<0?0:PARCEL_TURN_SPO[j];}
}
function parcelActors(){const a=PARCELIFE.sorter;buildParcelHandler(a);blitSprite(a.x,a.y,a.z,PARCEL.staffMirror(a.face),true);Object.assign(cat,PARCELIFE.pm);drawCat();const toy=PARCELIFE.toy;parcelBlit(PARCEL_CACHE.ball[Math.floor(toy.angle)%4],toy.x-247,toy.y-161,toy.z-1);const c=PARCELIFE.kitten;buildLoose(COAT_GINGER,c.pose===2?'walk':c.pose===1?'stretch':c.look?'sitfront':'sit',c.frame,0);blitSprite(c.x,c.y,c.z,c.face===1,true);}
function drawParcel(){

 }

}

function christmasActors(){if(typeof XC!=='undefined'&&xcEnding()){drawChristmasFinaleActors();return;}for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const a=XMASLIFE.staff[i];buildChristmasStaff(a,XMASR.staff[i].costume);const grip=XMAS_GRIPS.get(paperSpriteHash());blitSprite(a.x,a.y,a.z,PARCEL.staffMirror(a.face),true);if(!(typeof XC!=='undefined'&&CP.christmas&&CP.mode==='growth'&&i===1))christmasHeldProps(a,i,grip);}Object.assign(cat,XMASLIFE.pm);buildChristmasPM();blitSprite(cat.x,cat.y,cat.z,cat.face===1,true);for(let i=0;i<4;i++){buildNapSprite(COAT_IDS[(i*3+1)%COAT_IDS.length],i%2?'loaf':'curl',christmasGalleryFrame(XMASLIFE.frame,i),0);blitSprite(48+i*56,24,58,i%2===0,true);}buildNapSprite(COAT_IDS[6],'loaf',christmasGalleryFrame(XMASLIFE.frame,4),0);blitSprite(276,220,12,false,true);}
function drawChristmas(){nItems=0;const src=gBoardSource();if(src===G_SRC)gPrepareSrc();addBoardItems(src);sortItems();for(let k=0;k<nItems;k++){const id=ITEM_ID[k];if(drawItemHook)drawItemHook(id,false);shBoard=128;drawBoardItem(id-IT_BOARD0);if(drawItemHook)drawItemHook(id,true);}shBoard=0;shSunMax=0;if(drawItemHook)drawItemHook(40,false);parcelBlit(XMAS_CACHE.record[XMASLIFE.record]);christmasActors();if(typeof XC!=='undefined'&&CP.christmas)drawChristmasChapter();paperPropScope++;try{for(const p of XMASLIFE.snow)if(p.visible){const w=XMASR.windows[p.window];christmasFlake(w.wall==='back'?p.u:.6,w.wall==='back'?.6:p.u,p.z,p.seed%3);}for(let i=0;i<XMASR.decorations.length;i++){const d=XMASR.decorations[i];if(d.kind!=='garland')continue;for(let j=0;j<4;j++){const u=d.u+3+j*6,z=d.z-4-Math.sin(j*Math.PI/3)*2,c=(i+j+XMASLIFE.light)%3===0?C.GL0:C.BR3;dot3(d.wall==='back'?u:22.9,d.wall==='back'?22.9:u,z,c,0);}}}finally{paperPropScope--;}if(drawItemHook)drawItemHook(40,true);shSunMax=2;}

function christmasCacheCell(draw){
 const n=WW*WH,idx=new Uint8Array(n).fill(EMPTY),lvl=new Uint8Array(n),dep=new Int16Array(n).fill(DEPTH_EMPTY),state=paperStaticState();targetCustom(idx,lvl,dep,WW,WH,OX,OY);tSun=false;aoFn=null;lampFn=null;shFlag=0;shBoard=0;shBias=0;shFlat=false;shSunMax=0;
 let paperSource=null;paperCacheBuild=true;paperCachePiece=null;paperStaticAtlasBegin();paperPropScope++;
 try{draw();paperStaticAtlasFinish();paperSource=paperPropCachedTexture();}finally{paperPropScope--;paperStaticAtlasEnd();paperCacheBuild=false;}
 let count=0;for(let i=0;i<n;i++)if(idx[i]!==EMPTY)count++;const out={x:new Int16Array(count),y:new Int16Array(count),idx:new Uint8Array(count),lvl:new Uint8Array(count),dep:new Int16Array(count),paperSource};
 for(let i=0,k=0;i<n;i++)if(idx[i]!==EMPTY){out.x[k]=i%WW;out.y[k]=(i/WW)|0;out.idx[k]=idx[i];out.lvl[k]=lvl[i];out.dep[k++]=dep[i];}targetStatic();paperStaticRestore(state);return out;
}

function buildChristmasBaseStaff(a){Object.assign(XMAS_STAFF,a);if(a.action==='carry-walk'&&a.frame===3)XMAS_STAFF.frame=1;buildParcelHandler(XMAS_STAFF);}
function buildChristmasStaff(a,costume){buildChristmasBaseStaff(a);const dark=costume==='green'?C.PT0:costume==='cream'?C.PAPSH:C.RD0,mid=costume==='green'?C.PT1:costume==='cream'?C.CREAM:C.RD1,light=costume==='green'?C.PT2:costume==='cream'?C.WHITE:C.RD3;for(let y=AY-18;y<=AY-7;y++)for(let x=AX-11;x<=AX+7;x++){const i=y*SW+x,c=SPR[i];if(c===C.BL0||c===C.BL1||c===C.BL2){SPR[i]=(y===AY-13||y===AY-9)&&bdMod(x+y,3)===0?C.CREAM:c===C.BL0?dark:c===C.BL2?light:mid;}}}
function buildChristmasPM(){buildCat();const y=cat.state===CS_WALK?AY-3-(cat.frame&1):AY-2;for(let x=AX-3;x<=AX;x++)if(SPR[y*SW+x]!==EMPTY)sp(x,y,x===AX?C.RD3:C.RD1);if(SPR[(y+1)*SW+AX-1]!==EMPTY)sp(AX-1,y+1,C.RD0);}

function christmasClipboard(sign){const x=sign<0?-4:0,y=sign<0?0:-.5;box(x,y,-1,x+4,y+.6,4,shCityWood,C.WD0,0);faceY(x+.4,x+3.6,0,3.4,y+.65,shChristmasWhite,C.PAPSH,0);for(let i=0;i<3;i++)dpRod(x+1,y+.7,.6+i,x+2.8,y+.7,.6+i,C.PAPSH);}
function christmasPot(sign){cylinder(0,0,1.45,-1,1.7,shChristmasGreen,C.PT0,0);cylinder(0,0,1.1,1.7,2.1,shCityCream,C.PAPSH,0);dpRod(0,0,1.5,sign*3.3,0,2.2,C.BR2);dpRod(-sign*1.4,-.3,0,-sign*2,-.3,1.5,C.BR2);dpRod(-sign*2,-.3,1.5,-sign*1.4,-.3,1.7,C.BR2);}
function christmasBow(sign){christmasFace('wreath',sign<0?-3:0,.1,-1,3,3,F_Y);}
function christmasHeldProps(a,i,grip){const mirror=PARCEL.staffMirror(a.face),dx=mirror?.25-grip[0]:grip[0],dy=-dx,sign=dx<0?-1:1,k=sign<0?0:1,c=i===0?XMAS_CACHE.clipboard[k]:i===2?XMAS_CACHE.bow[k]:i===3?XMAS_CACHE.pot[k]:XMAS_CACHE.tray[k];parcelBlit(c,a.x+dx,a.y+dy,a.z+grip[2]);if(i===3&&a.face===0&&((XMASLIFE.frame+720)%1440)>=24&&((XMASLIFE.frame+720)%1440)<84){paperPropScope++;try{dpRod(a.x+dx-3.3,a.y+dy,a.z+grip[2]+2.2,a.x+dx-3.3,a.y+dy,11.5,C.BR1);}finally{paperPropScope--;}}}
function christmasFlake(x,y,z,pose){dot3(x,y,z,C.CREAM,0);if(pose===1)dot3(x+.35,y-.35,z,C.WHITE,0);else if(pose===2){dot3(x,y,z+.35,C.WHITE,0);dot3(x,y,z-.35,C.CREAM,0);}}

function christmasGalleryFrame(f,i){const q=(f+i*137)%720;return q<120?Math.floor(q/60)%2:q>=200&&q<212?2:q>=480&&q<504?4:0;}

// One new native paper prop. Baked once; live/export use identical indexed pixels.
// It uses the same camera-facing world/depth registration as depotSpriteAt,
// without resizing or changing the approved32x36 actor sprite architecture.
function christmasEvergreen(){
 const f=XMASR.furniture.find(f=>f.kind==='evergreen'),cx=f.anchor[0],cy=f.anchor[1],t=CHRISTMAS_FIR_PAPER,S=PAPER_SCALE,W=t.w/S,H=t.h/S,native=paperStaticTarget();
 shFlag=FL_ROOM;shBoard=0;shLvl=0;
 if(native)paperStaticPrimitive++;
 try{for(let y=0;y<H;y++)for(let x=0;x<W;x++){
  const c=t.pixels[(y*S+2)*t.w+x*S+2];if(c===EMPTY)continue;
  const dx=x+.5-W/2,z=H-y-.5,wx=cx+dx*.5,wy=cy-dx*.5,px=Math.floor(cx-cy+dx+tOX),py=Math.floor((cx+cy)*.5-z+tOY);
  if(px>=0&&py>=0&&px<tW&&py<tH)emit(px,py,wx,wy,z,F_Y,c);
 }}finally{if(native)paperStaticPrimitive--;}
 if(native){
  if(paperStaticAtlasActive)paperStaticAtlasFaces++;
  const x0=Math.round((cx-cy+tOX-W/2)*S),y0=Math.round(((cx+cy)*.5+tOY-H)*S);
  for(let y=0;y<t.h;y++)for(let x=0;x<t.w;x++){
   const c=t.pixels[y*t.w+x];if(c===EMPTY)continue;const px=x0+x,py=y0+y;if(px<0||py<0||px>=paperStaticWidth||py>=paperStaticLogicalH*S)continue;
   const dx=(x+.5)/S-W/2,z=H-(y+.5)/S;shLvl=0;paperStaticEmit(px,py,cx+dx*.5,cy-dx*.5,z,F_Y,c);
  }
 }
 christmasGift(cx-14,cy+15,0,10,9,7,false);christmasGift(cx-1,cy+17,0,9,8,6,true);christmasGift(cx+11,cy+10,0,8,10,8,false);
}

function xcPrepareFinale(){
 if(!XC.lights){XC.lights=[];for(let k=0;k<=16;k++){
  const res=Uint32Array.from(XMAS_LIGHT.res),gray=Uint32Array.from(XMAS_LIGHT.gray),factor=1-.28*k/16,half=NLVL*PAL_N;
  for(let i=0;i<res.length;i++){const c=i%PAL_N;if(c===C.GL0||c===C.BR3||c===C.BG0)continue;const v=res[i],r=Math.round((v&255)*factor),g=Math.round((v>>>8&255)*factor),b=Math.round((v>>>16&255)*(1-.32*k/16)),y=Math.round(r*.2126+g*.7152+b*.0722);res[i]=(0xff000000|b<<16|g<<8|r)>>>0;gray[i]=(0xff000000|y<<16|y<<8|y)>>>0;}
  XC.lights.push({res,gray});
 }}
 if(!XC.perches)XC.perches=christmasCacheCell(christmasFinalePerches);
 if(!XC.bench)XC.bench=christmasCacheCell(christmasFinaleBench);if(!XC.basket)XC.basket=christmasCacheCell(christmasFinaleBasket);
 if(!XC.lifts){XC.lifts=new Map();for(const coat of CHRISTMASCHAPTER.CAST){for(let f=0;f<4;f++){buildChristmasCollection(coat,f);xcRememberLift();}for(const pose of['loaf','curl'])for(let f=0;f<2;f++){buildNapSprite(coat,pose,f,0);xcRememberLift();}}for(let f=0;f<2;f++){Object.assign(cat,{state:CS_SIT,frame:f,head:0,flick:f});buildChristmasPM();xcRememberLift();}}

}
const XMAS_SEATED_POSE={action:'read',frame:0,handling:-1,face:0},XMAS_SEATED_SPR=new Uint8Array(SW*SHT),XMAS_SEATED_SPO=new Uint8Array(SW*SHT);
function buildChristmasSeated(){
 buildChristmasStaff(XMAS_SEATED_POSE,'red');XMAS_SEATED_SPR.set(SPR);XMAS_SEATED_SPO.set(SPO);sprClear();
 for(let y=0;y<=AY-9;y++)for(let x=0;x<SW;x++){const from=y*SW+x,to=(y+4)*SW+x;SPR[to]=XMAS_SEATED_SPR[from];SPO[to]=XMAS_SEATED_SPO[from];}
 rect(AX-2,AY-5,7,2,C.BL1);rect(AX+3,AY-4,2,4,C.BL0);run(AX+2,AY,4,C.WD1);outlineSprite();
}
function buildChristmasCollection(coat,frame){
 sprClear();const bob=frame&1?-1:0;
 for(let y=0;y<CAT_WALK_BODY.length;y++)for(let x=0;x<CAT_WALK_BODY[y].length;x++){
  let c=CAT_WALK_BODY[y][x];if(x<4&&c==='o')c='h';else if(x<4&&c==='O')c='H';const col=coatPixel(coat,c,x,y,0);if(col!==EMPTY)sp(AX-6+x,AY-6+bob+y,col);
 }
 const legs=CAT_LEGS[frame&3];for(let y=0;y<legs.length;y++)for(let x=0;x<legs[y].length;x++){const col=coatPixel(coat,legs[y][x],x,y+6,0);if(col!==EMPTY)sp(AX-6+x,AY-1+bob+y,col);}
 outlineSprite();
}
function drawChristmasFinaleActors(){
 const p=XC.poses;for(let i=0;i<4;i++)parcelBlit(XC.perches,28+i*56,24,42);parcelBlit(XC.bench,259,4,42);
 for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const a=p.staff[i];if(a.seated)buildChristmasSeated();else buildChristmasStaff(a,XMASR.staff[i].costume);blitSprite(a.x,a.y,a.z,PARCEL.staffMirror(a.face),true);}
 Object.assign(cat,p.pm);buildChristmasPM();blitSprite(cat.x,cat.y,cat.z+(cat.state===CS_SIT?xcSpriteLift():0),cat.face===1,true);
 for(let i=0;i<8;i++){
  const c=p.cats[i];if(!c.visible)continue;parcelBlit(XC.basket,12+i*16,234);
  if(c.phase==='walk')buildChristmasCollection(c.coat,c.frame);else buildNapSprite(c.coat,c.phase==='gallery'?'loaf':'curl',c.frame,0);
  blitSprite(c.x,c.y,c.z+xcSpriteLift(),c.face===1||c.face===3,true);
 }
 buildNapSprite(COAT_IDS[6],'loaf',christmasGalleryFrame(XMASLIFE.frame,4),0);blitSprite(276,220,12,false,true);
}
// Explicit finite inventory; old native source hashes are never replaced.
function christmasFinaleSpriteDescriptors(){
 const list=[{name:'christmas_finale-sorter-seated',kind:'seat',frames:[{tag:'rest',draw:buildChristmasSeated}]}];
 list.push({name:'christmas_finale-postmistress-seated',kind:'pm',frames:[0,1].map(frame=>({tag:'rest'+frame,draw:()=>{Object.assign(cat,{state:CS_SIT,frame,head:0,flick:frame,face:0});buildChristmasPM();},base:()=>{Object.assign(cat,{state:CS_SIT,frame,head:0,flick:frame,face:0});buildCat();}}))});
 for(const costume of['green','cream'])for(const face of[0,2])list.push({name:'christmas_finale-'+costume+'-walk-'+face,kind:'costume',frames:[0,1,2,3].map(frame=>{const a={action:'walk',frame,face,handling:-1};return {tag:'walk'+frame,draw:()=>buildChristmasStaff(a,costume),base:()=>buildChristmasBaseStaff(a)};})});
 for(const coat of CHRISTMASCHAPTER.CAST)list.push({name:'christmas_finale-'+coat+'-walk',kind:'coat',frames:[0,1,2,3].map(frame=>({tag:'walk'+frame,draw:()=>buildChristmasCollection(coat,frame),base:()=>buildLoose(COAT_GINGER,'walk',frame,0)}))});
 return list;
}
