function xcRememberLift(){
 const h=paperSpriteHash(),t=paperLookup.get(h);if(!t)throw Error('Missing ending foot texture '+h);let bottom=-Infinity;
 for(let y=0;y<t.h*4;y++)for(let x=0;x<t.w*4;x++)if(t.pixels[y*t.w*4+x]!==EMPTY)bottom=Math.max(bottom,t.dy+(y+1)/4);
 XC.lifts.set(h,Math.max(0,bottom));
}
function xcSpriteLift(){return XC.lifts.get(paperSpriteHash())||0;}
// ============================================================================
// 40 VIEWPORT MATHS (pure; Node-exported for tests). Device pixels in, whole
// scales out: scale = device px per logical px, and the logical canvas is
// floor(w / s) x floor(h / s). Swift: the same functions on UIScreen.nativeBounds.
// ============================================================================
const VIEWPORT = (function () {
  const MIN_PORTRAIT = 280, MIN_LANDSCAPE = 320, TILE_PX = 32, TAP_PT = 44;
  // The spec rule: the largest whole scale that keeps the logical width >= 280 (portrait) or >= 320 (landscape).
  function baseScale(w, h) {
    const min = h > w ? MIN_PORTRAIT : MIN_LANDSCAPE;
    let s = 1;
    while (Math.floor(w / (s + 1)) >= min) s++;
    return s;
  }
  // The game's default zoom: the smallest scale >= baseScale whose tiles are >= 44 pt, while the board still fits.
  function gameScale(w, h, dpr, boardPxW) {
    let s = baseScale(w, h);
    while (TILE_PX * s / dpr < TAP_PT && Math.floor(w / (s + 1)) >= boardPxW) s++;
    return s;
  }
  // The largest scale at which the whole room (plus margin) fits; 1 if it never does.
  function roomFitScale(w, h, roomW, roomH) {
    let s = 1;
    while (Math.floor(w / (s + 1)) >= roomW && Math.floor(h / (s + 1)) >= roomH) s++;
    return s;
  }
  // Closest zoom: about 3.5 tiles across the short side.
  function maxScale(w, h) { return Math.max(baseScale(w, h), Math.floor(Math.min(w, h * 2) / 112)); }
  return { baseScale, gameScale, roomFitScale, maxScale, TILE_PX, TAP_PT };
})();
if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) module.exports = VIEWPORT;
// ============================================================================
// 41 LOOP: fixed 60 Hz simulation; a frame is composed whenever the simulation
// has advanced or the view changed (zoom, pan, resize, grayscale), then blitted
// at a whole-number scale. Keys, view zoom/pan helpers and debug hooks.
// Swift: CADisplayLink at the display's rate, the same 60 Hz accumulator.
// ============================================================================
const screen = document.getElementById('screen');
const sctx = screen.getContext('2d', { alpha: false });
let scale = 1, dstX = 0, dstY = 0, devW = 1, devH = 1, devDpr = 1;
const BG_CSS = '#' + PAL_DEF[C.BG0][1];
const BG_GREY_CSS = (() => { const g = GRAY32[2 * PAL_N + C.BG0] & 255; return 'rgb(' + g + ',' + g + ',' + g + ')'; })();
let ROOM_FIT_W = 352, ROOM_FIT_H = 270;            // the diorama (336 x 254) plus an 8 px margin; a Box Room sets its own

let lastIW = -1, lastIH = -1, lastDpr = -1;
function onResize() {
  if(window.__chapterReady&&CP.mode==='station'){cpBegin();cpFinishEntry();}
  if(window.__chapterReady&&CP.mode==='enter')cpFinishEntry();
  const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  lastIW = window.innerWidth; lastIH = window.innerHeight; lastDpr = dpr;
  devW = Math.max(1, Math.round(window.innerWidth * dpr));
  devH = Math.max(1, Math.round(window.innerHeight * dpr));
  devDpr = dpr;
  screen.width = devW; screen.height = devH;
  applyScale();
  if(window.__chapterReady&&typeof XC!=='undefined'&&xcEnding())xcResize();
}
// Scale = the user's zoom if they've pinched, otherwise the mode's default (40_viewport),
// kept between "the whole room fits" and "about 3.5 tiles across".
function scaleLimits(w, h) {
  const hi = VIEWPORT.maxScale(w, h);
  return [Math.min(VIEWPORT.roomFitScale(w, h, ROOM_FIT_W, ROOM_FIT_H), hi), hi];
}
function defaultScale(w, h, dpr) {
  if (GAME_MODE) {
    const board=gBoardFitPx();let s=VIEWPORT.gameScale(w,h,dpr,board);
    if(gCityHallEditing()){
      const panel=document.getElementById('editor'),right=w>h?(panel.offsetWidth+24)*dpr:0,bottom=h>=w?(panel.offsetHeight+24)*dpr:0;
      const B=gm.L.board;
      while(s>1&&(Math.floor((w-right)/s)<(B.w+B.h)*TILE+32||Math.floor((h-bottom)/s)<(B.w+B.h)*TILE/2+32))s--;
      return s;
    }
    const train=gm.L&&(LEVELROOMS.id(gm.L)==='train'||LEVELROOMS.id(gm.L)==='cityhall'||LEVELROOMS.id(gm.L)==='parcel'||LEVELROOMS.id(gm.L)==='christmas');
    if(gm.L&&((gm.L.flaps||[]).length||train)&&w>h&&h/dpr<=520&&hud.root.classList.contains('on')){
      const side=hud.root.offsetWidth*dpr;
      const needed=train?(gm.L.board.w+gm.L.board.h)*TILE+16:board+16;
      while(s>1&&Math.floor(w/s)-Math.ceil(side/s)<needed)s--;
    }
    if(train&&hud.root.classList.contains('on')&&hud.root.offsetHeight>0){
      const bottom=w>h&&h/dpr<=520?0:hud.root.offsetHeight*dpr,needed=(gm.L.board.w+gm.L.board.h)*TILE/2+32;
      while(s>1&&Math.floor(h/s)-Math.ceil(bottom/s)-8<needed)s--;
    }
    return s;
  }
  return ACTIVE_ROOM.id !== 'office' ? VIEWPORT.roomFitScale(w, h, ROOM_FIT_W, ROOM_FIT_H) : VIEWPORT.baseScale(w, h);   // ?boxroom: the whole room
}
function applyScale() {
  const lim = scaleLimits(devW, devH);
  const s = clamp(view.zoom !== null ? view.zoom : defaultScale(devW, devH, devDpr), lim[0], lim[1]);
  view.scale = scale = s;
  LW = Math.floor(devW / s); LH = Math.floor(devH / s);
  dstX = Math.floor((devW - LW * s) / 2); dstY = Math.floor((devH - LH * s) / 2);
  ensureFrameBuffers(LW, LH);
  paperSetViewScale(scale);
  allocView();
  buildVignette();
  viewX0 = Math.round(VIEW_CX - LW / 2); viewY0 = Math.round(VIEW_CY - LH / 2);
  sctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
  if (GAME_MODE) gMeasureHud();
  dirty = true;
}
// Zoom one whole step, keeping the world point under device pixel (mx, my) where it is.
function zoomStep(dir, mx, my) {
  if(cpActive())return;
  const lim = scaleLimits(devW, devH), s0 = scale, s1 = clamp(s0 + dir, lim[0], lim[1]);
  if (s1 === s0) return;
  const ox = VIEW_CX + WS.camX + view.panX - LW / 2, oy = VIEW_CY + WS.camY + view.panY - LH / 2;   // current view origin
  const wx = ox + (mx - dstX) / s0, wy = oy + (my - dstY) / s0;
  view.zoom = s1;
  applyScale();
  const vx = wx - (mx - dstX) / s1, vy = wy - (my - dstY) / s1;          // view origin that keeps the point
  panBy(vx - (VIEW_CX + WS.camX + view.panX - LW / 2), vy - (VIEW_CY + WS.camY + view.panY - LH / 2));
}
let PAN_MAX_X = 168, PAN_MAX_Y = 127;              // the view centre stays over the diorama
// Capture before a saved depot level can activate during game initialisation.
const OFFICE_FIT = { w: ROOM_FIT_W, h: ROOM_FIT_H, px: PAN_MAX_X, py: PAN_MAX_Y };
function panBy(dx, dy) {
  if(cpActive())return;
  view.panX = clamp(view.panX + dx, -PAN_MAX_X, PAN_MAX_X);
  view.panY = clamp(view.panY + dy, -PAN_MAX_Y, PAN_MAX_Y);
  dirty = true;
}
function resetView() { if(cpActive())return; view.zoom = null; view.panX = 0; view.panY = 0; applyScale(); }

const DT = 1 / 60, DT_TOL = 0.00025;               // tolerance absorbs rAF jitter (120 Hz: 2 x 8.333 ms)
const LOOP_FRAMES = SCN.demo.loop * 60;            // 48 s: the room's routine repeats exactly
let loopFrame = 0, acc = 0, lastNow = -1, loopT = 0, dirty = true;
// One fixed step. The room (sim/14_world) is a pure function of the loop frame; the game runs
// its own clock on top, tells the Postmistress which cats to watch and holds the camera still.
function simulate(t, frame) {
  if (GAME_MODE) { if(!cpActive()||CP.mode==='exit'&&gm.mode==='won')gUpdate(); gWatch(); }
  if(ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='depot'){
    DEPOTWORLD.step(DPLIFE,frame,GAME_MODE?WS.watch:null);WS.frame=DPLIFE.frame;WS.camX=DPLIFE.camX;WS.camY=DPLIFE.camY;
  }else if(ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='christmas')christmasStep(frame);
  else if(ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='parcel')parcelStep(frame);
  else if(ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='cityhall')cityHallStep(frame);
  else if(ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='train')trainStep(frame);
  else WORLD.step(WS, frame);
  if (GAME_MODE && gm.shift) nsAnimateEndingActors();
  if (GAME_MODE) gCamera();
  else if (ACTIVE_ROOM.id === 'boxroom') { WS.camX = 0; WS.camY = 0; }      // the Box Room review holds still
  if(cpActive())cpWorld();
  updateParticles(DT);
}
function update() {
  if(cpActive())cpUpdate();
  if (BD) BD.elapsed += DT;
  loopFrame = (loopFrame + 1) % LOOP_FRAMES;
  loopT = loopFrame / 60;
  simulate(loopT, loopFrame);
}
function present() {                               // the frame, then only the thin bars it leaves (under one scale step)
  const W = screen.width, H = screen.height, w = LW * scale, h = LH * scale;
  const output=paperOutputCanvas(), resolution=paperOutputScale();
  if(cpActive()&&CP.mode==='station'){tcPresent();syncBackdropCSS();return;}
  if(cpActive()&&(CP.mode==='enter'||CP.christmas&&xcEnding())){cpPresent(output,resolution);syncBackdropCSS();return;}
  sctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
  sctx.drawImage(output, 0, 0, LW*resolution, LH*resolution, dstX, dstY, w, h);
  if (BD) {
    // Extend the actual edge pixels into integer-scaling remainders, including the changing skyline on each side.
    if (dstX) sctx.drawImage(output, 0, 0, resolution, LH*resolution, 0, dstY, dstX, h);
    if (dstX + w < W) sctx.drawImage(output, (LW-1)*resolution, 0, resolution, LH*resolution, dstX + w, dstY, W - dstX - w, h);
    if (dstY) sctx.drawImage(screen, 0, dstY, W, 1, 0, 0, W, dstY);
    if (dstY + h < H) sctx.drawImage(screen, 0, dstY + h - 1, W, 1, 0, dstY + h, W, H - dstY - h);
    syncBackdropCSS();
    return;
  }
  syncBackdropCSS();
  sctx.fillStyle = grayscale ? BG_GREY_CSS : BG_CSS;
  if (dstY > 0 || dstY + h < H) { sctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, dstY); sctx.fillRect(0, dstY + h, W, H - dstY - h); }
  if (dstX > 0 || dstX + w < W) { sctx.fillRect(0, dstY, dstX, h); sctx.fillRect(dstX + w, dstY, W - dstX - w, h); }
}
let backdropPageCSS = '';
function syncBackdropCSS() {
  const css = backdropEdgeCSS();
  if (css !== backdropPageCSS) { document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--page-background', css); backdropPageCSS = css; }
}
// One display frame: run the fixed steps that are due, then compose if anything changed.
function frame(now) {
  if (lastNow < 0) lastNow = now;
  acc += Math.min(0.25, (now - lastNow) / 1000);
  lastNow = now;
  let steps = 0;
  while (acc >= DT - DT_TOL && steps < 8) { if (!paused) update(); acc -= DT; steps++; }
  if (steps === 8 || acc < DT_TOL) acc = 0;
  if (steps > 0 || dirty) { renderFrame(); present(); dirty = false; }
  return steps;
}
function tick(now) {
  if (window.innerWidth !== lastIW || window.innerHeight !== lastIH || (window.devicePixelRatio || 1) !== lastDpr) onResize();
  if (PERF_ON) { const a = performance.now(), k = frame(now); perfFrame(now, k, performance.now() - a); }   // ?perf (46_perf)
  else frame(now);
  requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

function boot() {
  if (DEPOT_REVIEW) showRoom('depot', DEPOT.build());
  else if (BOX_REVIEW) showBoxRoom(boxRoomFor(BOX_REVIEW.args));   // ?boxroom (47_boxroom)
  else if (!(GAME_MODE && G_ROOM)) { bakeStatic(); bakeOutside(); initDust(); }   // (a daily puzzle opened in its Box Room is baked already)
  onResize();
  window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);
  window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  WS.mode = GAME_MODE ? 'game' : 'demo';
  simulate(0, 0);
  if(GAME_MODE&&window.__chapterReady)cpInitial();
  requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}
function onKey(e) {
  if(cpActive()&&e.key!=='g'&&e.key!=='G')return;
  const tag = e.target && e.target.tagName;
  if (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || tag === 'SELECT') return;     // typing in the editor
  if (e.key === 's' || e.key === 'S') toggleAtlas();
  else if (e.key === 'g' || e.key === 'G') { grayscale = !grayscale; dirty = true; }
  else if (e.key === '0') resetView();
  else if (e.key === '+' || e.key === '=') zoomStep(1, devW / 2, devH / 2);
  else if (e.key === '-' || e.key === '_') zoomStep(-1, devW / 2, devH / 2);
}
// Debug/test hook (used by the build review; harmless in production).
let paused = false;
window.__so = {
  seek(f) { loopFrame = ((f | 0) % LOOP_FRAMES + LOOP_FRAMES) % LOOP_FRAMES; loopT = loopFrame / 60; simulate(loopT, loopFrame); dirty = true; },
  pause(p) { paused = !!p; },
  snap(k) {
    return paperWithNativeView(()=>{
    renderFrame();
    const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = LW * k; c.height = LH * k;
    const x = c.getContext('2d'); x.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    const resolution=paperOutputScale();
    x.drawImage(paperOutputCanvas(), 0, 0, LW*resolution, LH*resolution, 0, 0, LW*k, LH*k);
    return c.toDataURL('image/png');
    });
  },
  crop(x, y, w, h, k) {                 // view-space crop, scaled k
    return paperWithNativeView(()=>{
    renderFrame();
    const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = w * k; c.height = h * k;
    const g = c.getContext('2d'); g.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    const resolution=paperOutputScale();
    g.drawImage(paperOutputCanvas(), x*resolution, y*resolution, w*resolution, h*resolution, 0, 0, w*k, h*k);
    return c.toDataURL('image/png');
    });
  },
  cropWorld(x0, y0, x1, y1, z1, k) {    // crop around a world box (floor up to z1), scaled k
    renderFrame();
    const vx0 = Math.floor(x0 - y1) + OX - viewX - 3, vx1 = Math.ceil(x1 - y0) + OX - viewX + 3;
    const vy0 = Math.floor((x0 + y0) * 0.5 - z1) + OY - viewY - 3, vy1 = Math.ceil((x1 + y1) * 0.5) + OY - viewY + 3;
    return this.crop(vx0, vy0, vx1 - vx0, vy1 - vy0, k);
  },
  viewFor(w, h, dpr) {                  // what the viewport rule gives a device size (no side effects)
    const lim = scaleLimits(w, h);
    const at = (s) => ({ scale: s, lw: Math.floor(w / s), lh: Math.floor(h / s), tilePt: +(32 * s / dpr).toFixed(1) });
    return { demo: at(VIEWPORT.baseScale(w, h)), game: at(VIEWPORT.gameScale(w, h, dpr, gBoardFitPx())), zoomOut: lim[0], zoomIn: lim[1] };
  },
  fakeRaf(hz, n) {                      // drive frame() with synthetic vsync timestamps and count the steps
    const keep = [lastNow, acc];
    lastNow = -1; acc = 0;
    let steps = 0, doubles = 0, idle = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) { const k = frame(i * 1000 / hz); steps += k; if (k > 1) doubles++; if (k === 0) idle++; }
    lastNow = keep[0]; acc = keep[1];
    return { frames: n, steps, doubles, idle };
  },
  // Review aid: lay out cat poses on the board tiles. spec: [[tx, ty, coat, anim, frame, dir, extra], ...]
  gallery(spec) {
    G_GALLERY.length = 0;
    for (const [tx, ty, coat, anim, frame, dir, ex] of spec) {
      const o = BOARDANIM.makeOut(), B = gm.L.board;
      Object.assign(o, { anim, frame, dir, head: dir, x: (gBoardX(B) + tx) * 16 + 8, y: (gBoardY(B) + ty) * 16 + 8 }, ex || {});
      if (o.boxFold >= 0) { o.boxX = o.x; o.boxY = o.y; }
      G_GALLERY.push({ o, tr: { coat } });
    }
    dirty = true;
    return G_GALLERY.length;
  },
  view() { return { scale, LW, LH, dstX, dstY, zoom: view.zoom, panX: view.panX, panY: view.panY, build: BUILD_ID }; },
  // Quality bar: no baked prop or scene item may draw into the 12 x 6 px core of any board tile.
  // Baskets and dogs may cover their own tile; cats, boxes and flat pieces may go anywhere on the board.
  // Demo: every 10th frame of the loop. Game: the given levels, solved, every 10th step of the run.
  coverage(levelIds, options) {
    const NAMES = ['clock', '', 'cubbies', '', 'shelf stack', 'sorter', 'Postmistress', 'conveyor', 'lamp', 'plants', 'counter', 'mug', 'walker', 'floor box'];
    const out = { frames: 0, checks: 0, count: 0, byOwner: {}, first: [] };
    let owner = null, stamp = null, coreSpots=null, rx0 = 0, ry0 = 0, rw = 0, rh = 0;
    const st = (i) => (fDep[i] & 0xffff) | (fIdx[i] << 16) | (fLvl[i] << 24);
    drawItemHook = (id, after) => {
      if(ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='depot'&&coreSpots){
        for(let k=0;k<coreSpots.length;k+=2){const i=coreSpots[k],j=coreSpots[k+1];if(!after)stamp[j]=st(i);else if(st(i)!==stamp[j])owner[j]=id;}
      }else for (let y = 0; y < rh; y++) for (let x = 0; x < rw; x++) {
        const i = (ry0 + y) * LW + rx0 + x, j = y * rw + x;
        if (!after) stamp[j] = st(i); else if (st(i) !== stamp[j]) owner[j] = id;
      }
    };
    const bad = (where, tile, who) => {
      out.count++; out.byOwner[who] = (out.byOwner[who] || 0) + 1;
      if (out.first.length < 20) out.first.push(where + ' tile ' + tile + ': ' + who);
    };
    const check = (src, where) => {
      WS.camX = 0; WS.camY = 0; view.panX = 0; view.panY = 0;
      viewX = Math.round(VIEW_CX - LW / 2); viewY = Math.round(VIEW_CY - LH / 2);
      rx0 = clamp(src.ox - (src.oy + src.h * TILE) + OX - viewX - 8, 0, LW - 1); ry0 = clamp((src.ox + src.oy) / 2 + OY - viewY - 8, 0, LH - 1);
      rw = clamp(src.w * TILE + src.h * TILE + 16, 1, LW - rx0); rh = clamp((src.w + src.h) * TILE / 2 + 16, 1, LH - ry0);
      if (!owner || owner.length < rw * rh) { owner = new Int16Array(rw * rh); stamp = new Int32Array(rw * rh); }
      if(ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='depot'){
        coreSpots=new Int32Array(src.w*src.h*72*2);let k=0;
        for(let ty=0;ty<src.h;ty++)for(let tx=0;tx<src.w;tx++){
          const cx=src.ox+tx*TILE+8,cy=src.oy+ty*TILE+8,sx=cx-cy+OX-viewX,sy=(cx+cy)/2+OY-viewY;
          for(let y=sy-3;y<sy+3;y++)for(let x=sx-6;x<sx+6;x++){coreSpots[k++]=y*LW+x;coreSpots[k++]=(y-ry0)*rw+x-rx0;}
        }
      }
      owner.fill(-1);
      composeStatic(); targetFrame(viewX, viewY); drawDynamic();
      if(ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='depot')postPasses();out.frames++;
      for (let ty = 0; ty < src.h; ty++) for (let tx = 0; tx < src.w; tx++) {
        const cx = src.ox + tx * TILE + 8, cy = src.oy + ty * TILE + 8;
        const sx = Math.round(cx - cy) + OX - viewX, sy = Math.round((cx + cy) / 2) + OY - viewY;
        for (let y = sy - 3; y < sy + 3; y++) for (let x = sx - 6; x < sx + 6; x++) {
          const j = (y - ry0) * rw + x - rx0, o = owner[j];
          out.checks++;
          if (o < 0) { if (!(sFlg[(y + viewY) * WW + x + viewX] & FL_FLOOR)) { bad(where, tx + ',' + ty, 'baked prop'); } continue; }
          if (o < IT_BOARD0) { bad(where, tx + ',' + ty, NAMES[o] || 'item ' + o); continue; }
          const k = o - IT_BOARD0;
          if (k >= BD_BASKET0 && k < BD_DOG0) { const b = src.baskets[k - BD_BASKET0]; if (b.x !== tx || b.y !== ty) bad(where, tx + ',' + ty, 'basket at ' + b.x + ',' + b.y); }
          else if (k >= BD_DOG0 && k < BD_CAT0) { const d = src.dogs[k - BD_DOG0]; if (d.x !== tx || d.y !== ty) bad(where, tx + ',' + ty, 'dog at ' + d.x + ',' + d.y); }
        }
      }
    };
    const keepStore = progressStore, keep = progress;               // the review runs aren't the player's progress
    progressStore = { get: () => null, set: () => {} };
    try {
      paused = true;
      if (!GAME_MODE) for (let f = 0; f < LOOP_FRAMES; f += ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='depot'?1:10) { this.seek(f); check(ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='depot'?DEPOT_SRC:DEMO_SRC, 'demo ' + (f / 60).toFixed(2) + ' s'); }
      else for (const id of levelIds || []) {
        // a level id, or daily:YYYY-MM-DD for that day's puzzle in its own Box Room
        const day = /^daily:(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})$/.exec(id), entry = day ? DAILY.puzzleFor(DAILY_DATA, day[1]) : null;
        const L = entry ? entry.level : LEVELS.find((l) => l.id === id);
        if (!L) continue;
        gLoadLevel(L, entry ? gDailyRoom(day[1], L) : null); gm.chutes = L.solution.map((c) => Object.assign({}, c)); gSyncSrc(); gSend();
        for (let n = 0, after = 0; (options&&options.loop?n<LOOP_FRAMES:after<240) && n < 60 * 60; n++) {
          update(); if (gm.mode !== 'running') after++;
          if (ACTIVE_ROOM.id === 'depot' || n % 10 === 0) check(G_SRC, id + ' step ' + n);
        }
      }
    } finally { drawItemHook = null; dirty = true; progressStore = keepStore; progress = keep; }
    return out;
  },
};
// ============================================================================
// 42 INPUT: one finger (or a mouse click) taps a tile when it lifts without
// moving; two fingers pinch to zoom in whole steps and drag to pan; a mouse drag
// pans; the mouse wheel (or a trackpad pinch) zooms in whole steps round the
// pointer; a double tap on empty scenery fits the board to the screen again.
// Swift: UITapGestureRecognizer (single and double), UIPinchGestureRecognizer
// (whole steps) and UIPanGestureRecognizer(minimumNumberOfTouches: 2).
// ============================================================================
const PTR = new Map();                 // pointerId -> { x, y, x0, y0 } in device px
const TAP_SLOP_CSS = 8;                // movement (CSS px) that still counts as a tap
const PINCH_STEP = 1.25;               // finger-distance ratio per zoom step
let multiTouch = false, pinchD0 = 0, pinchMX = 0, pinchMY = 0, mouseDrag = false;
// The CSS canvas can differ from the backing image while mobile browser chrome resizes.
function clientDeviceX(x,r) { return (x-r.left)*screen.width/r.width; }
function clientDeviceY(y,r) { return (y-r.top)*screen.height/r.height; }
function pointerStill(p,x,y) { return Math.hypot(x-p.cx0,y-p.cy0)<=TAP_SLOP_CSS; }
function devX(e) { return clientDeviceX(e.clientX,screen.getBoundingClientRect()); }
function devY(e) { return clientDeviceY(e.clientY,screen.getBoundingClientRect()); }
function pinchNow() {                  // [distance, mid x, mid y] of the first two pointers
  let a = null, b = null;
  for (const p of PTR.values()) { if (!a) a = p; else if (!b) { b = p; break; } }
  return [Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y), (a.x + b.x) / 2, (a.y + b.y) / 2];
}
screen.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {
  const x = devX(e), y = devY(e);
  PTR.set(e.pointerId, { x, y, x0: x, y0: y, cx0: e.clientX, cy0: e.clientY });
  if (PTR.size >= 2) { multiTouch = true; const p = pinchNow(); pinchD0 = p[0]; pinchMX = p[1]; pinchMY = p[2]; }
  try { screen.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (err) { /* pointer already gone: nothing to capture */ }
});
screen.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
  const p = PTR.get(e.pointerId);
  if (!p) { if (e.pointerType === 'mouse') onHover(e.clientX, e.clientY); return; }
  const x = devX(e), y = devY(e), dx = x - p.x, dy = y - p.y;
  p.x = x; p.y = y;
  if (PTR.size >= 2) {
    const [d, mx, my] = pinchNow();
    panBy(-(mx - pinchMX) / scale, -(my - pinchMY) / scale);
    pinchMX = mx; pinchMY = my;
    if (d > pinchD0 * PINCH_STEP) { zoomStep(1, mx, my); pinchD0 = d; }
    else if (d < pinchD0 / PINCH_STEP) { zoomStep(-1, mx, my); pinchD0 = d; }
  } else if (e.pointerType === 'mouse') {
    if (mouseDrag || !pointerStill(p,e.clientX,e.clientY)) {
      mouseDrag = true;
      panBy(-dx / scale, -dy / scale);
    } else onHover(e.clientX, e.clientY);
  }
});
function endPointer(e, cancelled) {
  const p = PTR.get(e.pointerId);
  if (!p) return;
  PTR.delete(e.pointerId);
  const still = pointerStill(p,e.clientX,e.clientY);
  if (!cancelled && !multiTouch && !mouseDrag && still && PTR.size === 0) onTap(e.clientX, e.clientY);
  if (PTR.size === 0) { multiTouch = false; mouseDrag = false; }
  else if (PTR.size >= 2) { const q = pinchNow(); pinchD0 = q[0]; pinchMX = q[1]; pinchMY = q[2]; }
}
screen.addEventListener('pointerup', (e) => endPointer(e, false));
screen.addEventListener('pointercancel', (e) => endPointer(e, true));

// The wheel zooms one whole step per notch (about 100 px of scroll) round the pointer; a trackpad
// pinch arrives as ctrl + wheel with small deltas and zooms the same way, a step per 40.
let wheelAcc = 0;
screen.addEventListener('wheel', (e) => {
  e.preventDefault();
  const unit = e.deltaMode === 1 ? 33 : e.deltaMode === 2 ? 400 : 1, step = e.ctrlKey ? 40 : 100;
  wheelAcc += e.deltaY * unit;
  while (wheelAcc <= -step) { zoomStep(1, devX(e), devY(e)); wheelAcc += step; }
  while (wheelAcc >= step) { zoomStep(-1, devX(e), devY(e)); wheelAcc -= step; }
}, { passive: false });
// Safari: stop the page zooming; a desktop trackpad pinch (no touch pointers) zooms the view.
let gestureScale = 1;
document.addEventListener('gesturestart', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); gestureScale = 1; });
document.addEventListener('gesturechange', (e) => {
  e.preventDefault();
  if (PTR.size > 0) return;                          // touch pinches are handled by pointer events
  if (e.scale > gestureScale * PINCH_STEP) { zoomStep(1, devW / 2, devH / 2); gestureScale = e.scale; }
  else if (e.scale < gestureScale / PINCH_STEP) { zoomStep(-1, devW / 2, devH / 2); gestureScale = e.scale; }
});

// Taps and hover go to the level editor when it's open, otherwise to the game. A second tap on empty
// scenery soon after the first (300 ms, 24 CSS px) fits the board to the screen.
const DOUBLE_TAP_MS = 300, DOUBLE_TAP_CSS = 24;
let lastTapT = -1e9, lastTapX = 0, lastTapY = 0;
function onTap(clientX, clientY) {
  if (!GAME_MODE) return;
  const now = performance.now(), again = now - lastTapT < DOUBLE_TAP_MS && Math.hypot(clientX - lastTapX, clientY - lastTapY) < DOUBLE_TAP_CSS;
  lastTapT = again ? -1e9 : now; lastTapX = clientX; lastTapY = clientY;
  if (gPick(clientX, clientY) < 0) { if (again) resetView(); return; }   // scenery: only a double tap does anything
  if (edActive()) edTapAt(clientX, clientY); else gTapAt(clientX, clientY);
}
function onHover(clientX, clientY) { if (GAME_MODE) gHover(clientX, clientY); }
// ============================================================================
// 42b PROGRESS STORE: progress (sim/15_progress) kept as JSON in a key-value
// store with 2 methods, get(key) -> string | null and set(key, string). This
// proof of concept uses localStorage; the iOS app passes UserDefaults instead.
// If storage is unavailable (private browsing, a full disk), progress just
// isn't kept between visits.
// ============================================================================
const LOCAL_STORE = {
  get(key) { try { return window.localStorage.getItem('catsByPost.parcelPaperStudy.v1:'+key); } catch (e) { return null; } },
  set(key, value) { try { window.localStorage.setItem('catsByPost.parcelPaperStudy.v1:'+key, value); } catch (e) { /* not kept */ } },
};
let progressStore = LOCAL_STORE;
let progress = PROGRESS.parse(progressStore.get(PROGRESS.KEY));
function progressSave() { progressStore.set(PROGRESS.KEY, PROGRESS.serialize(progress)); }
const migratedNightShift = PROGRESS.importLegacyNightBest(progress, Number(progressStore.get('catsByPost.nightShiftBest')));
if (migratedNightShift !== progress) { progress = migratedNightShift; progressSave(); }
function progressOpened(L) { if (progress.current !== L.id) { progress = PROGRESS.setCurrent(progress, L.id); progressSave(); } }
function progressWon(L, result) { progress = PROGRESS.won(progress, LEVELS, L.id, result); progressSave(); }   // result: { stars, turntables, ticks }
function progressDaily(key, won, result) { progress = PROGRESS.dailyAttempt(progress, key, won, result); progressSave(); }
function progressDailyHinted(key) { progress = PROGRESS.dailyHinted(progress, key); progressSave(); }
function progressPracticeOpen(seed, weekday, date, level) { progress = PROGRESS.practiceOpen(progress, seed, weekday, date, level); progressSave(); }
function progressPracticeAttempt(key, won, result) { progress = PROGRESS.practiceAttempt(progress, key, won, result); progressSave(); }
function progressPracticeHinted(key) { progress = PROGRESS.practiceHinted(progress, key); progressSave(); }
function progressNightShiftFinished(kind, weekKey, catsHome) { progress = PROGRESS.nightShiftFinished(progress, kind, weekKey, catsHome); progressSave(); }
// ============================================================================
// 43 GAME: levels (src/data/levels.json, inlined), the turntable tray and placing,
// pointer and keyboard input, the run on 300 ms ticks via grTick() (sim/10_rules),
// results, stars and hints (sim/16_score), the level list and progress
// (42b_progress), and the board drawn with the scene's own art. Add ?demo to the
// URL for the scripted scene loop, ?edit for the level editor.
// ============================================================================
const GAME_MODE = !/[?&](demo|boxroom|depot)\b/.test(location.search);   // ?boxroom: a Box Room on its own (47_boxroom)
window.__chapterReady=false;
const LEVELS = LEVELS_DATA.levels;
const G_KEEP_PROGRESS = GAME_MODE && !/[?&]edit\b/.test(location.search);   // the editor's trial runs aren't progress
// Null for the office, immutable depot data for chapter 3, generated Box Room data for dailies.
let G_ROOM = null;
let gDepotRoom = null, gDepotFlapRoom = null, gDepotFlapKey = '';
let gTrainRoom = null, gCityHallRoom = null, gParcelRoom = null, gChristmasRoom = null;
function gHandmadeRoom(L, frameOffset) {
  if(LEVELROOMS.id(L)==='christmas')return gChristmasRoom||(gChristmasRoom=CHRISTMAS.build());
  if(LEVELROOMS.id(L)==='parcel')return gParcelRoom||(gParcelRoom=PARCEL.build());
  if(LEVELROOMS.id(L)==='cityhall')return gCityHallRoom||(gCityHallRoom=CITYHALL.build());
  if(LEVELROOMS.id(L)==='train')return gTrainRoom||(gTrainRoom=TRAIN.build());
  if (LEVELROOMS.id(L) !== 'depot') return null;
  if((L.flaps||[]).length){
    const B=L.board,frames=frameOffset===undefined?(LEVELS.indexOf(L)>=0&&L.id[0]==='4'?(B.w===7?40:B.h===5?20:0):-1):frameOffset;
    const key=[B.roomX,B.roomY,B.w,B.h].join(':')+':'+L.flaps.map(f=>[f.x,f.y,f.side].join(',')).sort().join(';')+':frames'+frames;
    if(!gDepotFlapRoom||key!==gDepotFlapKey){
      const data=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEPOT_DATA)),move=EDGE_PORTAL_DATA.depotTrolleyOffset;
      if(frames>=0)data.chapterFramesOffset=frames;
      const trolley=data.furniture.find(f=>f.id==='trolley');
      trolley.anchor[0]+=move[0];trolley.anchor[1]+=move[1];
      for(const boxes of [trolley.parts,trolley.occluders||[]])for(const b of boxes){
        b[0]+=move[0];b[3]+=move[0];b[1]+=move[1];b[4]+=move[1];
      }
      data.flapKerbGaps=[];
      for(const f of L.flaps){
        const x=data.board.x+(B.roomX||0)*16+f.x*16+8,y=data.board.y+(B.roomY||0)*16+f.y*16+8;
        if(f.side==='R'&&x+8===data.board.x+data.board.w*16||f.side==='L'&&x-8===data.board.x||
          f.side==='D'&&y+8===data.board.y+data.board.h*16||f.side==='U'&&y-8===data.board.y)
          data.flapKerbGaps.push({side:f.side,at:f.side==='R'||f.side==='L'?y:x});
      }
      data.flapScene=true;gDepotFlapRoom=DEPOT.build(data);gDepotFlapKey=key;
    }
    return gDepotFlapRoom;
  }
  if (!gDepotRoom) gDepotRoom = DEPOT.build();
  return gDepotRoom;
}
// Board position, in tiles from world (0, 0): in the office, roomX/roomY count from the stone floor's back corner
// (room tile GRID_I, GRID_J); a Box Room puts its board where its data says (whole world units, so maybe half tiles).
const gBoardX = roomBoardX, gBoardY = roomBoardY;
const G_CAM_X = 26, G_CAM_Y = 3;                                 // office framing when the whole room fits (a Box Room centres)
const G_BOARD_FIT_PX = (GRID_W + GRID_H) * TILE + 32;           // widest office board (6 x 5) + 16 px each side, logical px
// The board width the default zoom fits: the office's widest, or a bigger Box Room board.
function gBoardFitPx() { const B = gm.L && gm.L.board; return G_ROOM && B ? Math.max(G_BOARD_FIT_PX, (B.w + B.h) * TILE + 32) : G_BOARD_FIT_PX; }
// The Box Room a daily puzzle is played in: the date's seed, the puzzle's board, the weekday's light, the season.
function gCollection() { return PROGRESS.collection(progress); }
function gDailyRoom(key, level) {
  const B = level.board, CB = BOXROOM.CFG.board;
  if (B.w < CB.minW || B.w > CB.maxW || B.h < CB.minH || B.h > CB.maxH) return null;   // too small for a Box Room: the office
  return BOXROOM.build(BOXROOM.seedForDate(key), B.w, B.h, key, 'daily', gCollection());
}
let gHudPx = 0, gFlapSidePx = 0;                                // measured control space, in logical pixels
let gEditorRightPx=0,gEditorBottomPx=0;
function gCityHallEditing(){return typeof edActive==='function'&&edActive()&&gm.L&&(LEVELROOMS.id(gm.L)==='cityhall'||LEVELROOMS.id(gm.L)==='parcel'||LEVELROOMS.id(gm.L)==='christmas')&&document.getElementById('editor').classList.contains('on');}
// When the whole room fits, keep it framed. Otherwise centre the board in the part of
// the screen the HUD doesn't cover, so every tile is visible and easy to tap.
function gCamera(atScale=scale) {
  const B = gm.L.board, cx = (gBoardX(B) + B.w / 2) * TILE, cy = (gBoardY(B) + B.h / 2) * TILE;
  const bx = OX + cx - cy - VIEW_CX, by = OY + (cx + cy) * 0.5 - 8 - VIEW_CY;
  const ratio=scale/atScale;
  if(gCityHallEditing()){WS.camX=Math.round(bx+gEditorRightPx*ratio/2);WS.camY=Math.round(by+gEditorBottomPx*ratio/2);return;}
  const shiftHud = gm.shift && devW > devH ? Math.round(hud.root.offsetWidth * devDpr / atScale / 2) : Math.round(gFlapSidePx * ratio / 2);
  if (Math.floor(devW/atScale)-(ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='christmas'?gFlapSidePx*ratio:0) >= ROOM_FIT_W && Math.floor(devH/atScale)-(ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='christmas'?gHudPx*ratio:0) >= ROOM_FIT_H) { WS.camX = (G_ROOM ? 0 : G_CAM_X) - shiftHud; WS.camY = (ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='cityhall'||ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='parcel'||ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='christmas') ? Math.round(gHudPx*ratio/2) : G_ROOM ? 0 : G_CAM_Y; return; }
  WS.camX = Math.round(bx) - shiftHud; WS.camY = Math.round(by + gHudPx * ratio / 2);
}
function gMeasureHud() {
  const train=gm.L&&(LEVELROOMS.id(gm.L)==='train'||LEVELROOMS.id(gm.L)==='cityhall'||LEVELROOMS.id(gm.L)==='parcel'||LEVELROOMS.id(gm.L)==='christmas');
  const hasHud=hud.root.classList.contains('on')&&(!train||hud.root.offsetHeight>0);
  const side = hasHud && devW > devH && window.innerHeight <= 520 && gm.L && ((gm.L.flaps || []).length||train);
  const on = hasHud && (devH > devW ||
    gm.L && gm.L.flaps && gm.L.flaps.length && window.innerHeight > 520 || train&&!side);
  gHudPx = on ? Math.ceil(hud.root.offsetHeight * (window.devicePixelRatio || 1) / scale) + 8 : 0;
  gFlapSidePx = side ? Math.ceil(hud.root.offsetWidth * devDpr / scale) + (train?8:16) : 0;
  const editing=gCityHallEditing(),panel=editing?document.getElementById('editor'):null;
  gEditorRightPx=editing&&devW>devH?Math.ceil((panel.offsetWidth+24)*devDpr/scale):0;
  gEditorBottomPx=editing&&devH>=devW?Math.ceil((panel.offsetHeight+24)*devDpr/scale):0;
  // Text can reflow after a level loads. Reserve both Train HUD layouts before fitting.
  if((editing||side||train&&hasHud)&&view.zoom===null&&defaultScale(devW,devH,devDpr)<scale)applyScale();
}
const G_CW = ['U', 'R', 'D', 'L'];                               // tap order (clockwise)
const G_TYPES = GR_TYPES;                                                  // plain, ginger, blue
const G_TT = { plain: TT_PLAIN, ginger: TT_GINGER, blue: TT_BLUE };        // turntable art per type
const G_NAME = { plain: 'plain', ginger: 'ginger', blue: 'blue' };          // type -> what players see
const MSG = {
  start: 'Place your turntables, then send the post.',
  running: 'The cats are on their way.',
  won: 'Delivered. Every cat is home.',
  time: 'Out of time. A cat is still travelling. Change your turntables and try again.',
  wrong: (c, b) => 'Wrong basket. A ' + c + ' cat ended up in a ' + b + ' basket. Change your turntables and try again.',
  empty: (t) => 'No ' + G_NAME[t] + ' turntables left in the tray.',
  hint: 'Hint: one turntable is locked in place. A hint costs the First post star.',
  noHint: 'Every turntable a hint would give is already down. Send the post.',
  locked: 'That turntable came from a hint, so it stays where it is.',
  gate: 'A flip-flap gate sends cats one way, then the other.',
  points: 'Railway points send cats towards one arrow, then the other. They reset on every attempt.',
  noDaily: "There's no daily puzzle today. Try free play instead.",
  dailyDone: "You've finished today's puzzle. Share your result, or try free play.",
  oneHint: 'The daily puzzle has 1 hint, and it is already on the board.',
  practiceHint: 'This Practice puzzle has used its one hint.',
  practiceChoose: 'Choose a day for a Practice puzzle.',
  practiceFinding: (day) => 'Finding a ' + day + ' puzzle…',
  practiceReplay: 'This is a saved Practice puzzle. Change your turntables and send the post.',
  practiceError: 'Could not make that puzzle. Please try a new seed.',
  copied: 'Copied your result to the clipboard.',
  done: "That's every level. Starting again from the first.",
};
// attempts: Send presses since the level was opened; hinted: a hint was used since then (sim/16_score);
// daily: { key, entry } while playing the daily puzzle (sim/17_daily), else null
// practice: { key, entry } while playing a seeded Practice puzzle (sim/19_generator), else null
const gm = { li: 0, L: null, custom: false, chutes: [], tray: { plain: 0, ginger: 0, blue: 0 }, sel: 'plain', cx: 0, cy: 0,
  mode: 'edit', sim: null, t: 0, cursor: false, slots: null, events: null, gateTrace: null, gateDust: 0,
  attempts: 0, hinted: false, daily: null, practice: null, shift: null };
// Today, for the daily puzzle: the player's local date, or ?date=YYYY-MM-DD for checking any day.
const G_DATE_ARG = (/[?&]date=(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})\b/.exec(location.search) || [])[1];
const G_TODAY = G_DATE_ARG && !Number.isNaN(DAILY.dayNumber(G_DATE_ARG)) ? G_DATE_ARG : DAILY.localKey(new Date());
const G_DAILY_HINTS = 1;
let gPracticeWorker = null, gPracticeURL = null, gPracticeToken = 0, gPracticeChoosing = false, gPracticePending = false;
const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
const hud = { root: $('hud'), pick: $('lvlPick'), send: $('btnSend'), clear: $('btnClear'), hint: $('btnHint'), next: $('btnNext'),
  status: $('status'), stars: $('stars'), share: $('btnShare'), dailyInfo: $('dailyInfo'),
  queue: $('catQueue'),
  practiceChoose: $('practiceChoose'), practiceDay: $('practiceDay'), practiceStart: $('btnPracticeStart'),
  practiceChange: $('btnPracticeChange'), practiceInfo: $('practiceInfo'),
  tray: Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('#tray button')) };

// ---- level + tray state -------------------------------------------------------------
function gLoad(i) {
  gm.li = (i + LEVELS.length) % LEVELS.length;
  gLoadLevel(LEVELS[gm.li]);
}
function gLoadLevel(level, room) {
  const errors = grValidate(level, null);
  if (errors.length) throw new RangeError('Invalid level: ' + errors.join('; '));
  if(window.__chapterReady)cpCancel();
  gPracticeCancel();
  gm.shift = null;
  gm.L = level; gm.custom = LEVELS.indexOf(level) < 0;              // editor, daily and Practice puzzles aren't level progress
  gUseRoom(room || gHandmadeRoom(level));
  gm.attempts = 0; gm.hinted = false; gm.daily = null; gm.practice = null; gPracticeChoosing = false;
  hud.stars.hidden = true; hud.next.hidden = false;   // the editor's Try it hides it again
  view.zoom = null; view.panX = 0; view.panY = 0;                 // each level opens at the default zoom
  if (devW > 1) applyScale();
  const errs = grValidate(gm.L, null);
  if (errs.length) console.warn('level ' + gm.L.id + ': ' + errs.join('; '));
  if (!room) for (const error of LEVELROOMS.check(level)) console.warn('level ' + level.id + ': ' + error);
  gm.chutes = [];
  gm.tray = { plain: gm.L.tray.plain | 0, ginger: gm.L.tray.ginger | 0, blue: gm.L.tray.blue | 0 };
  if (G_KEEP_PROGRESS && !gm.custom) progressOpened(gm.L);
  gm.sel = G_TYPES.find((t) => gm.tray[t] > 0) || 'plain';
  gm.cx = 0; gm.cy = 0; gm.mode = 'edit'; gm.sim = null;
  gStatus(level.id === '3-1' ? MSG.gate : MSG.start);
  gHud();
}
function gPieceAt(x, y) {
  const L = gm.L;
  for (const p of L.hatches || []) if (p.x === x && p.y === y) return 'hatch';
  for (const p of L.baskets || []) if (p.x === x && p.y === y) return 'basket';
  for (const p of L.dogs || []) if (p.x === x && p.y === y) return 'dog';
  for (const p of L.gates || []) if (p.x === x && p.y === y) return 'gate';
  if (tunnelPairAt(L.tunnels, x, y) >= 0) return 'tunnel';
  return null;
}
function gChuteAt(x, y) { return gm.chutes.findIndex((c) => c.x === x && c.y === y); }
function gToEdit() { if (gm.mode !== 'edit') { gm.mode = 'edit'; gm.sim = null; gHud(); } }
// Tap: place the selected turntable (pointing up), turn it clockwise, or after "left" pick it up.
function gTap(x, y) {
  if(cpActive())return;
  if (gm.shift) { nsTap(x, y); return; }
  if (gPieceAt(x, y) === 'gate') {
    const g=gm.L.gates.find(p=>p.x===x&&p.y===y);
    gStatus(((GR_DIR[g.exits[0]]+2)&3)===GR_DIR[g.exits[1]]?MSG.points:MSG.gate);return;
  }
  const tunnel = tunnelPairAt(gm.L.tunnels, x, y);
  if (tunnel >= 0) { gStatus('Linked ' + gm.L.tunnels[tunnel].symbol + ' tunnel: its partner is highlighted. Cats keep their direction.'); return; }
  if (gm.mode === 'running' || gPracticePending) return;
  gToEdit();
  if (gPieceAt(x, y)) return;
  const k = gChuteAt(x, y);
  if (k >= 0) {
    const c = gm.chutes[k], d = G_CW.indexOf(c.dir);
    if (c.locked) { gStatus(MSG.locked); return; }
    if (d === 3) { gm.chutes.splice(k, 1); gm.tray[c.type]++; }
    else c.dir = G_CW[d + 1];
  } else {
    let t = gm.sel;
    if (gm.tray[t] <= 0) t = G_TYPES.find((u) => gm.tray[u] > 0);
    if (!t) { gStatus(MSG.empty(gm.sel)); return; }
    gm.tray[t]--; gm.sel = gm.tray[t] > 0 ? t : (G_TYPES.find((u) => gm.tray[u] > 0) || t);
    gm.chutes.push({ x, y, type: t, dir: 'U' });
  }
  gStatus(MSG.start);
  gHud();
}
// ---- the run: rules ticks become per-cat traces; boardanim poses them every frame ------------
const G_TICK = GR_TICK_MS / 1000;                                   // seconds per rules tick
const G_TRACES = [], G_POSES = [];                                  // one per cat, reused between runs
function gTrace(i, ticks) {                                         // room for a whole run of the level's time limit
  while (G_TRACES.length <= i) { G_TRACES.push(BOARDANIM.makeTrace(ticks + 2)); G_POSES.push(makeBoardCatPose()); }
  if (G_TRACES[i].max < ticks + 2) G_TRACES[i] = BOARDANIM.makeTrace(ticks + 2);
  return G_TRACES[i];
}
function gSend() {
  if(cpActive())return;
  if (gm.shift) { nsSend(); return; }
  if (gPracticePending) return;
  if (gm.mode === 'running') { gToEdit(); gStatus(MSG.start); return; }        // Stop
  const s = gm.sim = grCreate(gm.L, gm.chutes);
  gm.t = 0; gm.mode = 'running'; gm.slots = {}; gm.events = []; gm.attempts++; hud.stars.hidden = true;
  gm.gateTrace = s.gates.length ? BOARDANIM.makeGateTrace(s.n * s.timeLimit) : null; gm.gateDust = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < s.n; i++) { const tr = gTrace(i, s.timeLimit); BOARDANIM.reset(tr); tr.rel = Infinity; tr.pop = G_TICK; }
  gTickRules();                                                              // tick 0: the first cats pop up
  gStatus(MSG.running);
  gHud();
}
function gTickRules() {
  const s = gm.sim, t = s.tick;
  grTick(s);
  if (gm.gateTrace) BOARDANIM.recordGates(gm.gateTrace, s, (t + 1) * G_TICK);
  for (let i = 0; i < s.n; i++) {
    const tr = G_TRACES[i], ev = s.ev[i];
    if (ev === GE_RELEASED) {
      tr.big = !!s.big[i]; tr.rel = (t + 1) * G_TICK; tr.tick = G_TICK; tr.pop = G_TICK; tr.hx = s.x[i]; tr.hy = s.y[i]; tr.hatchDir = s.dir[i];
      tr.ox = gTX(0); tr.oy = gTY(0); tr.bw = s.W; tr.bh = s.H; tr.coat = s.coat[i] === GR_GINGER ? COAT_GINGER : COAT_BLUE;
      tr.look = i & 3; tr.blinkPhase = (i * 13) % 40;
    } else if (ev === GE_MOVED || ev === GE_DELIVERED || ev === GE_WRONG || ev === GE_STUCK || ev === GE_TUNNEL || ev === GE_WAIT) {
      BOARDANIM.record(tr, s.px[i], s.py[i], s.pdir[i], s.x[i], s.y[i], s.dir[i], ev, s.bounced[i]);
      BOARDANIM.recordDestination(tr, s, i);
      if (ev === GE_DELIVERED) {                                   // two cats can share a basket: the second snuggles in beside
        if (tr.homeKind !== 'flap') {
          const key = s.x[i] + ',' + s.y[i], k = gm.slots[key] | 0;
          gm.slots[key] = k + 1;
          if (k > 0) { tr.homeDX = 3; tr.homeDY = -3; }
        }
        gm.events.push({ t: (t + 1) * G_TICK + BOARDANIM.T_NAP, x: gCX(s.x[i]) + tr.homeDX, y: gCY(s.y[i]) + tr.homeDY, col: s.coat[i] === GR_GINGER ? RED : BLUE, done: false });
      }
    }
  }
  if (s.outcome !== GO_RUNNING) for (let i = 0; i < s.n; i++) {
    const tr = G_TRACES[i];
    if (tr.end === BOARDANIM.END_RUNNING && tr.rel !== Infinity) tr.end = BOARDANIM.END_STOPPED;
  }
  gHudQueue();
}
function gClear() {
  if (gm.shift) { nsClear(); return; }
  if (gm.mode === 'running' || gPracticePending) return;
  gToEdit();
  for (const c of gm.chutes) if (!c.locked) gm.tray[c.type]++;
  gm.chutes = gm.chutes.filter((c) => c.locked);                   // hints stay
  gStatus(MSG.start);
  gHud();
}
// The daily puzzle for G_TODAY (false if there isn't one). Attempts and the hint carry over a reload.
function gLoadDaily() {
  const entry = DAILY.puzzleFor(DAILY_DATA, G_TODAY);
  if (!entry) { gStatus(MSG.noDaily); gHud(); return false; }
  gLoadLevel(entry.level, gDailyRoom(G_TODAY, entry.level));
  gm.daily = { key: G_TODAY, entry }; hud.next.hidden = true;
  const day = G_KEEP_PROGRESS ? progress.daily[G_TODAY] : null;
  if (day) {
    gm.attempts = day.attempts.length;
    if (day.hinted && !day.done) { const r = SCORE.applyHint(gm.L, gm.chutes, gm.tray); if (r) { gm.chutes = r.placements; gm.tray = r.tray; } gm.hinted = true; }
    if (day.done) { gShowStars(day.stars); gStatus(MSG.dailyDone); }
  }
  gHud();
  return true;
}
const gDailyDay = () => (gm.daily && G_KEEP_PROGRESS ? progress.daily[gm.daily.key] : null);
function gPracticeCancel() {
  gPracticeToken++;
  if (gPracticeWorker) gPracticeWorker.terminate();
  if (gPracticeURL) URL.revokeObjectURL(gPracticeURL);
  gPracticeWorker = null; gPracticeURL = null; gPracticePending = false;
}
function gPracticeSeed() {
  if (typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && crypto.getRandomValues) return crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint32Array(1))[0];
  return Math.floor(Math.random() * 4294967296);
}
function gPracticeRequest(seed, weekday, date) {
  gPracticeCancel();
  const token = gPracticeToken;
  gPracticeChoosing = true; gPracticePending = true;
  hud.practiceDay.value = String(weekday);
  gStatus(MSG.practiceFinding(DAILY.WEEKDAYS[weekday])); gHud();
  const accept = (puzzle) => {
    if (token !== gPracticeToken) return;
    gPracticeCancel();
    const room = BOXROOM.build(seed, puzzle.level.board.w, puzzle.level.board.h, date, 'daily', gCollection());
    gLoadLevel(puzzle.level, room);
    const key = weekday + ':' + seed;
    if (G_KEEP_PROGRESS) progressPracticeOpen(seed, weekday, date, puzzle.level);
    gm.practice = { key, entry: puzzle, date };
    const saved = G_KEEP_PROGRESS ? progress.practice.records[key] : null;
    if (saved) {
      gm.attempts = saved.attempts.length;
      gm.hinted = saved.hinted;
      if (saved.hinted) {
        const hint = SCORE.applyHint(gm.L, gm.chutes, gm.tray);
        if (hint) { gm.chutes = hint.placements; gm.tray = hint.tray; }
      }
      if (saved.done) { gShowStars(saved.stars); gStatus(MSG.practiceReplay); }
    }
    if (!saved || !saved.done) gStatus(MSG.start);
    gHud();
  };
  const fail = (message) => {
    if (token !== gPracticeToken) return;
    gPracticeCancel(); gPracticeChoosing = true;
    gStatus(MSG.practiceError + (message ? ' ' + message : '')); gHud();
  };
  const stored = G_KEEP_PROGRESS ? progress.practice.records[weekday + ':' + seed] : null;
  if (stored && stored.level) {
    try {
      if (!grValidate(stored.level, stored.level.solution).length) {
        setTimeout(() => accept({ seed, weekday, level: stored.level }), 0);
        return;
      }
    } catch (e) { /* corrupted saved level: regenerate from the seed */ }
  }
  if (typeof Worker === 'undefined') {
    setTimeout(() => { try { accept(PUZZLEGEN.practice(DAILY_SETTINGS, seed, weekday)); } catch (e) { fail(e.message); } }, 0);
    return;
  }
  gPracticeURL = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([PRACTICE_WORKER_SOURCE], { type: 'text/javascript' }));
  gPracticeWorker = new Worker(gPracticeURL);
  gPracticeWorker.onmessage = (event) => {
    if (token !== gPracticeToken) return;
    const m = event.data;
    if (m.type === 'ready') accept(m.puzzle);
    else if (m.type === 'error') fail(m.message);
    else if (m.type === 'progress') gStatus(MSG.practiceFinding(DAILY.WEEKDAYS[weekday]) + ' ' + m.tries.toLocaleString() + ' tries so far.');
  };
  gPracticeWorker.onerror = () => fail('The generator worker stopped.');
  gPracticeWorker.postMessage({ seed, weekday, allowedPieces: DAILY_SETTINGS.allowedPieces });
}
function gPracticeNew(weekday) {
  let seed = gPracticeSeed(), n = 0;
  while (G_KEEP_PROGRESS && progress.practice.records[weekday + ':' + seed] && n++ < 20) seed = gPracticeSeed();
  gPracticeRequest(seed, weekday, PUZZLEGEN.dateForWeekday(G_TODAY, weekday));
}
// Hint: the next turntable of the stored solution, the right way round, locked in place.
function gHint() {
  if (gm.mode === 'running' || gPracticePending) return;
  if (gm.daily && gm.hinted) { gStatus(MSG.oneHint); return; }
  if (gm.practice && gm.hinted) { gStatus(MSG.practiceHint); return; }
  gToEdit();
  const r = SCORE.applyHint(gm.L, gm.chutes, gm.tray);
  if (!r) { gStatus(MSG.noHint); return; }
  gm.chutes = r.placements; gm.tray = r.tray; gm.hinted = true;
  if (gm.daily && G_KEEP_PROGRESS) progressDailyHinted(gm.daily.key);
  if (gm.practice && G_KEEP_PROGRESS) progressPracticeHinted(gm.practice.key);
  if (gm.tray[gm.sel] <= 0) gm.sel = G_TYPES.find((u) => gm.tray[u] > 0) || gm.sel;
  gm.cx = r.hint.x; gm.cy = r.hint.y; gm.cursor = true;
  gStatus(MSG.hint);
  gHud();
}
function gNext() {
  if(cpActive())return;
  if (gm.mode !== 'won') return;
  if (gm.practice) { gPracticeNew(gm.practice.entry.weekday); return; }
  if(window.__chapterReady&&cpNext())return;
  const last = gm.li === LEVELS.length - 1;
  gLoad(gm.li + 1);
  if (last) gStatus(MSG.done);
}
// The run has ended (rule 8: every cat home or puzzled, or time up): show the result.
function gFinish() {
  const s = gm.sim;
  gm.mode = s.outcome === GO_WON ? 'won' : 'lost';
  const result = s.outcome === GO_WON ? { stars: SCORE.stars(gm.L, gm.chutes.length, gm.attempts, gm.hinted), turntables: gm.chutes.length, ticks: s.endTick } : null;
  if (gm.daily && G_KEEP_PROGRESS) progressDaily(gm.daily.key, !!result, result);   // nothing changes once the day is solved
  if (gm.practice && G_KEEP_PROGRESS) progressPracticeAttempt(gm.practice.key, !!result, result);
  if (result) {
    gStatus(MSG.won); gShowStars(result.stars);
    if (G_KEEP_PROGRESS && !gm.custom) progressWon(gm.L, result);
  }
  else if (s.outcome === GO_WRONG) {
    const lc = GR_COATS[s.coat[s.wrong]];
    gStatus(s.homeFlap[s.wrong] >= 0 ? 'Wrong flap. A ' + lc + ' cat needs a ' + lc + ' door. Change your turntables and try again.' : MSG.wrong(lc, lc === 'ginger' ? 'blue' : 'ginger'));
  } else gStatus(MSG.time);
  gHud();
}
// Called every fixed 60 Hz step from simulate(). The clock keeps running after the run
// ends, so the cats finish arriving, curl up and nap (or sit puzzled) until the next edit.
function gUpdate() {
  if (gm.shift) { nsUpdate(); return; }
  if (gm.mode === 'edit' || !gm.sim) return;
  gm.t += DT;
  const s = gm.sim;
  if (gm.mode === 'running') {
    while (s.outcome === GO_RUNNING && gm.t >= s.tick * G_TICK) gTickRules();
    if (s.outcome !== GO_RUNNING && gm.t >= s.tick * G_TICK) gFinish();
  }
  for (const e of gm.events) if (!e.done && gm.t >= e.t) { e.done = true; spawnSparkles(e.x, e.y, 6, e.col); spawnHeart(e.x, e.y, 9); }
  if(window.__chapterReady&&typeof xcMaybeFinale==='function')xcMaybeFinale();
  const gt = gm.gateTrace;
  if (gt) while (gm.gateDust < gt.n && gm.t >= gt.time[gm.gateDust]) {
    const g = gm.L.gates[gt.gate[gm.gateDust++]];
    spawnPuff(gCX(g.x), gCY(g.y), 1, 3);
  }
}
// The cats the Postmistress can watch: those out on the board and moving, posed from their traces
// (not from the last drawn frame, so the simulation never depends on drawing).
const G_WATCH_OUT = makeBoardCatPose();
function gWatch() {
  const W = WS.watch, o = G_WATCH_OUT;
  W.n = 0;
  if(cpActive()){cpWatch(W,o);return;}
  if (gm.shift) return;
  if (!gRunning()) return;
  for (let i = 0; i < gm.sim.n && W.n < W.xs.length; i++) {
    if (G_TRACES[i].rel === Infinity) continue;
    BOARDANIM.pose(G_TRACES[i], gm.t, o);
    if(paperEnabled&&G_TRACES[i].homeKind==='flap')paperBoardFlapPose(o,G_TRACES[i],gm.t);
    if (o.anim === BOARDANIM.A.HIDDEN || o.anim === BOARDANIM.A.NAP) continue;
    W.xs[W.n] = o.x; W.ys[W.n] = o.y; W.n++;
  }
}

// ---- HUD --------------------------------------------------------------------------
function gStatus(m) { hud.status.textContent = m; }
// The stars this win earned, under the result.
const G_STARS = [['delivered', 'Delivered'], ['tidy', 'Tidy'], ['firstPost', 'First post']];
function gShowStars(st) {
  hud.stars.textContent = '';
  for (const [k, name] of G_STARS) {
    const e = document.createElement('span');
    e.className = st[k] ? 'star on' : 'star'; e.textContent = (st[k] ? '★ ' : '☆ ') + name;
    hud.stars.appendChild(e);
  }
  hud.stars.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Stars earned: ' + (G_STARS.filter(([k]) => st[k]).map(([, n]) => n).join(', ') || 'none'));
  hud.stars.hidden = false;
}
// The level list: every level with its best stars; locked levels are listed but can't be picked.
const gStarText = (st) => '★'.repeat(PROGRESS.starCount(st)) + '☆'.repeat(3 - PROGRESS.starCount(st));
const G_QUEUE_FACES = {};
function gQueueFace(coat) {
  if (G_QUEUE_FACES[coat]) return G_QUEUE_FACES[coat];
  const rows = CB_HEAD_A.open, w = rows[0].length + 2, h = rows.length + 2;
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas'); canvas.width = w; canvas.height = h;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d'), frame = ctx.createImageData(w, h), pixels = new Uint32Array(frame.data.buffer);
  const key = coat === 'ginger' ? KEY_GIN : KEY_BLU_HEAD;
  for (let y = 0; y < rows.length; y++) for (let x = 0; x < rows[y].length; x++) if (rows[y][x] !== '.') {
    for (let dy = 0; dy < 3; dy++) for (let dx = 0; dx < 3; dx++) pixels[(y + dy) * w + x + dx] = PAL32[C.OUT];
  }
  for (let y = 0; y < rows.length; y++) for (let x = 0; x < rows[y].length; x++) if (rows[y][x] !== '.')
    pixels[(y + 1) * w + x + 1] = PAL32[key[rows[y].charCodeAt(x)]];
  ctx.putImageData(frame, 0, 0);
  return G_QUEUE_FACES[coat] = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
}
function gQueueLabel(hatches, q) {
  if (hatches.length === 1) return 'Hatch';
  if (hatches.length !== 2) return 'Hatch ' + (q + 1);
  const a = hatches[q], b = hatches[1 - q], dx = (a.x - a.y) - (b.x - b.y);
  return dx ? (dx < 0 ? 'Left hatch' : 'Right hatch') :
    (a.x + a.y < b.x + b.y ? 'Back hatch' : 'Front hatch');
}
function gHudQueue() {
  if (gm.shift) { hud.root.classList.remove('christmas-rush'); nsHudQueue(); return; }
  hud.queue.classList.remove('shift-queue');
  const hatches = gm.L.hatches || [];
  if(LEVELROOMS.id(gm.L)==='christmas'&&hatches.length>=4)hud.root.classList.add('christmas-rush');else hud.root.classList.remove('christmas-rush');
  hud.queue.hidden = !hatches.length || gPracticePending;
  if (hud.queue.hidden) return;
  hud.queue.textContent = '';
  hatches.forEach((h, q) => {
    const name = gQueueLabel(hatches, q);
    const group = document.createElement('div'); group.className = 'queue-hatch';
    group.setAttribute('role', 'group'); group.setAttribute('aria-label', name + ', column ' + (h.x + 1) + ', row ' + (h.y + 1));
    const label = document.createElement('span'); label.className = 'queue-label'; label.textContent = name + ' →';
    group.appendChild(label);
    const list = document.createElement('ol'); list.className = 'queue-faces';
    const simHatch = gm.sim && gm.mode !== 'edit' ? gm.sim.hatches[q] : null;
    const next = simHatch ? h.cats.findIndex((_, j) => gm.sim.st[simHatch.first + j] === GL_WAIT) : 0;
    h.cats.forEach((cat, j) => {
      const coat = grCatCoat(cat) === GR_GINGER ? 'ginger' : 'blue';
      const big = grCatBig(cat);
      const released = simHatch && gm.sim.st[simHatch.first + j] !== GL_WAIT;
      const item = document.createElement('li'); item.className = 'queue-cat' + (big ? ' queue-big' : '') + (released ? ' released' : j === next ? ' next' : '');
      item.dataset.order = String(j + 1);
      item.setAttribute('aria-label', (j + 1) + '. ' + (big ? 'big ' : '') + coat + ' cat' + (released ? ', released' : j === next ? ', next' : ''));
      const face = document.createElement('img'); face.src = gQueueFace(coat); face.alt = '';
      item.appendChild(face); if (big) { const badge = document.createElement('span'); badge.className = 'queue-size'; badge.textContent = 'Big'; item.appendChild(badge); } list.appendChild(item);
    });
    group.appendChild(list); hud.queue.appendChild(group);
  });
}
function gLevelPicker() {
  const sel = hud.pick, today = DAILY.puzzleFor(DAILY_DATA, G_TODAY);
  sel.textContent = '';
  if (G_KEEP_PROGRESS) {                                            // today's daily puzzle first
    const day = progress.daily[G_TODAY];
    const o = new Option(today ? 'Daily #' + today.number + ' · ' + today.weekday + (day && day.done ? '  ' + gStarText(day.stars) : '') : 'No daily puzzle today',
      'daily', false, !!gm.daily);
    o.disabled = !today; sel.add(o);
  }
  if (G_KEEP_PROGRESS) {
    sel.add(new Option(gm.practice ? 'Practice · ' + DAILY.WEEKDAYS[gm.practice.entry.weekday] : 'Practice · choose difficulty',
      'practice', false, gPracticeChoosing || !!gm.practice));
    sel.add(new Option('Night Shift', 'nightshift', false, !!gm.shift));
    if (progress.practice.order.length) {
      const history = document.createElement('optgroup'); history.label = 'Practice history';
      for (const key of progress.practice.order.slice().reverse()) {
        const record = progress.practice.records[key];
        history.appendChild(new Option(DAILY.WEEKDAYS[record.weekday] + ' · seed ' + record.seed +
          (record.done ? '  ' + gStarText(record.stars) : ''), 'practice:' + key));
      }
      sel.add(history);
    }
  }
  if (gm.custom && !gm.daily && !gm.practice && !gm.shift) sel.add(new Option(gm.L.id + '  ' + gm.L.title, '-1', true, !gPracticeChoosing));
  let group = null, groupId = null;
  LEVELS.forEach((L, i) => {
    const chapter = CHAPTERS.group(L);
    if (chapter.id !== groupId) {
      groupId = chapter.id; group = document.createElement('optgroup');
      group.label = chapter.title; sel.add(group);
    }
    const best = G_KEEP_PROGRESS ? progress.levels[L.id] : null, open = !G_KEEP_PROGRESS || PROGRESS.isUnlocked(progress, LEVELS, i);
    const o = new Option(L.id + '  ' + L.title + (best ? '  ' + gStarText(best.stars) : open ? '' : '  (locked)'), String(i), false,
      !gm.custom && !gm.daily && !gm.practice && !gm.shift && !gPracticeChoosing && i === gm.li);
    o.disabled = !open;
    group.appendChild(o);
  });
  sel.disabled = gm.mode === 'running' && !gm.shift;
}
function gHud() {
  gSyncSrc();
  gLevelPicker();
  gHudQueue();
  if(window.__chapterReady)cpHud();
  if (gm.shift) { nsHud(); return; }
  if (window.__nsReady) nsHideHud();
  if (devW > 1) requestAnimationFrame(gMeasureHud);             // after the text reflows
  for (const b of hud.tray) {
    const t = b.dataset.type, n = gm.tray[t], total = gm.L.tray[t];
    b.querySelector('b').textContent = '×' + n;
    b.hidden = total === 0;
    b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(t === gm.sel));
    b.disabled = gm.mode === 'running' || gPracticePending;
  }
  hud.send.textContent = gm.mode === 'running' ? 'Stop' : 'Send the post';
  hud.send.disabled = gPracticePending;
  hud.clear.disabled = gm.mode === 'running' || gPracticePending || !gm.chutes.some((c) => !c.locked);
  hud.hint.disabled = gm.mode === 'running' || gPracticePending || !SCORE.nextHint(gm.L, gm.chutes) || ((!!gm.daily || !!gm.practice) && gm.hinted);
  hud.next.textContent = gm.practice ? 'Next puzzle' : !gm.custom&&(gm.L.id==='4-15'||gm.L.id==='5-15') ? 'Finish chapter' : 'Next level';
  hud.next.disabled = gm.mode !== 'won' || gPracticeChoosing || gPracticePending;
  hud.practiceChange.hidden = !gm.practice || gm.mode !== 'won';
  hud.practiceChoose.hidden = !gPracticeChoosing;
  hud.practiceStart.disabled = gPracticePending;
  hud.practiceInfo.hidden = !gm.practice;
  if (gm.practice) hud.practiceInfo.textContent = 'Practice · ' + DAILY.WEEKDAYS[gm.practice.entry.weekday] + ' · seed ' + gm.practice.entry.seed;
  const day = gDailyDay();
  hud.share.hidden = !(day && day.done);
  hud.dailyInfo.hidden = !gm.daily;
  if (gm.daily) {
    const n = DAILY.streak((k) => PROGRESS.dailySolved(progress, k), G_TODAY);
    hud.dailyInfo.textContent = 'Daily #' + gm.daily.entry.number + ' · ' + gm.daily.entry.weekday + ' · ' +
      (G_KEEP_PROGRESS ? 'Streak: ' + n + (n === 1 ? ' day' : ' days') : 'Hints: ' + G_DAILY_HINTS);
  }
}
function gCopyText(text) {
  const fallback = () => {                                          // older browsers, or no clipboard permission
    const ta = document.createElement('textarea');
    ta.value = text; ta.setAttribute('readonly', ''); ta.style.position = 'fixed'; ta.style.opacity = '0';
    document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select();
    let ok = false; try { ok = document.execCommand('copy'); } catch (e) { ok = false; }
    ta.remove();
    gStatus(ok ? MSG.copied : text);
  };
  if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => gStatus(MSG.copied), fallback);
  else fallback();
}
// Share either the daily puzzle or a finished Night Shift, without showing a route or solution.
function gShare() {
  if (gm.shift) {
    if (gm.shift.phase === 'ended') gCopyText(NIGHTSHIFT_META.shareText(gm.shift.run, gm.shift.catsHome, gm.shift.round));
    return;
  }
  const day = gDailyDay();
  if (day && day.done) gCopyText(DAILY.shareText(gm.daily.entry, day.attempts, day.turntables, day.stars));
}
hud.send.addEventListener('click', gSend);
hud.clear.addEventListener('click', gClear);
hud.hint.addEventListener('click', gHint);
hud.next.addEventListener('click', gNext);
hud.practiceStart.addEventListener('click', () => gPracticeNew(+hud.practiceDay.value));
hud.practiceChange.addEventListener('click', () => {
  gPracticeChoosing = true; hud.practiceDay.value = String(gm.practice.entry.weekday);
  gStatus(MSG.practiceChoose); gHud(); hud.practiceDay.focus();
});
hud.pick.addEventListener('change', () => {
  if (hud.pick.value === 'nightshift') { nsLoad(); return; }
  if (hud.pick.value === 'practice') { gPracticeChoosing = true; gStatus(MSG.practiceChoose); gHud(); return; }
  if (hud.pick.value.startsWith('practice:')) {
    const record = progress.practice.records[hud.pick.value.slice(9)];
    if (record) gPracticeRequest(record.seed, record.weekday, record.date);
    return;
  }
  if (hud.pick.value === 'daily') { gLoadDaily(); return; }
  const i = +hud.pick.value; if (i >= 0) { gLoad(i); if(window.__chapterReady)cpPicked(); }
});
hud.share.addEventListener('click', gShare);
for (const b of hud.tray) b.addEventListener('click', () => { if (gm.shift || gm.mode !== 'running') { gm.sel = b.dataset.type; gHud(); } });

// ---- input: diamond-accurate picking + keyboard -------------------------------------------
// Screen -> logical pixel -> world floor point (z = 0) -> tile. Uses the same sample
// point as the rasteriser, so a tap on a drawn tile pixel picks exactly that tile.
// Taps resolve to the nearest tile centre on the board: a point on the board picks
// its tile; a point up to one tile outside snaps to the nearest edge tile.
function gPick(clientX, clientY) {
  const r=screen.getBoundingClientRect();if(r.width<=0||r.height<=0)return -1;
  const lx = Math.floor((clientDeviceX(clientX,r) - dstX) / scale), ly = Math.floor((clientDeviceY(clientY,r) - dstY) / scale);
  const a = (lx + viewX - OX + 0.5) * 0.5, b = ly + viewY - OY, B = gm.L.board;
  const fx = (b + a) / TILE - gBoardX(B), fy = (b - a) / TILE - gBoardY(B);    // floor point, in board tiles
  if (fx < -1 || fy < -1 || fx > B.w + 1 || fy > B.h + 1) return -1;          // more than a tile away
  const x = clamp(Math.floor(fx), 0, B.w - 1), y = clamp(Math.floor(fy), 0, B.h - 1);
  return y * B.w + x;
}
function gHover(clientX, clientY) {
  if(cpActive())return;
  const k = gPick(clientX, clientY);
  if (k >= 0) { gm.cx = k % gm.L.board.w; gm.cy = Math.floor(k / gm.L.board.w); gm.cursor = true; }
  screen.style.cursor = k >= 0 ? 'pointer' : 'default';
}
function gTapAt(clientX, clientY) {
  if(cpActive())return;
  const k = gPick(clientX, clientY);
  if (k < 0) return;
  gm.cx = k % gm.L.board.w; gm.cy = Math.floor(k / gm.L.board.w); gm.cursor = true;
  gTap(gm.cx, gm.cy);
}
window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
  if(cpActive())return;
  if (!GAME_MODE || edActive() || atlasEl.classList.contains('on')) return;
  if (e.target && (e.target.tagName === 'INPUT' || e.target.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' || e.target.tagName === 'SELECT')) return;
  const onButton = e.target && e.target.tagName === 'BUTTON';
  const B = gm.L.board;
  let used = true;
  switch (e.key) {
    case 'ArrowUp': gm.cy = Math.max(0, gm.cy - 1); gm.cursor = true; break;
    case 'ArrowDown': gm.cy = Math.min(B.h - 1, gm.cy + 1); gm.cursor = true; break;
    case 'ArrowLeft': gm.cx = Math.max(0, gm.cx - 1); gm.cursor = true; break;
    case 'ArrowRight': gm.cx = Math.min(B.w - 1, gm.cx + 1); gm.cursor = true; break;
    case 'Enter': if (onButton) return; gm.cursor = true; gTap(gm.cx, gm.cy); break;
    case ' ': if (onButton) return; gSend(); break;
    case '1': case '2': case '3': if (gm.shift || gm.mode !== 'running') { gm.sel = G_TYPES[+e.key - 1]; gHud(); } break;
    case 'n': case 'N': gNext(); break;
    case 'h': case 'H': gHint(); break;
    default: used = false;
  }
  if (used) e.preventDefault();
});

// ---- drawing ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function gTX(x) { return (gBoardX(gm.L.board) + x) * TILE; }
function gTY(y) { return (gBoardY(gm.L.board) + y) * TILE; }
function gCX(x) { return gTX(x) + 8; }
function gCY(y) { return gTY(y) + 8; }
// ---- drawing: the level, the turntables and the run feed the shared board view (32_board) ------
const G_SRC = makeBoardSrc(32), G_GALLERY = [];                       // full editor capacity, reserved before any frame
G_SRC.rails = true;
for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) G_SRC.queues.push({ coats: new Uint8Array(8), n: 0 });
// Rebuild the piece lists when the level, the editor or the turntables change (not every frame).
function gSyncSrc() {
  const L = gm.L, s = G_SRC;
  s.ox = gTX(0); s.oy = gTY(0); s.w = L.board.w; s.h = L.board.h;
  s.largeBaskets=false;for(const h of L.hatches||[])for(const cat of h.cats||[])if(cat&&cat.big===true)s.largeBaskets=true;
  if(gm.shift&&gm.shift.current)for(const big of gm.shift.current.sim.big)if(big)s.largeBaskets=true;
  s.rails = ACTIVE_ROOM.id !== 'boxroom';                        // handmade boards may be smaller than their stone floor
  s.hatches.length = 0; s.baskets.length = 0; s.dogs.length = 0; s.turntables.length = 0; s.gates.length = 0; s.edgeFlaps.length = 0; s.tunnels.length = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < (L.flaps || []).length; i++) {
    const f = L.flaps[i]; s.edgeFlaps.push({ x: f.x, y: f.y, side: GR_DIR[f.side], coat: GR_COAT[f.coat], index: i });
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < (L.tunnels || []).length; i++) {
    const t = L.tunnels[i], symbol = GR_TUNNEL_SYMBOLS.indexOf(t.symbol);
    s.tunnels.push({ x: t.a.x, y: t.a.y, pair: i, symbol }, { x: t.b.x, y: t.b.y, pair: i, symbol });
  }
  for (const h of L.hatches || []) {
    const dir=GR_DIR[h.dir],cueInset=L.hatches.some(other=>other!==h&&other.x===h.x+GR_DX[dir]&&other.y===h.y+GR_DY[dir])?1:0;
    s.hatches.push({ x: h.x, y: h.y, dir, cueAlpha: 1, cueFrom: 0, cuePausedEmpty: false, cueInset });
  }
  for (const b of L.baskets || []) s.baskets.push({ x: b.x, y: b.y, coat: b.coat === 'ginger' ? COAT_GINGER : COAT_BLUE });
  for (const d of L.dogs || []) s.dogs.push({ x: d.x, y: d.y });
  for (const g of L.gates || []) s.gates.push({ x: g.x, y: g.y, a: GR_DIR[g.exits[0]], b: GR_DIR[g.exits[1]], pose: BOARDANIM.makeGateOut() });
  for (const c of gm.chutes) s.turntables.push({ x: c.x, y: c.y, dir: GR_DIR[c.dir], kind: G_TT[c.type], locked: !!c.locked });
  while (s.queues.length < s.hatches.length) s.queues.push({ coats: new Uint8Array(8), n: 0 });
  if(ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='depot')depotSyncBaskets(s);
}
function gRunning() { return gm.sim && gm.mode !== 'edit'; }
// Every frame: the cats' clocks, the queues on the hatches, the cursor.
function gPrepareSrc() {
  if (gm.shift) { nsPrepareSrc(); return; }
  const s = G_SRC, run = gRunning();
  s.hatchCues=!(window.__chapterReady&&cpActive());
  s.hatchNumbers = false; s.tunnelFocus = gm.cursor ? tunnelPairAt(gm.L.tunnels, gm.cx, gm.cy) : -1;
  s.extras.length = 0; s.locked.length = 0; s.eventTiles.length = 0; s.eventFrame = 0;
  s.gallery = G_GALLERY.length ? G_GALLERY : null;
  s.n = run ? gm.sim.n : 0;
  for (let k = 0; k < s.gates.length; k++) BOARDANIM.gatePose(run ? gm.gateTrace : null, k, gm.t, s.gates[k].pose);
  for (let i = 0; i < s.n; i++) { s.traces[i] = G_TRACES[i]; s.poses[i] = G_POSES[i]; s.times[i] = G_TRACES[i].rel === Infinity ? -1 : gm.t; }
  const hatches = gm.L.hatches || [];
  for (let q = 0; q < hatches.length; q++) {                       // the cats still to come, next one out first
    const h = hatches[q], Q = s.queues[q];
    const H0=run?gm.sim.hatches[q]:null,tr=H0?G_TRACES[H0.first]:null;
    s.hatches[q].cueAlpha=!h.cats.length?0:run?hatchCueFade(gm.t-(tr.rel-tr.pop)):1;
    Q.n = 0;
    if (!run) { for (const c of h.cats) if (Q.n < 8) Q.coats[Q.n++] = grCatCoat(c) === GR_GINGER ? COAT_GINGER : COAT_BLUE; continue; }
    const H = gm.sim.hatches[q];
    for (let j = 0; j < H.count && Q.n < 8; j++) if (gm.sim.st[H.first + j] === GL_WAIT) Q.coats[Q.n++] = gm.sim.coat[H.first + j] === GR_GINGER ? COAT_GINGER : COAT_BLUE;
  }
  s.cursor = gm.cursor && gm.mode !== 'running' ? gm.cy * gm.L.board.w + gm.cx : -1;
}
if (GAME_MODE) {
  gLoad(G_KEEP_PROGRESS ? PROGRESS.startIndex(progress, LEVELS) : 0);
  if (G_KEEP_PROGRESS && (/[?&]daily\b/.test(location.search) || G_DATE_ARG)) gLoadDaily();   // ?daily, or ?date= to check any day
  if (G_KEEP_PROGRESS && /[?&]practice\b/.test(location.search)) {
    const seedArg = (/[?&]seed=(\d+)\b/.exec(location.search) || [])[1];
    const dayArg = (/[?&]weekday=(\d)\b/.exec(location.search) || [])[1];
    if (seedArg !== undefined && dayArg !== undefined && +seedArg <= 0xffffffff && +dayArg <= 6)
      gPracticeRequest(+seedArg, +dayArg, PUZZLEGEN.dateForWeekday(G_TODAY, +dayArg));
    else { gPracticeChoosing = true; gStatus(MSG.practiceChoose); gHud(); }
  }
  if (G_KEEP_PROGRESS) hud.root.classList.add('on');
}
// Night Shift's live Box Room controls. The deterministic rules stay in sim/20_nightshift.
const nsui = { time: 0, ending: 0, eventFrames: 0, record: null, playback: null, lastTick: -1, sleepSprites: new WeakMap(), walkiesFrom: null,
  savedEnd: false, unlocked: [] };
const nsEl = {
  pause: $('btnShiftPause'), fast: $('btnShiftFast'), next: $('btnShiftNext'), restartShift: $('btnShiftRestart'), weekly: $('btnShiftWeekly'), fresh: $('btnShiftNew'),
  info: $('shiftInfo'), result: $('shiftResult'), cards: $('shiftCards'), ending: $('shiftEnding'), debug: $('shiftDebug'),
  seed: $('shiftDebugSeed'), restart: $('shiftDebugRestart'), settings: $('shiftDebugSettings'), apply: $('shiftDebugApply'),
  round: $('shiftDebugRound'), skip: $('shiftDebugSkip'), record: $('shiftDebugRecord'), replay: $('shiftDebugReplay'),
  recording: $('shiftDebugRecording'), readout: $('shiftDebugReadout'), status: $('shiftDebugStatus'),
};
window.__nsReady = true;
const NS_DEBUG = /[?&]debug\b/.test(location.search);
const NS_TRACES = [], NS_POSES = [];
const nsClone = (value) => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
const NS_CARDS = {
  extra: ['Extra turntables', '+2 plain', '#c38f5a', '<rect x="4" y="8" width="8" height="8"/><rect x="13" y="8" width="8" height="8"/><path d="M8 5v9m-3-3 3 3 3-3M17 5v9m-3-3 3 3 3-3" stroke="#fff7e3" fill="none" stroke-width="2"/>'],
