  floor: ['More floor', '+1 row/column', '#a78d72', '<rect x="3" y="4" width="12" height="12"/><path d="M9 4v12M3 10h12" stroke="#fff7e3" stroke-width="2"/><rect x="16" y="4" width="5" height="5" fill="#edc361"/><rect x="16" y="11" width="5" height="5" fill="#edc361"/>'],
  wake: ['Wake-up call', 'Carry sleepers', '#d6493c', '<path d="M5 8h4l3-4 3 4h4v11H5z"/><rect x="8" y="12" width="2" height="2" fill="#fff7e3"/><rect x="15" y="12" width="2" height="2" fill="#fff7e3"/><path d="M10 17h4" stroke="#fff7e3" stroke-width="2"/>'],
  walkies: ['Walkies', 'Move one dog', '#71635f', '<path d="M6 8h11v9H6zM3 6h6v5H3zM16 7h5v4h-5z"/><rect x="8" y="17" width="2" height="3"/><rect x="14" y="17" width="2" height="3"/><rect x="6" y="8" width="1" height="1" fill="#fff7e3"/>'],
  slow: ['Slow day', '25% slower', '#7894a2', '<rect x="5" y="5" width="14" height="14"/><rect x="7" y="7" width="10" height="10" fill="#fff7e3"/><path d="M12 8v5h4" stroke="#7894a2" fill="none" stroke-width="2"/>'],
};
function nsCardArt(id) {
  const card = NS_CARDS[id];
  return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" shape-rendering="crispEdges"><rect x="1" y="1" width="22" height="22" fill="#1d1a22"/><rect x="2" y="2" width="20" height="20" fill="#e9e4d8"/><g fill="' + card[2] + '">' + card[3] + '</g></svg>';
}
function nsRenderCards() {
  const shift = gm.shift;
  nsEl.cards.hidden = shift.phase !== 'tea' || !!shift.cardChoice;
  if (nsEl.cards.hidden) { nsEl.cards.textContent = ''; return; }
  nsEl.cards.textContent = '';
  for (const id of shift.cardOffers) {
    const card = NS_CARDS[id], button = document.createElement('button');
    button.type = 'button'; button.innerHTML = '<b>' + card[0] + '</b>' + nsCardArt(id) + '<small>' + card[1] + '</small>';
    button.addEventListener('click', () => nsChooseCard(id)); nsEl.cards.appendChild(button);
  }
}
function nsInitialRun() {
  const arg = (/[?&]seed=(\d+)\b/.exec(location.search) || [])[1];
  if (arg !== undefined && +arg <= 0xffffffff) return { kind: 'random', seed: +arg };
  if (/[?&]random\b/.test(location.search)) return { kind: 'random', seed: gPracticeSeed() };
  return NIGHTSHIFT_META.weekForInstant(new Date());
}
function nsBest() {
  if (!G_KEEP_PROGRESS) return 0;
  return progress.nightShift.allTimeBest;
}
function nsSaveResult() {
  if (nsui.savedEnd) return;
  nsui.savedEnd = true;
  if (!G_KEEP_PROGRESS || NS_DEBUG || nsui.playback) return;
  const before = gCollection(), shift = gm.shift;
  progressNightShiftFinished(shift.run.kind, shift.run.weekKey || '', shift.catsHome);
  nsui.unlocked = gCollection().filter((coat) => !before.includes(coat));
  if (nsui.unlocked.length) nsRebuildRoom();
}
function nsLevel(shift) {
  const board = shift.board, hatches = shift.current ? shift.current.sim.hatches.map((h, q) => {
    const source = board.hatches[q], cats = [];
    for (let j = 0; j < h.count; j++) cats.push(GR_COATS[shift.current.sim.coat[h.first + j]]);
    return Object.assign({}, source, { cats });
  }) : board.hatches.map((h) => Object.assign({}, h, { cats: [] }));
  return { id: 'nightshift', title: 'Night Shift', board: { w: board.w, h: board.h },
    hatches, baskets: board.baskets, dogs: board.dogs, flaps: board.flaps, gates: board.gates, tunnels: board.tunnels, tray: shift.settings.start.tray };
}
function nsSync() {
  const shift = gm.shift;
  gm.L = nsLevel(shift); gm.tray = shift.tray; gm.chutes = shift.turntables;
  gm.sim = shift.current && shift.current.sim; gm.mode = shift.phase === 'ended' ? 'lost' : 'running';
  gSyncSrc(); dirty = true;
}
function nsRebuildRoom() {
  const shift = gm.shift;
  gUseRoom(BOXROOM.build(shift.seed, shift.board.w, shift.board.h, G_TODAY, 'free', gCollection()));
}
function nsLoad(run = nsInitialRun(), settings = NIGHTSHIFT.SETTINGS, quiet = false) {
  if(window.__chapterReady)cpCancel();
  gPracticeCancel();
  if (typeof run === 'number') run = { kind: 'random', seed: run };
  const shift = NIGHTSHIFT.create(run.seed, settings);
  shift.run = nsClone(run);
  NIGHTSHIFT.startRound(shift, true);
  gm.shift = shift; gm.daily = null; gm.practice = null; gm.custom = true; gPracticeChoosing = false;
  gm.sel = G_TYPES.find((t) => shift.tray[t] > 0) || 'plain'; gm.cursor = false;
  nsui.time = 0; nsui.ending = 0; nsui.eventFrames = 0; nsui.lastTick = -1; nsui.walkiesFrom = null; nsui.savedEnd = false; nsui.unlocked = [];
  nsEl.ending.hidden = true;
  nsui.sleepSprites = new WeakMap();
  nsui.playback = null;
  if (!quiet) nsui.record = null;
  nsRebuildRoom();
  view.zoom = null; view.panX = 0; view.panY = 0;
  if (devW > 1) applyScale();
  nsPrepareRound();
  nsSync();
  if (NS_DEBUG) { nsEl.seed.value = String(run.seed); nsEl.settings.value = JSON.stringify(shift.settings, null, 2); }
  gStatus('Check the hatch numbers, place turntables, then press Go.');
  gHud();
  return shift;
}
function nsPrepareRound() {
  const shift = gm.shift, sim = shift.current.sim, tick = shift.current.params.tickMs / 1000;
  nsui.time = 0; nsui.lastTick = -1;
  const travelTicks = Math.max(shift.settings.sleep.travelTicks, nsui.playback ? nsui.playback.travelTicks || 0 : 0);
  gm.gateTrace=sim.gates.length?BOARDANIM.makeGateTrace(sim.n*(travelTicks+1)):null;
  for (let i = 0; i < sim.n; i++) {
    const required = Math.max(64, travelTicks * (sim.big[i] ? 2 : 1) + 64);
    let tr = NS_TRACES[i] || (NS_TRACES[i] = BOARDANIM.makeTrace(required));
    if (tr.max < required) tr = NS_TRACES[i] = nsGrowTrace(tr, required);
    if (!NS_POSES[i]) NS_POSES[i] = makeBoardCatPose();
    BOARDANIM.reset(tr); tr.rel = Infinity; tr.tick = tick; tr.pop = tick;
  }
}
// Live settings may extend a round. Reserve outside update, keeping prior flips.
function nsReserveGateTrace(travelTicks) {
  const sim = gm.shift.current.sim;
  for (let i = 0; i < sim.n; i++) { const required = travelTicks * (sim.big[i] ? 2 : 1) + 64; if (NS_TRACES[i].max < required) NS_TRACES[i] = nsGrowTrace(NS_TRACES[i], required); }
  const tr = gm.gateTrace;
  if (!tr) return;
  const max = gm.shift.current.sim.n * (travelTicks + 1);
  if (max <= tr.time.length) return;
  const bigger = BOARDANIM.makeGateTrace(max);
  bigger.n = tr.n;
  for (const key of ['time', 'gate', 'cat', 'from']) bigger[key].set(tr[key]);
  gm.gateTrace = bigger;
}
function nsApplySettings(settings) {
  nsReserveGateTrace(settings.sleep.travelTicks);
  gm.shift.settings = nsClone(settings);
}
function nsRecordAction(action) {
  if (!nsui.record || nsui.playback) return;
  const shift = gm.shift;
  nsui.record.actions.push({ round: shift.round, tick: shift.current.sim.tick, action: nsClone(action) });
  nsRecordOutput();
}
function nsRecordOutput() {
  if (!nsui.record) return;
  const shift = gm.shift;
  nsui.record.stop = { round: shift.round, tick: shift.current.sim.tick, phase: shift.phase };
  nsEl.recording.value = JSON.stringify(nsui.record, null, 2);
}
function nsTap(x, y) {
  const shift = gm.shift;
  if (shift.pendingWalkies) {
    const dog = shift.board.dogs.find((p) => p.x === x && p.y === y);
    if (dog) { nsui.walkiesFrom = { x, y }; gStatus('Now tap an empty tile for the dog.'); return; }
    if (!nsui.walkiesFrom) { gStatus('Tap the dog you want to move.'); return; }
    const from = nsui.walkiesFrom, result = NIGHTSHIFT.moveDog(shift, from.x, from.y, x, y);
    if (!result.ok) { gStatus(result.reason + '.'); return; }
    nsRecordAction({ action: 'dog', fromX: from.x, fromY: from.y, x, y });
    nsui.walkiesFrom = null; nsSync(); gHud(); gStatus('Walkies complete. Set up the next round when ready.');
    return;
  }
  const at = shift.turntables.find((p) => p.x === x && p.y === y);
  let action;
  if (at) action = { action: at.dir === 'L' ? 'pickup' : 'turn', x, y };
  else {
    const type = shift.tray[gm.sel] > 0 ? gm.sel : G_TYPES.find((t) => shift.tray[t] > 0);
    if (!type) { gStatus('The turntable tray is empty.'); return; }
    action = { action: 'place', x, y, type, dir: 'U' };
  }
  const result = NIGHTSHIFT.edit(shift, action);
  if (!result.ok) { gStatus(result.reason === 'cat locks tile' ? 'A cat is on or heading for that tile.' : result.reason + '.'); return; }
  nsRecordAction(action);
  if (shift.tray[gm.sel] < 1) gm.sel = G_TYPES.find((t) => shift.tray[t] > 0) || gm.sel;
  nsSync(); gHud();
}
function nsClear() {
  const shift = gm.shift;
  let count = 0;
  for (const p of shift.turntables.slice()) {
    const change = { action: 'pickup', x: p.x, y: p.y };
    if (NIGHTSHIFT.edit(shift, change).ok) { count++; nsRecordAction(change); }
  }
  gStatus(count ? 'Turntables returned to the tray.' : 'Cats are locking the placed turntables.');
  nsSync(); gHud();
}
function nsSend() {
  const shift = gm.shift;
  if (shift.phase !== 'running') return;
  if (shift.paused) {
    if (shift.current.sim.tick === 0) nsRecordAction({ action: 'go' });
    NIGHTSHIFT.setPaused(shift, false); gStatus('The cats are on their way.');
  }
  else { NIGHTSHIFT.setPaused(shift, true); gStatus('Tea can wait. Edit while the shift is paused.'); }
  gHud();
}
function nsNext() {
  const shift = gm.shift;
  if (shift.phase !== 'tea') return;
  if (!shift.cardChoice || shift.pendingWalkies) { gStatus('Choose a tea card and finish its action first.'); return; }
  nsRecordAction({ action: 'next' });
  NIGHTSHIFT.startRound(shift, true);
  nsFitRoom();
  nsPrepareRound(); nsSync();
  nsShowEvents(); gHud();
}
function nsChooseCard(id) {
  const shift = gm.shift, result = NIGHTSHIFT.chooseCard(shift, id);
  if (!result.ok) return;
  nsRecordAction({ action: 'card', id });
  nsui.walkiesFrom = null;
  gStatus(id === 'walkies' ? 'Tap a dog, then tap its new tile.' : id === 'floor' ? 'A new row or column opens next round.' : 'Card chosen. Edit the board or set up the next round.');
  nsSync(); gHud();
}
function nsFitRoom() {
  const shift = gm.shift;
  if (G_ROOM && G_ROOM.board.w === shift.board.w && G_ROOM.board.h === shift.board.h) return;
  nsRebuildRoom();
}
function nsEventName(event) {
  if (event.type === 'hatch') return 'A new hatch opens';
  if (event.type === 'dog') return 'A dog wanders in';
  if (event.type === 'basket') return 'A new ' + event.coat + ' basket appears';
  if (event.type === 'moveBasket') return 'The ' + event.coat + ' basket moves';
  const skipped = { hatch: 'a new hatch', dog: 'a dog', basket: 'a new ' + event.coat + ' basket', moveBasket: 'a basket move' };
  return 'No fair space for ' + skipped[event.event];
}
function nsShowEvents() {
  const shift = gm.shift, events = shift.roundEvents;
  const placed = events.filter((event) => event.type !== 'skipped');
  nsui.eventFrames = placed.length ? Math.max(1, Math.round(shift.settings.events.animationMs / (DT * 1000))) : 0;
  for (const event of placed) {
    spawnSparkles(gCX(event.x), gCY(event.y), 6, event.coat === 'blue' ? BLUE : RED);
    if (event.type === 'moveBasket') spawnSparkles(gCX(event.fromX), gCY(event.fromY), 6, event.coat === 'blue' ? BLUE : RED);
  }
  const eventText = events.length ? events.map(nsEventName).join(' · ') + '. ' : '';
  gStatus(eventText + (shift.paused ? 'Round ' + shift.round + ' is ready. Check the hatch numbers, then press Go.' : 'Round ' + shift.round + ' is under way.'));
  dirty = true;
}
function nsSkipTo(round) {
  const shift = gm.shift, run = shift.run;
  nsRecordAction({ action: 'skip', round });
  NIGHTSHIFT.seekRound(shift, round); NIGHTSHIFT.setPaused(shift, true);
  shift.run = run;
  nsFitRoom();
  nsui.ending = 0; nsEl.ending.hidden = true; nsui.sleepSprites = new WeakMap(); nsui.walkiesFrom = null;
  nsPrepareRound(); nsSync(); nsShowEvents(); gHud();
  if (nsui.record) nsRecordOutput();
}
function nsCaptureTick(shift) {
  const sim = shift.current.sim, t = sim.tick - 1, tickSec = shift.current.params.tickMs / 1000;
  if(gm.gateTrace)BOARDANIM.recordGates(gm.gateTrace,sim,(t+1)*tickSec);
  for (let i = 0; i < sim.n; i++) {
    const ev = sim.ev[i], tr = NS_TRACES[i];
    if (ev === GE_RELEASED) {
      tr.big = !!sim.big[i]; tr.rel = (t + 1) * tickSec; tr.tick = tickSec; tr.pop = tickSec;
      tr.hx = sim.x[i]; tr.hy = sim.y[i]; tr.hatchDir = sim.dir[i];
      tr.ox = gTX(0); tr.oy = gTY(0); tr.bw = sim.W; tr.bh = sim.H;
      tr.coat = sim.coat[i] === GR_GINGER ? COAT_GINGER : COAT_BLUE;
      tr.look = i & 3; tr.blinkPhase = (i * 13) % 40;
    } else if (ev === GE_MOVED || ev === GE_DELIVERED || ev === GE_WRONG || ev === GE_STUCK || ev === GE_TUNNEL || ev === GE_WAIT) {
      if (tr.n >= tr.max) NS_TRACES[i] = nsGrowTrace(tr);
      BOARDANIM.record(NS_TRACES[i], sim.px[i], sim.py[i], sim.pdir[i], sim.x[i], sim.y[i], sim.dir[i], ev, sim.bounced[i]);
      BOARDANIM.recordDestination(NS_TRACES[i], sim, i);
      if (ev === GE_DELIVERED) { spawnSparkles(gCX(sim.x[i]), gCY(sim.y[i]), 6, sim.coat[i] === GR_GINGER ? RED : BLUE); spawnHeart(gCX(sim.x[i]), gCY(sim.y[i]), 9); }
    }
  }
  if (shift.phase === 'tea') {
    gStatus('Tea break. Choose one card, then set up the next round when ready.');
    nsSync(); gHud();
  } else if (shift.phase === 'ended') {
    nsSaveResult();
    nsui.ending = 108;
    nsEl.ending.hidden = false;
    gStatus('The Postmistress is shooing the sorter home for tea.');
    nsSync(); gHud();
  } else if (t !== nsui.lastTick) {
    nsui.lastTick = t;
    gHudQueue();
    if (NS_DEBUG) nsDebugReadout();
  }
  if (nsui.record) nsRecordOutput();
  if (nsui.playback) nsPlaybackActions();
}
function nsAnimateEndingActors() {
  if (!nsui.ending || !G_ROOM) return;
  const B = G_ROOM.board, f = 1 - nsui.ending / 108, y = B.y + B.h * TILE + 5;
  WS.pm.x = B.x + 8 + Math.min(1, f / 0.58) * 32;
  WS.pm.y = y; WS.pm.z = 0; WS.pm.state = f < 0.58 ? CS_WALK : CS_SIT;
  WS.pm.frame = Math.floor(f * 24) & 3; WS.pm.face = 1;
  WS.sorter.x = B.x + 53 + Math.max(0, (f - 0.62) / 0.38) * 28;
  WS.sorter.y = y; WS.sorter.face = FACE_E;
  WS.pose.walk = f > 0.62 ? Math.floor(f * 24) & 3 : -1;
}
function nsGrowTrace(tr, max = tr.max * 2) {
  const bigger = BOARDANIM.makeTrace(max);
  for (const key of Object.keys(tr)) {
    if (ArrayBuffer.isView(tr[key])) bigger[key].set(tr[key]);
    else bigger[key] = tr[key];
  }
  bigger.max = max;
  return bigger;
}
function nsUpdate() {
  const shift = gm.shift;
  // Settled rules stay stopped while opt-in doors and points finish their poses.
  if(shift.phase!=='running'&&shift.current&&(shift.current.sim.flaps.length||shift.current.sim.gates.length))nsui.time+=DT;
  if (nsui.eventFrames > 0) {
    nsui.eventFrames--;
    if (!nsui.eventFrames && shift.phase === 'running' && !shift.paused) gStatus('Round ' + shift.round + ' is under way.');
  }
  if (nsui.ending > 0) {
    nsui.ending--;
    if (!nsui.ending) { nsEl.ending.hidden = true; gStatus('Shift finished: too many wrong baskets. The sorter is off for tea.'); gHud(); }
    return;
  }
  if (shift.phase !== 'running' || shift.paused) return;
  nsui.time += DT * (shift.fastForward ? shift.settings.clock.fastMultiplier : 1);
  NIGHTSHIFT.advance(shift, DT * 1000, nsCaptureTick);
  if (nsui.playback) nsPlaybackActions();
}
function nsHudQueue() {
  const shift = gm.shift, current = shift.current, sim = current && current.sim;
  hud.queue.classList.add('shift-queue');
  hud.queue.hidden = shift.phase === 'ended' || !current || !sim;
  if (hud.queue.hidden) return;
  hud.queue.textContent = '';
  const group = document.createElement('div'); group.className = 'queue-hatch';
  const label = document.createElement('span'); label.className = 'queue-label'; label.textContent = 'Arriving →'; group.appendChild(label);
  const list = document.createElement('ol'); list.className = 'queue-faces';
  const from = Math.max(0, current.released - 1), until = Math.min(current.releaseOrder.length, from + 12);
  label.textContent = 'Arriving ' + (from + 1) + '–' + until + ' →';
  current.releaseOrder.slice(from, until).forEach((i, local) => {
    const order = from + local;
    const coat = GR_COATS[sim.coat[i]], hatch = current.releaseHatches[order] + 1;
    const released = sim.st[i] !== GL_WAIT, next = order === current.released;
    const li = document.createElement('li'); li.className = 'queue-cat' + (sim.big[i] ? ' queue-big' : '') + (released ? ' released' : next ? ' next' : '');
    li.dataset.order = String(order + 1); li.dataset.hatch = 'H' + hatch;
    li.title = 'Cat ' + (order + 1) + ': ' + (sim.big[i] ? 'big ' : '') + coat + ', hatch ' + hatch;
    li.setAttribute('aria-label', li.title + (released ? ', released' : next ? ', next' : ''));
    const img = document.createElement('img'); img.src = gQueueFace(coat); img.alt = ''; li.appendChild(img); if (sim.big[i]) { const badge = document.createElement('span'); badge.className = 'queue-size'; badge.textContent = 'Big'; li.appendChild(badge); } list.appendChild(li);
  });
  group.appendChild(list); hud.queue.appendChild(group);
}
function nsPrepareSrc() {
  const shift = gm.shift, s = G_SRC, current = shift.current, sim = current && current.sim;
  s.hatchCues=shift.phase==='running';
  for(let k=0;k<s.gates.length;k++)BOARDANIM.gatePose(gm.gateTrace,k,nsui.time,s.gates[k].pose);
  s.hatchNumbers = shift.paused; s.tunnelFocus = gm.cursor ? tunnelPairAt(gm.L.tunnels, gm.cx, gm.cy) : -1;
  s.gallery = null; s.n = sim ? sim.n : 0;
  if (s.times.length < s.n) { s.times = new Float64Array(s.n); }
  for (let i = 0; i < s.n; i++) {
    s.traces[i] = NS_TRACES[i]; s.poses[i] = NS_POSES[i];
    s.times[i] = NS_TRACES[i].rel === Infinity || sim.st[i] === NIGHTSHIFT.ASLEEP || sim.st[i] === NIGHTSHIFT.CARRIED || sim.st[i] === NIGHTSHIFT.LIFTED ? -1 : nsui.time;
  }
  s.extras.length = 0;
  for (const cat of shift.sleepers) {
    let sprite = nsui.sleepSprites.get(cat);
    if (!sprite) {
      const o = BOARDANIM.makeOut(); o.anim = BOARDANIM.A.NAP; o.x = gCX(cat.x); o.y = gCY(cat.y);
      o.z = 0; o.dir = o.head = 0; o.frame = 0; o.eyes = 0; o.ears = BOARDANIM.EARS_UP;
      sprite = { o, tr: { coat: cat.coat === 'ginger' ? COAT_GINGER : COAT_BLUE, ...(cat.big?{big:true}:{}) } };
      nsui.sleepSprites.set(cat, sprite);
    }
    if(cat.big){sprite.o.big=true;sprite.o.frame=Math.floor(nsui.time/1.5)&1;}
    s.extras.push(sprite);
  }
  s.locked = NIGHTSHIFT.lockedTiles(shift);
  s.eventTiles.length = 0; s.eventFrame = nsui.eventFrames;
  if (s.eventFrame) for (const event of shift.roundEvents) if (event.type !== 'skipped') {
    s.eventTiles.push(event.y * shift.board.w + event.x);
    if (event.type === 'moveBasket') s.eventTiles.push(event.fromY * shift.board.w + event.fromX);
  }
  for (let q = 0; q < s.hatches.length; q++) {
    const Q = s.queues[q]; Q.n = 0;
    for (let j = current.released; j < current.releaseOrder.length && Q.n < 8; j++) {
      if (current.releaseHatches[j] !== q) continue;
      Q.coats[Q.n++] = sim.coat[current.releaseOrder[j]] === GR_GINGER ? COAT_GINGER : COAT_BLUE;
    }
    const h=s.hatches[q],H=sim.hatches[q],tr=H.count?NS_TRACES[H.first]:null;
    if(shift.paused){h.cueFrom=nsui.time;h.cuePausedEmpty=!Q.n;h.cueAlpha=Q.n?1:0;}
    else h.cueAlpha=tr&&!h.cuePausedEmpty?hatchCueFade(nsui.time-Math.max(h.cueFrom,tr.rel-tr.pop)):0;
  }
  s.cursor = gm.cursor ? gm.cy * gm.L.board.w + gm.cx : -1;
}
function nsHideHud() {
  for (const el of [nsEl.pause, nsEl.fast, nsEl.next, nsEl.restartShift, nsEl.weekly, nsEl.fresh, nsEl.info, nsEl.result, nsEl.debug, nsEl.ending]) el.hidden = true;
  nsEl.cards.hidden = true;
  hud.send.hidden = false; hud.clear.hidden = false; hud.hint.hidden = false; hud.next.hidden = false;
}
function nsHud() {
  const shift = gm.shift, active = shift.phase === 'running', tea = shift.phase === 'tea', ended = shift.phase === 'ended';
  hud.stars.hidden = true; hud.next.hidden = true; hud.hint.hidden = true; hud.share.hidden = !ended || nsui.ending > 0;
  hud.practiceChoose.hidden = !gPracticeChoosing; hud.practiceStart.disabled = gPracticePending;
  hud.practiceChange.hidden = true; hud.practiceInfo.hidden = true; hud.dailyInfo.hidden = true;
  hud.send.hidden = true; hud.clear.hidden = ended; hud.clear.disabled = !shift.turntables.length || (tea && !shift.cardChoice);
  for (const b of hud.tray) {
    const type = b.dataset.type; b.hidden = !shift.settings.start.tray[type] && !shift.tray[type] && !shift.turntables.some((p) => p.type === type);
    b.querySelector('b').textContent = '×' + shift.tray[type]; b.disabled = ended;
    b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(type === gm.sel));
  }
  nsEl.pause.hidden = !active; nsEl.pause.textContent = shift.paused ? (shift.current.sim.tick ? 'Resume' : 'Go') : 'Pause';
  nsEl.fast.hidden = !active; nsEl.fast.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(shift.fastForward));
  nsEl.next.hidden = !tea; nsEl.next.disabled = !shift.cardChoice || shift.pendingWalkies;
  nsRenderCards(); nsEl.restartShift.hidden = false;
  nsEl.weekly.hidden = shift.run.kind === 'weekly' && shift.run.weekKey === NIGHTSHIFT_META.weekForInstant(new Date()).weekKey;
  nsEl.fresh.hidden = false;
  nsEl.info.hidden = false;
  const identity = shift.run.kind === 'weekly' ? 'Week ' + shift.run.week + ' (' + shift.run.year + ')' : 'Random seed ' + shift.seed;
  nsEl.info.textContent = 'Night Shift · ' + identity + ' · Round ' + shift.round + ' · ' + shift.catsHome + ' cats home · ' + shift.strikes + '/' + shift.settings.wrongBasket.strikesToEnd + ' strikes' + (ended ? ' · Shift finished' : tea ? ' · Tea break' : shift.paused ? ' · Paused' : shift.fastForward ? ' · Fast ×' + shift.settings.clock.fastMultiplier : '');
  nsEl.result.hidden = !ended || nsui.ending > 0;
  if (ended) {
    const weekly = shift.run.kind === 'weekly' ? ' · week best ' + Math.max(shift.catsHome, progress.nightShift.weeklyBest[shift.run.weekKey] || 0) : '';
    const unlocked = nsui.unlocked.length ? ' · New collection cat: ' + nsui.unlocked.map((coat) => COATS[coat].name).join(', ') : '';
    nsEl.result.textContent = shift.catsHome + ' cats home · round ' + shift.round + ' reached' + weekly + ' · all-time best ' + Math.max(shift.catsHome, nsBest()) + unlocked;
  }
  nsEl.debug.hidden = !NS_DEBUG;
  if (NS_DEBUG) nsDebugReadout();
  if (devW > 1) requestAnimationFrame(gMeasureHud);
}
function nsDebugReadout() {
  if (!gm.shift || !NS_DEBUG) return;
  const shift = gm.shift, current = shift.current, fair = NIGHTSHIFT.fairness(shift);
  const locks = NIGHTSHIFT.lockedTiles(shift).map((k) => '(' + (k % shift.board.w + 1) + ',' + (Math.floor(k / shift.board.w) + 1) + ')');
  const travel = current.releaseOrder.map((i, order) => (order + 1) + ':' + current.travel[i]);
  nsEl.readout.textContent = 'Locked: ' + (locks.join(' ') || 'none') + '\nFairness: ' + (fair.ok ? 'pass' : 'blocked ' + fair.missing.map((m) => 'hatch ' + (m.hatch + 1) + ' → ' + m.coat).join(', ')) + '\nEvents: ' + (shift.roundEvents.map(nsEventName).join('; ') || 'none') + '\nTravel ticks: ' + travel.join(' ');
}
function nsPlaybackActions() {
  const play = nsui.playback;
  if (!play) return;
  const shift = gm.shift;
  const hasGo = (round) => play.data.actions.some((a) => a.round === round && a.tick === 0 && a.action.action === 'go');
  while (play.index < play.data.actions.length) {
    const event = play.data.actions[play.index], tick = shift.current.sim.tick;
    if (event.round !== shift.round || event.tick > tick) break;
    if (event.tick < tick) { nsui.playback = null; nsEl.status.textContent = 'Replay diverged at round ' + shift.round + ', tick ' + tick; return; }
    play.index++;
    if (event.action.action === 'next') { nsNext(); if (!hasGo(shift.round)) NIGHTSHIFT.setPaused(shift, false); }
    else if (event.action.action === 'go') NIGHTSHIFT.setPaused(shift, false);
    else if (event.action.action === 'card') nsChooseCard(event.action.id);
    else if (event.action.action === 'dog') {
      const a = event.action, result = NIGHTSHIFT.moveDog(shift, a.fromX, a.fromY, a.x, a.y);
      if (!result.ok) { nsui.playback = null; nsEl.status.textContent = 'Replay dog move failed: ' + result.reason; return; }
      nsSync(); gHud();
    }
    else if (event.action.action === 'skip') { nsSkipTo(event.action.round); if (!hasGo(shift.round)) NIGHTSHIFT.setPaused(shift, false); }
    else if (event.action.action === 'settings') { nsApplySettings(event.action.settings); gHud(); }
    else {
      const result = NIGHTSHIFT.edit(shift, event.action);
      if (!result.ok) { nsui.playback = null; nsEl.status.textContent = 'Replay edit failed: ' + result.reason; return; }
      nsSync(); gHud();
    }
  }
  if (play.index === play.data.actions.length && shift.round === play.data.stop.round && shift.current.sim.tick >= play.data.stop.tick) {
    NIGHTSHIFT.setPaused(shift, true); nsui.playback = null; nsEl.status.textContent = 'Replay reached the recorded point.'; gHud();
  }
}
nsEl.pause.addEventListener('click', nsSend);
nsEl.fast.addEventListener('click', () => { NIGHTSHIFT.setFastForward(gm.shift, !gm.shift.fastForward); gHud(); });
nsEl.next.addEventListener('click', nsNext);
nsEl.restartShift.addEventListener('click', () => nsLoad(gm.shift.run, gm.shift.settings));
nsEl.weekly.addEventListener('click', () => nsLoad(NIGHTSHIFT_META.weekForInstant(new Date())));
nsEl.fresh.addEventListener('click', () => nsLoad({ kind: 'random', seed: gPracticeSeed() }));
nsEl.restart.addEventListener('click', () => {
  const seed = Number(nsEl.seed.value);
  if (!Number.isInteger(seed) || seed < 0 || seed > 0xffffffff) { nsEl.status.textContent = 'Seed must be a 32-bit unsigned integer.'; return; }
  try { nsLoad(seed, JSON.parse(nsEl.settings.value)); nsEl.status.textContent = 'Shift restarted.'; }
  catch (e) { nsEl.status.textContent = e.message; }
});
nsEl.apply.addEventListener('click', () => {
  try {
    const settings = JSON.parse(nsEl.settings.value), errors = NIGHTSHIFT.validateSettings(settings);
    if (errors.length) throw new Error(errors.join('; '));
    const growth = gm.shift.pendingGrowth;
    if (growth && gm.shift.board[growth] >= settings.board[growth === 'w' ? 'maxW' : 'maxH'])
      throw new Error('The chosen More floor card needs room under the new board maximum.');
    nsApplySettings(settings);
    if (gm.shift.phase === 'tea' && !gm.shift.cardChoice) NIGHTSHIFT.offerCards(gm.shift);
    nsRecordAction({ action: 'settings', settings });
    nsEl.status.textContent = 'Settings applied. Round timing updates next round; starting layout updates on restart.';
    gHud();
  } catch (e) { nsEl.status.textContent = e.message; }
});
nsEl.skip.addEventListener('click', () => {
  const round = Number(nsEl.round.value), shift = gm.shift;
  if (!Number.isInteger(round) || round < 1 || round > 1000) { nsEl.status.textContent = 'Choose a round from 1 to 1000.'; return; }
  nsSkipTo(round); nsEl.status.textContent = 'Ready at round ' + round + ' with prior seeded events and no skipped cat score.';
});
nsEl.record.addEventListener('click', () => {
  const seed = Number(nsEl.seed.value);
  try {
    const settings = JSON.parse(nsEl.settings.value);
    nsLoad(seed, settings);
    nsui.record = { seed, run: nsClone(gm.shift.run), settings: nsClone(settings), actions: [], stop: { round: 1, tick: 0, phase: 'running' } };
    nsRecordOutput(); nsEl.status.textContent = 'Recording from the beginning. Play the shift, then copy the JSON.';
  } catch (e) { nsEl.status.textContent = e.message; }
});
nsEl.replay.addEventListener('click', () => {
  try {
    const data = JSON.parse(nsEl.recording.value);
    if (!data || !Array.isArray(data.actions) || !data.stop) throw new Error('Recording needs actions and a stop point.');
    nsLoad(data.run || data.seed, data.settings, true);
    // Recorded settings are known up front, so replay never grows a trace in update.
    let travelTicks = gm.shift.settings.sleep.travelTicks;
    for (const event of data.actions) if (event.action.action === 'settings')
      travelTicks = Math.max(travelTicks, event.action.settings.sleep.travelTicks);
    nsReserveGateTrace(travelTicks);
    nsui.record = null; nsui.playback = { data, index: 0, travelTicks };
    if (!data.actions.some((a) => a.round === 1 && a.tick === 0 && a.action.action === 'go')) NIGHTSHIFT.setPaused(gm.shift, false);
    nsPlaybackActions(); gHud();
    nsEl.status.textContent = 'Replaying the recorded shift.';
  } catch (e) { nsEl.status.textContent = e.message; }
});
if (GAME_MODE && /[?&]nightshift\b/.test(location.search)) nsLoad();
// The chapter's cards own a separate presentation clock. The live puzzle, stars
// and placements stay intact while a precomputed demo is watched or replayed.
const CP={mode:'',frame:0,seen:false,seenTrain:false,train:false,seenHall:false,hall:false,seenParcel:false,parcel:false,seenChristmas:false,christmas:false,depotDemo:null,depotSrc:null,src:null,demo:null,room:null,stage:0,
  enterScale:1,targetScale:1,visualScale:1,enterX:0,enterY:0,sourceView:null,targetView:null,
  motion:{offset:0,driver:{x:0,y:0,face:1,walk:-1,hold:1,bob:0}},
  panel:$('chapterScene'),card:$('chapterCard'),title:$('chapterTitle'),text:$('chapterText'),
  primary:$('chapterPrimary'),secondary:$('chapterSecondary'),replay:null,focus:null};
function cpActive(){return !!window.__chapterReady&&!!CP.mode;}
function cpHud(){
  if(typeof XC!=='undefined')xcHud();
  if(CP.replay)CP.replay.hidden=!G_KEEP_PROGRESS||gm.custom||!/^[45678]-/.test(gm.L.id)||!!gm.shift||gm.mode==='running';
}
function cpCancel(){
  if(!CP.mode)return;
  CP.mode='';CP.panel.hidden=true;hud.root.inert=screen.inert=false;
  if(G_KEEP_PROGRESS)hud.root.classList.add('on');
  if(CP.room&&G_ROOM!==CP.room)gUseRoom(CP.room);
  if(CP.train)tcCancel();if(CP.hall)hcCancel();if(CP.parcel)pcCancel();if(CP.christmas)xcCancel();
  CP.room=null;CP.sourceView=CP.targetView=null;view.zoom=null;view.panX=view.panY=0;
  if(devW>1)applyScale();
}
function cpCard(mode,title,text,primary,secondary){
  if(!CP.mode)CP.focus=document.activeElement;
  CP.mode=mode;CP.frame=0;CP.panel.dataset.chapterMode=mode;CP.panel.hidden=false;
  $('chapterKicker').textContent=CP.christmas?'Christmas Eve · Chapter 8':CP.parcel?'The parcel room · Chapter 7':CP.hall?'The city sorting hall · Chapter 6':CP.train?'The mail train · Chapter 5':'The town depot · Chapter 4';
  CP.title.textContent=title;CP.text.textContent=text;CP.primary.textContent=primary;
  CP.secondary.textContent=secondary||'';CP.secondary.hidden=!secondary;
  hud.root.inert=screen.inert=true;hud.root.classList.remove('on');gm.cursor=false;
  PTR.clear();multiTouch=mouseDrag=false;screen.style.cursor='default';
  view.panX=view.panY=0;CP.primary.focus&&CP.primary.focus();dirty=true;
}
function cpIntro(){
  if(/^8-/.test(gm.L.id))return xcIntro();
  if(/^7-/.test(gm.L.id))return pcIntro();
  if(/^6-/.test(gm.L.id))return hcIntro();
  if(/^5-/.test(gm.L.id))return tcIntro();
  CP.train=CP.hall=CP.parcel=false;
  if(!G_KEEP_PROGRESS||gm.custom||!/^4-/.test(gm.L.id)||gm.mode==='running')return;
  CP.focus=document.activeElement;CP.room=G_ROOM;CP.seen=true;
  cpCard('intro','Doors of their own',
    'Back at the town depot, cats arrive from every street. The new cat flaps give them doors of their own — and there is a whole new round of post to sort.',
    'Start sorting','Watch the post');
  cpWorld();
}
function cpDemo(){
  if(CP.mode!=='intro'&&CP.mode!=='demo')return;
  if(CP.christmas)return xcDemo();
  if(CP.parcel)return pcDemo();
  if(CP.hall)return hcDemo();
  if(CP.train)return tcDemo();
  CP.demo=CP.depotDemo;CP.src=CP.depotSrc;
  if(!CP.demo){
    const d=CP.demo=DEPOTCHAPTER.demo(DEPOT_DATA.board.x,DEPOT_DATA.board.y),s=CP.src=makeBoardSrc(3);
    s.ox=64;s.oy=48;s.w=6;s.h=4;s.rails=true;s.n=3;s.traces=d.traces;
    for(const h of d.level.hatches)s.hatches.push({x:h.x,y:h.y,dir:GR_DIR[h.dir]});
    for(const b of d.level.baskets)s.baskets.push({x:b.x,y:b.y,coat:GR_COAT[b.coat]});
    for(const t of d.turns)s.turntables.push({x:t.x,y:t.y,dir:GR_DIR[t.dir],kind:GR_TYPE[t.type]});
    for(const p of d.level.dogs)s.dogs.push(p);
    for(const g of d.level.gates)s.gates.push({x:g.x,y:g.y,a:GR_DIR[g.exits[0]],b:GR_DIR[g.exits[1]],pose:BOARDANIM.makeGateOut()});
    for(let i=0;i<d.level.flaps.length;i++){const f=d.level.flaps[i];s.edgeFlaps.push({x:f.x,y:f.y,side:GR_DIR[f.side],coat:GR_COAT[f.coat],index:i});}
  }
  CP.depotDemo=CP.demo;CP.depotSrc=CP.src;
  gUseRoom(gHandmadeRoom(CP.demo.level,0));depotSyncBaskets(CP.src);
  cpCard('demo','A door for every cat',
    'Ginger takes the turn. Blue bounces off the dog. The last cat passes the ginger turntable and finds a basket. The gate remembers whose turn comes next.',
    'Start sorting','Replay demo');cpWorld();
}
function cpPrepareDemo(){
  const s=CP.src,d=CP.demo,t=(CP.frame%(CP.demo.loop*60))/60;
  for(let i=0;i<s.n;i++)s.times[i]=t>=d.traces[i].rel-.3&&t<d.ends[i]?t:-1;
  for(let i=0;i<s.gates.length;i++)BOARDANIM.gatePose(d.gates,i,t,s.gates[i].pose);
  return s;
}
function cpWatch(W,o){
  if(CP.mode!=='demo')return;
  const t=(CP.frame%(CP.demo.loop*60))/60;
  for(let i=0;i<CP.demo.traces.length;i++){
    const tr=CP.demo.traces[i];BOARDANIM.pose(tr,t,o);
    if(t<tr.rel||t>=tr.rel+tr.n*tr.tick)continue;
    W.xs[W.n]=o.x;W.ys[W.n++]=o.y;
  }
}
function gBoardSource(){if(CP.christmas&&CP.mode==='growth')return XC.stageSrc;if(CP.parcel&&CP.mode==='growth')return PC.stageSrc;if(CP.hall&&CP.mode==='growth')return HC.stageSrc;if(CP.train&&TC.preparing)return TC.stageSrc;return cpActive()&&CP.mode==='demo'?cpPrepareDemo():G_SRC;}
function cpBegin(){
  if(CP.christmas&&xcEnding())return xcPrimary();
  if(!CP.mode)return;
  if(CP.mode==='enter')return;
  if(CP.mode==='exit'&&gm.L.id==='7-15'){gLoad(85);xcIntro();return;}
  if(CP.mode==='exit'&&gm.L.id==='6-15'){gLoad(70);pcIntro();return;}
  if(CP.mode==='exit'&&gm.L.id==='5-15'){gLoad(55);hcIntro();return;}
  if(CP.mode==='exit'&&gm.L.id==='4-15'){gLoad(40);tcIntro();return;}
  if(CP.parcel)loopFrame=PARCELIFE.frame;
  if(CP.hall)loopFrame=CITYLIFE.frame;
  if(CP.train){loopFrame=TC.lightFrame;TC.carriage=TC.carriageGrey=null;TC.layout=null;}
  CP.enterScale=scale;CP.enterX=WS.camX;CP.enterY=WS.camY;
  if(CP.room&&G_ROOM!==CP.room)gUseRoom(CP.room);
  CP.mode='enter';CP.frame=0;CP.panel.hidden=true;
  if(G_KEEP_PROGRESS)hud.root.classList.add('on');
  gHud();cpPrepareEntry();dirty=true;
}
// Prepare just the two endpoints on the button event. The transition renders
// through the wide view and scales its presentation; no intermediate view or
// image/depot buffers are created by the animation loop.
function cpSaveView(){
  return {scale,LW,LH,dstX,dstY,viewX0,viewY0,img,img32,vig:VIG.slice(),depot:DP_VIEW,backdrop:BD?BD.frame:null,
    density:paperViewScale,paper:paperImage,rgba:paperRGBA,depth:paperDepth,index:paperIndex,hud:gHudPx,side:gFlapSidePx};
}
function cpUseView(v){
  scale=view.scale=v.scale;LW=v.LW;LH=v.LH;dstX=v.dstX;dstY=v.dstY;viewX0=v.viewX0;viewY0=v.viewY0;
  img=v.img;img32=v.img32;VIG=v.vig;DP_VIEW=v.depot;if(BD)BD.frame=v.backdrop;
  paperViewScale=v.density;paperImage=v.paper;paperRGBA=v.rgba;paperDepth=v.depth;paperIndex=v.index;
  gHudPx=v.hud;gFlapSidePx=v.side;paperReady=false;
}
function cpPrepareEntry(){
  CP.sourceView=CP.targetView=null;CP.visualScale=CP.enterScale;
  const target=defaultScale(devW,devH,devDpr);
  if(target===scale){CP.targetScale=scale;return;}
  view.zoom=CP.enterScale;if(scale!==CP.enterScale)applyScale();
  gMeasureHud();CP.sourceView=cpSaveView();
  view.zoom=target;applyScale();CP.targetScale=scale;CP.targetView=cpSaveView();
  offCanvas.width=Math.max(CP.sourceView.LW,CP.targetView.LW);offCanvas.height=Math.max(CP.sourceView.LH,CP.targetView.LH);
  if(CP.sourceView.paper&&CP.targetView.paper){
    paperCanvas.width=Math.max(CP.sourceView.paper.width,CP.targetView.paper.width);paperCanvas.height=Math.max(CP.sourceView.paper.height,CP.targetView.paper.height);
  }
  cpUseView(CP.sourceView);view.zoom=CP.enterScale;
}
function cpFinishEntry(){
  if(CP.targetView)cpUseView(CP.targetView);
  CP.mode='';if(CP.train)tcCancel();if(CP.hall)hcCancel();if(CP.parcel)pcCancel();if(CP.christmas)xcCancel();CP.room=null;CP.sourceView=CP.targetView=null;view.zoom=null;hud.root.inert=screen.inert=false;
  const target=CP.focus&&CP.focus.tagName==='BUTTON'&&!CP.focus.disabled?CP.focus:hud.send;
  if(target.focus)target.focus();
}
function cpPresent(output,resolution){
  const w=LW*CP.visualScale,h=LH*CP.visualScale;
  if(w<devW||h<devH){sctx.fillStyle=grayscale?BG_GREY_CSS:BG_CSS;sctx.fillRect(0,0,devW,devH);}
  sctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
  sctx.drawImage(output,0,0,LW*resolution,LH*resolution,Math.floor((devW-w)/2),Math.floor((devH-h)/2),w,h);
}
function cpNext(){
  if(!G_KEEP_PROGRESS||gm.custom||gm.daily||gm.practice||gm.shift)return false;
  if(xcNext()||pcNext()||hcNext()||tcNext())return true;
  if(gm.L.id==='3-10'){gLoad(25);cpIntro();return true;}
  if(gm.L.id==='4-6'||gm.L.id==='4-9'){
    const stage=gm.L.id==='4-6'?1:2;gLoad(gm.li+1);CP.room=G_ROOM;CP.stage=stage;
    gUseRoom(gHandmadeRoom(gm.L,-1));
    cpCard('growth',stage===1?'A little more room':'The whole sorting floor',
      stage===1?'The sorting frames wheel aside. There is another row to work with — and more post on its way.':'One last shuffle of the frames opens the full floor. Seven columns, five rows, and a door on every side.',
      'Continue sorting');cpWorld();return true;
  }
  if(gm.L.id==='4-15'){
    CP.room=G_ROOM;cpCard('exit','Every cat delivered',
      'The last flap settles. The satchel is packed for the night mail, and the train is waiting beyond the yard.',
      'Board the mail train','Watch the post');cpWorld();return true;
  }
  return false;
}
function cpPicked(){if(gm.L.id==='8-1'&&!CP.seenChristmas&&!progress.levels['8-1'])xcIntro();if(gm.L.id==='7-1'&&!CP.seenParcel&&!progress.levels['7-1'])pcIntro();if(gm.L.id==='6-1'&&!CP.seenHall&&!progress.levels['6-1'])hcIntro();if(gm.L.id==='5-1'&&!CP.seenTrain&&!progress.levels['5-1'])tcIntro();if(gm.L.id==='4-1'&&!CP.seen&&!progress.levels['4-1'])cpIntro();}
function cpInitial(){cpHud();cpPicked();}
function cpUpdate(){
  CP.frame++;
  if(CP.christmas)xcUpdate();
  if(CP.mode==='enter'&&CP.frame>=72)cpFinishEntry();
}
function cpWorld(){
  if(CP.christmas)xcWorld();else if(CP.parcel)pcWorld();else if(CP.hall)hcWorld();else if(CP.train)tcWorld();
  if(CP.christmas&&xcEnding()){dirty=true;return;}
  const mode=CP.mode,small=devW>devH&&window.innerHeight<=520;
  const panel=mode==='enter'?0:small?0:Math.ceil(CP.card.offsetHeight*devDpr);
  const wide=VIEWPORT.roomFitScale(devW,Math.max(100,devH-panel-32*devDpr),ROOM_FIT_W,ROOM_FIT_H);
  const target=defaultScale(devW,devH,devDpr),u=Math.min(1,CP.frame/72),v=u*u*(3-2*u);
  const hi=VIEWPORT.maxScale(devW,devH),lo=Math.min(VIEWPORT.roomFitScale(devW,devH,ROOM_FIT_W,ROOM_FIT_H),hi);
  const desired=clamp(mode==='demo'?target:wide,lo,hi);
  if(mode!=='enter'&&scale!==desired){view.zoom=desired;applyScale();}
  if(mode==='intro'||mode==='growth'||mode==='exit'||mode==='station'){
    WS.camX=small?-Math.ceil(CP.card.offsetWidth*devDpr/scale/2):0;
    WS.camY=small?0:Math.round(panel/scale/2);
  }else if(mode==='demo'){
    const B=CP.demo.level.board,cx=CP.src.ox+B.w*8,cy=CP.src.oy+B.h*8;
    WS.camX=Math.round(OX+cx-cy-VIEW_CX)-(small?Math.ceil(CP.card.offsetWidth*devDpr/scale/2):0);
    WS.camY=Math.round(OY+(cx+cy)/2-8-VIEW_CY+panel/scale/2);
  }else if(mode==='enter'){
    CP.visualScale=CP.enterScale+(CP.targetScale-CP.enterScale)*v;
    gCamera(CP.targetScale);const x=WS.camX,y=WS.camY;
    WS.camX=Math.round(CP.enterX+(x-CP.enterX)*v);WS.camY=Math.round(CP.enterY+(y-CP.enterY)*v);
  }
  if(CP.train||CP.hall||CP.parcel||CP.christmas){dirty=true;return;}
  if(mode==='growth'){
    DEPOTCHAPTER.growth(CP.stage,CP.frame,CP.motion);
    Object.assign(DPLIFE.driver,CP.motion.driver);DPLIFE.driver.reach=0;DPLIFE.driver.pencil=false;DPLIFE.driver.pour=false;DPLIFE.driver.scratch=0;
    DPLIFE.cargo.kind=DPLIFE.parcel.kind='none';DPLIFE.mug.on=false;
  }
  if(mode==='exit'){
    // Reuse the accepted collect-satchel route to the yard, then hold at the van.
    DEPOTWORLD.step(DPLIFE,Math.min(1320,1092+CP.frame));
    DPLIFE.driver.hold=1;DPLIFE.cargo.kind='full-bag';DPLIFE.cargo.place='hand';
    const d=DPLIFE.driver,c=DPLIFE.cargo,right=d.face===1||d.face===3;
    c.x=d.x+(right?3:-3);c.y=d.y+(right?-1:1);c.z=14;c.fold=0;
    WS.camX=small?-Math.ceil(CP.card.offsetWidth*devDpr/scale/2):0;WS.camY=small?0:Math.round(panel/scale/2);
  }
  dirty=true;
}
CP.primary.addEventListener('click',cpBegin);
CP.secondary.addEventListener('click',()=>{
  if(CP.christmas)return xcSecondary();
  if(CP.mode==='exit'){CP.mode='intro';cpDemo();}else cpDemo();
});
CP.panel.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
  if(e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();if(CP.christmas&&xcEnding())xcEscape();else cpBegin();}
  if(e.key==='Tab'){
    const last=CP.christmas&&!XC.back.hidden?XC.back:CP.secondary.hidden?CP.primary:CP.secondary;
    if(e.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===CP.primary){e.preventDefault();last.focus();}
    else if(!e.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===last){e.preventDefault();CP.primary.focus();}
  }
});

  for(const p of d.level.gates)s.gates.push({x:p.x,y:p.y,a:GR_DIR[p.exits[0]],b:GR_DIR[p.exits[1]],pose:BOARDANIM.makeGateOut()});
 }

function pcWorld(){
 if(CP.mode==='exit')parcelStep(PARCELCHAPTER.dispatch(CP.frame,PC.dispatch).worldFrame);
 else{
  parcelStep(CP.mode==='enter'?loopFrame:CP.frame+600);
  if(CP.mode==='growth'){
   const m=PARCELCHAPTER.growth(PC.stage,CP.frame,PC.motion),a=PARCELIFE.sorter,s=PC.stageSrc,B=gm.L.board;
   s.w=m.revealed?B.w:B.w-(PC.stage===2?0:1);s.h=m.revealed?B.h:B.h-(PC.stage===2?1:0);
   a.x=m.workerX;a.y=m.workerY;a.z=0;a.face=m.face;a.action='push';a.frame=Math.max(0,m.walk);a.handling=-1;
   PARCELIFE.parcels[0].visible=false;
  }
 }
}

// Inspect warehouse life without taking a gameplay step. Used by the Scene controls.
function parcelInspect(frame) {
  if (ACTIVE_ROOM.id !== 'parcel' || !Number.isFinite(frame)) return false;
  paused = true;
  loopFrame = ((Math.floor(frame) % LOOP_FRAMES) + LOOP_FRAMES) % LOOP_FRAMES;
  loopT = loopFrame / 60;
  parcelStep(loopFrame);
  dirty = true;
  return true;
}

 }

 XC.seen=true;progressStore.set(XC.key,'seen');xcStart(false);return true;
}

// ============================================================================
// 44 LEVEL EDITOR (hidden: add ?edit to the URL). Set the board size and time
// limit, place hatches / baskets / dogs, set each hatch's cats and the tray,
// press Check to run the solver, Try it to play it, Export JSON to copy the
// level (levels.json format 1). Taps on the board: Hatch places one (tap it again to
// turn it), Ginger/Blue basket places or recolours (the same coat again removes
// it), Dog toggles, Gate cycles its twelve pairs/start exits, Erase removes.
// A hatch's cats are typed as g (ginger) / b (blue).
// ============================================================================
const EDIT_MODE = GAME_MODE && /[?&]edit\b/.test(location.search);
const ed = { L: null, tool: 'hatch', side: 'R', sel: -1, cat: 0, trying: false, pending: null };
const ED_GATES = [['R','D'], ['D','R'], ['D','L'], ['L','D'], ['L','U'], ['U','L'], ['U','R'], ['R','U'], ['R','L'], ['L','R'], ['D','U'], ['U','D']];
function edActive() { return EDIT_MODE && !ed.trying; }
function edTapAt(clientX, clientY) {                 // from 42_input: a tap on the board while editing
  const k = gPick(clientX, clientY);
  if (k < 0) return;
  gm.cx = k % ed.L.board.w; gm.cy = Math.floor(k / ed.L.board.w); gm.cursor = true;
  edTap(gm.cx, gm.cy);
}
const E = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
const ED_MAX_CATS = 16, ED_MAX_TOTAL_CATS = 32, ED_MIN_TIME = 6, ED_MAX_TIME = 240; // bounded mixed-size trial buffers
const edCatLetters = (cats) => cats.map((c) => (grCatCoat(c) === GR_GINGER ? 'g' : 'b')).join('');
function edLimits(L) {
  const errors = [];
  if (L.timeLimit < ED_MIN_TIME || L.timeLimit > ED_MAX_TIME) errors.push('editor time limit must be ' + ED_MIN_TIME + '–' + ED_MAX_TIME + ' ticks');
  if (GR_TYPES.some(type => (L.tray[type] || 0) > 6)) errors.push('editor tray allows at most 6 of each turntable');
  if (L.hatches.some(h => h.cats.length > ED_MAX_CATS)) errors.push('editor queues allow at most ' + ED_MAX_CATS + ' cats per hatch');
  if (L.hatches.reduce((n,h) => n + h.cats.length, 0) > ED_MAX_TOTAL_CATS) errors.push('this editor trial supports at most ' + ED_MAX_TOTAL_CATS + ' cats in total');
  return errors;
}
function edSetCats(value) {
  const h = ed.L.hatches[ed.sel]; if (!h) return '';
  const letters = value.toLowerCase().replace(/[^gb]/g, '');
  if (letters.length > ED_MAX_CATS || ed.L.hatches.reduce((n,q) => n + (q === h ? Math.max(1,letters.length) : q.cats.length),0) > ED_MAX_TOTAL_CATS) { E('edStatus').textContent = 'Queues allow16 cats per hatch and32 in total. No cats were removed.'; return edCatLetters(h.cats); }
  const next = (letters || 'g').split('').map(ch => ch === 'g' ? 'ginger' : 'blue'), previous = h.cats;
  const matches = (i,j) => GR_COATS[grCatCoat(previous[i])] === next[j];
  let prefix = 0, suffix = 0;
  while (prefix < Math.min(previous.length,next.length) && matches(prefix,prefix)) prefix++;
  while (suffix < Math.min(previous.length,next.length)-prefix && matches(previous.length-1-suffix,next.length-1-suffix)) suffix++;
  h.cats = next.map((coat,i) => {
    if (i < prefix) return previous[i];
    if (i >= next.length-suffix) return previous[previous.length-next.length+i];
    const old = previous.length === next.length ? previous[i] : null;
    return old && typeof old === 'object' ? { ...old, coat } : coat;
  });
  edChanged(); return letters;
}

function edSetBig(value) {
  const h = ed.L.hatches[ed.sel];
  if (!h || !h.cats[ed.cat] || typeof value !== 'boolean') return;
  const old = h.cats[ed.cat], coat = GR_COATS[grCatCoat(old)];
  h.cats[ed.cat] = old && typeof old === 'object' ? { ...old, big: value } : { coat, big: value };
  edChanged();
}

function edBlank() {
  return { id: 'new-1', title: 'New level', idea: '', board: { w: 5, h: 3, roomX: 0, roomY: 0 }, timeLimit: GR_TIME_LIMIT,
    hatches: [{ x: 0, y: 1, dir: 'R', cats: ['ginger'] }], baskets: [{ x: 4, y: 1, coat: 'ginger' }], dogs: [],
    tray: { plain: 1, ginger: 0, blue: 0 }, solution: [], minTurntables: 0 };
}
function edUse(L, keepOffset = false) {
  ed.L = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(L));
  if (!keepOffset) { ed.L.board.roomX = 0; ed.L.board.roomY = 0; } // the stone floor's back corner
  for (const k of ['hatches', 'baskets', 'dogs', 'gates', 'flaps', 'tunnels']) if (!ed.L[k]) ed.L[k] = [];
  if (!ed.L.timeLimit) ed.L.timeLimit = GR_TIME_LIMIT;
  ed.pending = null;
  ed.sel = ed.L.hatches.length ? 0 : -1; ed.cat = 0;
  edShow('');
}
function edShow(msg) {
  gm.L = ed.L; gm.chutes = []; gm.mode = 'edit'; gm.sim = null;
  if(LEVELROOMS.id(ed.L)==='parcel'||LEVELROOMS.id(ed.L)==='christmas')E('editor').classList.add('cityhall');
  gUseRoom(gHandmadeRoom(ed.L));
  gSyncSrc();
  if (ed.sel >= 0 && ed.L.hatches[ed.sel]) { gm.cx = ed.L.hatches[ed.sel].x; gm.cy = ed.L.hatches[ed.sel].y; gm.cursor = true; }
  edPanel();
  E('edStatus').textContent = msg;
}
function edSetRoom(id) {
  const floor = LEVELROOMS.rooms[id];
  if (!floor) return;
  if (ed.L.board.w > floor.w || ed.L.board.h > floor.h) {
    E('edRoom').value = LEVELROOMS.id(ed.L);
    E('edStatus').textContent = 'Make the board at most ' + floor.w + ' × ' + floor.h + ' before changing room.';
    return;
  }
  edCancelTunnel();
  if (id === 'office') delete ed.L.room; else ed.L.room = id;
  view.zoom = null; view.panX = 0; view.panY = 0;
  edChanged();
}
function edChanged() {                   // any edit invalidates the last check and its solution
  const x=gm.cx,y=gm.cy;
  ed.L.solution = []; ed.L.minTurntables = 0;
  E('edOut').hidden = true; E('edOutRow').hidden = true;
  edShow('');
  gm.cx=clamp(x,0,ed.L.board.w-1);gm.cy=clamp(y,0,ed.L.board.h-1);
}
function edPieceAt(x, y) {
  const L = ed.L;
  let k = L.hatches.findIndex((p) => p.x === x && p.y === y); if (k >= 0) return ['hatch', k];
  k = L.baskets.findIndex((p) => p.x === x && p.y === y); if (k >= 0) return ['basket', k];
  k = L.dogs.findIndex((p) => p.x === x && p.y === y); if (k >= 0) return ['dog', k];
  k = L.gates.findIndex((p) => p.x === x && p.y === y); if (k >= 0) return ['gate', k];
  k = tunnelPairAt(L.tunnels,x,y); if (k >= 0) return ['tunnel',k];
  return null;
}
function edRemove(p) {
  if (!p) return;
  const list = p[0] === 'hatch' ? ed.L.hatches : p[0] === 'basket' ? ed.L.baskets : p[0] === 'gate' ? ed.L.gates : p[0] === 'tunnel' ? ed.L.tunnels : ed.L.dogs;
  list.splice(p[1], 1);
  if (p[0] === 'hatch') ed.sel = ed.sel === p[1] ? -1 : ed.sel > p[1] ? ed.sel - 1 : ed.sel;
}
function edCancelTunnel() { ed.pending = null; }
function edSetTool(tool) { edCancelTunnel(); ed.tool = tool; edPanel(); E('edStatus').textContent = ''; }
function edTunnelTap(x, y) {
  const symbol = ed.tool.slice(7), L = ed.L;
  if (L.tunnels.some(t => t.symbol === symbol)) { E('edStatus').textContent = 'That symbol is already paired. Erase either end to replace the pair.'; return; }
  if (edPieceAt(x,y) || L.flaps.some(f => f.x === x && f.y === y)) { E('edStatus').textContent = 'Choose an empty tile for the tunnel.'; return; }
  if (L.hatches.some(h => h.x + GR_DX[GR_DIR[h.dir]] === x && h.y + GR_DY[GR_DIR[h.dir]] === y)) {
    E('edStatus').textContent = 'A tunnel cannot be the hatch exit tile.'; return;
  }
  if (!ed.pending || ed.pending.symbol !== symbol) {
    ed.pending = { x, y, symbol }; E('edStatus').textContent = 'Choose the partner tile for ' + symbol + '. Escape cancels.'; return;
  }
  const pair = { a: { x: ed.pending.x, y: ed.pending.y }, b: { x, y }, symbol };
  const errors = grValidate({ ...L, tunnels: L.tunnels.concat([pair]) }, null);
  if (errors.length) { E('edStatus').textContent = errors.join('; '); return; }
  L.tunnels.push(pair); edCancelTunnel(); edChanged();
}
function edTap(x, y) {
  const p = edPieceAt(x, y), t = ed.tool;
  if (t.startsWith('tunnel-')) { edTunnelTap(x,y); return; }
  edCancelTunnel();
  if (t === 'flap-ginger' || t === 'flap-blue') {
    const f = { x, y, side: ed.side, coat: t === 'flap-ginger' ? 'ginger' : 'blue' };
    const k = ed.L.flaps.findIndex(q => q.x === x && q.y === y && q.side === ed.side);
    const errors = grValidate({ ...ed.L, flaps: ed.L.flaps.filter((q, i) => i !== k).concat([f]) }, null);
    if (errors.length) { E('edStatus').textContent = errors.join('; '); return; }
    if (k < 0) ed.L.flaps.push(f);
    else if (ed.L.flaps[k].coat === f.coat) ed.L.flaps.splice(k, 1);
    else ed.L.flaps[k].coat = f.coat;
    edChanged(); return;
  }
  if (t === 'erase') {
    const k = ed.L.flaps.findIndex(q => q.x === x && q.y === y && q.side === ed.side);
    if (k >= 0) { ed.L.flaps.splice(k, 1); edChanged(); return; }
  }
  if (t === 'hatch') {
    if (p && p[0] === 'hatch') {
      const h = ed.L.hatches[p[1]];
      if (ed.sel === p[1]) h.dir = G_CW[(G_CW.indexOf(h.dir) + 1) & 3]; else ed.sel = p[1];
    } else { edRemove(p); ed.L.hatches.push({ x, y, dir: 'R', cats: ['ginger'] }); ed.sel = ed.L.hatches.length - 1; }
  } else if (t === 'basket-ginger' || t === 'basket-blue') {
    const coat = t === 'basket-ginger' ? 'ginger' : 'blue';
    if (p && p[0] === 'basket' && ed.L.baskets[p[1]].coat === coat) edRemove(p);
    else if (p && p[0] === 'basket') ed.L.baskets[p[1]].coat = coat;
    else { edRemove(p); ed.L.baskets.push({ x, y, coat }); }
  } else if (t === 'dog') {
    if (p && p[0] === 'dog') edRemove(p); else { edRemove(p); ed.L.dogs.push({ x, y }); }
  } else if (t === 'gate') {
    if (p && p[0] === 'gate') {
      const g = ed.L.gates[p[1]], k = ED_GATES.findIndex(e => e[0] === g.exits[0] && e[1] === g.exits[1]);
      g.exits = ED_GATES[(k + 1) % ED_GATES.length].slice();
    } else { edRemove(p); ed.L.gates.push({ x, y, exits: ED_GATES[0].slice() }); }
  } else edRemove(p);
  edChanged();
}
function edResize(dw, dh) {
  edCancelTunnel();
  const B = ed.L.board, floor = LEVELROOMS.floor(ed.L);
  B.w = clamp(B.w + dw, 2, floor.w); B.h = clamp(B.h + dh, 2, floor.h);
  const inside = (p) => p.x < B.w && p.y < B.h;
  ed.L.hatches = ed.L.hatches.filter(inside); ed.L.baskets = ed.L.baskets.filter(inside); ed.L.dogs = ed.L.dogs.filter(inside);
  ed.L.gates = ed.L.gates.filter(inside);
  ed.L.tunnels = ed.L.tunnels.filter(t => inside(t.a) && inside(t.b));
  ed.L.flaps = ed.L.flaps.filter(f => inside(f) && ((f.side === 'U' && f.y === 0) ||
    (f.side === 'D' && f.y === B.h - 1) || (f.side === 'L' && f.x === 0) || (f.side === 'R' && f.x === B.w - 1)));
  if (ed.sel >= ed.L.hatches.length) ed.sel = ed.L.hatches.length - 1;
  edChanged();
}
function edPanel() {
  const L = ed.L;
  if(LEVELROOMS.id(L)==='cityhall'||LEVELROOMS.id(L)==='parcel'||LEVELROOMS.id(L)==='christmas')E('editor').classList.add('cityhall');else E('editor').classList.remove('cityhall');
  E('edRoom').value = LEVELROOMS.id(L);
  E('edSide').value = ed.side;
  E('edW').textContent = L.board.w; E('edH').textContent = L.board.h;
  if (document.activeElement !== E('edTime')) E('edTime').value = L.timeLimit;
  E('edTimeHint').textContent = 'ticks (' + (L.timeLimit * GR_TICK_MS / 1000).toFixed(1) + ' s)';
  E('edId').value = L.id; E('edTitle').value = L.title;
  for (const b of document.querySelectorAll('#editor [data-tool]')) b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(b.dataset.tool === ed.tool));
  const h = L.hatches[ed.sel], cats = E('edCats');
  cats.disabled = !h;
  if (document.activeElement !== cats) cats.value = h ? edCatLetters(h.cats) : '';
  const pick = E('edCatIndex'), size = E('edCatBig'); pick.textContent = '';
  ed.cat = h ? clamp(ed.cat,0,h.cats.length-1) : 0;
  if (h) h.cats.forEach((c,i) => pick.add(new Option((i+1)+'. '+GR_COATS[grCatCoat(c)]+(grCatBig(c)?' · Big':''),String(i))));
  pick.disabled = size.disabled = !h; pick.value = String(ed.cat); size.checked = !!h && grCatBig(h.cats[ed.cat]);
  E('edCatsHint').textContent = h ? 'g ginger, b blue; hatch at ' + h.x + ',' + h.y + ' faces ' + { U: 'up', R: 'right', D: 'down', L: 'left' }[h.dir] : 'tap a hatch to edit its cats';
  E('edTP').value = L.tray.plain; E('edTG').value = L.tray.ginger; E('edTB').value = L.tray.blue;
  if(gCityHallEditing()){gMeasureHud();if(view.zoom===null)applyScale();gCamera();dirty=true;}
}
function edProblems() {
  const L = ed.L, errs = grValidate(L, null).concat(LEVELROOMS.check(L));
  if (ed.pending) errs.push('finish or cancel the pending tunnel pair');
  if (errs.length) return errs;
  errs.push(...edLimits(L));
  if (!L.hatches.length) errs.push('add at least one hatch');
  const coats = new Set(); L.hatches.forEach((h) => h.cats.forEach((c) => coats.add(GR_COATS[grCatCoat(c)])));
  for (const c of coats) if (!L.baskets.some((b) => b.coat === c) && !L.flaps.some(f => f.coat === c)) errs.push('no ' + c + ' destination');
  return errs;
}
const ED_ARROW = { U: '↑', R: '→', D: '↓', L: '←' };
function edCheck() {
  const errs = edProblems();
  if (errs.length) { E('edStatus').textContent = 'Fix first: ' + errs.join('; ') + '.'; return; }
  E('edStatus').textContent = 'Checking…';
  setTimeout(() => {                       // let the status paint before the (synchronous) search
    const t0 = performance.now(), r = SOLVER.solve(ed.L, { maxNodes: 400000 }), ms = Math.round(performance.now() - t0);
    let msg;
    if (r.truncated) msg = 'Search stopped after ' + r.nodes.toLocaleString() + ' positions: at least ' + r.count + ' solutions. Try a smaller tray.';
    else if (!r.solvable) msg = 'Not solvable with this tray.';
    else {
      ed.L.solution = r.best; ed.L.minTurntables = r.minTurntables;
      gm.chutes = r.best.map((c) => ({ x: c.x, y: c.y, type: c.type, dir: c.dir }));
      const ends = grRun(ed.L, r.best).endTick;
      msg = (r.count === 1 ? '1 solution' : r.count + ' solutions') + ', fewest turntables ' + r.minTurntables + ' (shown): ' +
        r.best.map((c) => G_NAME[c.type] + ' ' + ED_ARROW[c.dir] + ' at ' + c.x + ',' + c.y).join(', ') + '. It finishes on tick ' + ends +
        ' of ' + ed.L.timeLimit + '.' + (r.count > 3 ? ' More than 3 solutions may be too easy.' : '');
    }
    E('edStatus').textContent = msg + ' (' + ms + ' ms)';
  }, 20);
}
function edJSON() {                      // one entry for levels.json (format 1)
  const L = ed.L, out = { id: L.id, title: L.title };
  if (L.room) out.room = L.room;
  if (L.idea) out.idea = L.idea;
  Object.assign(out, { board: L.board, timeLimit: L.timeLimit || GR_TIME_LIMIT, hatches: L.hatches, baskets: L.baskets, dogs: L.dogs,
    tray: L.tray, solution: L.solution, minTurntables: L.minTurntables | 0 });
  if (L.gates.length) out.gates = L.gates;
  if (L.flaps.length) out.flaps = L.flaps;
  if (L.tunnels.length) out.tunnels = L.tunnels;
  return JSON.stringify(out, null, 2);
}
function edExport() {
  const errors = edProblems();
  if (errors.length) { E('edStatus').textContent = 'Fix first: ' + errors.join('; ') + '.'; return; }
  const ta = E('edOut');
  ta.value = edJSON(); ta.hidden = false; E('edOutRow').hidden = false;
  ta.focus(); ta.select();
  if (!ed.L.solution.length) E('edStatus').textContent = 'Exported without a solution: press Check to add the fewest-turntable one.';
}
function edImport(text) {
  try {
    const L = JSON.parse(text);
    if (!L || !L.board || !Array.isArray(L.hatches) || !L.tray) throw new Error('level needs a board, hatches and tray');
    if (!L.baskets) L.baskets = [];
    if (!L.dogs) L.dogs = [];
    if (!L.solution) L.solution = [];
    if (L.timeLimit === undefined) L.timeLimit = GR_TIME_LIMIT;
    let errors = grValidate(L, L.solution).concat(LEVELROOMS.check(L));
    if (!errors.length) errors = edLimits(L);
    if (errors.length) throw new Error(errors.join('; '));
    ed.trying = false; edUse(L, true); E('edStatus').textContent = 'Imported ' + L.id + '.';
    return true;
  } catch (error) { E('edStatus').textContent = 'Could not import: ' + error.message; return false; }
}
function edTry() {
  const errs = edProblems();
  if (errs.length) { E('edStatus').textContent = 'Fix first: ' + errs.join('; ') + '.'; return; }
  ed.trying = true;
  E('editor').classList.remove('on');
  gLoadLevel(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(ed.L)));
  hud.next.hidden = true; E('edBack').style.display = 'inline-block';
  hud.root.classList.add('on');
}
function edBack() {
  ed.trying = false;
  hud.root.classList.remove('on'); hud.next.hidden = false; E('edBack').style.display = 'none';
  E('editor').classList.add('on');
  edShow('');
}
if (EDIT_MODE) {
  const sel = E('edLoad');

  LEVELS.forEach((L, i) => sel.add(new Option(L.id + ' ' + L.title, String(i))));

    const file = event.target.files[0];
    if (file) file.text().then(edImport, error => { E('edStatus').textContent = 'Could not read: ' + error.message; });

  });
  E('edId').addEventListener('input', (e) => { ed.L.id = e.target.value.trim() || 'new'; });

  for (const b of document.querySelectorAll('#editor [data-ed]')) b.addEventListener('click', () => {

    e.target.value = edSetCats(e.target.value);
  });
  E('edCatIndex').addEventListener('change', e => { ed.cat = +e.target.value; edPanel(); });
  E('edCatBig').addEventListener('change', e => edSetBig(e.target.checked));
  E('edTime').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
    const v = parseInt(e.target.value, 10);
    if (v >= ED_MIN_TIME && v <= ED_MAX_TIME) { ed.L.timeLimit = v; edChanged(); }
  });
  E('edTime').addEventListener('change', (e) => { e.target.value = ed.L.timeLimit; });   // out of range: show the limit in use
  const tray = (id, key) => E(id).addEventListener('input', (e) => { ed.L.tray[key] = clamp(parseInt(e.target.value, 10) || 0, 0, 6); edChanged(); });
  tray('edTP', 'plain'); tray('edTG', 'ginger'); tray('edTB', 'blue');
  E('edCheck').addEventListener('click', edCheck);
  E('edTry').addEventListener('click', edTry);
  E('edExport').addEventListener('click', edExport);
  E('edBack').addEventListener('click', edBack);
  E('edCopy').addEventListener('click', () => {
    const ta = E('edOut'); ta.select();
    if (navigator.clipboard) navigator.clipboard.writeText(ta.value).then(() => { E('edStatus').textContent = 'Copied.'; }, () => document.execCommand('copy'));
    else document.execCommand('copy');
  });
  E('edDl').addEventListener('click', () => {
    const errors = edProblems();
    if (errors.length) { E('edStatus').textContent = 'Fix first: ' + errors.join('; ') + '.'; return; }
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([edJSON() + '\n'], { type: 'application/json' }));
    a.download = (ed.L.id || 'level') + '.json';
    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 1000);
  });
  E('editor').classList.add('on');
  edUse(LEVELS[0]);
  sel.value = '0';
}
window.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
  if (edActive() && event.key === 'Escape' && ed.pending) { edCancelTunnel(); E('edStatus').textContent = 'Tunnel pair cancelled.'; event.preventDefault(); return; }
  if (!edActive() || (event.target && ['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT', 'BUTTON'].includes(event.target.tagName))) return;
  const B = ed.L.board;
  if (event.key === 'ArrowUp') gm.cy = Math.max(0, gm.cy - 1);
  else if (event.key === 'ArrowDown') gm.cy = Math.min(B.h - 1, gm.cy + 1);
  else if (event.key === 'ArrowLeft') gm.cx = Math.max(0, gm.cx - 1);
  else if (event.key === 'ArrowRight') gm.cx = Math.min(B.w - 1, gm.cx + 1);
  else if (event.key === 'Enter') edTap(gm.cx, gm.cy);
  else return;
  gm.cursor = true; dirty = true; event.preventDefault();
});
// ============================================================================
// 45 ATLAS OVERLAY (S): the atlas (33_atlas) at 3x on a checker, with downloads
// of atlas.png, atlas-index.png, atlas.json, palette.json and backdrop data. S closes it.
// ============================================================================
const atlasEl = document.getElementById('atlas'), atlasCanvas = document.getElementById('atlasCanvas');
let ATLAS = null, ATLAS_CNV = null, ATLAS_SOURCE = null, ATLAS_VERSION = -1;
function atlasData() {                                               // built once, on first use
  const source=paperEnabled?(paperLookup.size?paperLookup:paperPieceRecords.length?paperPieceRecords:null):null;
  if (!ATLAS || ATLAS_SOURCE !== source || ATLAS_VERSION !== paperVersion) { ATLAS = source?paperAtlasData():buildAtlas(); ATLAS_CNV = atlasCanvases(ATLAS); ATLAS_SOURCE=source; ATLAS_VERSION=paperVersion; }
  return ATLAS;
}
// The colour image (alpha 0 round the frames) and the index image (R index, G outline, B sunlit outline colour).
function atlasCanvases(A) {
  const mk = () => { const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = A.w; c.height = A.h; return c; };
  const col = mk(), ind = mk(), gc = col.getContext('2d'), gi = ind.getContext('2d');
  const ic = gc.createImageData(A.w, A.h), ii = gi.createImageData(A.w, A.h), pc = new Uint32Array(ic.data.buffer), d = ii.data;
  for (let i = 0; i < A.w * A.h; i++) {
    const c = A.idx[i];
    if (c === 255) continue;                                         // transparent in both
    pc[i] = PAL32[c];
    d[i * 4] = c; d[i * 4 + 1] = A.cls[i]; d[i * 4 + 2] = A.sel[i]; d[i * 4 + 3] = 255;
  }
  gc.putImageData(ic, 0, 0); gi.putImageData(ii, 0, 0);
  return { col, ind };
}
// name -> () => Promise<Blob>
function atlasFiles() {
  const A = atlasData(), png = (c) => () => new Promise((r) => c.toBlob(r, 'image/png'));
  const json = (o) => () => Promise.resolve(new Blob([JSON.stringify(o, null, 1)], { type: 'application/json' }));
  return { 'atlas.png': png(ATLAS_CNV.col), 'atlas-index.png': png(ATLAS_CNV.ind), 'atlas.json': json(A.json), 'palette.json': json(A.paletteJson), 'backdrops.json': json(A.backdropsJson) };
}
function drawAtlasOverlay() {
  const A = atlasData(), k = A.json.scale?1:3;
  atlasCanvas.width = A.w * k; atlasCanvas.height = A.h * k;
  const g = atlasCanvas.getContext('2d');
  g.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
  for (let y = 0; y < A.h; y += 4) for (let x = 0; x < A.w; x += 4) {   // soft checker so transparency reads
    g.fillStyle = ((x + y) >> 2) & 1 ? '#' + PAL_DEF[C.BG0][1] : '#' + PAL_DEF[C.BG1][1];
    g.fillRect(x * k, y * k, 4 * k, 4 * k);
  }
  g.drawImage(ATLAS_CNV.col, 0, 0, A.w * k, A.h * k);
}
function toggleAtlas() {
  if (!atlasEl.classList.contains('on')) drawAtlasOverlay();
  atlasEl.classList.toggle('on');
}
for (const b of atlasEl.querySelectorAll('button[data-file]')) b.addEventListener('click', () => {
  const name = b.dataset.file;
  atlasFiles()[name]().then((blob) => {
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob); a.download = name;
    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 1000);
  });
});
// Review hooks: what the atlas holds, and a round trip through the PNG encoder
// (every opaque index pixel's PAL32[R] must equal the colour image's pixel).
__so.atlasInfo = () => {
  const A = atlasData(), S = A.json.sprites;
  return { w: A.w, h: A.h, sprites: S.length, frames: S.reduce((n, s) => n + s.frames.length, 0), names: S.map((s) => s.name) };
};
__so.atlasCheck = async () => {
  const F = atlasFiles(), sizes = {};
  for (const n of Object.keys(F)) sizes[n] = (await F[n]()).size;
  const decode = async (blob) => {
    const bm = await createImageBitmap(blob, { premultiplyAlpha: 'none', colorSpaceConversion: 'none' });
    const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = bm.width; c.height = bm.height;
    const g = c.getContext('2d'); g.drawImage(bm, 0, 0);
    return g.getImageData(0, 0, c.width, c.height).data;
  };
  const col = await decode(await F['atlas.png']()), ind = await decode(await F['atlas-index.png']()), A=atlasData();
  let opaque = 0, mismatches = 0, indexMismatches=0, transparencyMismatches=0;
  for (let i = 0; i < col.length; i += 4) {
    const k=i/4,alpha=A.idx[k]===255?0:255;
    if(col[i+3]!==alpha||ind[i+3]!==alpha)transparencyMismatches++;
    if(alpha&&(ind[i]!==A.idx[k]||ind[i+1]!==A.cls[k]||ind[i+2]!==A.sel[k]))indexMismatches++;
    if (ind[i + 3] === 0) continue;
    opaque++;
    const p = PAL32[ind[i]];
    if (col[i] !== (p & 255) || col[i + 1] !== ((p >> 8) & 255) || col[i + 2] !== ((p >> 16) & 255) || col[i + 3] !== 255) mismatches++;
  }
  return { sizes, opaque, mismatches:mismatches+indexMismatches+transparencyMismatches, colourMismatches:mismatches, indexMismatches, transparencyMismatches };
};
// ============================================================================
// 46 PERF (?perf): frame pacing read on the device itself, for checking a phone
// without a cable. Once a second it shows the display's frame rate, the
// simulation's steps per second (60 on any display), the JS time per frame
// (simulate, compose and present), late frames (a gap over 1.5 x the usual
// one) and frames that ran 2 or more steps. Totals skip the first second
// (start-up); gaps over 0.5 s (page hidden, phone locked) are counted apart.
// It only watches the loop: the game runs the same with it on or off.
// Swift: Instruments, or the same counters on a debug overlay.
// ============================================================================
const PERF_ON = /[?&]perf\b/.test(location.search);
const PERF_N = 240;                                  // recent gaps kept for the display rate (median)
const perf = { el: null, gaps: new Float64Array(PERF_N), sorted: new Float64Array(PERF_N), n: 0, k: 0, nominal: 1000 / 60,
  last: -1, t0: -1, shown: false, frames: 0, steps: 0, work: 0, workMax: 0, late: 0, multi: 0,
  lateTotal: 0, multiTotal: 0, worstGap: 0, workWorst: 0, pauses: 0 };
function perfInit() {
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.id = 'perf'; el.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true'); el.textContent = 'perf: measuring…';
  document.body.appendChild(el);
  perf.el = el;
}
// One display frame: its rAF timestamp, the fixed steps it ran and the JS time it took (ms).
function perfFrame(now, steps, ms) {
  const P = perf;
  if (P.last >= 0) {
    const gap = now - P.last;
    if (gap > 500) P.pauses++;
    else {
      P.gaps[P.k] = gap; P.k = (P.k + 1) % PERF_N; if (P.n < PERF_N) P.n++;
      if (gap > P.nominal * 1.5) { P.late++; if (P.shown) P.lateTotal++; }
      if (P.shown && gap > P.worstGap) P.worstGap = gap;
    }
  }
  P.last = now;
  if (P.t0 < 0) P.t0 = now;
  P.frames++; P.steps += steps; P.work += ms;
  if (ms > P.workMax) P.workMax = ms;
  if (P.shown && ms > P.workWorst) P.workWorst = ms;
  if (steps > 1) { P.multi++; if (P.shown) P.multiTotal++; }
  if (now - P.t0 >= 1000) perfShow(now);
}
function perfShow(now) {
  const P = perf, secs = (now - P.t0) / 1000;
  if (P.n > 0) {                                     // the usual gap: the median of the recent ones
    const s = P.sorted.subarray(0, P.n);
    s.set(P.gaps.subarray(0, P.n)); s.sort();
    P.nominal = s[P.n >> 1];
  }
  const f1 = (v) => v.toFixed(1);
  P.el.textContent =
    Math.round(1000 / P.nominal) + ' Hz display · ' + Math.round(P.steps / secs) + ' steps/s\n' +
    'JS ' + f1(P.work / Math.max(1, P.frames)) + ' ms/frame · max ' + f1(P.workMax) + ' (worst ' + f1(P.workWorst) + ')\n' +
    'late ' + P.late + ' · total ' + P.lateTotal + ' · worst gap ' + Math.round(P.worstGap) + ' ms\n' +
    '2+ steps ' + P.multi + ' · total ' + P.multiTotal + (P.pauses ? ' · pauses ' + P.pauses : '') + '\n' +
    devW + '×' + devH + ' @' + devDpr + ' · scale ' + scale;
  P.shown = true; P.t0 = now; P.frames = 0; P.steps = 0; P.work = 0; P.workMax = 0; P.late = 0; P.multi = 0;
}
if (PERF_ON) perfInit();
// ============================================================================
// 47 BOX ROOM REVIEW: ?boxroom shows one generated Box Room on its own, framed whole, with an empty board:
//   ?boxroom&seed=7&w=6&h=5&date=2026-10-16&mode=daily
// Defaults: today (the player's local date), a 6 x 5 board, that date's seed, daily. mode=free is the night
// shift. Review hooks for tools (tools/snap.js): __so.boxShot(args, k) -> PNG data URL of the whole room,
// __so.boxSheet(list, k, cols) -> a labelled contact sheet, __so.boxCoverage(list) -> tile-core check.
// args are [seed, w, h, date, mode], as in buildBoxRoom.
// ============================================================================
const BOX_REVIEW = (function () {
  const q = location.search;
  if (!/[?&]boxroom\b/.test(q)) return null;
  const arg = (k) => (new RegExp('[?&]' + k + '=([^&]*)').exec(q) || [])[1];
  const date = arg('date') && !Number.isNaN(DAILY.dayNumber(arg('date'))) ? arg('date') : DAILY.localKey(new Date());
  const num = (k, d, lo, hi) => { const v = parseInt(arg(k), 10); return Number.isNaN(v) ? d : clamp(v, lo, hi); };
  const seed = arg('seed') !== undefined && !Number.isNaN(parseInt(arg('seed'), 10)) ? parseInt(arg('seed'), 10) >>> 0 : BOXROOM.seedForDate(date);
  return { args: [seed, num('w', 6, 5, 10), num('h', 5, 4, 8), date, arg('mode') === 'free' ? 'free' : 'daily'] };
})();
if (/[?&]backdrop=floating\b/.test(location.search)) backdropTreatment = 'floating';
function boxRoomFor(args) { return BOXROOM.build(args[0], args[1], args[2], args[3], args[4], gCollection()); }
// Office (null), a generated Box Room, or the cached depot: bake only on room changes.
function gUseRoom(room) {
  if (room === G_ROOM) return;
  const wasRoom = !!G_ROOM;
  G_ROOM = room;
  if (room) { showRoom(room.id === 'depot' ? 'depot' : room.id === 'train' ? 'train' : room.id === 'cityhall' ? 'cityhall' : room.id === 'parcel' ? 'parcel' : room.id === 'christmas' ? 'christmas' : 'boxroom', room); return; }
  if (wasRoom) {
    useRoom('office'); ACTIVE_ROOM.bake();
    ROOM_FIT_W = OFFICE_FIT.w; ROOM_FIT_H = OFFICE_FIT.h; PAN_MAX_X = OFFICE_FIT.px; PAN_MAX_Y = OFFICE_FIT.py;
    if (devW > 1) applyScale();
    dirty = true;
  }
}
function showBoxRoom(room) { showRoom('boxroom', room); }
function showRoom(id, room) {
  useRoom(id, room);
  ACTIVE_ROOM.bake();
  const b = room.bounds;
  ROOM_FIT_W = b.sx1 - b.sx0 + 16; ROOM_FIT_H = b.sy1 - b.sy0 + 16;
  PAN_MAX_X = Math.ceil((b.sx1 - b.sx0) / 2); PAN_MAX_Y = Math.ceil((b.sy1 - b.sy0) / 2);
  if (devW > 1) applyScale();
  dirty = true;
}
// The whole room at k x, without the vignette: a canvas.
function boxRenderFull(k) {
  const b = BOXR.bounds, pad = 8, w = b.sx1 - b.sx0 + 2 * pad, h = b.sy1 - b.sy0 + 2 * pad;
  const keep = { LW, LH, panX: view.panX, panY: view.panY, paperScale:paperViewScale };
  paperSetViewScale(PAPER_SCALE);
  LW = w; LH = h; ensureFrameBuffers(LW, LH); allocView();
  view.panX = 0; view.panY = 0; WS.camX = 0; WS.camY = 0;
  viewX = Math.round(VIEW_CX - LW / 2); viewY = Math.round(VIEW_CY - LH / 2);
  composeStatic(); targetFrame(viewX, viewY); drawDynamic(); postPasses(); resolve();
  const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = w * k; c.height = h * k;
  const g = c.getContext('2d'); g.imageSmoothingEnabled = false; g.drawImage(paperOutputCanvas(), 0, 0, w * k, h * k);
  LW = keep.LW; LH = keep.LH; view.panX = keep.panX; view.panY = keep.panY;
  paperSetViewScale(keep.paperScale);
  ensureFrameBuffers(LW, LH); allocView(); buildVignette(); dirty = true;
  return c;
}
const BOX_DAY = { mon: 'Monday', tue: 'Tuesday', wed: 'Wednesday', thu: 'Thursday', fri: 'Friday', sat: 'Saturday', sun: 'Sunday', night: 'night shift' };
function boxCaption(r) {
  return r.board.w + ' × ' + r.board.h + ' · seed ' + r.inputs.seed + ' · ' + r.inputs.date + ' · ' + BOX_DAY[r.light.set] + ', ' + r.season +
    (r.rain ? ', rain' : '') + (r.light.sun ? ' · ' + Math.round(r.light.sunlit / 10) + '% sunlit' : '');
}
if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.__so) {

  // Rooms in a grid, each captioned underneath (the captions are the sheet's, not the scene's).
  __so.boxSheet = (list, k, cols) => {
    k = k || 2; cols = cols || 3;
    const shots = list.map((a) => { const r = boxRoomFor(a); showBoxRoom(r); return { c: boxRenderFull(k), cap: boxCaption(r) }; });
    const cw = Math.max(...shots.map((s) => s.c.width)), ch = Math.max(...shots.map((s) => s.c.height)) + 22 * k / 2 + 8;
    const rows = Math.ceil(shots.length / cols), out = document.createElement('canvas');
    out.width = cols * cw; out.height = rows * ch;
    const g = out.getContext('2d');
    g.fillStyle = BG_CSS; g.fillRect(0, 0, out.width, out.height);
    g.imageSmoothingEnabled = false; g.fillStyle = '#4f3f45'; g.font = (7 * k) + 'px Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif'; g.textAlign = 'center';
    shots.forEach((s, i) => {
      const x = (i % cols) * cw, y = Math.floor(i / cols) * ch;
      g.drawImage(s.c, x + (cw - s.c.width) / 2, y + (ch - 22 * k / 2 - 8 - s.c.height));
      g.fillText(s.cap, x + cw / 2, y + ch - 8);
    });
    if (BOX_REVIEW) showBoxRoom(boxRoomFor(BOX_REVIEW.args));
    return out.toDataURL('image/png');
  };
  // The coats: each in every napping pose, in four lights (Monday, Friday, Sunday, night), in sunlight and in
  // grayscale, beside the board's ginger and blue. Returns a PNG data URL.
  __so.coatSheet = (k) => {
    k = k || 4;
    const SETS = ['mon', 'fri', 'sun', 'night'], cols = [], CW = 26, CH = 14;
    for (const s of SETS) for (const p of NAP_POSE_IDS) cols.push({ set: s, pose: p, lit: false });
    for (const p of NAP_POSE_IDS) cols.push({ set: 'fri', pose: p, lit: true });
    cols.push({ set: 'fri', pose: 'curl', lit: false, gray: true });
    const rows = COAT_IDS.map((id) => ({ id, name: COATS[id].name })).concat([{ ref: COAT_GINGER, name: 'board: ginger' }, { ref: COAT_BLUE, name: 'board: blue' }]);
    const out = document.createElement('canvas'), LBL = 70 * k / 4 * 1.6;
    out.width = Math.round(LBL + cols.length * CW * k); out.height = Math.round((rows.length * CH + 12) * k);
    const g = out.getContext('2d');
    g.fillStyle = BG_CSS; g.fillRect(0, 0, out.width, out.height);
    g.fillStyle = '#4f3f45'; g.font = (3 * k) + 'px Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif';
    const heads = ['Monday', 'Friday', 'Sunday', 'night', 'in the sun', 'grey'];
    [0, 3, 6, 9, 12, 15].forEach((c, i) => g.fillText(heads[i], LBL + c * CW * k + 2 * k, 8 * k));
    const img = g.createImageData(CW * k, CH * k), px = new Uint32Array(img.data.buffer);
    rows.forEach((r, ri) => {
      g.fillStyle = '#4f3f45'; g.fillText(r.name, 3 * k, (12 + ri * CH + CH * 0.6) * k);
      cols.forEach((col, ci) => {
        const cell = r.ref ? boardCatCell(r.ref, col.lit) : coatCell(r.id, col.pose, 0, ri * 3 + ci, col.lit);
        const T = LIGHT_TABLES[col.set], tab = col.gray ? T.gray : T.res;
        px.fill(0);
        for (let y = 0; y < CH; y++) for (let x = 0; x < CW; x++) {
          const sx = AC / 2 - CW / 2 + x, sy = ACY - CH + 3 + y, i = sy * AC + sx;
          if (sx < 0 || sy < 0 || sx >= AC || sy >= AC || cell.idx[i] === 255) continue;
          const c = tab[cell.lvl[i] * PAL_N + cell.idx[i]];
          for (let j = 0; j < k; j++) for (let q = 0; q < k; q++) px[(y * k + j) * CW * k + x * k + q] = c;
        }
        const tmp = document.createElement('canvas'); tmp.width = CW * k; tmp.height = CH * k; tmp.getContext('2d').putImageData(img, 0, 0);
        g.drawImage(tmp, LBL + ci * CW * k, (12 + ri * CH) * k);
      });
    });
    return out.toDataURL('image/png');
  };
  // Bake each room and check every tile core shows its own stone floor; the review room comes back after.
  __so.boxCoverage = (list) => {
    const out = { rooms: 0, tiles: 0, bad: [], ms: 0 };
    const t0 = performance.now();
    for (const a of list) {
      const r = boxRoomFor(a);
      useBoxRoom(r); bakeBoxRoom();
      out.rooms++; out.tiles += r.board.w * r.board.h;
      for (const t of bxCheckCores()) if (out.bad.length < 40) out.bad.push(a.join(',') + ' tile ' + t[0] + ',' + t[1] + ' ' + t[2]);
    }
    out.ms = Math.round(performance.now() - t0);
    if (BOX_REVIEW) showBoxRoom(boxRoomFor(BOX_REVIEW.args));
    return out;
  };
}
