// ============================================================================
// 48 BACKDROP REVIEW: actual room renders at phone aspect, no mock RGB repaint. Review helpers restore
// the room, camera, time, light, grayscale and buffers so making a sheet cannot change a running game.
// ============================================================================
function backdropPhone(args, treatment, gray) {
  const keep = { room: BOXR, bd: BD, treatment: backdropTreatment, gray: grayscale, LW, LH, panX: view.panX, panY: view.panY,
    camX: WS.camX, camY: WS.camY, loopT, fitW: ROOM_FIT_W, fitH: ROOM_FIT_H, maxX: PAN_MAX_X, maxY: PAN_MAX_Y };
  try {
    const room = boxRoomFor(args);
    useBoxRoom(room); bakeBoxRoom(); backdropTreatment = treatment; grayscale = !!gray;
    const b = room.bounds, s = Math.max(1, VIEWPORT.roomFitScale(1179, 2556, b.sx1 - b.sx0 + 16, b.sy1 - b.sy0 + 16));
    LW = Math.floor(1179 / s); LH = Math.floor(2556 / s);
    ensureFrameBuffers(LW, LH); allocView(); buildVignette();
    view.panX = 0; view.panY = 0; WS.camX = 0; WS.camY = 0; loopT = 10;
    // Review an empty room even if this helper was called from the game.
    viewX = Math.round(VIEW_CX - LW / 2); viewY = Math.round(VIEW_CY - LH / 2);
    composeStatic(); targetFrame(viewX, viewY);
    drawBoxNaps(loopT); drawBoxDressing(loopT); if (room.light.string) drawBoxString(loopT);
    applyVignette(); resolve();
    const out = document.createElement('canvas'); out.width = 1179; out.height = 2556;
    const ctx = out.getContext('2d'); ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    ctx.drawImage(offCanvas, 0, 0, LW, LH, 0, 0, LW * s, LH * s);
    if (LW * s < 1179) ctx.drawImage(offCanvas, LW - 1, 0, 1, LH, LW * s, 0, 1179 - LW * s, LH * s);
    if (LH * s < 2556) ctx.drawImage(out, 0, LH * s - 1, 1179, 1, 0, LH * s, 1179, 2556 - LH * s);
    return out;
  } finally {
    if (keep.room) { useBoxRoom(keep.room); bakeBoxRoom(); } else { useOffice(); bakeStatic(); bakeOutside(); initDust(); }
    BD = keep.bd; backdropTreatment = keep.treatment; grayscale = keep.gray;
    LW = keep.LW; LH = keep.LH; view.panX = keep.panX; view.panY = keep.panY; WS.camX = keep.camX; WS.camY = keep.camY; loopT = keep.loopT;
    ROOM_FIT_W = keep.fitW; ROOM_FIT_H = keep.fitH; PAN_MAX_X = keep.maxX; PAN_MAX_Y = keep.maxY;
    ensureFrameBuffers(LW, LH); allocView(); buildVignette(); dirty = true;
  }
}
__so.backdropShot = (args, treatment, gray) => backdropPhone(args, treatment || 'cutaway', gray).toDataURL('image/png');
__so.backdropSheet = (list, treatment, gray, cols) => {
  cols = cols || 4;
  const cw = 393, ch = 886, gap = 12, out = document.createElement('canvas');
  out.width = cols * (cw + gap) + gap; out.height = Math.ceil(list.length / cols) * (ch + gap) + gap;
  const ctx = out.getContext('2d'); ctx.fillStyle = '#242430'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, out.width, out.height);
  ctx.font = '12px ui-monospace, monospace'; ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
  list.forEach((args, i) => {
    const x = gap + (i % cols) * (cw + gap), y = gap + Math.floor(i / cols) * (ch + gap);
    const mode = Array.isArray(treatment) ? treatment[i] : treatment;
    const c = backdropPhone(args, mode, gray);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#e9e4d8'; ctx.fillText(mode + ' · ' + args[1] + '×' + args[2] + ' · #' + args[0], x, y + 12);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#abb5c7'; ctx.fillText(args[3] + ' · ' + (args[4] === 'free' ? 'night' : BOX_DAY[boxRoomFor(args).light.set]), x, y + 27);
    ctx.drawImage(c, x, y + 34, cw, 852);
  });
  return out.toDataURL('image/png');
};

    const button = document.createElement('button'); button.textContent = treatment === 'cutaway' ? 'Cutaway' : 'Floating';
    button.onclick = () => { backdropTreatment = treatment; dirty = true; };
    bar.appendChild(button);
  }
  const gray = document.createElement('button'); gray.textContent = 'Grayscale'; gray.onclick = () => { grayscale = !grayscale; dirty = true; }; bar.appendChild(gray);
  document.body.appendChild(bar);
}

/* Locally adapted from approved Office projection; original remains unchanged. */
(function (root, factory) {
  'use strict';
  const api = factory();
  if (typeof module === 'object' && module.exports) module.exports = api;
  root.ParcelProjection = api;
})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this, function () {
  'use strict';
  const HW = 380 / 6;
  const HH = 190 / 6;

  function project(x, y, z = 0) {
    return { x: 760 + (x - y) * HW, y: 465 + (x + y) * HH - z };
  }
  function centre(x, y) { return project(x + 0.5, y + 0.5); }
  function unproject(px, py, z = 0) {
    const difference = (px - 760) / HW;
    const sum = (py - 465 + z) / HH;
    return { x: (sum + difference) / 2, y: (sum - difference) / 2 };
  }
  function corners(w, h) {
    return [project(0, 0), project(w, 0), project(w, h), project(0, h)];
  }
  function pick(px, py, w, h) {
    const point = unproject(px, py);
    if (point.x < 0 || point.y < 0 || point.x >= w || point.y >= h) return null;
    return { x: Math.floor(point.x), y: Math.floor(point.y) };
  }
  function camera({ width, height, w, h, mode = 'play', zoom = 1, panX = 0, panY = 0, sceneBounds = null }) {
    // The canvas occupies its own DOM region: HUD space is already excluded.
    let left = -200, right = 1700, top = 0, bottom = 1100;
    if(mode==='explore'&&sceneBounds)({left,right,top,bottom}=sceneBounds);
    if (mode !== 'explore') {
      const c = corners(w, h);
      left = Math.min(...c.map(p => p.x)) - 80;
      right = Math.max(...c.map(p => p.x)) + 80;
      top = Math.min(...c.map(p => p.y)) - 120;
      bottom = Math.max(...c.map(p => p.y)) + 50;
    }
    const viewportWidth = Math.max(1, width), viewportHeight = Math.max(1, height);
    const scale = Math.min(viewportWidth / (right - left), viewportHeight / (bottom - top)) * Math.max(0.01, zoom);
    return { scale, tx: viewportWidth / 2 - (left + right) * scale / 2 + panX,
      ty: viewportHeight / 2 - (top + bottom) * scale / 2 + panY };
  }
  function toScreen(point, camera) {
    return { x: point.x * camera.scale + camera.tx, y: point.y * camera.scale + camera.ty };
  }
  function fromScreen(point, camera) {
    return { x: (point.x - camera.tx) / camera.scale, y: (point.y - camera.ty) / camera.scale };
  }
  function zoomAt(config, point, newZoom) {
    const anchor = fromScreen(point, camera(config));
    const next = toScreen(anchor, camera({ ...config, zoom: newZoom, panX: 0, panY: 0 }));
    return { panX: point.x - next.x, panY: point.y - next.y };
  }
  return { HW, HH, project, centre, unproject, corners, pick, camera, toScreen, fromScreen, zoomAt };
});

 function vanMesh(closed,cargo=false,complete=false){
  const out=[];
  function face(part,v,fill,texture,normal){out.push({part,vertices:v,fill,texture,...(complete&&normal?{normal}:{}),depth:v.reduce((s,p)=>s+p[0]+p[1]+p[2],0)/v.length});}
  function box(part,x,y,z,X,Y,Z,col){
   face(part,[[X,y,z],[X,Y,z],[X,Y,Z],[X,y,Z]],col[2],null,[1,0,0]);
   face(part,[[x,Y,z],[X,Y,z],[X,Y,Z],[x,Y,Z]],col[1],null,[0,1,0]);
   face(part,[[x,y,Z],[X,y,Z],[X,Y,Z],[x,Y,Z]],col[0]);
   if(complete){face(part,[[x,y,z],[x,Y,z],[x,Y,Z],[x,y,Z]],col[1],null,[-1,0,0]);face(part,[[x,y,z],[X,y,z],[X,y,Z],[x,y,Z]],col[2],null,[0,-1,0]);}
  }
  const red=['#c8765e','#b45948','#934439'],metal=['#c6c7b6','#9daaa5','#788d8e'];
  box('chassis',170,-37,4,200,-5,8,['#637679','#3f535a','#334750']);
  for(const y of [-34,-11])for(const x of [169,203]){
   for(let i=0;i<32;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/16,b=(i+1)*Math.PI/16;face('tread',[[x-1,y+5*Math.cos(a),5+5*Math.sin(a)],[x+1,y+5*Math.cos(a),5+5*Math.sin(a)],[x+1,y+5*Math.cos(b),5+5*Math.sin(b)],[x-1,y+5*Math.cos(b),5+5*Math.sin(b)]],i<16?'#54594e':'#343d3a');}
   const points=[];for(let i=0;i<40;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/20;points.push([x+1,y+5*Math.cos(a),5+5*Math.sin(a)]);}
   face('tyre',points,'#3e4542');
   const hub=[];for(let i=0;i<32;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/16;hub.push([x+1.08,y+2.7*Math.cos(a),5+2.7*Math.sin(a)]);}
   face('hub',hub,'#c8b18d');
   const centre=[];for(let i=0;i<24;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/12;centre.push([x+1.12,y+1.1*Math.cos(a),5+1.1*Math.sin(a)]);}face('hub',centre,'#7f8174');
  }
  box('floor',170,-24,9,201,-7,11,['#bc9d6e','#93784e','#8e7454']);
  box('body',169,-24,9,172,-7,26,red);box('body',199,-24,9,202,-7,26,red);
  box('bulkhead',172,-25,10,199,-22,27,red);
  face('rear-opening',[[172,-21.9,11],[199,-21.9,11],[199,-21.9,25.5],[172,-21.9,25.5]],'#493d31');
  if(cargo){box('cargo',181,-16,11,189,-8,17,['#e9c38c','#c69a64','#b58b5c']);box('cargo-tape',184.4,-16,17.02,185.5,-8,17.04,['#f1dcb0','#f1dcb0','#f1dcb0']);}
  for(let i=0;i<32;i++){
   const a=-Math.PI/2+i*Math.PI/32,b=a+Math.PI/32,x=185.5+16.5*Math.sin(a),X=185.5+16.5*Math.sin(b),z=26+4*Math.cos(a),Z=26+4*Math.cos(b),k=Math.round(160+26*Math.cos(a));
   face('roof',[[x,-24,z],[X,-24,Z],[X,-7,Z],[x,-7,z]],`rgb(${k+23},${Math.round(k*.56)},${Math.round(k*.44)})`);
  }
  const cap=[[169,-6.98,26],[202,-6.98,26]];for(let i=32;i>=0;i--){const a=-Math.PI/2+i*Math.PI/32;cap.push([185.5+16.5*Math.sin(a),-6.98,26+4*Math.cos(a)]);}face('roof-end',cap,'#b0604d');
  box('cab',173,-33,9,199,-25,24,red);
  face('cab',[[199.1,-32,9],[199.1,-25,9],[199.1,-25,24],[199.1,-32,24]],'#ae5544',{name:'cab-door',origin:[199.11,-25,24],u:[0,-7,0],v:[0,0,-15]});
  for(let i=0;i<20;i++){
   const y=-42+i*11/20,Y=y+11/20,w=12*Math.min(1,(y+43)/7),W=12*Math.min(1,(Y+43)/7),z=16+Math.sin((y+42)/12*Math.PI)*3,Z=16+Math.sin((Y+42)/12*Math.PI)*3;
   face('bonnet',[[185-w,y,z],[185+w,y,z],[185+W,Y,Z],[185-W,Y,Z]],'#bd6752');
   face('bonnet',[[185+w,y,8],[185+W,Y,8],[185+W,Y,Z],[185+w,y,z]],'#a74f3e');
  }
  box('bumper',171,-42,7,200,-40.8,9,metal);
  // The two door leaves use independent planes, never a stretched whole van.
  if(closed){
   box('rear-doors',170,-7,10,201,-6,26,red);
   face('rear-doors',[[170,-5.98,10],[201,-5.98,10],[201,-5.98,26],[170,-5.98,26]],'#b05a47',{name:'rear-closed',origin:[170,-5.97,26],u:[31,0,0],v:[0,0,-16]});
  }else{
   face('left-leaf',[[169,-7,10],[173,0,10],[173,0,25],[169,-7,25]],'#bb624c',{name:'rear-open',rect:'leftLeafRect',origin:[169,-7,25],u:[4,7,0],v:[0,0,-15]});
   face('right-leaf',[[201,-7,10],[197,0,10],[197,0,25],[201,-7,25]],'#a74e3e',{name:'rear-open',rect:'rightLeafRect',origin:[197,0,25],u:[4,-7,0],v:[0,0,-15]});
   face('ramp',[[177,-8,11],[193,-8,11],[193,0,10],[177,0,10]],'#b9a378');
  }
  box('bumper',167,-5,5,203,-3,7,metal);
  // Visible-side envelope emblem is a small inset face on the body.
  face('badge',[[202.08,-21,16],[202.08,-13,16],[202.08,-13,22],[202.08,-21,22]],'#e8d5ad');
  face('badge-flap',[[202.1,-21,22],[202.1,-17,18.8],[202.1,-13,22],[202.1,-17,20]],'#b4624a');
  if(complete){
   // The reverse side and front become visible only during the visual turn.
   face('cab',[[172.9,-25,9],[172.9,-32,9],[172.9,-32,24],[172.9,-25,24]],'#b35e49',{name:'cab-door',origin:[172.89,-32,24],u:[0,7,0],v:[0,0,-15]},[-1,0,0]);
   face('windscreen',[[175,-33.05,18],[197,-33.05,18],[197,-33.05,23],[175,-33.05,23]],'#829e98',null,[0,-1,0]);
   box('windscreen-bar',185,-33.13,18,185.5,-33.06,23,['#dccb9f','#dccb9f','#c2b18b']);
   for(let i=0;i<20;i++){const y=-42+i*11/20,Y=y+11/20,w=12*Math.min(1,(y+43)/7),W=12*Math.min(1,(Y+43)/7),z=16+Math.sin((y+42)/12*Math.PI)*3,Z=16+Math.sin((Y+42)/12*Math.PI)*3;face('bonnet',[[185-w,y,8],[185-W,Y,8],[185-W,Y,Z],[185-w,y,z]],'#bd6752',null,[-1,0,0]);}
   face('grille',[[183.3,-42.05,9],[186.7,-42.05,9],[186.7,-42.05,16],[183.3,-42.05,16]],'#c9b88e',null,[0,-1,0]);
   for(const x of [183.8,184.6,185.4,186.2])face('grille-slat',[[x,-42.08,9.5],[x+.3,-42.08,9.5],[x+.3,-42.08,15.5],[x,-42.08,15.5]],'#576762',null,[0,-1,0]);
   for(const x of [177,193])box('headlamp',x,-39.7,11,x+2,-38.5,13,['#fff0b4','#e0c99c','#f3d598']);
   face('badge',[[168.92,-13,16],[168.92,-21,16],[168.92,-21,22],[168.92,-13,22]],'#e8d5ad',null,[-1,0,0]);
   face('badge-flap',[[168.9,-21,22],[168.9,-17,18.8],[168.9,-13,22],[168.9,-17,20]],'#b4624a',null,[-1,0,0]);
   for(const y of [-34,-11])for(const x of [169,203])for(const [r,fill,offset] of [[5,'#3e4542',-1],[2.7,'#c8b18d',-1.08],[1.1,'#7f8174',-1.12]]){const pts=[];for(let i=0;i<40;i++){const t=i*Math.PI/20;pts.push([x+offset,y+r*Math.cos(t),5+r*Math.sin(t)]);}face(r===5?'tyre':'hub',pts,fill,null,[-1,0,0]);}
   // Add normals to pre-existing detail planes without changing parked artwork.
   for(const f of out)if(!f.normal){if(['tyre','hub','badge','badge-flap'].includes(f.part)||f.texture?.name==='cab-door')f.normal=[1,0,0];else if(f.part==='roof-end'||f.texture?.name==='rear-closed')f.normal=[0,1,0];}
   // Surface details belong to their receiving face. Their centroids can sit
   // farther away than that face during a turn, so depth alone is insufficient.
   for(const f of out){
    if(f.part==='cab'&&!f.texture){if(f.normal?.[0]===1)f.surface='cab-right';else if(f.normal?.[0]===-1)f.surface='cab-left';else if(f.normal?.[1]===-1)f.surface='cab-front';}
    if(f.texture?.name==='cab-door')f.onSurface=f.normal[0]===1?'cab-right':'cab-left';
    if(f.part==='windscreen'){f.surface='windscreen';f.onSurface='cab-front';}
    if(f.part==='windscreen-bar')f.onSurface='windscreen';
   }
  }
  return out.sort((a,b)=>a.depth-b.depth);
 }
 function vanCacheBounds(meshes,unit){
  let left=Infinity,right=-Infinity,top=Infinity,bottom=-Infinity;
  for(const mesh of meshes)for(const f of mesh)for(const p of f.vertices){const x=(p[0]-p[1]-185)*unit,y=(.5*(p[0]+p[1]-185)-p[2])*unit;left=Math.min(left,x);right=Math.max(right,x);top=Math.min(top,y);bottom=Math.max(bottom,y);}
  left=Math.floor(left)-4;top=Math.floor(top)-4;right=Math.ceil(right)+4;bottom=Math.ceil(bottom)+4;
  return {left,top,width:right-left,height:bottom-top};
 }
 function clipBuffers(){return {a:Array.from({length:64},()=>[0,0,0]),b:Array.from({length:64},()=>[0,0,0]),result:null};}
 function plane(src,dst,n,a,b,c,d){let count=0;if(!n)return 0;let p=src[n-1],v=a*p[0]+b*p[1]+c*p[2]+d;
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const q=src[i],w=a*q[0]+b*q[1]+c*q[2]+d;
   if((v>=0)!==(w>=0)){const t=v/(v-w),r=dst[count++];for(let k=0;k<3;k++)r[k]=p[k]+(q[k]-p[k])*t;}
   if(w>=0){const r=dst[count++];r[0]=q[0];r[1]=q[1];r[2]=q[2];}p=q;v=w;
  }return count;
 }
 function clipVanFace(face,dx,dy,inside,scratch){
  let n=face.vertices.length,a=scratch.a,b=scratch.b,tmp;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){a[i][0]=face.vertices[i][0]+dx;a[i][1]=face.vertices[i][1]+dy;a[i][2]=face.vertices[i][2];}
  n=plane(a,b,n,0,inside?1:-1,0,inside?-4:4);tmp=a;a=b;b=tmp;
  if(!inside){
   // View rays advance equally in x,y,z. At the wall's front y=4,
   // x becomes x+4-y and z becomes z+4-y: clip to the actual opening.
   n=plane(a,b,n,1,-1,0,-162);tmp=a;a=b;b=tmp;
   n=plane(a,b,n,-1,1,0,242);tmp=a;a=b;b=tmp;
   n=plane(a,b,n,0,1,-1,62);tmp=a;a=b;b=tmp;
  }
  scratch.result=a;return n;
 }
 return {frame,bay,yard,racks,scales,vanBounds,vanMesh,vanCacheBounds,clipBuffers,clipVanFace};
});

/* Visual heading only. Canonical centre, rear contact, clocks and cargo stay native. */
(function(root,factory){const api=factory();if(typeof module==='object'&&module.exports)module.exports=api;root.ParcelVanHeading=api;})(globalThis,function(){'use strict';
 const smooth=t=>{t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t));return t*t*(3-2*t);};
 function angle(frame){const f=((Math.floor(frame)%2880)+2880)%2880;return f<144?Math.PI/2*(1-smooth((f-124)/20)):f<=2400?0:-Math.PI/2*smooth((f-2400)/60);}
 function index(frame){return Math.round(angle(frame)*60/Math.PI)+30;}
 function point(p,a,out){if(a===0){out[0]=p[0];out[1]=p[1];out[2]=p[2];return out;}const c=Math.cos(a),s=Math.sin(a),x=p[0]-185,y=p[1]+22;out[0]=185+x*c-y*s;out[1]=-22+x*s+y*c;out[2]=p[2];return out;}
 function mesh(source,a){
  const c=Math.cos(a),s=Math.sin(a),vector=v=>[v[0]*c-v[1]*s,v[0]*s+v[1]*c,v[2]];
  const faces=source.filter(f=>!f.normal||vector(f.normal).reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0)>.001).map(f=>{const vertices=f.vertices.map(p=>point(p,a,[0,0,0])),texture=f.texture?{...f.texture,origin:point(f.texture.origin,a,[0,0,0]),u:vector(f.texture.u),v:vector(f.texture.v)}:undefined;return {...f,vertices,texture,depth:vertices.reduce((n,p)=>n+p[0]+p[1]+p[2],0)/vertices.length};}).sort((a,b)=>a.depth-b.depth);
  const children=new Map(),out=[];
  for(const f of faces)if(f.onSurface){if(!children.has(f.onSurface))children.set(f.onSurface,[]);children.get(f.onSurface).push(f);}
  const append=f=>{out.push(f);for(const child of children.get(f.surface)||[])append(child);};
  for(const f of faces)if(!f.onSurface)append(f);
  return out;
 }
 return {angle,index,point,mesh};
});

/* Native van artwork. The rear receiving floor and loading contacts stay exact. */
(function(root,factory){const C=typeof module==='object'&&module.exports?require('./construction.js'):root.ParcelConstruction,api=factory(C);if(typeof module==='object'&&module.exports)module.exports=api;root.ParcelVanBody=api;})(globalThis,function(C){'use strict';
 const wheels=[[169,-34,5],[203,-34,5],[169,-11,5],[203,-11,5]];
 function normal(v){const a=v[1].map((n,i)=>n-v[0][i]),b=v[2].map((n,i)=>n-v[0][i]),n=[a[1]*b[2]-a[2]*b[1],a[2]*b[0]-a[0]*b[2],a[0]*b[1]-a[1]*b[0]],l=Math.hypot(...n)||1;return n.map(x=>x/l);}
 function mesh(closed,cargo=false){
  const out=[];
  function face(part,vertices,material='red',guide,extra={}){let n=normal(vertices);if(guide&&n.reduce((s,v,i)=>s+v*guide[i],0)<0)n=n.map(v=>-v);out.push({part,vertices,material,normal:n,...extra});}
  function box(part,x,y,z,X,Y,Z,material){
   face(part,[[X,y,z],[X,Y,z],[X,Y,Z],[X,y,Z]],material,[1,0,0]);face(part,[[x,Y,z],[X,Y,z],[X,Y,Z],[x,Y,Z]],material,[0,1,0]);face(part,[[x,y,Z],[X,y,Z],[X,Y,Z],[x,Y,Z]],material,[0,0,1]);
   face(part,[[x,y,z],[x,Y,z],[x,Y,Z],[x,y,Z]],material,[-1,0,0]);face(part,[[x,y,z],[X,y,z],[X,y,Z],[x,y,Z]],material,[0,-1,0]);face(part,[[x,y,z],[X,y,z],[X,Y,z],[x,Y,z]],material,[0,0,-1]);
  }
  function tube(part,a,b,r,material,sides=10){
   const d=b.map((v,i)=>v-a[i]),length=Math.hypot(...d),n=d.map(v=>v/length),ref=Math.abs(n[2])<.9?[0,0,1]:[1,0,0],u=[n[1]*ref[2]-n[2]*ref[1],n[2]*ref[0]-n[0]*ref[2],n[0]*ref[1]-n[1]*ref[0]],ul=Math.hypot(...u);for(let k=0;k<3;k++)u[k]/=ul;const v=[n[1]*u[2]-n[2]*u[1],n[2]*u[0]-n[0]*u[2],n[0]*u[1]-n[1]*u[0]],point=(o,t)=>o.map((x,i)=>x+r*(u[i]*Math.cos(t)+v[i]*Math.sin(t)));
   for(let i=0;i<sides;i++){const t=i*2*Math.PI/sides,T=(i+1)*2*Math.PI/sides;face(part,[point(a,t),point(a,T),point(b,T),point(b,t)],material,point([0,0,0],(t+T)/2));}
   face(part,Array.from({length:sides},(_,i)=>point(a,i*2*Math.PI/sides)),material,n.map(x=>-x));face(part,Array.from({length:sides},(_,i)=>point(b,i*2*Math.PI/sides)),material,n);
  }
  function disk(part,x,y,z,r,material,axis='x',sign=1){const vertices=[];for(let i=0;i<48;i++){const t=i*Math.PI/24;vertices.push(axis==='x'?[x,y+r*Math.cos(t),z+r*Math.sin(t)]:[x+r*Math.cos(t),y,z+r*Math.sin(t)]);}face(part,vertices,material,axis==='x'?[sign,0,0]:[0,sign,0]);}
  function roundPoints(o,u,v,w,h,r){
   const verts=[];for(const [cx,cy,start] of [[r,r,Math.PI],[w-r,r,Math.PI*1.5],[w-r,h-r,0],[r,h-r,Math.PI*.5]])for(let i=0;i<=6;i++){const t=start+i*Math.PI/12,a=cx+r*Math.cos(t),b=cy+r*Math.sin(t);verts.push(o.map((x,k)=>x+u[k]*a+v[k]*b));}return verts;
  }
  function rounded(part,o,u,v,w,h,r,material,guide){face(part,roundPoints(o,u,v,w,h,r),material,guide);}
  function ring(part,o,u,v,w,h,r,t,material,guide){const outer=roundPoints(o,u,v,w,h,r),inner=roundPoints(o.map((n,i)=>n+t*u[i]+t*v[i]-guide[i]*.025),u,v,w-2*t,h-2*t,Math.max(.1,r-t));for(let i=0;i<outer.length;i++){const j=(i+1)%outer.length;face(part,[outer[i],outer[j],inner[j],inner[i]],material,guide);}}
  function emblem(x,sign){const q=x+sign*.035;face('postal-badge',[[q,-21.5,15.5],[q,-14.5,15.5],[q,-14.5,21],[q,-21.5,21]],'ivory',[sign,0,0]);face('postal-flap',[[q+sign*.025,-21.1,20.6],[q+sign*.025,-18,18.3],[q+sign*.025,-14.9,20.6],[q+sign*.025,-18,19]],'red',[sign,0,0]);}
  box('chassis',170,-37,4,200,-5,7,'chassis');
  // Rolled tyre shoulders and inset wheel centres, all four with z0 contacts.
  for(const [x,y,z] of wheels){
   const rings=[[-1,4.35],[-.75,4.85],[-.45,5],[.45,5],[.75,4.85],[1,4.35]];
   for(let k=0;k<rings.length-1;k++)for(let i=0;i<48;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/24,b=(i+1)*Math.PI/24,[xx,r]=rings[k],[X,R]=rings[k+1],pt=(dx,rr,t)=>[x+dx,y+rr*Math.cos(t),z+rr*Math.sin(t)];face('tyre',[pt(xx,r,a),pt(xx,r,b),pt(X,R,b),pt(X,R,a)],'rubber',[0,Math.cos((a+b)/2),Math.sin((a+b)/2)]);}
   for(const sign of [-1,1]){disk('tyre',x+sign,y,z,4.35,'rubber','x',sign);disk('wheel-rim',x+sign*1.025,y,z,2.9,'metal','x',sign);disk('hub-recess',x+sign*1.04,y,z,2.3,'chassis','x',sign);disk('hub',x+sign*1.08,y,z,1.75,'cream-metal','x',sign);disk('hub-button',x+sign*1.12,y,z,.65,'metal','x',sign);for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const t=i*Math.PI*.4;disk('wheel-bolt',x+sign*1.09,y+Math.cos(t)*2.05,z+Math.sin(t)*2.05,.18,'cream-metal','x',sign);}}
  }
  // Side skirts have genuine wheel openings; the arches are separate curved strips.
  for(const [x,sign] of [[169,-1],[202,1]]){
   const bottom=y=>Math.abs(y+11)<6.15?Math.max(6.3,5+Math.sqrt(6.15**2-(y+11)**2)):6.3;
   for(let y=-24;y<-7;y+=.5){const Y=Math.min(-7,y+.5);face('body',[[x,y,bottom(y)],[x,Y,bottom(Y)],[x,Y,24],[x,y,24]],'red',[sign,0,0]);}
   box('rear-pillar',sign<0?169:199,-8.4,11,sign<0?172:202,-7,25.8,'red');
   face('inner-lining',[[sign<0?172:199,-23.8,11],[sign<0?172:199,-8.4,11],[sign<0?172:199,-8.4,24],[sign<0?172:199,-23.8,24]],'wood',[-sign,0,0]);
   emblem(x,sign);tube('body-seam',[x+sign*.08,-23.5,13.4],[x+sign*.08,-8,13.4],.09,'edge');
  }
  for(const [x,y] of wheels){const sign=x<185?-1:1,inner=sign<0?173.7:198.3,outer=sign<0?167.6:204.1;
   for(let i=0;i<36;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/36,b=(i+1)*Math.PI/36,pt=(xx,r,t)=>[xx,y+r*Math.cos(t),5+r*Math.sin(t)];
    face('wheel-arch',[pt(inner,6.25,a),pt(inner,6.25,b),pt(outer,6.25,b),pt(outer,6.25,a)],'red',[0,Math.cos((a+b)/2),Math.sin((a+b)/2)]);
    face('wheel-arch',[pt(outer,5.5,a),pt(outer,5.5,b),pt(outer,6.25,b),pt(outer,6.25,a)],'roof',[sign,0,0]);
    face('arch-underlip',[pt(inner,5.5,a),pt(outer,5.5,a),pt(outer,5.5,b),pt(inner,5.5,b)],'chassis',[0,-Math.cos((a+b)/2),-Math.sin((a+b)/2)]);
   }
  }
  for(const [x,X] of [[168,174],[198,204]]){box('running-board',x,-27.7,5.7,X,-17.2,6.35,'chassis');tube('running-board-rim',[x,-27.7,6.4],[x,-17.2,6.4],.2,'metal');for(let y=-26.8;y<-18;y+=1.4)tube('step-grip',[x+.4,y,6.38],[X-.4,y,6.38],.07,'rubber',6);}
  // A rounded cargo roof with laminated edge and continuous corner radii.
  const roof=[];for(let i=0;i<=12;i++){const t=Math.PI-i*Math.PI/24;roof.push([175+6*Math.cos(t),24+6*Math.sin(t)]);}for(let i=1;i<=12;i++){const t=Math.PI/2-i*Math.PI/24;roof.push([196+6*Math.cos(t),24+6*Math.sin(t)]);}
  // The shallow crown joins the two quarter circles without a wedge.
  roof.splice(13,0,[185.5,30]);
  for(let i=0;i<roof.length-1;i++){const [x,z]=roof[i],[X,Z]=roof[i+1];face('roof',[[x,-24,z],[X,-24,Z],[X,-7,Z],[x,-7,z]],'roof',[0,0,1]);face('roof-end',[[x,-6.98,25.5],[X,-6.98,25.5],[X,-6.98,Z],[x,-6.98,z]],'red',[0,1,0]);face('roof-front',[[x,-24.02,24],[X,-24.02,24],[X,-24.02,Z],[x,-24.02,z]],'red',[0,-1,0]);tube('roof-rim',[x,-6.92,z],[X,-6.92,Z],.12,'edge',6);}
  // The cab has a lower front, a recessed two-pane windscreen and a softly rolled roof.
  box('cab',173,-33,8,199,-24,18,'red');box('cab-header',173,-31.9,23.5,199,-24,24.8,'red');
  for(const [x,sign] of [[173,-1],[199,1]]){
   face('cab',[[x,-33,18],[x,-31.9,24.8],[x,-24,24.8],[x,-24,18]],'red',[sign,0,0]);
   const xx=x+sign*.04,o=[xx,sign>0?-24.2:-32,24],u=[0,sign>0?-7.8:7.8,0],v=[0,0,-14.5];face('cab-door',[o,o.map((n,i)=>n+u[i]),o.map((n,i)=>n+u[i]+v[i]),o.map((n,i)=>n+v[i])],'red',[sign,0,0],{texture:{name:'cab-door',origin:o,u,v}});
   tube('cab-drip',[x+sign*.12,-31.8,24.9],[x+sign*.12,-24.1,24.9],.18,'edge');
   tube('mirror-arm',[x,-31.1,21],[x+sign*3.3,-31.8,21.6],.14,'metal');tube('mirror-case',[x+sign*3.25,-31.8,21.6],[x+sign*3.45,-31.8,21.6],1.05,'cream-metal',32);disk('mirror-glass',x+sign*3.48,-31.8,21.6,.82,'glass','x',sign);
  }
  for(const [x,w] of [[174.3,10.9],[185.8,11.9]]){
   ring('window-frame',[x,-33.19,18.1],[1,0,0],[0,.176,1],w,5.65,.65,.28,'cream-metal',[0,-1,0]);
   ring('window-gasket',[x+.28,-33.12,18.38],[1,0,0],[0,.176,1],w-.56,5.09,.5,.16,'chassis',[0,-1,0]);
   rounded('windscreen',[x+.43,-33.064,18.53],[1,0,0],[0,.176,1],w-.86,4.79,.42,'glass',[0,-1,0]);
   tube('wiper',[x+2,-33.35,18.45],[x+5,-32.98,20.4],.065,'chassis',6);
  }
  tube('windscreen-mullion',[185.5,-33.18,18.1],[185.5,-32.185,23.75],.24,'cream-metal',10);
  for(let i=0;i<20;i++){const a=-Math.PI/2+i*Math.PI/20,b=a+Math.PI/20,x=186+13.25*Math.sin(a),X=186+13.25*Math.sin(b),z=24.6+1.7*Math.cos(a),Z=24.6+1.7*Math.cos(b);face('cab-roof',[[x,-32,z],[X,-32,Z],[X,-23.8,Z],[x,-23.8,z]],'roof',[0,0,1]);}
  // The nose has a curved section and a rounded forward end, with a proper grille.
  for(let j=0;j<18;j++){const y=-41+j*9/18,Y=y+.5,t=(y+41)/9,T=(Y+41)/9,w=7.2+4.6*Math.sin(t*Math.PI/2),W=7.2+4.6*Math.sin(T*Math.PI/2),h=14.8+1.4*t,H=14.8+1.4*T;
  for(let i=0;i<20;i++){const a=-Math.PI/2+i*Math.PI/20,b=a+Math.PI/20,pt=(yy,ww,hh,q)=>[185.5+ww*Math.sin(q),yy,hh+2.1*Math.cos(q)];face('bonnet',[pt(y,w,h,a),pt(y,w,h,b),pt(Y,W,H,b),pt(Y,W,H,a)],'roof',[0,0,1]);}
   for(const sign of [-1,1])face('bonnet-side',[[185.5+sign*w,y,8],[185.5+sign*W,Y,8],[185.5+sign*W,Y,H],[185.5+sign*w,y,h]],'red',[sign,0,0]);
  }
  rounded('nose',[178.3,-41.02,8],[1,0,0],[0,0,1],14.4,8.8,2.4,'red',[0,-1,0]);rounded('grille-rim',[181.2,-41.13,8.9],[1,0,0],[0,0,1],8.6,7.25,2,'cream-metal',[0,-1,0]);rounded('grille',[181.55,-41.18,9.2],[1,0,0],[0,0,1],7.9,6.55,1.7,'chassis',[0,-1,0]);
  for(let x=182.4;x<189;x+=.65)tube('grille-bar',[x,-41.24,10],[x,-41.24,14.8],.12,'metal',8);
  tube('bonnet-seam',[185.5,-40.3,17.07],[185.5,-32.3,18.25],.09,'edge',6);
  for(const x of [174,197]){tube('lamp-stalk',[x,-38.1,10.7],[x,-38.1,12.6],.3,'metal');tube('headlamp-case',[x,-38.1,13],[x,-39.8,13],1.45,'red',32);disk('headlamp-rim',x,-39.84,13,1.45,'cream-metal','y',-1);disk('headlamp',x,-39.9,13,1.17,'lamp','y',-1);disk('headlamp-glint',x-.25,-39.93,13.35,.43,'ivory','y',-1);}
  for(const y of [-41.5,-3.5]){tube('bumper',[169,y,7.2],[202,y,7.2],.6,'metal',12);for(const x of [172,199])tube('bumper-stop',[x,y-.2,6],[x,y-.2,8.5],.35,'cream-metal');}
  // Original rear floor, cargo, leaves and ramp are kept byte-for-byte in native coordinates.
  const retain=new Set(['floor','cargo','cargo-tape','rear-opening','rear-doors','left-leaf','right-leaf','ramp']);
  for(const f of C.vanMesh(closed,cargo))if(retain.has(f.part))out.push({...f,material:f.part==='floor'||f.part==='ramp'?'wood':f.part==='cargo'?'kraft':f.part==='cargo-tape'?'ivory':f.part==='rear-opening'?'interior':'red',normal:(()=>{const n=normal(f.vertices);return n[0]+n[1]+n[2]<0?n.map(v=>-v):n;})()});
  if(cargo){box('cargo-contact',180.8,-16.2,11.005,189.2,-7.8,11.02,'interior');face('cargo-label',[[183,-7.97,13],[187,-7.97,13],[187,-7.97,15],[183,-7.97,15]],'ivory',[0,1,0]);}
  tube('rear-sill',[172,-6.8,11.12],[199,-6.8,11.12],.16,'metal');
  return out;
 }
 return {mesh,wheels,normal};
});

/* Private van artwork bake. Depth and native y are retained for portal masking. */
(function(root,factory){const api=factory();if(typeof module==='object'&&module.exports)module.exports=api;root.ParcelVanRaster=api;})(globalThis,function(){'use strict';
 function render(source,angle,materials,unit,density){
  const co=Math.cos(angle),si=Math.sin(angle),rotate=p=>{const x=p[0]-185,y=p[1]+22;return [185+x*co-y*si,-22+x*si+y*co,p[2]];},faces=[];let left=Infinity,right=-Infinity,top=Infinity,bottom=-Infinity;
  for(const f of source){
   const n=f.normal||[0,0,1],nn=[n[0]*co-n[1]*si,n[0]*si+n[1]*co,n[2]];
   if(nn[0]+nn[1]+nn[2]<-.00001)continue;
   const verts=f.vertices.map((v,i)=>{const p=rotate(v),x=unit*(p[0]-p[1]-185),y=unit*(.5*(p[0]+p[1]-185)-p[2]);left=Math.min(left,x);right=Math.max(right,x);top=Math.min(top,y);bottom=Math.max(bottom,y);
    let u,vv;const ax=Math.abs(n[0]),ay=Math.abs(n[1]),az=Math.abs(n[2]);if(ax>=ay&&ax>=az){u=v[1];vv=-v[2];}else if(ay>=az){u=v[0];vv=-v[2];}else{u=v[0];vv=v[1];}
    let du=0,dv=0;if(f.texture){const t=f.texture,d=v.map((x,k)=>x-t.origin[k]);du=d.reduce((s,x,k)=>s+x*t.u[k],0)/t.u.reduce((s,x)=>s+x*x,0);dv=d.reduce((s,x,k)=>s+x*t.v[k],0)/t.v.reduce((s,x)=>s+x*x,0);}
    return {x,y,depth:p[0]+p[1]+p[2],nativeY:p[1],u,v:vv,du,dv};
   });
   const dot=(-.38*nn[0]+.56*nn[1]+.74*nn[2]),light=.79+.24*Math.max(0,dot)-.045*Math.max(0,-dot);
   faces.push({f,verts,light});
  }
  left=Math.floor(left)-3;right=Math.ceil(right)+3;top=Math.floor(top)-3;bottom=Math.ceil(bottom)+3;
  const bounds={left,top,width:right-left,height:bottom-top},width=bounds.width*density,height=bounds.height*density,pixels=new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4),ys=new Int16Array(width*height),depth=new Float32Array(width*height);depth.fill(-Infinity);
  for(const {f,verts,light} of faces){
   const mat=materials[f.material],decal=f.texture?materials['decal:'+f.texture.name+(f.texture.rect?':'+f.texture.rect:'')]:null;if(!mat)throw Error('Van material '+f.material);
   for(const v of verts){v.x=(v.x-left)*density;v.y=(v.y-top)*density;}
   for(let k=1;k<verts.length-1;k++){
    const a=verts[0],b=verts[k],c=verts[k+1],area=(b.x-a.x)*(c.y-a.y)-(b.y-a.y)*(c.x-a.x);if(Math.abs(area)<1e-9)continue;
    const x0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Math.min(a.x,b.x,c.x))),X=Math.min(width-1,Math.ceil(Math.max(a.x,b.x,c.x))),y0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Math.min(a.y,b.y,c.y))),Y=Math.min(height-1,Math.ceil(Math.max(a.y,b.y,c.y)));
    const inv=1/area;
    for(let y=y0;y<=Y;y++)for(let x=x0;x<=X;x++){
     const px=x+.5,py=y+.5,w1=((b.x-px)*(c.y-py)-(b.y-py)*(c.x-px))*inv,w2=((c.x-px)*(a.y-py)-(c.y-py)*(a.x-px))*inv,w3=1-w1-w2;if(w1<-.00001||w2<-.00001||w3<-.00001)continue;
     const d=w1*a.depth+w2*b.depth+w3*c.depth,i=y*width+x;if(d<depth[i]-.00001)continue;
     let r=mat.rgb[0],g=mat.rgb[1],bb=mat.rgb[2];
     if(mat.data){const u=(w1*a.u+w2*b.u+w3*c.u)/(mat.scale||24),v=(w1*a.v+w2*b.v+w3*c.v)/(mat.scale||24),tx=Math.floor((u-Math.floor(u))*mat.width),ty=Math.floor((v-Math.floor(v))*mat.height),j=(ty*mat.width+tx)*4,delta=(mat.data[j]*.2126+mat.data[j+1]*.7152+mat.data[j+2]*.0722-mat.mean)*(mat.gain||.6);r+=delta;g+=delta;bb+=delta;}
     r*=light;g*=light;bb*=light;
     if(decal){const u=Math.max(0,Math.min(.999999,w1*a.du+w2*b.du+w3*c.du)),v=Math.max(0,Math.min(.999999,w1*a.dv+w2*b.dv+w3*c.dv)),j=(Math.floor(v*decal.height)*decal.width+Math.floor(u*decal.width))*4,alpha=decal.data[j+3]/255,shade=.9+light*.1;r=r*(1-alpha)+decal.data[j]*alpha*shade;g=g*(1-alpha)+decal.data[j+1]*alpha*shade;bb=bb*(1-alpha)+decal.data[j+2]*alpha*shade;}
     const j=i*4;pixels[j]=r;pixels[j+1]=g;pixels[j+2]=bb;pixels[j+3]=255;ys[i]=Math.round((w1*a.nativeY+w2*b.nativeY+w3*c.nativeY)*256);depth[i]=d;
    }
   }
  }
  return {bounds,width,height,density,pixels,words:new Uint32Array(pixels.buffer),ys};
 }
 function bake(source,angle,materials,unit,density=2){
  const raw=render(source,angle,materials,unit,density*2),width=raw.width/2,height=raw.height/2,pixels=new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4),ys=new Int16Array(width*height);
  // Four coverage samples produce a soft paper edge without blurring the faces.
  for(let y=0;y<height;y++)for(let x=0;x<width;x++){let count=0,r=0,g=0,b=0,ny=0;for(let j=0;j<2;j++)for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const k=(y*2+j)*raw.width+x*2+i,q=k*4;if(raw.pixels[q+3]){count++;r+=raw.pixels[q];g+=raw.pixels[q+1];b+=raw.pixels[q+2];ny+=raw.ys[k];}}if(count){const k=y*width+x,q=k*4;pixels[q]=r/count;pixels[q+1]=g/count;pixels[q+2]=b/count;pixels[q+3]=count*255/4;ys[k]=Math.round(ny/count);}}
  return {bounds:raw.bounds,width,height,density,pixels,words:new Uint32Array(pixels.buffer),ys};
 }
 function clip(sprite,dx,dy,dest,bounds,unit,density=2){
  dest.fill(0);const w=sprite.width,h=sprite.height,ox=Math.round((sprite.bounds.left-bounds.left)*density),oy=Math.round((sprite.bounds.top-bounds.top)*density),stride=bounds.width*density,words=sprite.words;
  for(let y=0;y<h;y++){const py=sprite.bounds.top+(y+.5)/density;for(let x=0;x<w;x++){const i=y*w+x,colour=words[i];if(!colour)continue;const px=sprite.bounds.left+(x+.5)/density,wallX=185+px/unit+4+dx-dy,wallZ=(.5*px-py)/unit+4-dy;if(sprite.ys[i]/256+dy>=4||wallX>=166&&wallX<=246&&wallZ<=66)dest[(y+oy)*stride+x+ox]=colour;}}
 }
 return {bake,clip};
});

/* Room-owned van cutouts prepared from native geometry; fixed-size live masking. */
(function(root){'use strict';
const U=ParcelLayout.unit;
class VanFinish{
 constructor(r){this.r=r;this.wheelPoint=[0,0,0];}
 prepare(){
  const started=performance.now(),r=this.r,materials={},spec={red:['cranberry-paper',[180,77,57],26,.64],roof:['roof-paper',[196,100,76],28,.55],edge:['roof-paper',[224,149,108],24,.5],metal:['pewter-paper',[162,168,158],16,.5],'cream-metal':['pewter-paper',[221,204,165],16,.45],rubber:['rubber-paper',[49,52,48],5,.45],chassis:['rubber-paper',[58,66,62],16,.32],glass:['glass-vellum',[143,177,171],12,.33],wood:['birch-ply',[176,137,92],18,.5],ivory:['birch-ply',[236,221,180],22,.18],kraft:['birch-ply',[201,171,120],18,.38],interior:['birch-ply',[76,67,49],18,.3],lamp:['glass-vellum',[241,220,155],12,.2]};
  const crop=(image,q)=>{const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=q[2];c.height=q[3];const ctx=c.getContext('2d');ctx.drawImage(image,...q,0,0,c.width,c.height);const data=ctx.getImageData(0,0,c.width,c.height).data;let mean=0;for(let i=0;i<data.length;i+=4)mean+=data[i]*.2126+data[i+1]*.7152+data[i+2]*.0722;return{data,width:c.width,height:c.height,mean:mean/(c.width*c.height)};};
  const swatches={};for(const [name,[key,rgb,scale,gain]]of Object.entries(spec)){if(!swatches[key])swatches[key]=crop(r.images.van,r.assets.van.materials[key].rect);materials[name]={...swatches[key],rgb,scale,gain};}
  for(const name of ['rear-closed','rear-open','cab-door']){const m=r.assets.mechanical.sprites[name];materials['decal:'+name]=crop(r.images.mechanical,m.atlasRect);if(name==='rear-open')for(const rect of ['leftLeafRect','rightLeafRect'])materials['decal:'+name+':'+rect]=crop(r.images.mechanical,m[rect]);}
  const closed=ParcelVanBody.mesh(true),indices=new Set([30]);for(let f=0;f<2880;f++)indices.add(ParcelVanHeading.index(f));this.headings=[];this.meshes=[ParcelVanBody.mesh(false,false),ParcelVanBody.mesh(false,true),closed];
  const all=[];for(const i of indices){const baked=ParcelVanRaster.bake(closed,(i-30)*Math.PI/60,materials,U);this.headings[i]=baked;all.push(baked);}
  this.poses=[ParcelVanRaster.bake(this.meshes[0],0,materials,U),ParcelVanRaster.bake(this.meshes[1],0,materials,U),this.headings[30]];all.push(this.poses[0],this.poses[1]);
  const left=Math.min(...all.map(v=>v.bounds.left)),top=Math.min(...all.map(v=>v.bounds.top)),right=Math.max(...all.map(v=>v.bounds.left+v.bounds.width)),bottom=Math.max(...all.map(v=>v.bounds.top+v.bounds.height));this.bounds={left,top,width:right-left,height:bottom-top};
  this.cache={x:NaN,y:NaN,pose:-1,heading:-1,canvas:document.createElement('canvas')};this.cache.canvas.width=this.bounds.width*2;this.cache.canvas.height=this.bounds.height*2;this.c=this.cache.canvas.getContext('2d');this.frame=this.c.createImageData(this.cache.canvas.width,this.cache.canvas.height);this.words=new Uint32Array(this.frame.data.buffer);
  this.metrics={prepareMs:performance.now()-started,poses:all.length,cacheBytes:all.reduce((n,v)=>n+v.pixels.byteLength+v.ys.byteLength,0)+this.frame.data.byteLength,faces:closed.length};
 }
 draw(s){
  const v=s.van,pose=v.closed?2:v.cargo==='empty'?0:1,heading=v.closed?ParcelVanHeading.index(s.frame):30,cache=this.cache,b=this.bounds;
  if(v.x!==cache.x||v.y!==cache.y||pose!==cache.pose||heading!==cache.heading){cache.x=v.x;cache.y=v.y;cache.pose=pose;cache.heading=heading;const art=pose===2?this.headings[heading]:this.poses[pose];ParcelVanRaster.clip(art,v.x-185,v.y,this.words,b,U);this.c.putImageData(this.frame,0,0);}
  const x=760+(v.x-v.y)*U,y=465+(v.x+v.y-160)*U*.5;this.r.ctx.drawImage(cache.canvas,x+b.left,y+b.top,b.width,b.height);
 }
 shadow(s){
  const v=s.van,a=v.closed?(ParcelVanHeading.index(s.frame)-30)*Math.PI/60:0,c=this.r.ctx,p=this.wheelPoint;
  const x=760+(v.x-v.y+22)*U,y=465+(v.x+v.y-182)*U*.5;c.save();c.translate(x,y);c.transform(1,.5,-1,.5,0,0);c.rotate(a);this.r.shadow(0,0,16*U,19*U,.10);c.restore();
  for(const w of ParcelVanBody.wheels){ParcelVanHeading.point(w,a,p);const wx=p[0]+v.x-185,wy=p[1]+v.y,sx=760+(wx-wy)*U,sy=465+(wx+wy-160)*U*.5;c.save();c.translate(sx,sy);c.transform(1,.5,-1,.5,0,0);c.rotate(a);this.r.shadow(0,0,1.45*U,2.25*U,.25);this.r.shadow(0,0,.8*U,1.3*U,.23);c.restore();}
 }
}
root.ParcelVanFinish=VanFinish;
})(globalThis);

/* Office material projection adapted locally; no Office mutation. */
(function(root){'use strict';const P=ParcelProjection;
const palette={stone:{top:'#f8ead2',left:'#dfc8ab',right:'#bea48b'},oak:{top:'#dbb17e',left:'#ba845d',right:'#91664e'},teal:{top:'#86aaa0',left:'#467c75',right:'#2b5756'},brick:{top:'#dda28b',left:'#c2826b',right:'#9f6458'}};
function tint(hex,target,amount){const a=parseInt(hex.slice(1),16),b=parseInt(target.slice(1),16);return '#'+[16,8,0].map(s=>Math.round(((a>>s)&255)*(1-amount)+((b>>s)&255)*amount).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');}
class Painter{
 constructor(c,images,assets){this.ctx=c;this.images=images;this.assets=assets;this.patterns={};}
 path(points,fill,stroke,width=1){const c=this.ctx;c.beginPath();points.forEach((p,i)=>i?c.lineTo(p.x,p.y):c.moveTo(p.x,p.y));c.closePath();if(fill){c.fillStyle=fill;c.fill();}if(stroke){c.strokeStyle=stroke;c.lineWidth=width;c.stroke();}}
 line(a,b,colour='#ae8c5c',width=1){const c=this.ctx;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(a.x,a.y);c.lineTo(b.x,b.y);c.strokeStyle=colour;c.lineWidth=width;c.stroke();}
 shadow(x,y,rx,ry,alpha=.13){const c=this.ctx;c.save();c.translate(x,y);c.scale(rx,ry);const g=c.createRadialGradient(0,0,0,0,0,1);g.addColorStop(0,'#403b35');g.addColorStop(.4,'#403b35b0');g.addColorStop(1,'#403b3500');c.globalAlpha*=alpha;c.fillStyle=g;c.fillRect(-1,-1,2,2);c.restore();}
 label(text,p,colour='#496b64',size=12){const c=this.ctx;c.font=size+'px Georgia,serif';c.textAlign='center';c.fillStyle=colour;c.fillText(text,p.x,p.y);}
 sprite(sheet,index,x,bottom,width){const r=this.assets[sheet].rects[index];this.ctx.drawImage(this.images[sheet],...r,x-width/2,bottom-width*r[3]/r[2],width,width*r[3]/r[2]);}
 material(name){
  if(this.patterns[name])return this.patterns[name];
  const meta=this.assets.materials;if(!meta||!this.images.materials)return null;
  const key=name==='stone'?'ivory':name==='brick'?'terracotta':name;
  const r=meta.materials?.[key]?.atlasRect;if(!r)return null;
  const q=r,t=document.createElement('canvas');t.width=t.height=256;const tc=t.getContext('2d');tc.drawImage(this.images.materials,...q,0,0,256,256);
  // Use the authored fibres as albedo variation. Their photographed yellow
  // stock must not tint every painted face; lighting is a separate bake.
  const pixels=tc.getImageData(0,0,256,256),data=pixels.data;let mean=0;
  for(let i=0;i<data.length;i+=4)mean+=data[i]*.2126+data[i+1]*.7152+data[i+2]*.0722;
  mean/=256*256;for(let i=0;i<data.length;i+=4){const v=Math.max(72,Math.min(184,128+(data[i]*.2126+data[i+1]*.7152+data[i+2]*.0722-mean)*2.2));data[i]=data[i+1]=data[i+2]=v;}tc.putImageData(pixels,0,0);
  return this.patterns[name]=this.ctx.createPattern(t,'repeat');
 }
 poly(vertices,fill,material='stone',stroke){
  const c=this.ctx,pts=vertices.map(v=>P.project(...v)),xs=pts.map(p=>p.x),ys=pts.map(p=>p.y),x0=Math.min(...xs),y0=Math.min(...ys),x1=Math.max(...xs),y1=Math.max(...ys);
  let face=fill;
  if(material!=='none'&&/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(fill)){
   const flat=vertices.every(v=>Math.abs(v[2]-vertices[0][2])<.01),g=c.createLinearGradient(x0,y0,x0+(x1-x0)*.25,y1||y0+1);
   g.addColorStop(0,tint(fill,'#fff4df',flat?.065:.11));g.addColorStop(.38,fill);g.addColorStop(1,tint(fill,'#314f55',flat?.035:.12));face=g;
  }
  this.path(pts,face,stroke,.7);
  const pattern=this.material(material);if(pattern){c.save();c.clip();c.globalCompositeOperation='soft-light';c.globalAlpha=.58;c.fillStyle=pattern;
   c.fillRect(x0-1,y0-1,x1-x0+2,y1-y0+2);c.restore();}
 }
 surfaceLight(x,y,z,rx,ry,colour='#ffe5ae',alpha=.22){
  const c=this.ctx,o=P.project(x,y,z);c.save();c.transform(P.HW,P.HH,-P.HW,P.HH,o.x,o.y);c.scale(rx,ry);
  const g=c.createRadialGradient(0,0,0,0,0,1);g.addColorStop(0,colour);g.addColorStop(.3,colour+'a0');g.addColorStop(1,colour+'00');c.globalAlpha=alpha;c.fillStyle=g;c.fillRect(-1,-1,2,2);c.restore();
 }
 box(x,y,w,h,z,height,mat='stone',custom){
  const q=custom||palette[mat],t=z+height;
  if(z===0&&height>=10){const c=this.ctx;c.save();c.shadowColor='#413b3438';c.shadowBlur=6;c.shadowOffsetY=1.5;this.path([[x,y,z],[x+w,y,z],[x+w,y+h,z],[x,y+h,z]].map(v=>P.project(...v)),'#51433324');c.restore();}
  this.poly([[x+w,y,z],[x+w,y+h,z],[x+w,y+h,t],[x+w,y,t]],q.right,mat);
  this.poly([[x,y+h,z],[x+w,y+h,z],[x+w,y+h,t],[x,y+h,t]],q.left,mat);
  this.poly([[x,y,t],[x+w,y,t],[x+w,y+h,t],[x,y+h,t]],q.top,mat);
  // Cut-card bevels stay inside the accepted prism. No new lip can lift a
  // supported parcel, interrupt a tread or change the floor's contact plane.
  if(height>=2&&w*P.HW>3&&h*P.HW>3){
   const edge=Math.min(1.05,height*.19),drop=Math.min(2.1,height*.28),c=this.ctx;c.save();c.lineJoin='round';
   this.line3([x,y+h,t-.2],[x+w,y+h,t-.2],'#fff4df8c',edge);
   this.line3([x+w,y,t-.2],[x+w,y+h,t-.2],'#f4e7d065',edge*.8);
   this.line3([x,y+h,t-drop],[x+w,y+h,t-drop],'#514b4930',.65);
   this.line3([x+w,y,t-drop],[x+w,y+h,t-drop],'#34464b38',.65);c.restore();
  }
 }
 line3(a,b,color,width=1){this.line(P.project(...a),P.project(...b),color,width);}
 face(axis,start,fixed,zTop,fn){const c=this.ctx,p=axis==='x'?P.project(start,fixed,zTop):P.project(fixed,start,zTop);c.save();c.transform(1,axis==='x'?.5:-.5,0,1,p.x,p.y);fn(c);c.restore();}
 cutout(name,x,y,z,width,sy=1){const a=this.assets.acting?.sprites[name];if(!a)return;const p=P.project(x,y,z),r=a.rect,h=width*r[3]/r[2]*sy;this.ctx.drawImage(this.images.acting,...r,p.x-width/2,p.y-h,width,h);}
 decor(name,x,y,w,h){const q=this.assets.decor?.sprites[name]?.atlasRect;if(q)this.ctx.drawImage(this.images.decor,...q,x,y,w,h);}
 front(name,x,y,w,h){const q=this.assets.fronts?.sprites[name]?.atlasRect;if(q)this.ctx.drawImage(this.images.fronts,...q,x,y,w,h);}
 brickWall(axis,start,fixed,width,height){
  this.face(axis,start,fixed,height,c=>{const W=width*P.HW,rowH=15;
   c.beginPath();c.rect(0,0,W,height);c.clip();
   c.fillStyle='#dcaa8138';c.fillRect(0,0,W,height);
   for(let row=0;row<height/rowH;row++)for(let col=-1;col<W/30;col++){
    const x=col*30+(row%2)*15,y=row*rowH;c.fillStyle=['#f5c29c23','#703e2e12','#eec09b1c','#8d4b3713'][(row*7+col+5)%4];c.fillRect(Math.max(0,x+.8),y+1,Math.min(29,W-Math.max(0,x+.8)),13.4);
    c.strokeStyle='#ecc5a545';c.lineWidth=.6;c.strokeRect(x+.5,y+.5,29.4,14.5);
   }
  });
 }
 arch(c,x,y,w,h){const r=w/2;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,y+h);c.lineTo(x,y+r);c.arc(x+r,y+r,r,Math.PI,Math.PI*2);c.lineTo(x+w,y+h);c.closePath();}
 window(axis,start,fixed,width,zTop,height){
  this.face(axis,start,fixed,zTop,c=>{
   const W=width*P.HW,H=height;
   if(height>180&&this.images.fronts){const iw=H*465/1119;c.save();c.shadowColor='#66503333';c.shadowBlur=3;c.shadowOffsetY=2;this.front('archedWindow',(W-iw)/2,0,iw,H);c.restore();return;}
   c.save();c.translate(2.5,3);this.arch(c,0,0,W,H);c.fillStyle='#5b443b44';c.fill();c.restore();
   this.arch(c,0,0,W,H);const frame=c.createLinearGradient(0,0,W,H);frame.addColorStop(0,'#fff1d0');frame.addColorStop(.45,'#eac99a');frame.addColorStop(1,'#c8a36e');c.fillStyle=frame;c.fill();
   this.arch(c,7,8,W-14,H-15);c.fillStyle='#97774a';c.fill();
   c.save();this.arch(c,10,11,W-20,H-21);c.clip();
   const glass=c.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,H);glass.addColorStop(0,'#b8d9d5');glass.addColorStop(.38,'#e1e8d8');glass.addColorStop(.7,'#94c3b8');glass.addColorStop(1,'#5a9e99');c.fillStyle=glass;c.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
   for(let k=0;k<6;k++){const xx=k*W/5-12,hh=17+(k*17)%29,yy=H*.48;c.fillStyle=['#d1c6b3','#d7b6a2','#b7c6b6'][k%3];c.fillRect(xx,yy-hh,23,hh);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(xx-2,yy-hh);c.lineTo(xx+11,yy-hh-13);c.lineTo(xx+25,yy-hh);c.closePath();c.fillStyle='#9caeb0';c.fill();c.fillStyle='#f6e5b7';c.fillRect(xx+5,yy-hh+7,5,8);}
   c.fillStyle='#e6eee161';for(let k=0;k<9;k++)c.fillRect((k*31)%W,H*.61+k*6,17+(k%3)*7,1.5);
   c.fillStyle='#fff8df25';c.beginPath();c.moveTo(W*.2,0);c.lineTo(W*.38,0);c.lineTo(W*.05,H);c.lineTo(-W*.1,H);c.fill();c.beginPath();c.moveTo(W*.8,0);c.lineTo(W*.87,0);c.lineTo(W*.5,H);c.lineTo(W*.43,H);c.fill();
   c.strokeStyle='#e8bc72';c.lineWidth=3.4;for(const x of [W/3,W*2/3]){c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,0);c.lineTo(x,H);c.stroke();}for(const y of [W*.51,H*.48,H*.72]){c.beginPath();c.moveTo(0,y);c.lineTo(W,y);c.stroke();}
   c.strokeStyle='#fff1c7';c.lineWidth=1;for(const x of [W/3-1,W*2/3-1]){c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,0);c.lineTo(x,H);c.stroke();}c.restore();
   this.arch(c,10,11,W-20,H-21);c.strokeStyle='#f5dba6';c.lineWidth=1.2;c.stroke();
   c.fillStyle='#e7cfaa';c.fillRect(-3,H-4,W+6,8);c.fillStyle='#fff0d2';c.fillRect(-3,H-4,W+6,2);
   for(let k=0;k<9;k++){const a=Math.PI+k*Math.PI/8,xx=W/2+Math.cos(a)*(W/2-3),yy=W/2+Math.sin(a)*(W/2-3);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(xx,yy);c.lineTo(W/2+Math.cos(a)*(W/2+1),W/2+Math.sin(a)*(W/2+1));c.strokeStyle='#c4a176';c.lineWidth=.7;c.stroke();}
  });
 }
 column(x,y,height){
  this.poly([[x-.17,y-.17,.02],[x+.19,y-.17,.02],[x+.19,y+.2,.02],[x-.17,y+.2,.02]],'#72593424','none');
  this.box(x-.15,y-.15,.3,.3,0,10,'stone');this.box(x-.105,y-.105,.21,.21,10,height-19,'teal');
  this.box(x-.16,y-.16,.32,.32,height-9,9,'stone');
  this.line3([x-.09,y+.11,17],[x-.09,y+.11,height-15],'#9cb9a66b',1.2);
 }
 brace(x,y,axis,sign,z){
  const a=axis==='x'?[[x,y,z-46],[x+sign*.82,y,z],[x,y,z]]:[[x,y,z-46],[x,y+sign*.82,z],[x,y,z]];
  this.poly(a,'#345f59','teal');const p0=P.project(...a[0]),p1=P.project(...a[1]),p2=P.project(...a[2]),c=this.ctx;
  c.beginPath();c.moveTo(p0.x,p0.y);c.quadraticCurveTo(p2.x+(p1.x-p2.x)*.12,p2.y+22,p1.x,p1.y);c.strokeStyle='#8ba89b';c.lineWidth=3;c.stroke();
 }
 rail(axis,start,fixed,length,z){
  const at=(u,h)=>axis==='x'?[start+u,fixed,z+h]:[fixed,start+u,z+h];
  this.line3(at(0,13),at(length,13),'#94734e',3.3);this.line3(at(0,42),at(length,42),'#b28c59',4.7);this.line3(at(0,44),at(length,44),'#f1d5a1',1.7);
  const n=Math.ceil(length/.23);for(let i=0;i<=n;i++){const u=i*length/n;this.line3(at(u,12),at(u,42),'#c7a674',2.7);this.line3(at(u,13),at(u,40),'#f0d7aa',.8);}
  for(let u=0;u<=length+.01;u+=Math.max(.8,length/4)){this.line3(at(u,0),at(u,48),'#9c703a',5);this.line3(at(u,0),at(u,48),'#d9ae61',3.2);const p=P.project(...at(u,50));this.ctx.beginPath();this.ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,4.2,0,Math.PI*2);this.ctx.fillStyle='#e9bd64';this.ctx.fill();this.ctx.beginPath();this.ctx.arc(p.x-1.2,p.y-1.3,1.4,0,Math.PI*2);this.ctx.fillStyle='#fff0ae';this.ctx.fill();}
 }
 plant(x,y,z,size=1,hanging=false){
  const p=P.project(x,y,z),c=this.ctx;this.shadow(p.x,p.y,13*size,4*size,.13);
  this.box(x-.12*size,y-.12*size,.24*size,.24*size,z,13*size,'stone');
  c.save();c.translate(p.x,p.y-13*size);c.scale(size,size);
  const n=hanging?13:9;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const a=-Math.PI*.95+i*.74,rr=13+(i%3)*4,ex=Math.cos(a)*rr,ey=hanging?i*5-4:Math.sin(a)*rr-8;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(0,0);c.quadraticCurveTo(ex*.7-10,ey-14,ex,ey);c.quadraticCurveTo(ex*.7+7,ey-6,0,0);c.fillStyle=['#6f8e45','#98a74f','#b8bb66','#819f50'][i%4];c.fill();c.beginPath();c.moveTo(0,0);c.lineTo(ex,ey);c.strokeStyle='#d0d28a77';c.lineWidth=.7;c.stroke();}c.restore();
 }
 parcel(x,y,z,w=.35,h=.32,height=18){this.poly([[x-.02,y-.02,z+.01],[x+w+.035,y-.02,z+.01],[x+w+.035,y+h+.035,z+.01],[x-.02,y+h+.035,z+.01]],'#72593424','none');this.box(x,y,w,h,z,height,'oak',{top:'#e9c38c',left:'#c69a64',right:'#b58b5c'});this.poly([[x+w*.44,y,z+height+.2],[x+w*.57,y,z+height+.2],[x+w*.57,y+h,z+height+.2],[x+w*.44,y+h,z+height+.2]],'#f5dfad');this.poly([[x+w*.44,y+h,z],[x+w*.57,y+h,z],[x+w*.57,y+h,z+height],[x+w*.44,y+h,z+height]],'#ead29f');}
 lamp(x,y,top){
  const c=this.ctx,p=P.project(x,y,top-43);this.line3([x,y,top],[x,y,top-32],'#a37e43',1.8);
  const glow=c.createRadialGradient(p.x,p.y+4,0,p.x,p.y+4,42);glow.addColorStop(0,'#ffe99d50');glow.addColorStop(1,'#ffe99d00');c.fillStyle=glow;c.fillRect(p.x-42,p.y-38,84,84);
  c.beginPath();c.moveTo(p.x-16,p.y);c.quadraticCurveTo(p.x-13,p.y-17,p.x,p.y-19);c.quadraticCurveTo(p.x+13,p.y-17,p.x+16,p.y);c.closePath();const g=c.createLinearGradient(p.x-16,p.y,p.x+16,p.y);g.addColorStop(0,'#365b52');g.addColorStop(.5,'#779583');g.addColorStop(1,'#496b5e');c.fillStyle=g;c.fill();c.beginPath();c.ellipse(p.x,p.y,16,4,0,0,Math.PI*2);c.fillStyle='#fff1b6';c.fill();c.strokeStyle='#e4b350';c.lineWidth=1.4;c.stroke();
 }
}

root.ParcelPainter=Painter;})(globalThis);

/* Locally adapted from approved Office pieces; original remains unchanged. */
/* Registered paper components. Simulation coordinates stay in source16wu;
 * every visible floor contact passes through the accepted Civic projection. */
(function(root){
'use strict';
const P=ParcelProjection,DX=[1,0,-1,0],DY=[0,1,0,-1],DIR=['R','D','L','U'];
const clamp=v=>Math.max(0,Math.min(1,v)),ease=v=>{v=clamp(v);return v*v*(3-2*v);};
const TAIL=.82,SHAFT=.18,SHOULDER=-.15;
const HEAD=3*SHAFT*(TAIL*TAIL-SHOULDER*SHOULDER)/((1-SHOULDER)*(2*SHOULDER+1));
const ARROW=[[1,0],[SHOULDER,-HEAD],[SHOULDER,-SHAFT],[-TAIL,-SHAFT],[-TAIL,SHAFT],[SHOULDER,SHAFT],[SHOULDER,HEAD]];
const HATCH=[[.14,.14],[.86,.14],[.86,.86],[.14,.86]];
const CARRIER=['carrier-R','carrier-D','carrier-L','carrier-U'],DOG=['dog-0','dog-1','dog-2','dog-3'];
const ART={};
for(const coat of ['ginger','blue']){const names=ART[coat]={};for(const suffix of ['bust-front','bust-rear','bump-front','bump-rear','blink','nap','puzzled','wake','crouch-rear','crouch-front','push-rear','push-front','walk-rear-0','walk-rear-1','walk-front-0','walk-front-1'])names[suffix]=coat+'-'+suffix;names.basket='basket-'+coat;}
function projectTo(out,x,y,z=0){out.x=760+(x-y)*P.HW;out.y=465+(x+y)*P.HH-z;return out;}
function points(n){const out=[];for(let i=0;i<n;i++)out.push({x:0,y:0});return out;}
class Pieces{
 constructor(renderer){this.r=renderer;this.c=renderer.ctx;this.motion=true;this.pointCursor=0;this.points=points(512);this.arrowPoints=points(7);this.floorPoints=points(4);this.lidPoints=points(4);this.frontPoints=points(3);this.labelPoint={x:0,y:0};this.walls=[];this.prepareBoard(6,4);this.carriers=this.bakeCarriers();}
 beginFrame(){this.pointCursor=0;}
 project(x,y,z=0){if(this.pointCursor===this.points.length)this.pointCursor=0;return projectTo(this.points[this.pointCursor++],x,y,z);}
 centre(x,y){return this.project(x+.5,y+.5);}
 point(x,y,z=0){return this.project(x/16,y/16,z*P.HW/16);}
 prepareBoard(w,h){for(let y=0;y<h;y++)for(let x=0;x<w;x++){const key=y*16+x;if(this.walls[key])continue;const px=760+(x-y)*P.HW,py=465+(x+y+1)*P.HH-3;const g=this.c.createLinearGradient(px-30,py,px+30,py+15);g.addColorStop(0,'#b99b6c');g.addColorStop(.5,'#ead5a7');g.addColorStop(1,'#b29464');this.walls[key]=g;}}
 label(text,x,y,colour,size){this.labelPoint.x=x;this.labelPoint.y=y;this.r.label(text,this.labelPoint,colour,size);}
 asset(name,x,bottom,width,mirror=false,sy=1,alpha=1,front=false){
  const c=this.c,a=this.r.assets.acting.sprites[name];if(!a)throw Error('Missing piece '+name);
  const q=a.rect,h=width*q[3]/q[2]*sy;
  c.save();c.globalAlpha*=alpha;c.translate(x,bottom);if(mirror)c.scale(-1,1);
  if(front){c.beginPath();c.moveTo(-width/2,-h*.52);if(name==='basket-ginger')c.quadraticCurveTo(0,h*.06,width/2,-h*.52);else{c.lineTo(0,-h*.19);c.lineTo(width/2,-h*.52);}c.lineTo(width/2,2);c.lineTo(-width/2,2);c.closePath();c.clip();}
  c.drawImage(this.r.images.acting,q[0],q[1],q[2],q[3],-width/2,-h,width,h);c.restore();
 }
 anchored(name,x,y,width,u,v,mirror=false,sy=1){
  const a=this.r.assets.acting.sprites[name],h=width*a.rect[3]/a.rect[2]*sy;
  const anchorX=mirror?1-u:u;this.asset(name,x+(.5-anchorX)*width,y+(1-v)*h,width,mirror,sy);
 }
 ellipse(x,y,rx,ry,fill,stroke){const c=this.c;c.beginPath();c.ellipse(x,y,rx,ry,0,0,Math.PI*2);c.fillStyle=fill;c.fill();if(stroke){c.strokeStyle=stroke;c.lineWidth=1;c.stroke();}}
 arrow(x,y,d,z,fill){
  this.arrowAngle(x,y,d*Math.PI/2,z,fill);
 }
 arrowAngle(x,y,angle,z,fill){
  // The arrow's area centroid AND its projected bounding-box centre are zero.
  // Tail + shaft half-width = 1 balances the tip in both projected axes;
  // head width balances the triangle's mass against the rectangular shaft.
  const points=this.arrowPoints,size=.26,dx=Math.cos(angle),dy=Math.sin(angle);
  for(let i=0;i<ARROW.length;i++){const u=ARROW[i][0],v=ARROW[i][1];projectTo(points[i],x+.5+size*(dx*u-dy*v),y+.5+size*(dy*u+dx*v),z);}
  this.r.path(points,fill);
 }
 table(t,spin){
  const c=this.c,base=this.centre(t.x,t.y),p=this.project(t.x+.5,t.y+.5,3),colour=t.type==='ginger'?'#d9913f':t.type==='blue'?'#627f9e':'#467e7b';
  this.r.shadow(base.x,base.y+2,30,13,.15);
  const wall=this.walls[t.y*16+t.x];
  this.ellipse(base.x,base.y,30,15,wall);this.ellipse(p.x,p.y,30,15,'#f4dfb5','#bca16d');this.ellipse(p.x,p.y,25,12.5,colour);
  const turn=spin*.25;
  for(let i=0;i<12;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/6+turn,x=p.x+27.4*Math.cos(a),y=p.y+13.6*Math.sin(a);if(t.type==='ginger'){c.strokeStyle='#9d5a2d';c.lineWidth=2.6;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x-1,y-1);c.lineTo(x+1,y+1);c.stroke();}else if(t.type==='blue')this.ellipse(x,y,1.35,.7,'#f7edd3');}
  // Rim, face and printed arrow share one raised plane and centre.
  this.arrow(t.x,t.y,OfficeRules.GR_DIR[t.dir],3,'#fff0cf');
  if(t.locked){this.ellipse(p.x+22,p.y+12,7,5,'#f7e7c3','#aa874d');c.strokeStyle='#667a72';c.lineWidth=1.5;c.beginPath();c.arc(p.x+22,p.y+8,2.5,Math.PI,0);c.stroke();c.fillStyle='#667a72';c.fillRect(p.x+19.5,p.y+8,5,4);}
 }
 hatch(h,state){
  const d=OfficeRules.GR_DIR[h.dir],p=this.centre(h.x,h.y),lo=.14,hi=.86;
  const corners=HATCH,floor=this.floorPoints;for(let i=0;i<4;i++)projectTo(floor[i],h.x+corners[i][0],h.y+corners[i][1]);
  this.r.path(floor,'#274d51','#ead6ad',5);
  const theta=state.open===2?1.25:state.open===1?.55:0;
  const lid=this.lidPoints;for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const v=corners[i];let x=v[0],y=v[1],dist;if(d===0){dist=x-lo;x=lo+dist*Math.cos(theta);}else if(d===2){dist=hi-x;x=hi-dist*Math.cos(theta);}else if(d===1){dist=y-lo;y=lo+dist*Math.cos(theta);}else{dist=hi-y;y=hi-dist*Math.cos(theta);}projectTo(lid[i],h.x+x,h.y+y,dist*P.HW*Math.sin(theta));}
  this.r.path(lid,theta?'#d1b37e':'#cebc94','#f5e4be',2.7);
  if(!theta)this.arrow(h.x,h.y,d,0,'#456e6d');

 }
 hatchFront(h){const pts=this.frontPoints;projectTo(pts[0],h.x+.14,h.y+.86);projectTo(pts[1],h.x+.86,h.y+.86);projectTo(pts[2],h.x+.86,h.y+.14);this.c.beginPath();this.c.moveTo(pts[0].x,pts[0].y);this.c.lineTo(pts[1].x,pts[1].y);this.c.lineTo(pts[2].x,pts[2].y);this.c.strokeStyle='#efdbb2';this.c.lineWidth=4;this.c.stroke();}
 dog(d,index,state){const p=this.centre(d.x,d.y),frame=this.motion||state.earLift?state.frame:0;this.r.shadow(p.x,p.y+16,31,11,.14);this.asset(DOG[frame],p.x,p.y+22,80,!!state.mirror);if(this.motion&&!state.earLift){const t=state.glyph;if(t.visible){this.c.save();this.c.globalAlpha=1-t.fade;this.label('z',p.x+24,p.y-35-t.z*2,'#8a9d99',t.big?14:10);this.c.restore();}}}
 // Bake the same square paper shell for occupied, empty and folding boxes.
 // Its floor root and near rim stay fixed while the scored walls collapse.
 bakeCarriers(){
  const frames=[];
  for(let d=0;d<4;d++)for(let fold=0;fold<=4;fold++)for(let front=0;front<2;front++){
   const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=256;canvas.height=192;
   const c=canvas.getContext('2d');c.scale(2,2);c.translate(64,56);
   const height=[27,21,14,8,3][fold],spread=[7,8,10,12,14][fold];
   const point=(x,y,z=0)=>({x:(x-y)*32,y:(x+y)*16+4-z});
   const A=point(-.5,-.5),B=point(.5,-.5),C=point(.5,.5),D=point(-.5,.5);
   const a=point(-.5,-.5,height),b=point(.5,-.5,height),cc=point(.5,.5,height),dd=point(-.5,.5,height);
   const trace=pts=>{c.beginPath();c.moveTo(pts[0].x,pts[0].y);for(let i=1;i<pts.length;i++)c.lineTo(pts[i].x,pts[i].y);c.closePath();};
   // These are real layered card panels: directional face shading, fibres and
   // a separate cut edge. The bake carries that relief into every fold state.
   const face=(pts,light,dark,grain=true)=>{
    const ys=pts.map(p=>p.y),top=Math.min(...ys),bottom=Math.max(...ys);
    const shade=c.createLinearGradient(-24,top,18,bottom+8);shade.addColorStop(0,light);shade.addColorStop(.25,light);shade.addColorStop(1,dark);
    trace(pts);c.fillStyle=shade;c.fill();
    if(grain){c.save();c.clip();
     for(let i=0;i<1650;i++){
      const hash=((i+17)*2654435761)>>>0,x=(hash%193)/2-48,y=((hash>>>10)%173)/2-52;
      c.fillStyle=i%3?'rgba(255,238,196,.13)':'rgba(96,58,28,.14)';c.fillRect(x,y,.3+(i%3)*.13,.35+(i%2)*.2);
     }
     c.strokeStyle='rgba(104,64,31,.09)';c.lineWidth=.32;
     for(let i=0;i<90;i++){const x=((i*37)%89)-44,y=((i*19)%83)-48;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,y);c.lineTo(x+1+(i%3)*.55,y-.2);c.stroke();}c.restore();}
   };
   const line=(x,y,u,v,colour,width)=>{c.strokeStyle=colour;c.lineWidth=width;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,y);c.lineTo(u,v);c.stroke();};
   const edge=(p,q,width=2)=>{
    const lowerP={x:p.x,y:p.y+width},lowerQ={x:q.x,y:q.y+width};
    face([p,q,lowerQ,lowerP],'#d5ad74','#a87744',false);
    const length=Math.hypot(q.x-p.x,q.y-p.y),n=Math.ceil(length/1.65);
    for(let k=0;k<n;k++){const u=(k+.3)/n,x=p.x+(q.x-p.x)*u,y=p.y+(q.y-p.y)*u;line(x,y+.45,x+.1,y+width-.2,'rgba(119,77,38,.48)',.42);}
    line(p.x,p.y,q.x,q.y,'#f0d6a4',.6);
   };
   const flap=(p,q,dx,dy,light,dark)=>{
    const farP={x:p.x+dx,y:p.y+dy},farQ={x:q.x+dx,y:q.y+dy};
    face([p,q,farQ,farP],light,dark);
    edge(farP,farQ);edge(q,farQ,1.2);
    line(p.x,p.y,q.x,q.y,'#956a3c',1.2);line(p.x,p.y-.6,q.x,q.y-.6,'#e3c18b',.65);
   };
   if(!front){
    // Back panels and outward folded flaps expose the darker uncoated interior.
    face([A,B,b,a],'#af8554','#745132');face([A,D,dd,a],'#bf9765','#87613c');
    flap(a,b,spread,-spread*.5,'#d7b582','#b68a54');
    flap(a,dd,-spread,-spread*.5,'#e3c391','#bf9766');
    face([A,B,C,D],'#af8656','#795334');
    // The floor seam is visible once the cat leaves, without a separate empty sprite.
    line(-15,4,15,4,'#bb925c',3);line(-15,3.3,15,3.3,'#d6b987',.6);
   }
   face([D,C,cc,dd],'#d1aa74','#ac7b47');face([B,C,cc,b],'#bd8d55','#946335');
   // Compressed bottom folds and the rounded paper-wrapped corner give the
   // carrier weight. Soft under-flap shadows belong to its walls, not the cat.
   line(D.x,D.y-1,C.x,C.y-1,'#a77944',1.5);line(B.x,B.y-1,C.x,C.y-1,'#886033',1.5);
   line(cc.x,cc.y+1,C.x,C.y-1,'#e4be87',1.7);line(C.x+1,cc.y+2,C.x+1,C.y-1,'#ac7a42',.65);
   c.save();trace([D,C,cc,dd]);c.clip();line(dd.x,dd.y+2.8,cc.x,cc.y+2.8,'rgba(103,66,31,.20)',4.5);c.restore();
   c.save();trace([B,C,cc,b]);c.clip();line(b.x,b.y+2.8,cc.x,cc.y+2.8,'rgba(87,53,24,.22)',4.5);c.restore();
   flap(dd,cc,-spread,spread*.5+1.25,'#e4c28c','#bd905a');
   flap(b,cc,spread,spread*.5+1.25,'#d9b47d','#af7d47');
   // Fold scores, a narrow paper tape repair and a small pasted postal ticket.
   line(dd.x,dd.y,cc.x,cc.y,'#e0bf8b',.9);line(b.x,b.y,cc.x,cc.y,'#dfbb81',.8);
   if(fold<3){
    const left=d===1||d===2,sign=left?-1:1,x=sign*13,y=13.5-height*.5;
    c.save();c.translate(x,y);c.transform(1,sign*-.5,0,1,0,0);
    c.fillStyle='rgba(244,218,166,.36)';c.fillRect(-2,-height*.28,4,height*.58);
    c.fillStyle='rgba(77,48,25,.18)';c.fillRect(-6.6,-3.5,14,8);
    c.fillStyle='#f1e2bf';c.fillRect(-7,-4,14,8);c.strokeStyle='#fbedd0';c.lineWidth=.45;c.strokeRect(-7,-4,14,8);
    line(-4,-1,2,-1,'#786b52',.6);line(-4,1,4,1,'#94846a',.45);c.fillStyle='#966d43';c.fillRect(3,-2.8,2,2);c.restore();
   }
   frames.push(canvas);
  }
  return frames;
 }
 carrier(x,y,d,fold=0,fade=0,front=false){
  if(fold<0||fade>=1)return;const p=this.point(x,y),c=this.c;
  const frame=this.carriers[(d*5+Math.min(4,Math.max(0,fold)))*2+(front?1:0)];
  if(!front)this.r.shadow(p.x,p.y+11,32,11,.12*(1-fade));
  c.save();c.globalAlpha*=1-fade;c.drawImage(frame,p.x-64,p.y-56,128,96);c.restore();
 }
 boxed(o,tr,now){
  const p=this.point(o.x,o.y,o.z),ground=this.point(o.x,o.y),c=this.c,coat=tr.coat===2?'blue':'ginger';
  c.save();if(o.anim===BOARDANIM.A.POP){c.beginPath();c.rect(ground.x-65,ground.y-160,130,175);c.clip();}
  c.save();c.translate(0,p.y-ground.y-(o.bob||0));this.carrier(o.x,o.y,o.dir);
  const rear=o.head===2||o.head===3,mirror=o.head===0||o.head===3;
  let face=rear?'bust-rear':'bust-front';if(o.ears===BOARDANIM.EARS_BACK)face=rear?'bump-rear':'bump-front';else if(this.motion&&o.eyes&&!rear)face='blink';
  const lean=o.head!==o.dir?((o.head-o.dir+4)%4===1?3:-3):0;
  const breath=this.motion&&o.anim===BOARDANIM.A.IDLE?Math.sin(now*2)*.008:0;
  this.asset(ART[coat][face],ground.x+lean,ground.y+3,48,mirror,1+(o.anim===BOARDANIM.A.STRETCH?.05:breath));
  this.carrier(o.x,o.y,o.dir,0,0,true);c.restore();c.restore();
 }
 basket(b,front=false){const p=this.centre(b.x,b.y);if(!front)this.r.shadow(p.x,p.y+18,35,12,.12);this.asset(ART[b.coat].basket,p.x,p.y+26,91,false,.78,1,front);}
 // Physical anchor for each authored crouch/push is its hind sole, rather than
 // the whole sprite's bounding-box bottom. Flight uses those same sole roots.
 loose(o,tr,now){
  const c=this.c,coat=tr.coat===2?'blue':'ginger',A=BOARDANIM.A;
  const j=tr.n-1,T=now-(tr.rel+tr.n*tr.tick),d=j>=0?tr.d0[j]:o.dir;
  const rear=d===2||d===3,mirror=d===0||d===3;
  if(o.takeoff>=0){
    const hx=tr.ox+tr.x1[j]*16+8,hy=tr.oy+tr.y1[j]*16+8,bx=hx-DX[d]*14,by=hy-DY[d]*14;
    const base=this.point(bx,by),target=this.point(hx+(tr.end===BOARDANIM.END_WRONG?3:tr.homeDX),hy+(tr.end===BOARDANIM.END_WRONG?3:tr.homeDY));
    const u=clamp((T-.4)/.2),v=ease(u),sx=base.x,sy=base.y-2;
    const tx=target.x,ty=target.y-3;
    const x=sx+(tx-sx)*v,y=sy+(ty-sy)*v-Math.sin(u*Math.PI)*22;
    this.r.shadow(base.x+(target.x-base.x)*v,base.y+10+(target.y-base.y)*v,23,8,.11);
    const pose=o.takeoff===0?'crouch':o.takeoff===1?'push':'push';
    const name=ART[coat][rear?(pose==='crouch'?'crouch-rear':'push-rear'):(pose==='crouch'?'crouch-front':'push-front')];
    // Sole positions are authored metadata for the component master. The
    // departing feet stay on the carrier's interior until clearance at.4s.
    const anchorU=rear?.63:pose==='crouch'?.84:.94,anchorV=rear?.90:pose==='crouch'?.88:.75;
    // Blend from supported hind soles to the body's floor centre for landing.
    const au=anchorU+(.5-anchorU)*v,av=anchorV+(1-anchorV)*v;
    this.anchored(name,x,y,67,au,av,mirror,1);
    if(o.takeoffSupported)this.carrier(o.boxX,o.boxY,d,0,0,true);
    return;
  }
  const p=this.point(o.x,o.y,o.z),dir=o.dir,back=dir===2||dir===3,flip=dir===0||dir===3;
  if(o.anim===A.NAP){const breath=this.motion?Math.sin(now*Math.PI/1.5)*.012:0;this.asset(ART[coat].nap,p.x,p.y+13,62,false,1+breath);}
  else if(o.anim===A.CURL){const amount=(o.frame+1)/3;this.asset(o.frame>=1?ART[coat].nap:ART[coat].puzzled,p.x,p.y+13,62,false,1-.05*(1-amount));}
  else if(o.anim===A.PUZZLED){this.asset(ART[coat].puzzled,p.x,p.y+15,52,false);if(this.motion&&Math.floor(now*2)%2===0)this.label('?',p.x+20,p.y-38,'#a27640',16);}
  else if(o.anim===A.WAKE){this.asset(ART[coat][o.frame===0?'nap':o.frame===1?'wake':'puzzled'],p.x,p.y+13,o.frame===0?62:57,flip);}
  else if(o.anim===A.LEAVE){this.asset(ART[coat][back?(o.frame&1?'walk-rear-1':'walk-rear-0'):(o.frame&1?'walk-front-1':'walk-front-0')],p.x,p.y+15,65,flip);}
  else{this.asset(ART[coat][back?'crouch-rear':'puzzled'],p.x,p.y+13,back?65:53,flip);}
 }
}
root.ParcelPieces=Pieces;
})(globalThis);

/* Locally adapted Office paper presentation. No assets, renderer, clocks or rule changes. */
(function(root,factory){
  if(typeof module==='object'&&module.exports)module.exports=factory();
  else root.ParcelActing=factory();
})(typeof globalThis!=='undefined'?globalThis:this,function(){
  'use strict';
  function create(BOARDANIM){
    const BA=BOARDANIM,A=BA.A,DX=[1,0,-1,0],DY=[0,1,0,-1],dirs={R:0,D:1,L:2,U:3};
    const defaults=BA.makeOut(),keys=Object.keys(defaults),names=[];
    for(const name of Object.keys(A))names[A[name]]=name.toLowerCase();
    const clamp=v=>v<0?0:v>1?1:v,ease=v=>{v=clamp(v);return v*v*(3-2*v);};
    const q=v=>Math.round(v*2)/2,q2=v=>Math.round(v*.5)*2,lerp=(a,b,t)=>a+(b-a)*t,fract=v=>v-Math.floor(v);
    const coat=c=>typeof c==='number'?c:(typeof c==='object'?coat(c.coat):c==='blue'?2:1);
    function finite(time){if(!Number.isFinite(time))throw new RangeError('Acting time must be finite seconds');}
    function resetBase(out){for(let k=0;k<keys.length;k++)out[keys[k]]=defaults[keys[k]];}

    /* makeOut() allocates all records once. sample mutates and returns that same
       object, with no frame allocations. Flat fields are BOARDANIM-compatible,
       plus takeoff (-1 or frame 0..3), takeoffSupported (0/1).
       All x/y/z values below are SOURCE WORLD UNITS (16 per tile).
       root: source pose and phase; terminalAge is -Infinity without a terminal.
       cat: explicit artwork anchor, root+(4,4) boxed and root otherwise; bodyDir
         and headDir remain independent. stretch is boxed stage 0/1/2; mirror
         matches the original blit (boxed false, TURN middle frame mirrored).
         frame selects takeoff art when active;
         pose names match BA enums otherwise. clipFloor requests floor-z=0 clip.
       box: ROOT anchor; artwork is drawn at (x+4,y+4), z; occupied carrier follows
         root, empty carrier uses boxX/Y. No occupied box under loose cat art.
       rim: ART anchor (boxX+4,boxY+4), z0; only the takeoff support rim, not basket.
       effects: tile targets (-1 absent); home occupancy only CURL/NAP/WAKE.
       base is private reusable BOARDANIM scratch; callers must not draw it.
       All public fields are rewritten even after a hidden/rewound sample. */
    function makeOut(){
      const out=BA.makeOut();out.takeoff=-1;out.takeoffSupported=0;out.base=BA.makeOut();
      out.root={visible:false,x:0,y:0,z:0,anim:A.HIDDEN,frame:0,phase:'hidden',terminalAge:-Infinity};
      out.cat={visible:false,coat:1,boxed:false,bodyDir:0,headDir:0,ears:0,eyes:0,bob:0,stretch:0,pose:'hidden',frame:0,x:0,y:0,z:0,mirror:false,clipFloor:false,supported:false};
      out.box={visible:false,occupied:false,x:0,y:0,z:0,dir:0,fold:-1,fade:0};
      out.rim={visible:false,kind:'carrier',x:0,y:0,z:0,coat:1};
      out.effects={hatchX:-1,hatchY:-1,hatchOpen:0,spinX:-1,spinY:-1,spin:0,bumpX:-1,bumpY:-1,bumpAge:-1,homeX:-1,homeY:-1,cushionOccupied:false,flapOpen:0,puzzled:false};
      return out;
    }
    // The rule trace still supplies every action and deadline. Its original
    // two-world-unit pixel snapping becomes large jumps at this scene's scale,
    // so sample floor travel continuously without changing those action fields.
    function glide(out,tr,now){
      if(out.anim===A.POP){
        const f=(now-(tr.rel-tr.pop))/tr.pop;
        out.z=-8+ease((f-.15)/.6)*8;return;
      }
      if(out.anim!==A.TRAVEL&&out.anim!==A.BOUNCE&&out.anim!==A.STOP)return;
      const k=Math.floor((now-tr.rel)/tr.tick);
      if(k<0||k>=tr.n||tr.homeKind==='flap'||tr.ev[k]===BA.EV_TUNNEL)return;
      const f=(now-tr.rel-k*tr.tick)/tr.tick,d=tr.d0[k],ev=tr.ev[k];
      const x0=tr.ox+tr.x0[k]*16+8,y0=tr.oy+tr.y0[k]*16+8;
      let x1=tr.ox+tr.x1[k]*16+8,y1=tr.oy+tr.y1[k]*16+8;
      if(tr.bn[k]){
        const F=Math.max(1,Math.round(tr.tick*BA.FPS)),b0=F>=4?1:0,fTurn=Math.min(.99,(b0+3)/F);
        const nudge=3*ease(f/.2),nx=x0+DX[d]*nudge,ny=y0+DY[d]*nudge;
        const g=F<4?(f-2/F)*F:(f-fTurn)/(1-fTurn),m=ease(g);
        out.x=lerp(nx,x1,m);out.y=lerp(ny,y1,m);return;
      }
      const turned=tr.d1[k]!==d&&ev===BA.EV_MOVED;
      // Straight tiles form one uninterrupted route. A real turn retains its
      // original arrival/looking/turning hold; basket approach retains support.
      let m=turned?ease(f/.6):clamp(f);
      if(ev!==BA.EV_MOVED){x1-=DX[d]*14;y1-=DY[d]*14;m=ease(f/.6);}
      out.x=lerp(x0,x1,m);out.y=lerp(y0,y1,m);
      out.bob=Math.sin(Math.PI*m)*.6;
    }
    // Study-local supported paper override. It deliberately has no art gate:
    // missing artwork must never change the accepted arrival clock.
    function paper(out,tr,now){
      if(tr.homeKind==='flap'||tr.n===0||tr.coat<1||tr.coat>2||(tr.end!==BA.END_HOME&&tr.end!==BA.END_WRONG))return;
      const j=tr.n-1,d=tr.d0[j],T=now-(tr.rel+tr.n*tr.tick);
      const hx=tr.ox+tr.x1[j]*16+8,hy=tr.oy+tr.y1[j]*16+8,bx=hx-DX[d]*14,by=hy-DY[d]*14;
      if(T<0){
        if(T<-tr.tick||tr.bn[j]||out.anim===A.HIDDEN||out.anim===A.POP)return;
        const u=ease((T+tr.tick)/(tr.tick*.6));
        out.x=lerp(tr.ox+tr.x0[j]*16+8,bx,u);out.y=lerp(tr.oy+tr.y0[j]*16+8,by,u);return;
      }
      const wrong=tr.end===BA.END_WRONG;
      if(!wrong&&now>=tr.napUntil){
        if(out.anim===A.WAKE)out.z=3;
        else if(out.anim===A.LEAVE)out.z+=Math.round(3*(1-clamp((now-tr.napUntil-BA.WAKE_LEN)/BA.LEAVE_LEN)));
        return;
      }
      if(T<.2){out.x=bx;out.y=by;return;}
      const fade=wrong?0:clamp((T-.75)/(1.2-.75)),slide=ease(fade);
      out.boxX=bx-Math.SQRT1_2*12*slide;out.boxY=by+Math.SQRT1_2*12*slide;out.boxFade=fade;
      out.boxFold=wrong||T<.45?0:T<.75?1+Math.floor((T-.45)*10+1e-6):T<1.2?4:-1;
      if(T>=.6){if(!wrong)out.z=3;return;}
      const mirrored=d===0||d===3,away=d===2||d===3,sx=mirrored?-2:2,u=clamp((T-.4)/(.6-.4));
      const tx=wrong?q2(hx+3):q2(hx+tr.homeDX),ty=wrong?q2(hy+3):q2(hy+tr.homeDY),startZ=away?-1:5,endZ=wrong?1:-1;
      out.x=q(lerp(bx+4+sx,tx+(mirrored?-3:3),u));out.y=q(lerp(by+4-sx,ty+(mirrored?3:-3),u));
      out.z=T<.4?startZ:Math.round(lerp(startZ,endZ,u)+Math.sin(u*Math.PI)*3+(1-u)**4);
      out.takeoff=T<.3?0:T<.4?1:T<.45?2:3;out.takeoffSupported=T<.4?1:0;
    }
    function sample(tr,time,out){
      finite(time);const now=time+1e-9; // Exactly one effective production epsilon.
      resetBase(out.base);BA.pose(tr,now,out.base);
      for(let k=0;k<keys.length;k++)out[keys[k]]=out.base[keys[k]];
      out.takeoff=-1;out.takeoffSupported=0;glide(out,tr,now);paper(out,tr,now);
      const visible=out.anim!==A.HIDDEN,terminal=tr.n>0&&(tr.end===BA.END_HOME||tr.end===BA.END_WRONG),T=terminal?now-(tr.rel+tr.n*tr.tick):-Infinity;
      const loose=out.takeoff>=0||out.anim===A.TURN||out.anim===A.CURL||out.anim===A.NAP||out.anim===A.WAKE||out.anim===A.LEAVE||out.anim===A.PUZZLED||out.anim===A.HOP;
      const boxed=visible&&!loose,r=out.root,c=out.cat,b=out.box,rim=out.rim,e=out.effects;
      let phase=names[out.anim];
      if(out.takeoff>=0)phase=out.takeoff===0?'crouch':out.takeoff===1?'push':'flight';
      else if(boxed)phase='boxed';else if(out.anim===A.TURN||out.anim===A.CURL)phase='settling';
      r.visible=visible;r.x=out.x;r.y=out.y;r.z=out.z;r.anim=out.anim;r.frame=out.frame;r.phase=phase;r.terminalAge=T;
      c.visible=visible;c.coat=tr.coat;c.boxed=boxed;c.bodyDir=out.dir;c.headDir=out.head;c.ears=out.ears;c.eyes=out.eyes;c.bob=out.bob;
      c.stretch=out.anim===A.STRETCH?Math.min(2,out.frame+1):0;c.pose=out.takeoff>=0?'takeoff':names[out.anim];c.frame=out.takeoff>=0?out.takeoff:out.frame;
      c.x=out.x+(boxed?4:0);c.y=out.y+(boxed?4:0);c.z=out.z;c.mirror=!boxed&&(out.dir===0||out.dir===3||(out.anim===A.TURN&&(out.frame&1)!==0));c.clipFloor=out.anim===A.POP;c.supported=out.takeoffSupported===1;
      b.visible=boxed||visible&&out.boxFold>=0;b.occupied=boxed;b.x=boxed?out.x:out.boxFold>=0?out.boxX:0;b.y=boxed?out.y:out.boxFold>=0?out.boxY:0;
      b.z=boxed?out.z:0;b.dir=boxed?out.dir:terminal?tr.d0[tr.n-1]:out.dir;b.fold=boxed?0:out.boxFold;b.fade=boxed?0:out.boxFade;
      rim.visible=visible&&out.takeoffSupported===1;rim.kind='carrier';rim.x=rim.visible?out.boxX+4:0;rim.y=rim.visible?out.boxY+4:0;rim.z=0;rim.coat=tr.coat;
      e.hatchX=out.anim===A.POP?tr.hx:-1;e.hatchY=out.anim===A.POP?tr.hy:-1;e.hatchOpen=out.hatchOpen;
      e.spinX=out.spinX;e.spinY=out.spinY;e.spin=out.spin;e.bumpX=out.bumpX;e.bumpY=out.bumpY;e.bumpAge=out.bumpAge;
      e.homeX=tr.n>0?tr.x1[tr.n-1]:-1;e.homeY=tr.n>0?tr.y1[tr.n-1]:-1;
      e.cushionOccupied=out.anim===A.CURL||out.anim===A.NAP||out.anim===A.WAKE;e.flapOpen=out.flapOpen;e.puzzled=out.anim===A.PUZZLED;
      return out;
    }

    /* hatch(levelHatch,traces,time,out): raw level hatch {x,y,dir,cats}, full
       global trace array. Optional catIds or first explicitly identifies global
       cats; otherwise infer contiguous first from a matching released trace.
       Before Send (empty traces) every cat waits. Unreleased Infinity traces
       retain their level coat. pop-start (rel-pop), not first movement, removes
       a head. n is visible count <=4; waiting is total; queue entries are ordered
       nearest-first and must draw in REVERSE order. queue x/y are ART anchors,
       hatch x/y are centre. open uses production 0/1/2 (closed/ajar/open).
       An entirely unreleased non-empty scene requires first/catIds, since the
       original empty trace does not yet store hatch identity. */
    function makeHatchOut(){
      const out={x:0,y:0,dir:0,open:0,n:0,waiting:0,queue:[],base:BA.makeOut()};
      for(let k=0;k<4;k++)out.queue.push({visible:false,id:-1,coat:1,x:0,y:0,z:.5,dir:0});return out;
    }
    function hatch(h,traces,time,out){
      finite(time);resetBase(out.base);const now=time+1e-9,d=typeof h.dir==='number'?h.dir:dirs[h.dir],ox=h.ox||0,oy=h.oy||0;
      out.x=ox+h.x*16+8;out.y=oy+h.y*16+8;out.dir=d;out.open=0;out.n=0;out.waiting=0;
      let first=h.first;
      if(first===undefined){first=0;for(let k=0;k<traces.length;k++)if(traces[k].rel!==Infinity&&traces[k].hx===h.x&&traces[k].hy===h.y){first=k;break;}}
      for(let k=0;k<4;k++){const q=out.queue[k];q.visible=false;q.id=-1;q.coat=1;q.x=0;q.y=0;q.z=.5;q.dir=d;}
      for(let k=0;k<h.cats.length;k++){
        const id=h.catIds?h.catIds[k]:first+k,tr=traces[id];
        if(tr&&now>=tr.rel-tr.pop){
          resetBase(out.base);BA.pose(tr,now,out.base);if(out.base.anim===A.POP&&out.base.hatchOpen>out.open)out.open=out.base.hatchOpen;
        }else{
          out.waiting++;
          if(out.n<4){const j=out.n++,q=out.queue[j];q.visible=true;q.id=id;q.coat=coat(h.cats[k]);q.x=out.x+DX[d]*(3-4*j);q.y=out.y+DY[d]*(3-4*j);q.z=.5;q.dir=d;}
        }
      }
      return out;
    }

    /* dog(levelDog,index,poses,ambientTime,out): resolve only real matching tile
       bumps (0<=age<.6). index controls fixed mirror/phase. x/y are the original
       dog ART anchor (tile centre+2). glyph is the original source-world Z rig;
       fade is dither progress, not opacity. It remains ambient even ear-up.
       poses may be Civic outputs or unchanged BOARDANIM outputs. No arrival
       epsilon is added to ambientTime. Use makeDogOut once per scene dog. */
    function makeDogOut(){return {x:0,y:0,z:0,frame:0,mirror:false,phase:0,earLift:0,bumpAge:-1,glyph:{visible:false,x:0,y:0,z:0,big:false,fade:0}};}
    function dog(d,index,poses,ambientTime,out){
      finite(ambientTime);const mirror=(index&1)===1,tt=ambientTime+index*1.7;
      out.x=(d.ox||0)+d.x*16+10;out.y=(d.oy||0)+d.y*16+10;out.z=0;out.mirror=mirror;out.phase=index*1.7;out.earLift=0;out.bumpAge=-1;
      for(let k=0;k<poses.length;k++){
        const p=poses[k].effects||poses[k];
        if(p.bumpX===d.x&&p.bumpY===d.y&&p.bumpAge>=0&&p.bumpAge<.6){out.earLift=1;if(out.bumpAge<0||p.bumpAge<out.bumpAge)out.bumpAge=p.bumpAge;}
      }
      const fl=Math.floor(fract(tt/12)*120);
      out.frame=out.earLift?3:fl>=60&&fl<63?2:fract(tt/1.5)<.34?1:0;
      const zt=fract(tt/3),g=out.glyph;g.visible=zt<.8;g.x=0;g.y=0;g.z=0;g.big=false;g.fade=0;
      if(g.visible){const k=zt/.8,big=k>.45,sxo=mirror?9-(big?4:3)-Math.round(k*2):-9+Math.round(k*2);g.x=out.x+sxo*.5;g.y=out.y-sxo*.5;g.z=6+Math.round(k*6);g.big=big;g.fade=k;}
      return out;
    }
    return {makeOut,sample,makeHatchOut,hatch,makeDogOut,dog};
  }
  return {create};
});

/* Parcel material library, extended from the reviewed rear-corner study.
   All work is baked once in surface coordinates;
   the construction bounds, shelf contacts and canonical actors stay unchanged. */
(function(root){'use strict';
const U=ParcelLayout.unit,P=ParcelProjection;
const point=q=>P.project((q[0]-80)/16,(q[1]-80)/16,q[2]*U);
const add=(a,b)=>a.map((v,i)=>v+b[i]);
const length=a=>Math.hypot(...a);
const ink=['#c29660','#c9a270','#b18454','#b27659','#cbb991','#b58e72'];
class Corner{
 constructor(a){this.a=a;this.c=a.c;this.patterns={};this.swatches={};this.light=[44,34,70];}
 plane(o,u,v,draw){
  const c=this.c,p=point(o),q=point(add(o,u)),r=point(add(o,v)),w=length(u),h=length(v);
  c.save();c.transform((q.x-p.x)/w,(q.y-p.y)/w,(r.x-p.x)/h,(r.y-p.y)/h,p.x,p.y);
  c.beginPath();c.rect(0,0,w,h);c.clip();draw(c,w,h);c.restore();
 }
 pattern(name){
  if(this.patterns[name])return this.patterns[name];
  const keys={oak:'oak-longgrain',floor:'oak-longgrain',endgrain:'oak-endgrain',kraft:'folded-kraft',steel:'teal-painted-steel',slate:'teal-painted-steel',rubber:'teal-painted-steel',plaster:'warm-lime-plaster','oak-worn':'weathered-oak-longgrain',brick:'aged-brick',cobbles:'warm-cobblestones',red:'postal-red-metal',ivory:'ivory-wrapping-paper',silver:'warm-silver-roller-metal',brass:'warm-silver-roller-metal',sage:'sage-enamel'};
  const key=keys[name],source=this.a.r.assets.corner?.materials[key]?'corner':'workroom',meta=this.a.r.assets[source]?.materials[key],r=meta?.rect,im=this.a.r.images[source];if(!r||!im)return null;
  const t=document.createElement('canvas');t.width=r[2];t.height=r[3];const tc=t.getContext('2d');tc.drawImage(im,...r,0,0,r[2],r[3]);
  const pixels=tc.getImageData(0,0,t.width,t.height),d=pixels.data,mean=meta.meanRGB,lum=mean[0]*.2126+mean[1]*.7152+mean[2]*.0722,base={oak:[185,139,98],floor:[218,183,132],endgrain:[197,153,109],kraft:[202,169,119],steel:[83,111,114],slate:[67,91,95],rubber:[55,65,62],plaster:[237,221,193],'oak-worn':[173,126,86],red:[181,87,66],ivory:[239,222,184],silver:[169,176,166],brass:[184,157,98],sage:[124,143,124],brick:[184,110,83],cobbles:[181,171,151]}[name];
  for(let i=0;i<d.length;i+=4){const v=(d[i]*.2126+d[i+1]*.7152+d[i+2]*.0722-lum)*(name==='plaster'?.6:name==='floor'?.22:1.05);for(let k=0;k<3;k++)d[i+k]=base[k]+v;}tc.putImageData(pixels,0,0);
  this.swatches[name]=t;return this.patterns[name]=this.c.createPattern(t,'repeat');
 }
 texture(name,w,h,vertical=false,seed=0,alpha=1){
  const c=this.c,pat=this.pattern(name);if(!pat)return;
  if(['steel','slate','rubber','kraft','plaster','red','ivory','silver','brass','sage'].includes(name)){
   c.save();c.globalAlpha=alpha;const m=name==='steel'||name==='slate'?54:0;let crop=[m,m,512-2*m,512-2*m];
   if(name==='kraft'){crop=[[0,0,230,230],[260,0,240,240],[40,80,256,256],[0,270,220,220],[260,20,220,220],[0,0,512,512]][seed%6];if(seed%2){c.translate(w,0);c.scale(-1,1);}}
   c.drawImage(this.swatches[name],...crop,0,0,w,h);c.restore();return;
  }
  const size=name==='oak'||name==='oak-worn'||name==='floor'?40:name==='endgrain'?10:name==='brick'?60:44;
  c.save();c.globalAlpha=alpha;c.translate(-seed%3*4,-seed%5*3);if(vertical){c.translate(w,0);c.rotate(Math.PI/2);}
  c.scale(size/512,(name==='floor'?.25:1)*size/512);c.fillStyle=pat;c.fillRect(-4096,-4096,8192,8192);c.restore();
 }
 face(o,u,v,name,colour,shade=0,vertical=false,seed=0){
  this.plane(o,u,v,(c,w,h)=>{
   c.fillStyle=colour;c.fillRect(0,0,w,h);this.texture(name,w,h,vertical,seed,name==='kraft'?.64:1);
