   if(shade){c.fillStyle=shade>0?`rgba(44,44,36,${shade})`:`rgba(255,226,174,${-shade})`;c.fillRect(0,0,w,h);}
   // A broad light gradient describes the face before the fibres become visible.
   const g=c.createLinearGradient(0,0,w*.25,h);g.addColorStop(0,name==='floor'?'#fff1c00a':'#fff1c01a');g.addColorStop(.45,'#fff0d000');g.addColorStop(1,name==='floor'?'#20313e08':'#20313e20');c.fillStyle=g;c.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
  });
 }
 solid(b,kind='oak',seed=0,joined=false){
  const [x,y,z,X,Y,Z]=b,w=X-x,d=Y-y,h=Z-z,vertical=h>w&&h>d;
  const base=kind==='steel'?'#536d71':kind==='kraft'?ink[seed%ink.length]:'#bb8958';
  if(!joined||d>w)this.face([X,y,Z],[0,d,0],[0,0,-h],kind,base,.19,vertical,seed+2);
  if(!joined||w>d)this.face([x,Y,Z],[w,0,0],[0,0,-h],kind,base,.035,vertical,seed);
  this.face([x,y,Z],[w,0,0],[0,d,0],vertical&&kind==='oak'?'endgrain':kind,base,-.07,d>w,seed+1);
  // The small exposed beam end has cross-grain, not a continuation of its length.
  if(kind==='oak'&&!vertical&&!joined){if(w>d)this.face([X,y,Z],[0,d,0],[0,0,-h],'endgrain',base,.12,false,seed);else this.face([x,Y,Z],[w,0,0],[0,0,-h],'endgrain',base,.02,false,seed);}
  const a=this.a;
  a.line([x,Y,Z-.1],[X,Y,Z-.1],kind==='steel'?'#acc0b2b3':'#f5d8a8a0',.85);
  a.line([X,y,Z-.1],[X,Y,Z-.1],kind==='steel'?'#89aaa9a0':'#efc78ba0',.7);
  a.line([X,Y,z+.1],[X,Y,Z-.15],kind==='steel'?'#162f3d88':'#6f4b2c66',.6);
  if(kind==='steel'){
   // Thin rolled edge and a few chips; never a uniform rusty outline.
   a.line([x,Y,z+.15],[X,Y,z+.15],'#203d4caa',.8);
   for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const t=(seed*7+i*19)%31/32;a.line([x+w*t,Y,Z-.25],[Math.min(X,x+w*t+.6),Y,Z-.25],'#d0bc88a0',.8);}
  }
 }
 timber(b,seed=0,joined=false){
  this.solid(b,'oak',seed,joined);
  const [x,y,z,X,Y,Z]=b,vertical=Z-z>Math.max(X-x,Y-y);
  if(vertical){
   this.a.line([x+.3,Y+.015,z+2],[x+.3,Y+.015,Z-.2],'#f6d7a271',.6);
   this.a.line([X-.24,Y+.02,z+1],[X-.24,Y+.02,Z-.2],'#67432e69',.65);
   for(const h of [4,75.5])this.a.bolt(X-x>Y-y?'x':'y',X-x>Y-y?(x+X)/2:(y+Y)/2,X-x>Y-y?Y+.03:X+.03,h,.33);
   // Exposed end grain belongs to the joint's oak dowel, not a painted stripe
   // across a continuous beam. The small protrusion stays on the post face.
   this.peg([x+.85,Y+.045,78.4],[2.3,0,0]);
  }else{
   // Face checks run with the fibres. End grain remains on the physical cut.
   if(X-x>Y-y)this.a.line([x+3,Y+.02,Z-3.5],[Math.min(X-1,x+13),Y+.02,Z-3.7],'#68473065',.65);
   else this.a.line([X+.02,y+3,Z-3.4],[X+.02,Math.min(Y-1,y+12),Z-3.65],'#68473065',.65);
  }
 }
 peg(o,u){
  this.plane(o,u,[0,0,-2.3],(c,w,h)=>{c.beginPath();c.arc(w/2,h/2,1.04,0,Math.PI*2);c.fillStyle='#563f2c';c.fill();c.beginPath();c.arc(w/2-.08,h/2-.08,.92,0,Math.PI*2);c.clip();this.pattern('endgrain');c.drawImage(this.swatches.endgrain,80,80,340,340,0,0,w,h);});
 }
 brace(b){
  const a=this.a,c=this.c,point3=(v,d)=>b.axis==='x'?[d,v[0],v[1]]:[v[0],d,v[1]],rear=b.outline.map(v=>point3(v,b.fixed)),front=b.outline.map(v=>point3(v,b.fixed+b.depth));
  for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const j=(i+1)%4;a.poly([rear[i],rear[j],front[j],front[i]],i===1?'#d1a16b':'#85603f','none');}
  const v=b.outline,axis=b.axis==='x'?'y':'x',lo=Math.min(...v.map(q=>q[0])),hi=Math.max(...v.map(q=>q[0]));
  const o=axis==='x'?[lo,b.fixed+b.depth,80]:[b.fixed+b.depth,hi,80],u=axis==='x'?[hi-lo,0,0]:[0,lo-hi,0];
  this.plane(o,u,[0,0,-14],(ctx,w,h)=>{ctx.beginPath();v.forEach((q,i)=>{const xx=axis==='x'?q[0]-lo:hi-q[0];i?ctx.lineTo(xx,80-q[1]):ctx.moveTo(xx,80-q[1]);});ctx.closePath();ctx.clip();ctx.fillStyle='#ac774c';ctx.fill();ctx.save();ctx.translate(0,6);ctx.rotate(axis==='x'?-.73:.73);this.texture('oak-worn',w,h,false,3,.9);ctx.restore();});
  for(const t of [.2,.8]){const q=v[0][0]+(v[3][0]-v[0][0])*t;a.bolt(axis,q,b.fixed+b.depth+.03,69+11*t,.46);}
 }
 walls(){
  for(const [axis,lo,hi,top,bottom] of [['y',4,224,80,22],['x',4,166,80,22],['x',166,246,80,69],['x',246,260,80,22]]){
   const left=axis==='y',o=left?[.02,lo,top]:[lo,.02,top],u=left?[0,hi-lo,0]:[hi-lo,0,0],v=[0,0,bottom-top];
   this.face(o,u,v,'plaster','#e8d4af',left?.07:0,false,Math.floor(lo));
   this.plane(o,u,v,(c,w,h)=>{
    for(const at of left?[48,140]:[40,110,220]){const light=c.createRadialGradient(at-lo,18,1,at-lo,18,39);light.addColorStop(0,'#ffdc9490');light.addColorStop(.45,'#ffe3a948');light.addColorStop(1,'#ffd68300');c.fillStyle=light;c.fillRect(0,0,w,h);}
    let g=c.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,8);g.addColorStop(0,'#3b302a65');g.addColorStop(1,'#54402a00');c.fillStyle=g;c.fillRect(0,0,w,8);
    for(const post of left?[0,70,140,220]:[0,76,158,248]){g=c.createLinearGradient(post-lo,0,post-lo+8,0);g.addColorStop(0,'#463a3045');g.addColorStop(1,'#463a3000');c.fillStyle=g;c.fillRect(post-lo,0,8,h);}
   });
  }
  for(const left of [true,false])for(let t=0;t<(left?224:166);t+=8){
   const o=left?[.55,t,22]:[t,.55,22],u=left?[0,8,0]:[8,0,0];this.face(o,u,[0,0,-22],'slate','#435b62',left?.1:0,false,t);
   this.a.line(left?[.57,t,1]:[t,.57,1],left?[.57,t,22]:[t,.57,22],'#243d4666',.8);
  }
  this.solid([.2,0,21.4,1,224,22.4],'steel',3);this.solid([0,.2,21.4,166,1,22.4],'steel',4);
 }

 back(r){
  const b=r.bounds,left=r.left,max=left?b[4]:b[3];
  this.plane(left?[.7,b[1],64]:[b[0],12.7,64],left?[0,max-b[1],0]:[max-b[0],0,0],[0,0,-63],(c,w,h)=>{
   c.fillStyle='#314d56';c.fillRect(0,0,w,h);this.texture('steel',w,h,false,4,.35);
   const g=c.createLinearGradient(0,0,w,h);g.addColorStop(0,'#233c4e8c');g.addColorStop(.6,'#253c4e48');g.addColorStop(1,'#132f3d9c');c.fillStyle=g;c.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
   for(const z of [21,42,63]){const y=64-z;const shadow=c.createLinearGradient(0,y,0,y+4);shadow.addColorStop(0,'#112731b0');shadow.addColorStop(1,'#11273100');c.fillStyle=shadow;c.fillRect(0,y,w,4);}
  });
 }
 selected(){return true;}
 steel(b,left){
  if(!this.selected(b,left))return false;
  const q=b;this.solid(q,'steel',Math.round(b[0]+b[1]+b[2]));
  if(q[2]===63&&!left)this.plane([q[0],q[1],q[5]+.01],[q[3]-q[0],0,0],[0,q[4]-q[1],0],(c,w,h)=>{const g=c.createRadialGradient(44-q[0],34-q[1],0,44-q[0],34-q[1],25);g.addColorStop(0,'#ffdb88a6');g.addColorStop(.45,'#ffe2a258');g.addColorStop(1,'#ffe2a200');c.fillStyle=g;c.fillRect(0,0,w,h);});
  return true;
 }
 contact(b,left){
  const [x,y,z,X,Y,Z]=b;
  // A load footprint and a short soft shadow are painted ON its shelf plane.
  this.plane([x-.45,y-.45,z+.015],[X-x+1.4,0,0],[0,Y-y+1.4,0],(c,w,h)=>{const g=c.createRadialGradient(w*.5,h*.5,1,w*.5,h*.5,Math.max(w,h)*.7);g.addColorStop(0,'#152b35a0');g.addColorStop(.65,'#26383b70');g.addColorStop(1,'#26383b00');c.fillStyle=g;c.fillRect(0,0,w,h);});
 }
 parcel(item,left){
  const q=item.bounds,[x,y,z,X,Y,Z]=q,w=X-x,d=Y-y,h=Z-z,id=item.seed+(left?40:0);this.contact(q,left);this.solid(q,'kraft',id);
  const axis=left?'y':'x',o=left?[X+.015,Y,Z]:[x,Y+.015,Z],u=left?[0,-d,0]:[w,0,0];
  // A supported paper cuboid, with folds on the actual face and lid seam.
  this.plane(o,u,[0,0,-h],(c,W,H)=>{
   const foot=c.createLinearGradient(0,H-1.3,0,H);foot.addColorStop(0,'#614b3500');foot.addColorStop(1,'#614b3559');c.fillStyle=foot;c.fillRect(0,H-1.3,W,1.3);
   const spill=c.createRadialGradient(W*.4,-H*.2,1,W*.4,-H*.2,H*1.5);spill.addColorStop(0,'#ffdc9435');spill.addColorStop(1,'#ffdc9400');c.fillStyle=spill;c.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
   c.fillStyle='#553c2c2a';c.beginPath();c.moveTo(0,H);c.lineTo(W*.34,H);c.lineTo(0,H-1.8);c.fill();
   c.lineWidth=.12;c.strokeStyle='#f5d9a885';c.strokeRect(.28,.3,W-.56,H-.55);
   c.strokeStyle='#71503870';c.beginPath();c.moveTo(.2,.3);c.lineTo(1.1,1.05);c.lineTo(1.4,H*.55);c.moveTo(W-.2,H-.2);c.lineTo(W-1,H-1.1);c.stroke();
   if(id%3!==1){c.fillStyle='#e7ce9c';c.fillRect(W*.44,0,W*.13,H*.53);c.fillStyle='#f8e3b52a';c.fillRect(W*.46,.1,W*.035,H*.48);c.strokeStyle='#93754750';c.beginPath();c.moveTo(W*.44,0);c.lineTo(W*.44,H*.53);c.stroke();}
   else{c.lineWidth=.23;c.strokeStyle='#76532b';c.beginPath();c.moveTo(W*.5,0);c.lineTo(W*.5,H);c.moveTo(0,H*.47);c.lineTo(W,H*.47);c.stroke();c.lineWidth=.11;c.strokeStyle='#f2d6a0';c.stroke();}
   const lx=W*.56,ly=H*.58,lw=Math.min(9,W*.35),lh=Math.max(2.8,lw*.65);c.save();c.translate(lx,ly);c.rotate((id%3-1)*.055);c.fillStyle='#694b302e';c.fillRect(.15,.2,lw,lh);c.fillStyle=id%4===0?'#dfc68b':'#f0e4c3';c.fillRect(0,0,lw,lh);c.strokeStyle='#a9553c';c.lineWidth=.14;c.strokeRect(.35,.4,lw-.7,lh-1.2);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(.35,.4);c.lineTo(lw*.5,lh*.5);c.lineTo(lw-.35,.4);c.stroke();c.fillStyle='#65554690';c.fillRect(.6,lh-.5,lw*.6,.12);c.restore();
   if(id%4===0){c.strokeStyle='#884c3580';c.lineWidth=.16;c.beginPath();c.arc(W*.27,H*.72,.85,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();}
  });
  this.plane([x,y,Z+.012],[w,0,0],[0,d,0],(c,W,H)=>{
   c.strokeStyle='#735032a0';c.lineWidth=.14;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(W*.5,0);c.lineTo(W*.5,H);c.moveTo(0,H*.15);c.lineTo(W*.5,H*.28);c.lineTo(W,H*.15);c.moveTo(0,H*.85);c.lineTo(W*.5,H*.72);c.lineTo(W,H*.85);c.stroke();
   c.fillStyle='#eed6a4d9';if(id%3!==1)c.fillRect(W*.43,0,W*.14,H);else{c.strokeStyle='#e8d09d';c.lineWidth=.22;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(W*.5,0);c.lineTo(W*.5,H);c.moveTo(0,H*.48);c.lineTo(W,H*.48);c.stroke();}
  });
 }
 lamp(x=44,y=34,z=70,left=false){
  const a=this.a,c=this.c;

/* Static fixtures and setting use the reviewed materials and native contacts. */
(function(root){'use strict';
const U=ParcelLayout.unit;
class Workroom{
 constructor(a){this.a=a;this.m=a.corner;}
 cylinder(o,axis,len,radius,mat,seed=0){
  const a=this.a,m=this.m,faces=[],at=(t,r,angle)=>axis==='x'?[o[0]+t,o[1]+Math.cos(angle)*r,o[2]+Math.sin(angle)*r]:axis==='y'?[o[0]+Math.cos(angle)*r,o[1]+t,o[2]+Math.sin(angle)*r]:[o[0]+Math.cos(angle)*r,o[1]+Math.sin(angle)*r,o[2]+t];

 planter(x,y,w,d){

/* Static paper solids. Original support footprints and board origin are retained. */
(function(root){'use strict';
const P=ParcelProjection,L=ParcelLayout,U=L.unit,C=ParcelConstruction;
class Architecture{
 constructor(r){this.r=r;this.bounds=L.bounds;this.bakes=0;this.canvas=null;this.views={};this.viewBakes=0;}
 bake(){
  const b=this.bounds,d=1.5,c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=Math.ceil((b.right-b.left)*d);c.height=Math.ceil((b.bottom-b.top)*d);
  const ctx=c.getContext('2d');ctx.setTransform(d,0,0,d,-b.left*d,-b.top*d);
  this.p=new ParcelPainter(ctx,this.r.images,this.r.assets);this.c=ctx;
  this.corner=new ParcelCorner(this);this.work=new ParcelWorkroom(this);this.floor();this.floorCanvas=c;
  const wall=document.createElement('canvas');const wallDensity=2;wall.width=Math.ceil((b.right-b.left)*wallDensity);wall.height=Math.ceil((b.bottom-b.top)*wallDensity);const wc=wall.getContext('2d');wc.setTransform(wallDensity,0,0,wallDensity,-b.left*wallDensity,-b.top*wallDensity);this.p=new ParcelPainter(wc,this.r.images,this.r.assets);this.c=wc;this.corner=new ParcelCorner(this);this.work=new ParcelWorkroom(this);this.walls();this.fixtures();this.canvas=wall;
  // The loading-bay lamp is inside the room, in front of the van in projection.
  const lp=P.project((220-80)/16,(34-80)/16,70*U),fb=this.foregroundBounds={left:Math.floor(lp.x-80),right:Math.ceil(lp.x+150),top:Math.floor(lp.y-135),bottom:Math.ceil(lp.y+65)};const foreground=document.createElement('canvas');foreground.width=(fb.right-fb.left)*wallDensity;foreground.height=(fb.bottom-fb.top)*wallDensity;const fc=foreground.getContext('2d');fc.setTransform(wallDensity,0,0,wallDensity,-fb.left*wallDensity,-fb.top*wallDensity);this.p=new ParcelPainter(fc,this.r.images,this.r.assets);this.c=fc;this.corner=new ParcelCorner(this);this.work=new ParcelWorkroom(this);this.corner.lamp(220,34,70);this.foregroundCanvas=foreground;this.bakes++;
 }
 drawLayer(name,source,b=this.bounds){
  const r=this.r,key=name;let v=this.views[key];
  if(!v)v=this.views[key]={canvas:document.createElement('canvas'),x:NaN,y:NaN,scale:0,dpr:0};
  if(v.x!==r.cam.tx||v.y!==r.cam.ty||v.scale!==r.cam.scale||v.dpr!==r.dpr||v.canvas.width!==r.canvas.width||v.canvas.height!==r.canvas.height){
   v.x=r.cam.tx;v.y=r.cam.ty;v.scale=r.cam.scale;v.dpr=r.dpr;v.canvas.width=r.canvas.width;v.canvas.height=r.canvas.height;
   const c=v.canvas.getContext('2d');c.setTransform(r.dpr*r.cam.scale,0,0,r.dpr*r.cam.scale,r.cam.tx*r.dpr,r.cam.ty*r.dpr);c.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;c.imageSmoothingQuality='high';c.drawImage(source,b.left,b.top,b.right-b.left,b.bottom-b.top);this.viewBakes++;
  }
  const c=r.ctx;c.save();c.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);c.drawImage(v.canvas,0,0);c.restore();
 }
 drawFloor(){this.drawLayer('floor',this.floorCanvas);}
 draw(){this.drawLayer('walls',this.canvas);}
 drawForeground(){this.drawLayer('foreground',this.foregroundCanvas,this.foregroundBounds);}
 box(x,y,w,h,z,height,mat='oak',col){this.p.box((x-80)/16,(y-80)/16,w/16,h/16,z*U,height*U,mat,col);}
 line(a,b,col,width=1){this.p.line3([(a[0]-80)/16,(a[1]-80)/16,a[2]*U],[(b[0]-80)/16,(b[1]-80)/16,b[2]*U],col,width);}
 front(name,axis,start,fixed,z,w,h){this.p.face(axis,(start+(axis==='y'?w:0)-80)/16,(fixed-80)/16,z*U,c=>{const im=this.r.images[name];if(im)c.drawImage(im,0,0,w*U,h*U);});}
 solid(b,mat='oak',col){this.box(b[0],b[1],b[3]-b[0],b[4]-b[1],b[2],b[5]-b[2],mat,col);}
 poly(v,fill,mat='oak',stroke){this.p.poly(v.map(q=>[(q[0]-80)/16,(q[1]-80)/16,q[2]*U]),fill,mat,stroke);}
 brace(b){
  const point=(v,d)=>b.axis==='x'?[d,v[0],v[1]]:[v[0],d,v[1]],back=b.outline.map(v=>point(v,b.fixed)),front=b.outline.map(v=>point(v,b.fixed+b.depth));
  this.poly(back,'#956544');
  for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const j=(i+1)%4;this.poly([back[i],back[j],front[j],front[i]],i===1?'#e0b184':'#9b6e4c');}
  this.poly(front,'#b7865c');
  const v=b.outline;for(const t of [.2,.8]){const u=v[0][0]+(v[3][0]-v[0][0])*t,z=69+11*t;this.bolt(b.axis==='x'?'y':'x',u,b.fixed+b.depth+.03,z,.42);}
 }
 bolt(axis,start,fixed,z,r=.35){this.p.face(axis,(start-80)/16,(fixed-80)/16,z*U,c=>{c.fillStyle='#624f39';c.beginPath();c.arc(0,0,r*U,0,Math.PI*2);c.fill();c.fillStyle='#ddbd7d';c.beginPath();c.arc(-.14*U,-.12*U,r*.55*U,0,Math.PI*2);c.fill();});}
 dial(d){
  // A shallow cylinder, with the weighing face attached to its front plane.
  const back=[],front=[];for(let i=0;i<48;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/24,x=d.x+d.radius*Math.cos(a),z=d.z+d.radius*Math.sin(a);back.push([x,d.y-d.depth,z]);front.push([x,d.y,z]);}
  this.poly(back,'#716f5d','none');
  for(let i=0;i<48;i++){const j=(i+1)%48;this.poly([back[i],back[j],front[j],front[i]],'#aa9871','none','#aa9871');}
  this.poly(front,'#cbb17e','oak');this.front('mechanical-scale-dial','x',d.x-d.radius,d.y+.02,d.z+d.radius,d.radius*2,d.radius*2);
 }
 floor(){
  const p=this.p,c=this.c;
  c.save();c.shadowColor='#49534440';c.shadowBlur=30;c.shadowOffsetY=12;
  p.poly([[-5.25,-5.25,-40],[11.25,-5.25,-40],[11.25,9.25,-40],[-5.25,9.25,-40]],'#49534422','none');c.restore();
  this.work.floor();
 }
 walls(){
  const p=this.p;
  const cream={top:'#f9e8cb',left:'#ecdbba',right:'#d3c5aa'},teal={top:'#7c9694',left:'#526e73',right:'#405b64'};
  this.box(-4,-4,4,232,0,84,'stone',cream);
  this.box(0,-4,166,4,0,84,'stone',cream);this.box(246,-4,14,4,0,84,'stone',cream);this.box(166,-4,80,4,66,18,'stone',cream);
  this.box(-.1,0,.5,224,0,22,'teal',teal);this.box(0,-.1,166,.5,0,22,'teal',teal);
  for(let y=0;y<224;y+=8)this.line([.5,y,1],[.5,y,22],'#a5b7a144',.8);
  for(let x=0;x<166;x+=8)this.line([x,.5,1],[x,.5,22],'#a5b7a144',.8);
  this.corner.walls();
  for(const b of C.frame.braces)this.corner.brace(b);
  for(const b of C.frame.posts){this.corner.timber(b,b[0]+b[1]);this.box(b[0]-.15,b[1]-.15,4.3,4.3,0,1.2,'stone');}
  for(const b of C.frame.beams)this.corner.timber(b,Math.round(b[3]+b[4]));
  // Real aperture: no painted wall behind the loading bay.
  this.box(163,-2,3,5,0,68,'teal',teal);this.box(246,-2,3,5,0,68,'teal',teal);this.box(162,-2,88,5,66,3,'teal',teal);
  for(const x of [163.08,246.08]){this.corner.face([x,3.04,12],[2.82,0,0],[0,0,-11.85],'steel','#637d7a',.1);for(const z of [1.4,10.5])this.bolt('x',x+1.4,3.08,z,.2);}
  this.solid(C.bay.door,'teal',teal);this.front('face-door','x',114,3.05,63,49,58);
  this.box(179,.2,54,.8,72,8,'teal',teal);p.face('x',(181-80)/16,(1.1-80)/16,78*U,c=>{c.font='bold 15px Georgia,serif';c.fillStyle='#f3dfb3';c.textAlign='center';c.fillText('PARCEL ROOM',25*U,0);});
  this.solid(C.bay.track,'teal',teal);this.line([113,4.1,69],[250,4.1,69],'#c2c3a6',1.5);
  for(const x of [120,156]){this.box(x-.5,3.2,1,1,61,7,'teal');this.bolt('x',x,4.25,68,1.45);this.bolt('x',x,4.28,62,.4);}
  this.front('face-coats','y',108,.6,66,18,29);this.front('face-correspondence','y',79,.6,64,25,24);

 }
 rack(r){
  const col={top:'#849b9a',left:'#58777d',right:'#3d5e68'},b=r.bounds,left=r.left;
  this.box(b[0],b[1],left?.6:b[3]-b[0],left?b[4]-b[1]:.6,0,64,'teal',{top:'#697e81',left:'#344e59',right:'#344e59'});
  this.corner.back(r);
  for(let i=0;i<r.posts.length;i+=2){if(!this.corner.steel(r.posts[i],left))this.solid(r.posts[i],'teal',col);}
  // Each load sits on a visible board. Higher boards and near uprights then
  // occlude the load instead of having every parcel paint over the assembly.
  for(const s of r.shelves){
   this.solid(s,'teal',col);this.corner.steel(s,left);
   for(const item of r.parcels){const q=item.bounds;if(q[2]!==s[5])continue;
    if(this.corner.selected(q,left)){this.corner.parcel(item,left);continue;}
    this.p.parcel((q[0]-80)/16,(q[1]-80)/16,q[2]*U,(q[3]-q[0])/16,(q[4]-q[1])/16,(q[5]-q[2])*U);
    this.front('face-parcel',left?'y':'x',left?q[1]+1:q[0]+1,(left?q[3]:q[4])+.03,q[5]-2,Math.min(6,(left?q[4]-q[1]:q[3]-q[0])*.65),3.5);
   }
   if(left)this.corner.steel([b[3]-.35,b[1],s[2]-.7,b[3],b[4],s[2]+1],true);
   else{this.box(b[0],b[4]-.35,b[3]-b[0],.35,s[2]-.7,1.7,'teal',col);this.corner.steel([b[0],b[4]-.35,s[2]-.7,b[3],b[4],s[2]+1],false);}
  }
  for(let i=1;i<r.posts.length;i+=2){const q=r.posts[i];if(!this.corner.steel(q,left))this.solid(q,'teal',col);
   this.box(q[0]-.3,q[1]-.3,q[3]-q[0]+.6,q[4]-q[1]+.6,0,.5,'teal',col);
   for(const z of [5,18,26,39,47,60])this.bolt(left?'y':'x',left?(q[1]+q[4])/2:(q[0]+q[3])/2,left?q[3]+.03:q[4]+.03,z,.22);
  }
 }
 fixtures(){
  const p=this.p;
  // The side rack's rear foot is behind the first bay of the back rack.
  // Painting that whole side assembly last exposed its foot through a load.
  this.rack(C.racks[1]);this.rack(C.racks[0]);
  this.corner.lamp();this.corner.lamp(113,34,70);this.corner.lamp(34,143,70,true);
  this.work.bench();
  for(const x of [12,35])for(const y of [84,102])this.corner.solid([x,y,0,x+2,y+2,21],'oak',5);this.corner.solid([13,102,5,36,104,7],'oak',9);this.corner.solid([11,83,21,38,105,22.5],'oak',4);this.front('face-tea','x',16,104,39,17,17);
  const f=C.scales.floor;this.corner.solid(f.base,'steel',8);this.corner.solid([22.7,114.5,3,37.3,121.3,3.3],'silver',4);this.corner.solid(f.stem,'steel',6);this.dial(f.dial);
  this.corner.parcel({bounds:[12,178,0,40,206,35],seed:31},false);this.corner.parcel({bounds:[43,168,0,55,180,16],seed:24},false);
  this.work.conveyor();
  const h=C.scales.hanging;
  this.box(152,1,4,1,66,14,'teal');this.line([154,2,68],[154,34,77],'#536f70',4);this.corner.solid(h.arm,'steel',8);
  this.line(h.suspension[0],h.suspension[1],'#8a784f',3);
  this.box(153.3,49.4,1.4,1.2,61,1.5,'teal');this.bolt('x',154,50.7,61.8,.55);
  for(const a of [[149,45],[159,45],[149,55],[159,55]])this.line([154,50,47],[a[0],a[1],8],'#9a7e4d',1.5);
  this.corner.solid(h.pan,'oak',12);this.line([147.5,56.5,8],[160.5,56.5,8],'#e8c58b',2);this.dial(h.dial);
  this.cart(45,55,16,12,8);this.cart(48,185,12,10,3);
  for(let i=0;i<5;i++)this.box(236,185+i*.6,17,22,2+i*.8,.65,'oak');
  this.work.planter(5,211,24,9);this.work.planter(238,41,16,10);
 }
 cart(x,y,w,h,z){this.work.cart(x,y,w,h,z);}
}
root.ParcelArchitecture=Architecture;
})(globalThis);

/* Room-owned native-plane materials. Prepared once, no screen-space noise. */
(function(root){'use strict';
const styles={
 limestone:{atlas:'court',key:'limestone',rgb:[218,193,151],scale:[24,24],gain:.78},
 ashlar:{atlas:'court',key:'ashlar',rgb:[203,177,137],scale:[59,45],gain:.9},
 slate:{atlas:'court',key:'roof-slate',rgb:[99,119,121],scale:[52,48],gain:.7},
 lead:{atlas:'court',key:'lead',rgb:[111,135,126],scale:[28,28],gain:1},
 soil:{atlas:'court',key:'soil',rgb:[84,76,54],scale:[22,22],gain:.7},
 sack:{atlas:'court',key:'sackcloth',rgb:[210,191,151],scale:[13,13],gain:.65},
 brick:{atlas:'workroom',key:'aged-brick',rgb:[175,113,89],scale:[60,60],gain:.85},
 cobbles:{atlas:'workroom',key:'warm-cobblestones',rgb:[175,178,164],scale:[44,44],gain:.8},
 paving:{atlas:'workroom',key:'warm-cobblestones',rgb:[196,184,158],scale:[54,54],gain:.65},
 oak:{atlas:'corner',key:'weathered-oak-longgrain',rgb:[175,130,88],scale:[34,20],gain:.7},
 steel:{atlas:'corner',key:'teal-painted-steel',rgb:[66,94,95],scale:[20,20],gain:.65},
 plaster:{atlas:'corner',key:'warm-lime-plaster',rgb:[228,212,181],scale:[54,54],gain:.7},
 red:{atlas:'workroom',key:'postal-red-metal',rgb:[177,79,57],scale:[24,24],gain:.8}
};
class Surfaces{
 constructor(a){this.a=a;this.c=a.c;this.m=new ParcelCorner(a);this.patterns={};this.debug=false;}
 pattern(name){
  if(this.patterns[name])return this.patterns[name];const s=styles[name],meta=this.a.r.assets[s.atlas].materials[s.key],im=this.a.r.images[s.atlas],q=meta.rect;
  const tile=document.createElement('canvas');tile.width=tile.height=512;const c=tile.getContext('2d');
  // Steel uses the interior of its swatch: structural edges supply edge wear.
  const pad=name==='steel'?54:0;c.drawImage(im,q[0]+pad,q[1]+pad,q[2]-pad*2,q[3]-pad*2,0,0,512,512);
  const px=c.getImageData(0,0,512,512),d=px.data;let mean=0;for(let i=0;i<d.length;i+=4)mean+=d[i]*.2126+d[i+1]*.7152+d[i+2]*.0722;mean/=262144;
  for(let i=0;i<d.length;i+=4){const lum=(d[i]*.2126+d[i+1]*.7152+d[i+2]*.0722-mean)*s.gain;for(let k=0;k<3;k++)d[i+k]=s.rgb[k]+lum;}
  c.putImageData(px,0,0);return this.patterns[name]=this.c.createPattern(tile,'repeat');
 }
 paint(c,w,h,name,o,u,v,shade=0){
  const s=styles[name],pat=this.pattern(name);c.fillStyle=`rgb(${s.rgb})`;c.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
  const vertical=Math.abs(v[2])>Math.abs(v[1]),tx=Math.abs(u[0])>Math.abs(u[1])?o[0]:o[1],ty=vertical?-o[2]:o[1];
  c.save();c.scale(s.scale[0]/512,s.scale[1]/512);c.translate(-tx/s.scale[0]*512,-ty/s.scale[1]*512);c.fillStyle=pat;c.fillRect(tx/s.scale[0]*512,ty/s.scale[1]*512,w/s.scale[0]*512,h/s.scale[1]*512);c.restore();
  if(shade){c.fillStyle=shade>0?`rgba(36,58,65,${shade})`:`rgba(255,234,184,${-shade})`;c.fillRect(0,0,w,h);}
  const g=c.createLinearGradient(0,0,w*.3,h);g.addColorStop(0,'#fff1d011');g.addColorStop(.5,'#fff1d000');g.addColorStop(1,'#34494b12');c.fillStyle=g;c.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
 }
 face(o,u,v,kind='limestone',shade=0){this.m.plane(o,u,v,(c,w,h)=>this.paint(c,w,h,kind,o,u,v,shade));}
 solid(q,kind='limestone'){
  const [x,y,z,X,Y,Z]=q,w=X-x,d=Y-y,h=Z-z;
  this.face([X,y,Z],[0,d,0],[0,0,-h],kind,.19);this.face([x,Y,Z],[w,0,0],[0,0,-h],kind,.055);
  this.face([x,y,Z],[w,0,0],[0,d,0],kind==='ashlar'?'limestone':kind,-.06);
  if(h>.7){this.a.line([x,Y,Z-.12],[X,Y,Z-.12],'#fff0c480',.9);this.a.line([X,y,Z-.12],[X,Y,Z-.12],'#f4e5bd60',.8);this.a.line([x,Y,z+.1],[X,Y,z+.1],'#3a4c4b28',.7);}
 }
 polygon(v,kind='limestone',shade=.1){
  let axis=v.every(p=>p[0]===v[0][0])?'x':v.every(p=>p[1]===v[0][1])?'y':v.every(p=>p[2]===v[0][2])?'z':null;if(!axis)return false;
  const xs=v.map(p=>p[0]),ys=v.map(p=>p[1]),zs=v.map(p=>p[2]),x=Math.min(...xs),y=Math.min(...ys),z=Math.min(...zs),X=Math.max(...xs),Y=Math.max(...ys),Z=Math.max(...zs);
  const o=axis==='z'?[x,y,z]:[x,y,Z],u=axis==='x'?[0,Y-y,0]:[X-x,0,0],vv=axis==='z'?[0,Y-y,0]:[0,0,z-Z];
  this.m.plane(o,u,vv,(c,w,h)=>{c.beginPath();v.forEach((p,i)=>{const a=axis==='x'?p[1]-y:p[0]-x,b=axis==='z'?p[1]-y:Z-p[2];if(i)c.lineTo(a,b);else c.moveTo(a,b);});c.closePath();c.clip();this.paint(c,w,h,kind,o,u,vv,shade);});return true;
 }
}
root.ParcelCourtSurfaces=Surfaces;
})(globalThis);

/* Morning Dispatch service court: registered surfaces on the reviewed solids. */
(function(root){'use strict';
const E=ParcelExteriorLayout,U=ParcelLayout.unit,P=ParcelProjection;
const stone={top:'#e0d9c9',left:'#c6bead',right:'#aaa99c'},wall={top:'#e9dfca',left:'#d8ceba',right:'#bdb5a2'},steel={top:'#8d9b97',left:'#6f827f',right:'#586e70'},paving={top:'#b8c1ba',left:'#9fa9a1',right:'#909f99'};
class Exterior{
 constructor(r){this.r=r;this.bounds=E.bounds;this.bakes=0;}
 bake(){
  const b=this.bounds,d=1.35,c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=Math.ceil((b.right-b.left)*d);c.height=Math.ceil((b.bottom-b.top)*d);
  const ctx=c.getContext('2d');ctx.setTransform(d,0,0,d,-b.left*d,-b.top*d);
  this.a=new ParcelArchitecture(this.r);this.a.p=new ParcelPainter(ctx,this.r.images,this.r.assets);this.a.c=ctx;
  this.c=ctx;this.s=new ParcelCourtSurfaces(this.a);this.plants={};this.sky();
  this.ground();this.shadows();
  this.civic();this.stair();this.yard();this.street();this.canvas=c;this.bakes++;
 }
 box(q,col=stone,kind){
  if(this.debug){this.a.solid(q,'none',col);return;}
  this.s.solid(q,kind||(col===steel?'steel':col===wall?'limestone':col===paving?'cobbles':'limestone'));
 }
 sky(){
  if(this.debug)return;const im=this.r.images.town,c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=im.width;c.height=im.height;const q=c.getContext('2d');q.drawImage(im,0,0);q.globalCompositeOperation='destination-in';let g=q.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,c.height);g.addColorStop(0,'#000');g.addColorStop(.62,'#000');g.addColorStop(.92,'#0000');q.fillStyle=g;q.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
  g=q.createLinearGradient(0,0,c.width,0);g.addColorStop(0,'#0000');g.addColorStop(.1,'#000');g.addColorStop(.9,'#000');g.addColorStop(1,'#0000');q.fillStyle=g;q.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
  this.skyCanvas=c;const b=this.bounds,w=b.right-b.left+1600;this.skyBounds={left:b.left-800,right:b.right+800,top:b.top-1300,bottom:b.top-1300+w*2/3};
 }
 surface(o,u,v,kind,shade=0){if(!this.debug)this.s.face(o,u,v,kind,shade);}
 plant(name,x,y,z,width,flip=false){
  if(this.debug)return;const m=this.r.assets.plants.sprites[name],q=m.rect;let im=this.plants[name];if(!im){im=this.plants[name]=document.createElement('canvas');im.width=q[2];im.height=q[3];const c=im.getContext('2d');c.drawImage(this.r.images.plants,...q,0,0,q[2],q[3]);const px=c.getImageData(0,0,q[2],q[3]);for(let i=3;i<px.data.length;i+=4)if(px.data[i]<=8)px.data[i]=0;c.putImageData(px,0,0);}
  const w=ParcelLayout.world(x,y,z),p=P.project(w.x,w.y,w.z),c=this.c,scale=width*U/q[2],ax=(m.anchor[0]-q[0])*scale,ay=(m.anchor[1]-q[1])*scale;
  c.save();c.translate(p.x,p.y);if(flip)c.scale(-1,1);c.drawImage(im,-ax,-ay,q[2]*scale,q[3]*scale);c.restore();
 }
 poly(v,fill,stroke){this.a.poly(v,fill,'none',stroke);}
 line(a,b,col='#8d998e',w=1){this.a.line(a,b,col,w);}
 plane(x,y,z,X,Y,col){this.poly([[x,y,z],[X,y,z],[X,Y,z],[x,Y,z]],col);}
 contact(x,y,X,Y,z){
  const c=this.c;c.save();c.shadowColor='#394a4650';c.shadowBlur=7;c.shadowOffsetY=2;this.plane(x,y,z,X,Y,'#47524b24');c.restore();
 }
 ground(){
  // Cool court, warmer lower street. Both use real native plane mappings.
  for(const q of E.court)this.box(q,paving);
  // Rail and building shadows lie on the receiving surfaces.
  if(!this.debug){this.poly([[72,-61,.025],[174,-61,.025],[206,-30,.025],[104,-30,.025]],'#354c4a17');this.poly([[274,-56,.04],[317,-56,.04],[335,-23,.04],[292,-23,.04]],'#304d4b25');}
  const r=E.ramp;this.poly([[r.x,r.y,0],[r.X,r.y,0],[r.X,r.Y,-18],[r.x,r.Y,-18]],'#b3bdb5');this.surface([r.x,r.y,.02],[r.X-r.x,0,0],[0,r.Y-r.y,-18],'cobbles',.03);
  for(const x of [r.x,r.X])this.poly([[x,r.y,-18],[x,r.y,0],[x,r.Y,-18]],'#9baa9f');
  for(let y=118;y<182;y+=8){const z=E.rampHeight(y);this.line([r.x,y,z+.03],[r.X,y,z+.03],'#b9c1af50',.7);}
  for(const q of [[260,0,-1,264,109,1],[316,0,-1,320,110,1]])this.box(q,stone);
  // Return kerb ends at the top of the ramp; the ramp stays open at both ends.
  for(const x of [268,312])this.poly([[x,110,1],[x+2,110,1],[x+2,182,-17],[x,182,-17]],'#d7d6c6');
 }
 shadows(){
  if(this.debug)return;
  const c=this.c;c.save();c.shadowColor='#41535020';c.shadowBlur=8;
  // Morning light falls from the left. Every shadow is on its receiving plane.
  this.poly([[0,224,-17.98],[260,224,-17.98],[275,237,-17.98],[15,237,-17.98]],'#3c51482c');
  this.poly([[260,0,-17.98],[260,224,-17.98],[275,237,-17.98],[275,13,-17.98]],'#3c514826');
  this.poly([[-136,-126,-17.97],[146,-126,-17.97],[207,-72,-17.97],[-75,-72,-17.97]],'#3d514b26');
  for(const [x,y,w] of [[-155,217,102],[338,20,65],[338,158,80]]){
   this.s.m.plane([x-7,y-5,-17.96],[w*.52,0,0],[0,w*.38,0],(q,W,H)=>{const g=q.createRadialGradient(W*.53,H*.53,1,W*.53,H*.53,W*.5);g.addColorStop(0,'#42584a38');g.addColorStop(.65,'#42584a25');g.addColorStop(1,'#42584a00');q.fillStyle=g;q.fillRect(0,0,W,H);});
   this.poly([[x-.7,y,-17.95],[x+.7,y,-17.95],[x+17,y+14,-17.95],[x+15,y+14,-17.95]],'#43584927');
  }
  c.restore();
 }
 civic(){
  this.box([-136,-126,-18,146,-88,60],stone,'ashlar');this.box(E.terrace,stone,'ashlar');this.box(E.wing,wall,'brick');
  this.box([-137,-89,59,175,-61,60],stone);
  this.surface([-136,-88,60.02],[310,0,0],[0,26,0],'paving',-.05);
  for(let x=-133;x<174;x+=18)this.line([x,-88,60.04],[x,-62,60.04],'#8b806d50',.6);
  // Front pilasters, recessed windows and cornice share the wing's planes.
  for(const x of [-58,-20,11,42,73,104,141]){this.box([x,-91,60,x+5,-87,143],wall);this.box([x-1,-92,60,x+6,-86,65],stone);this.box([x-1,-92,138,x+6,-86,143],stone);}
  for(const x of [-43,-12,19,50,81,112])this.window(x,-89.96,88,18,43);
  this.box([-43,-90,60,-25,-88.5,85],steel);this.line([-41,-88.45,82],[-27,-88.45,82],'#cbd0be',1);this.line([-28,-88.4,70],[-28,-88.4,73],'#d5d5bf',2);
  this.box([-60,-126,143,148,-88,147],stone);
  this.poly([[-60,-126,147],[148,-126,147],[148,-107,159],[-60,-107,159]],'#8d9c99');this.surface([-60,-126,147.02],[208,0,0],[0,19,12],'slate',.08);
  this.poly([[-60,-107,159],[148,-107,159],[148,-88,147],[-60,-88,147]],'#9eaaa1');this.surface([-60,-107,159.02],[208,0,0],[0,19,-12],'slate',-.03);
  this.poly([[148,-126,147],[148,-107,159],[148,-88,147]],'#758b89');
  this.line([-60,-107,159],[148,-107,159],'#c8d0c1',2);
  // Small clock lantern above the service wing, with a real curb and roof.
  this.box([32,-120,146,57,-94,171],wall);this.box([30,-122,169,59,-92,173],stone);
  this.poly([[29,-123,173],[60,-123,173],[45,-108,183]],'#8a9a97');this.poly([[60,-123,173],[60,-91,173],[45,-108,183]],'#738a8a');this.poly([[60,-91,173],[29,-91,173],[45,-108,183]],'#a2ada2');

 window(x,y,z,w,h){
  if(this.debug){this.box([x,y,z,x+w,y+.02,z+h],wall);return;}

   const arch=(pad)=>{const r=(W-pad*2)/2;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(pad,H);c.lineTo(pad,pad+r);c.arc(W/2,pad+r,r,Math.PI,Math.PI*2);c.lineTo(W-pad,H);c.closePath();};

 }
 wallLamp(x,y,z){
  const m=this.s.m,c=this.c;

 }
 envelope(x,y,z,w,h){this.s.m.plane([x,y,z],[w,0,0],[0,0,-h],(c,W,H)=>{c.fillStyle='#efdfbc';c.fillRect(0,0,W,H);c.strokeStyle='#ac6045';c.lineWidth=.15;c.strokeRect(.3,.3,W-.6,H-.6);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(.3,.3);c.lineTo(W/2,H*.57);c.lineTo(W-.3,.3);c.stroke();});}
 stair(){
  const s=E.stairs;
  // Continuous stringers meet the landings. Rail posts are seated on treads.

 }
 yard(){

 sack(x,y,z,seed=0){
  this.contact(x-5,y-5,x+6,y+6,z+.01);
  const rings=[[3.8,0],[5.8,6],[4.8,13],[1.2,16.5]];
  const pieces=[];for(let k=0;k<rings.length-1;k++)for(let i=0;i<12;i++){
   const a=i*Math.PI/6,b=(i+1)*Math.PI/6,[r,h]=rings[k],[R,H]=rings[k+1],v=[[x+r*Math.cos(a),y+r*Math.sin(a),z+h],[x+r*Math.cos(b),y+r*Math.sin(b),z+h],[x+R*Math.cos(b),y+R*Math.sin(b),z+H],[x+R*Math.cos(a),y+R*Math.sin(a),z+H]];
   pieces.push({v,d:v.reduce((s,p)=>s+p[0]+p[1]+p[2],0)/4,i});
  }
  pieces.sort((a,b)=>a.d-b.d);for(const f of pieces){const [a,b,c,d]=f.v,wide=Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1])>=Math.hypot(c[0]-d[0],c[1]-d[1]),o=wide?a:d,u=(wide?b:c).map((v,i)=>v-o[i]),v=(wide?d:a).map((v,i)=>v-o[i]);this.clipped(f.v,o,u,v,'sack',.06+.06*Math.sin(f.i*Math.PI/6));}
  this.line([x-1.3,y,z+16],[x+1.3,y,z+16],'#806b45',1.9);this.line([x,y,z+16.5],[x-1,y,z+18],'#c6b086',3);
  this.envelope(x-2,y+5.4,z+10,4,3);
 }
 clipped(vertices,o,u,v,kind,shade){
  if(this.debug)return;const c=this.c;c.save();c.beginPath();vertices.forEach((v,i)=>{const w=ParcelLayout.world(...v),p=P.project(w.x,w.y,w.z);if(i)c.lineTo(p.x,p.y);else c.moveTo(p.x,p.y);});c.closePath();c.clip();this.s.face(o,u,v,kind,shade);c.restore();
 }
 disc(x,y,z,r,fill,stroke,width=1){const v=[];for(let i=0;i<64;i++){const t=i*Math.PI/32;v.push([x+Math.cos(t)*r,y,z+Math.sin(t)*r]);}this.poly(v,fill);for(let i=0;i<64;i++)this.line(v[i],v[(i+1)%64],stroke,width);}
 street(){
  // Low street edges frame rather than screen the maximum board.
  for(const q of [[-58,266,-18,154,273,-8],[326,34,-18,333,212,-8]]){this.contact(q[0],q[1],q[3],q[4],-18);this.box(q,stone,'ashlar');this.box([q[0]-1,q[1]-1,-8,q[3]+1,q[4]+1,-6.8],wall);}

   this.plant('sage',x+5,282,-14.9,21);this.plant('hydrangea',x+16,284,-14.9,23,x===30);this.plant('lavender',x+28,284,-14.9,19);

  // Bicycle construction includes two ground-contact tyres and connected frame.
  const y=261,z=-18;this.contact(204,258,238,266,-18);
  for(const x of [210,232]){this.disc(x,y,z+6,6,null,'#424f49',2.8);this.disc(x,y+.03,z+6,4.8,null,'#c4b28c',1.2);for(let i=0;i<8;i++){const t=i*Math.PI/4;this.line([x,y+.06,z+6],[x+4.8*Math.cos(t),y+.06,z+6+4.8*Math.sin(t)],'#a4af9f',.6);}}
  const lines=[[[210,6],[219,15]],[[219,15],[223,6]],[[223,6],[210,6]],[[219,15],[229,16]],[[229,16],[223,6]],[[229,16],[232,6]],[[219,15],[219,18]],[[217,18],[222,18]],[[229,16],[227,20]],[[227,20],[231,20]]];
  for(const [a,b] of lines)this.line([a[0],y+.1,z+a[1]],[b[0],y+.1,z+b[1]],'#426e65',3.2);
  if(!this.debug){this.box([216,260.5,-.5,222,262,.2],steel,'oak');this.box([229,260,-3,238,267,1],stone,'oak');this.envelope(231,267.03,.2,5,2.3);}
  for(const x of [208,233])this.box([x,263,-18,x+1,264,-4],steel);this.box([208,263,-5,234,264,-4],steel);
  this.contact(295,214,304,223,-18);this.box([297,215,-18,302,220,-15],stone);this.box([298,216,-15,301,219,-5],steel);this.box([295,213,-5,304,222,10],{top:'#abb6a1',left:'#829a8b',right:'#6b877c'},'red');this.line([296,222.02,6],[303,222.02,6],'#603c30',2);if(!this.debug)this.envelope(296,222.1,3,7,4);
 }
 drawStreet(){
  const r=this.r;let v=this.streetView;if(!v){
   v=this.streetView={canvas:document.createElement('canvas'),x:NaN,y:NaN,scale:0,dpr:0};
   const a=new ParcelArchitecture(r);a.c=v.canvas.getContext('2d');a.p=new ParcelPainter(a.c,r.images,r.assets);v.materials=new ParcelCourtSurfaces(a);
  }
  if(v.x!==r.cam.tx||v.y!==r.cam.ty||v.scale!==r.cam.scale||v.dpr!==r.dpr||v.canvas.width!==r.canvas.width||v.canvas.height!==r.canvas.height){
   v.x=r.cam.tx;v.y=r.cam.ty;v.scale=r.cam.scale;v.dpr=r.dpr;v.canvas.width=r.canvas.width;v.canvas.height=r.canvas.height;
   const c=v.canvas.getContext('2d'),s=r.dpr*r.cam.scale;c.setTransform(s,0,0,s,r.cam.tx*r.dpr,r.cam.ty*r.dpr);
   v.materials.face([-2000,-2000,-18],[4000,0,0],[0,4000,0],'paving',.015);
   c.globalCompositeOperation='destination-in';const g=c.createLinearGradient(0,this.bounds.top+370,0,this.bounds.top+900);g.addColorStop(0,'#0000');g.addColorStop(1,'#000');c.fillStyle=g;c.fillRect(-10000,-10000,20000,20000);c.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';
  }
  const c=r.ctx;c.save();c.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);c.drawImage(v.canvas,0,0);c.restore();
 }
 draw(){if(this.skyCanvas)this.r.architecture.drawLayer('court-sky',this.skyCanvas,this.skyBounds);this.drawStreet();this.r.architecture.drawLayer('court',this.canvas,this.bounds);}
}
root.ParcelExterior=Exterior;
})(globalThis);

/* Room-local artwork on the original PARCEL life anchors and chapter clock. */
(function(root){'use strict';
const P=ParcelProjection,U=ParcelLayout.unit;
class Life{
 constructor(r){this.r=r;this.c=r.ctx;this.bodies=[];this.frozen=PARCEL.create(PARCEL.CFG);this.left={};this.right={};}
 point(x,y,z=0){return this.r.pieces.project((x-80)/16,(y-80)/16,z*U);}
 bake(draw){
  const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=900;c.height=700;const ctx=c.getContext('2d');ctx.translate(450-760,520-465);const old=this.r.ctx;this.r.ctx=ctx;const a=new ParcelArchitecture(this.r);a.p=new ParcelPainter(ctx,this.r.images,this.r.assets);a.c=ctx;a.corner=new ParcelCorner(a);a.work=new ParcelWorkroom(a);draw(a,ctx);this.r.ctx=old;
  // Trim transparent working space once. Live copies touch only authored art.
  const data=ctx.getImageData(0,0,c.width,c.height).data;let x0=c.width,y0=c.height,x1=0,y1=0;
  for(let y=0;y<c.height;y++)for(let x=0;x<c.width;x++)if(data[(y*c.width+x)*4+3]){x0=Math.min(x0,x);y0=Math.min(y0,y);x1=Math.max(x1,x);y1=Math.max(y1,y);}
  const image=document.createElement('canvas');image.width=x1-x0+1;image.height=y1-y0+1;image.getContext('2d').drawImage(c,x0,y0,image.width,image.height,0,0,image.width,image.height);
  return{image,x:x0-450,y:y0-520};
 }
 prepare(){
  this.finishedVan=new ParcelVanFinish(this.r);this.finishedVan.prepare();
  this.vanMeshes=this.finishedVan.meshes;this.vanHeadings=this.finishedVan.headings;this.vanBounds=this.finishedVan.bounds;this.vanCache=this.finishedVan.cache;this.vanCtx=this.finishedVan.c;
  for(let back=0;back<2;back++)for(let walk=-1;walk<4;walk++)for(let bend=0;bend<3;bend++){
   const im=document.createElement('canvas');im.width=128;im.height=144;const c=im.getContext('2d');c.scale(4,4);
   const stride=walk===0?-2:walk===2?2:0,bob=walk>=0?walk&1:0,y=6+bob+bend;
   c.lineWidth=3;c.strokeStyle='#4c6070';c.beginPath();c.moveTo(14,25);c.lineTo(14+stride,31);c.moveTo(19,25);c.lineTo(19-stride,31);c.stroke();
   const part=(name,x,y,w,h)=>c.drawImage(this.r.images['actor-'+name],x,y,w,h);
   part('shoe',12+stride,28,4,6);part('shoe',17-stride,28,4,6);
   part(back?'coat-back':'coat-front',10.8+stride/4,y+9,11.8,16-bend);part(back?'head-back':'head-front',10,y-1,13,12);this.bodies.push(im);
  }
  this.parcel=this.bake(a=>a.corner.parcel({bounds:[76,76,0,84,84,6],seed:17},false));
  this.satchel=this.bake(a=>{
   a.box(77,78.5,6,3,0,5,'oak',{top:'#d9bd8d',left:'#bc9a6d',right:'#9d7c57'});
   a.p.poly([[-3/16,1.55/16,5*U],[3/16,1.55/16,5*U],[2/16,1.56/16,3*U],[-2/16,1.56/16,3*U]],'#d1b27e','oak');
   a.line([80,81.6,.8],[80,81.6,4.6],'#77533a',4);a.box(79.5,81.6,1,.1,2,1,'oak',{top:'#edd89d',left:'#d6b767',right:'#a78646'});
   a.line([78.5,80,5],[79,80,6.5],'#77533a',3);a.line([79,80,6.5],[81,80,6.5],'#77533a',3);a.line([81,80,6.5],[81.5,80,5],'#77533a',3);
  });
  this.cart=this.bake(a=>a.cart(74,74,14,14,9));
  this.rollers=[];for(let frame=0;frame<4;frame++)this.rollers.push(this.bake(a=>{
   a.work.rollers(54,73,8.3,54,frame);
  }));
  this.trucks=[];for(let lift=0;lift<4;lift++)this.trucks.push(this.bake(a=>{
   a.cart(74,75,12,10,3);for(const x of [75,84])a.box(x,74,1.1,1.1,3,22,'brick');
   a.line([75,72.5,11],[84,72.5,11],'#59797b',3);a.line([75,74,25],[75,72.5,11],'#59797b',3);a.line([84,74,25],[84,72.5,11],'#59797b',3);
   a.box(73.5,76,13,11,5+lift*2,2);a.p.parcel(-5/16,-3/16,(7+lift*2)*U,9/16,7/16,8*U);
  }));

 }
 cached(im,x,y,z=0){const p=this.point(x,y,z);this.c.drawImage(im.image,p.x+im.x,p.y+im.y);}
 image(im,x,y,z=0,mirror=false){const p=this.point(x,y,z),c=this.c;c.save();c.translate(p.x,p.y);if(mirror)c.scale(-1,1);c.drawImage(im,-16*U,-34*U,32*U,36*U);c.restore();}
 van(s){this.finishedVan.draw(s);}
 vanShadow(s){this.finishedVan.shadow(s);}
 arm(h,a,side,x,y,z,bend){h.sx=a.x+side*2;h.sy=a.y-side*2;h.sz=16-bend;h.x=x;h.y=y;h.z=z;h.ex=(h.sx+x)/2+side*.3;h.ey=(h.sy+y)/2-side*.3;h.ez=(h.sz+z)/2-1.5;}
 limb(h){const c=this.c,p=this.point(h.sx,h.sy,h.sz),q=this.point(h.ex,h.ey,h.ez),e=this.point(h.x,h.y,h.z);c.lineCap='round';c.beginPath();c.moveTo(p.x,p.y);c.lineTo(q.x,q.y);c.lineTo(e.x,e.y);c.strokeStyle='#3d5261';c.lineWidth=3*U;c.stroke();c.strokeStyle='#6b7d83';c.lineWidth=.6*U;c.stroke();}
 hand(h){const p=this.point(h.x,h.y,h.z);this.c.drawImage(this.r.images['actor-hand'],p.x-1.5*U,p.y-1.5*U,3*U,3*U);}
 worker(s){
  const a=s.sorter,p=s.parcels[0],back=a.face>=2?1:0,mirror=PARCEL.staffMirror(a.face),side=mirror?1:-1;
  const walk=a.action==='walk'||a.action==='carry-walk'||a.action==='push'?a.frame:-1,bend=a.handling===0||a.handling===1?2:a.handling===2?1:0;
  if(CP.parcel&&CP.mode==='growth'){const m=PC.motion;this.arm(this.left,a,side,m.x+1,m.y-7.5,11,bend);this.arm(this.right,a,-side,m.x+3.8,m.y-7.5,11,bend);}
  else if(a.action==='push'||a.action==='carry-walk'&&p.place!=='hand'&&!a.grip){const t=s.dispatch;this.arm(this.left,a,side,t.x-2,t.y+8,10,bend);this.arm(this.right,a,-side,t.x+4,t.y+8,10,bend);}
  else if(a.action==='sort'){this.arm(this.left,a,side,p.x-2,p.y+2,p.z+6.2,bend);this.arm(this.right,a,-side,p.x+2,p.y+2,p.z+6.2,bend);}
  else if(a.handling>=0||a.grip||a.action==='carry'||a.action==='carry-walk'){this.arm(this.left,a,side,p.x-2,p.y+3.8,p.z+.8,bend);this.arm(this.right,a,-side,p.x+3.8,p.y-2,p.z+.8,bend);}
  else{const stride=walk===0?-1.2:walk===2?1.2:0;this.arm(this.left,a,side,a.x+side*(2.4+stride),a.y-side*(2.4+stride),9,bend);this.arm(this.right,a,-side,a.x-side*(2.4-stride),a.y+side*(2.4-stride),10,bend);}
  const ground=this.point(a.x,a.y);this.r.shadow(ground.x,ground.y+1,14,5,.2);this.limb(this.right);this.image(this.bodies[(back*5+walk+1)*3+bend],a.x,a.y,a.z,mirror);this.limb(this.left);
  if(p.visible&&p.place==='hand')this.cached(this.parcel,p.x,p.y,p.z);
  this.hand(this.right);this.hand(this.left);
 }
 draw(s){
  const p=s.parcels[0];this.cached(this.rollers[s.roller],120,47);this.cached(this.cart,s.dispatch.x,s.dispatch.y);
  if(CP.parcel&&CP.mode==='growth'){const m=PC.motion;this.cached(this.trucks[m.lift],m.x,m.y);}else this.cached(this.trucks[s.lift],55,191);
  if(p.visible&&p.place!=='hand'&&p.place!=='van')this.cached(this.parcel,p.x,p.y,p.z);
  this.worker(s);
  const pm=s.pm,key=(pm.state===0?'curl':pm.state===1?'stretch':'watch-'+pm.head)+'-'+(pm.flick?1:0);this.image(this.r.images['actor-pm-'+key],pm.x,pm.y,pm.z,pm.face===1);
  const k=s.kitten,q=this.point(k.x,k.y,k.z);this.r.shadow(q.x,q.y+1,14,5,.16);this.r.pieces.asset(k.pose===2?'ginger-walk-front-'+(k.frame&1):k.pose===1?'ginger-wake':'ginger-puzzled',q.x,q.y+3,48,k.face===1);
  const t=this.point(s.toy.x,s.toy.y,s.toy.z);this.r.shadow(t.x,t.y+4,5,2,.17);this.c.fillStyle='#f5e3ba';this.c.beginPath();this.c.arc(t.x,t.y,5,0,7);this.c.fill();
  if(CP.parcel&&CP.mode==='exit'&&PC.dispatch.visible&&p.place!=='van'){const b=PC.dispatch;this.cached(this.satchel,b.bagX,b.bagY,b.bagZ);}
 }
}
root.ParcelLifeArt=Life;
})(globalThis);

/* Parcel presentation reads canonical board sources without writing to them. */
(function(root){'use strict';
const P=ParcelProjection,U=ParcelLayout.unit,DIR=['R','D','L','U'],TYPES=['plain','ginger','blue'];
const SYMBOLS=['●','■','▲','◆'];
const order=(a,b)=>a.depth-b.depth||a.kind-b.kind;
class Renderer{
 constructor(canvas,assets){
  this.canvas=canvas;this.ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');this.assets=assets;this.images={};this.bigImages=[];
  this.dpr=1;this.cam={tx:0,ty:0,scale:1};this.config={width:1,height:1};this.gray=false;this.motion=true;this.ambient=0;
  this.boardCaches=new Map();this.fonts=Array.from({length:41},(_,i)=>i+'px Trebuchet MS,sans-serif');
  this.acting=ParcelActing.create(BOARDANIM);this.poses=Array.from({length:32},()=>this.acting.makeOut());
  this.previews=Array.from({length:8},()=>this.acting.makeOut());this.previewTraces=Array.from({length:8},()=>({coat:1,big:false}));
  this.traceSources=[];this.traces=[];this.items=[];this.pool=Array.from({length:180},()=>({kind:0,index:0,depth:0}));
  this.tables=Array.from({length:64},()=>({x:0,y:0,dir:'R',type:'plain',locked:false}));
  this.hatches=Array.from({length:8},()=>({x:0,y:0,dir:'R'}));this.hatchStates=Array.from({length:8},()=>({open:0}));
  this.baskets=Array.from({length:16},()=>({x:0,y:0,coat:'ginger'}));this.dogs=Array.from({length:16},()=>this.acting.makeDogOut());
  this.pieces=new ParcelPieces(this);this.architecture=new ParcelArchitecture(this);this.life=new ParcelLifeArt(this);this.exterior=new ParcelExterior(this);this.point={x:0,y:0};this.polygon=Array.from({length:4},()=>({x:0,y:0}));
 }
 async load(){
  const names=Object.keys(this.assets.files);
  await Promise.all(names.map(name=>new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const im=new Image();im.onload=()=>{this.images[name]=im;resolve();};im.onerror=()=>reject(Error('Missing Parcel artwork: '+name));im.src=this.assets.files[name];})));
  for(const [id,m] of Object.entries(this.assets.original))if(Number.isInteger(m.bigIndex))this.bigImages[m.bigIndex]=this.images[id];
  const mapping={'face-door':'loading-door','face-cabinet':'bench-front','face-dial':'scale-dial','face-coats':'coat-scarf','face-correspondence':'correspondence','face-tea':'tea'};
  for(const [id,name] of Object.entries(mapping)){const q=this.assets.fronts.sprites[name].atlasRect,c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=q[2];c.height=q[3];c.getContext('2d').drawImage(this.images.fronts,...q,0,0,q[2],q[3]);this.images[id]=c;}
  const dial=this.assets.mechanical.sprites['scale-dial'].atlasRect,dc=document.createElement('canvas');dc.width=dial[2];dc.height=dial[3];dc.getContext('2d').drawImage(this.images.mechanical,...dial,0,0,dial[2],dial[3]);this.images['mechanical-scale-dial']=dc;
  this.bedArt={};for(const coat of ['ginger','blue'])for(const state of ['empty','compressed']){const id='big_basket_'+coat+'/'+state;this.bedArt[coat+state]={image:this.images[id],rect:this.assets.original[id].rect};}
  this.architecture.bake();this.exterior.bake();this.life.prepare();for(const [w,h] of ParcelLayout.sizes){this.bakeBoard(w,h);this.pieces.prepareBoard(w,h);}this.ready=true;
 }
 sprite(sheet,index,x,bottom,width){const r=this.assets[sheet].rects[index];this.ctx.drawImage(this.images[sheet],r[0],r[1],r[2],r[3],x-width/2,bottom-width*r[3]/r[2],width,width*r[3]/r[2]);}
 path(points,fill,stroke,width=1){const c=this.ctx;c.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<points.length;i++){const p=points[i];if(i)c.lineTo(p.x,p.y);else c.moveTo(p.x,p.y);}c.closePath();if(fill){c.fillStyle=fill;c.fill();}if(stroke){c.strokeStyle=stroke;c.lineWidth=width;c.stroke();}}
 shadow(x,y,rx,ry,alpha=.13){const c=this.ctx,old=c.globalAlpha;c.globalAlpha=old*alpha;c.fillStyle='#413323';c.beginPath();c.ellipse(x,y,rx,ry,0,0,Math.PI*2);c.fill();c.globalAlpha=old;}
 line(a,b,color='#71918e',width=1.3){const c=this.ctx;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(a.x,a.y);c.lineTo(b.x,b.y);c.strokeStyle=color;c.lineWidth=width;c.stroke();}
 label(text,p,colour='#5e7774',size=12){const c=this.ctx;c.font=this.fonts[size];c.textAlign='center';c.fillStyle=colour;c.fillText(text,p.x,p.y);}
 paintBoard(w,h){
  const c=this.ctx,[a,b,e,d]=P.corners(w,h);
  // One sheet of card: thickness belongs only to its two outside front edges.
  this.path([d,e,{x:e.x,y:e.y+3},{x:d.x,y:d.y+3}],'#bba079');
  this.path([b,e,{x:e.x,y:e.y+3},{x:b.x,y:b.y+3}],'#a88d68');
  if(!this.paper){
   const paper=document.createElement('canvas');paper.width=paper.height=64;
   // Only blue paper grain, well inside the source tile. None of its painted
   // edges, perspective or bevels can enter the floor geometry.
   paper.getContext('2d').drawImage(this.images.pieces,128,161,64,32,0,0,64,64);
   this.paper=c.createPattern(paper,'repeat');
  }
  c.save();this.path([a,b,e,d],'#7c8da7');c.clip();
  c.transform(P.HW/96,P.HH/96,-P.HW/96,P.HH/96,a.x,a.y);
  c.fillStyle=this.paper;c.fillRect(0,0,w*96,h*96);c.restore();
  // Small stock variations stay coplanar; they add no raised tile faces.
  for(let y=0;y<h;y++)for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
   const tint=((x*17+y*31)%7-3)*.008;
   this.path([P.project(x,y),P.project(x+1,y),P.project(x+1,y+1),P.project(x,y+1)],tint<0?'rgba(35,53,78,'+(-tint)+')':'rgba(255,248,226,'+tint+')');
  }
  // Each seam is a single continuous line through the shared grid vertices.
  c.beginPath();
  for(let x=1;x<w;x++){const p=P.project(x,0),q=P.project(x,h);c.moveTo(p.x,p.y);c.lineTo(q.x,q.y);}
  for(let y=1;y<h;y++){const p=P.project(0,y),q=P.project(w,y);c.moveTo(p.x,p.y);c.lineTo(q.x,q.y);}
  c.strokeStyle='#ead8b5';c.lineWidth=2.1;c.stroke();
  this.path([a,b,e,d],null,'#eddbb8',2.6);
 }
 bakeBoard(w,h){
  const key=w*16+h;if(this.boardCaches.has(key))return this.boardCaches.get(key);
  const corners=P.corners(w,h),left=Math.floor(760-h*P.HW-2),top=463,right=Math.ceil(760+w*P.HW+2),bottom=Math.ceil(465+(w+h)*P.HH+5),density=2;
  const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=(right-left)*density;canvas.height=(bottom-top)*density;
  const context=this.ctx,cacheCtx=canvas.getContext('2d');cacheCtx.setTransform(density,0,0,density,-left*density,-top*density);
  this.ctx=cacheCtx;try{this.paintBoard(w,h);}finally{this.ctx=context;}
  const labels=[];for(let x=0;x<w;x++)labels.push({text:String.fromCharCode(65+x),point:P.project(x+.5,h+.28)});for(let y=0;y<h;y++)labels.push({text:String(y+1),point:P.project(w+.28,y+.5)});
  const out={canvas,left,top,width:right-left,height:bottom-top,corners,labels};this.boardCaches.set(key,out);return out;
 }
 board(w,h){const b=this.boardCaches.get(w*16+h);this.ctx.drawImage(b.canvas,b.left,b.top,b.width,b.height);}
 item(kind,index,depth){const it=this.pool[this.items.length];it.kind=kind;it.index=index;it.depth=depth;this.items.push(it);}
 original(im,x,y,z=0,mirror=false){if(!im)return;const p=this.pieces.point(x,y,z),c=this.ctx;c.save();c.translate(p.x,p.y);if(mirror)c.scale(-1,1);c.drawImage(im,-16*U,-34*U,32*U,36*U);c.restore();}
 bigBox(o,tr){if(o.boxFold<0||o.boxFade>=1)return;const fade=Math.floor(Math.max(0,Math.min(1,o.boxFade))*16),index=fade?152+(tr.coat-1)*15+fade-1:140+(tr.coat-1)*6+o.boxFold;this.original(this.bigImages[index],o.boxX+6,o.boxY+6);}
 big(o,tr){
  const index=paperBigCatIndex(o,tr),A=BOARDANIM.A;if(index<0)return;
  const loose=o.takeoff>=0||o.anim===A.HOP||o.anim===A.TURN||o.anim===A.CURL||o.anim===A.NAP||o.anim===A.WAKE||o.anim===A.LEAVE||o.anim===A.PUZZLED;
  const foot=loose&&!o.takeoffSupported&&tr.end!==BOARDANIM.END_WRONG?4:0;
  const c=this.ctx;c.save();
  if(o.anim===A.POP||o.tunnelPair>=0&&o.z<0){const p=this.pieces.point(o.x,o.y);c.beginPath();c.rect(p.x-80,p.y-210,160,228);c.clip();}
  this.original(this.bigImages[index],o.x+(loose?foot:6),o.y+(loose?foot:6),o.z,loose&&(o.dir===0||o.dir===3));c.restore();
  if(o.takeoffSupported)this.original(this.bigImages[145+(tr.coat-1)*6],o.boxX+6,o.boxY+6);
 }
 tunnel(t,front=false,focus=false){
  const p=this.pieces.centre(t.x,t.y),c=this.ctx;
  if(!front){this.shadow(p.x,p.y+8,39,17,.2);this.pieces.ellipse(p.x,p.y+2,36,18,'#c6ac7a','#f0deba');this.pieces.ellipse(p.x,p.y,29,13,'#223e48');}
  else{c.beginPath();c.ellipse(p.x,p.y+2,33,16,0,0,Math.PI);c.strokeStyle='#d8c49b';c.lineWidth=5;c.stroke();}
  // Matched pairs retain distinct symbols as well as colour in grayscale.
  if(front){const symbol=SYMBOLS[t.symbol]||'◆';this.point.x=p.x;this.point.y=p.y+5;this.label(symbol,this.point,'#f6dfac',22);}
  if(focus){c.beginPath();c.ellipse(p.x,p.y,38,20,0,0,Math.PI*2);c.strokeStyle='#fff3bd';c.lineWidth=3;c.stroke();}
 }
 gate(g){
  const p=this.pieces.centre(g.x,g.y),c=this.ctx,d=g.pose.state?g.b:g.a;
  this.shadow(p.x,p.y+4,32,14,.15);this.pieces.ellipse(p.x,p.y+2,32,16,'#b5945d');this.pieces.ellipse(p.x,p.y-1,31,15,'#f4dfaf','#f7ecd0');
  this.pieces.arrow(g.x,g.y,g.a,2,'#a6a58c');this.pieces.arrow(g.x,g.y,g.b,2,'#a6a58c');
  const from=g.pose.from?g.b:g.a,start=from*Math.PI/2;let delta=d*Math.PI/2-start;
  if(delta>Math.PI)delta-=Math.PI*2;if(delta<-Math.PI)delta+=Math.PI*2;
  this.pieces.arrowAngle(g.x,g.y,start+delta*g.pose.frame/2,5,'#365b60');this.pieces.ellipse(p.x,p.y-5,4,3,'#c49a51','#fae5ad');
 }
 prepare(src){
  this.pieces.beginFrame();this.pieces.motion=this.motion;
  for(let i=0;i<src.n;i++){
   const tr=src.traces[i];if(this.traceSources[i]!==tr){this.traceSources[i]=tr;const v=this.traces[i]=Object.create(tr);v.ox=v.oy=0;}
   const o=this.poses[i],v=this.traces[i];if(src.times[i]<0){o.anim=BOARDANIM.A.HIDDEN;o.boxFold=-1;continue;}if(v.big){BOARDANIM.pose(v,src.times[i],o);o.takeoff=-1;o.takeoffSupported=0;o.flapPose=-1;paperBigArrivalPose(o,v,src.times[i]);}else this.acting.sample(v,src.times[i],o);
  }
  for(let i=src.n;i<this.poses.length;i++){this.poses[i].anim=BOARDANIM.A.HIDDEN;this.poses[i].bumpAge=-1;}
  for(let i=0;i<src.hatches.length;i++){
   const h=src.hatches[i],v=this.hatches[i],st=this.hatchStates[i];v.x=h.x;v.y=h.y;v.dir=DIR[h.dir];st.open=0;
   for(let k=0;k<src.n;k++){const tr=src.traces[k],o=this.poses[k];if(tr.hx===h.x&&tr.hy===h.y)st.open=Math.max(st.open,o.hatchOpen);}
   const o=this.previews[i],tr=this.previewTraces[i],cat=gm.L.hatches[i]?.cats[0];tr.coat=(typeof cat==='string'?cat:cat?.coat)==='blue'?2:1;tr.big=!!cat?.big;
   o.anim=BOARDANIM.A.IDLE;o.x=h.x*16+8;o.y=h.y*16+8;o.z=0;o.dir=o.head=h.dir;o.takeoff=-1;o.flapPose=-1;o.eyes=this.motion&&(Math.floor(this.ambient*10)+i*13)%60===0?1:0;
  }
  for(let i=0;i<src.baskets.length;i++){const b=src.baskets[i],v=this.baskets[i];v.x=b.x;v.y=b.y;v.coat=b.coat===2?'blue':'ginger';}
 }
 draw(src){
  if(!this.ready)return;const c=this.ctx,B=src;
  c.setTransform(this.dpr,0,0,this.dpr,0,0);c.clearRect(0,0,this.config.width,this.config.height);
  c.fillStyle='#d8dbd0';c.fillRect(0,0,this.config.width,this.config.height);
  c.save();if(this.gray)c.filter='grayscale(1)';c.translate(this.cam.tx,this.cam.ty);c.scale(this.cam.scale,this.cam.scale);c.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;c.imageSmoothingQuality='high';
  this.prepare(src);const life=this.motion||CP.parcel?PARCELIFE:this.life.frozen;
  this.exterior.draw();this.architecture.drawFloor();this.life.vanShadow(life);this.architecture.draw();this.life.van(life);this.architecture.drawForeground();this.board(B.w,B.h);
  if(this.selected){const s=this.selected;const points=this.polygon;Object.assign(points[0],this.pieces.project(s.x,s.y));Object.assign(points[1],this.pieces.project(s.x+1,s.y));Object.assign(points[2],this.pieces.project(s.x+1,s.y+1));Object.assign(points[3],this.pieces.project(s.x,s.y+1));this.path(points,'#fff8da33','#fff0b8',3);}
  for(let i=0;i<src.turntables.length;i++){const t=src.turntables[i],v=this.tables[i];v.x=t.x;v.y=t.y;v.dir=DIR[t.dir];v.type=TYPES[t.kind];v.locked=t.locked;let spin=0;for(let j=0;j<src.n;j++){const o=this.poses[j];if(o.spinX===t.x&&o.spinY===t.y)spin=Math.max(spin,o.spin);}this.pieces.table(v,spin);}
  for(const g of src.gates)this.gate(g);
  for(const t of src.tunnels)this.tunnel(t);
  for(let i=0;i<src.hatches.length;i++)this.pieces.hatch(this.hatches[i],this.hatchStates[i]);
  this.items.length=0;
  for(let i=0;i<src.baskets.length;i++){const b=src.baskets[i];this.item(0,i,b.x+b.y+.9);this.item(1,i,b.x+b.y+1.8);}
  for(let i=0;i<src.dogs.length;i++){const d=src.dogs[i];this.acting.dog(d,i,this.poses,this.motion?this.ambient:0,this.dogs[i]);this.item(2,i,d.x+d.y+1);}
  if(!src.n&&gm.mode==='edit'&&!cpActive())for(let i=0;i<src.hatches.length;i++){const h=src.hatches[i];this.item(3,i,h.x+h.y+1);}
  for(let i=0;i<src.n;i++){const o=this.poses[i];if(o.anim===BOARDANIM.A.HIDDEN)continue;if(o.boxFold>=0&&o.boxFade<1)this.item(4,i,(o.boxX+o.boxY)/16);this.item(5,i,(o.x+o.y)/16+.04);}
  this.items.sort(order);
  for(const it of this.items){const i=it.index,o=this.poses[i],tr=this.traces[i];switch(it.kind){
   case 0:case 1:{const b=this.baskets[i];if(src.largeBaskets){if(!it.kind){let occupied=false;for(let k=0;k<src.n;k++){const o=this.poses[k],tr=this.traces[k],A=BOARDANIM.A;if((o.anim===A.NAP||o.anim===A.CURL||o.anim===A.WAKE)&&tr.n&&tr.x1[tr.n-1]===b.x&&tr.y1[tr.n-1]===b.y)occupied=true;}const art=this.bedArt[b.coat+(occupied?'compressed':'empty')],m=art.rect,p=this.pieces.centre(b.x,b.y);this.shadow(p.x,p.y+10,33,12,.18);c.drawImage(art.image,p.x+m.dx*U,p.y+m.dy*U,m.w*U,m.h*U);}}else this.pieces.basket(b,!!it.kind);break;}
   case 2:this.pieces.dog(src.dogs[i],i,this.dogs[i]);break;
   case 3:{const t=this.previewTraces[i];if(t.big)this.big(this.previews[i],t);else this.pieces.boxed(this.previews[i],t,this.ambient);break;}
   case 4:if(tr.big)this.bigBox(o,tr);else this.pieces.carrier(o.boxX,o.boxY,tr.d0[tr.n-1],o.boxFold,o.boxFade);break;
   case 5:if(tr.big)this.big(o,tr);else{c.save();if(o.tunnelPair>=0&&o.z<0){const p=this.pieces.point(o.x,o.y);c.beginPath();c.rect(p.x-70,p.y-160,140,176);c.clip();}if(o.cat.boxed)this.pieces.boxed(o,tr,src.times[i]);else this.pieces.loose(o,tr,src.times[i]);c.restore();}break;
  }}
  for(const t of src.tunnels)this.tunnel(t,true,src.tunnelFocus===t.pair);
  for(let i=0;i<src.hatches.length;i++)if(this.hatchStates[i].open)this.pieces.hatchFront(this.hatches[i]);
  this.life.draw(life);
  for(const v of this.boardCaches.get(B.w*16+B.h).labels)this.label(v.text,v.point,'#576a65',10);
  c.restore();
 }
}
root.ParcelRenderer=Renderer;
})(globalThis);

/* Private study adapter. Integration owns production installation and camera hooks. */
const OfficeRules={GR_DIR};
const parcelPaper={ready:false,error:null,motion:true,view:'room',renderer:null,canvas:null};
window.ParcelPaper=parcelPaper;
const parcelPaperNative={resolve,paperOutputCanvas,paperOutputScale,gLoad,gHud,cpBegin,gCamera,defaultScale,scaleLimits,cpWorld,present,renderFrame};
const parcelPaperScale=16/ParcelProjection.HW,parcelPaperSelection={x:0,y:0};
let parcelPaperOutput=false,parcelPaperDensity=1;
const parcelPaperMedia=matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
// This private camera experiment reserves actual control space. Production
// adoption belongs to integration; input still uses native scale/viewX/viewY.
const parcelPaperFrame={x:0,y:0,w:1,h:1,cx:0,cy:0,width:1,height:1};
const parcelPaperReserve={id:'',width:0,height:0,w:0,h:0};
let parcelPaperLoadingLevel=false;
function parcelPaperReserveControls(force=false){
 const q=parcelPaperReserve;
 if(parcelPaperLoadingLevel||!force&&q.id===gm.L.id&&q.width===innerWidth&&q.height===innerHeight)return;
 const el=hud.root,display=el.style.display,visibility=el.style.visibility;
 // Chapter cards temporarily hide the HUD. Measure its current responsive
 // layout without displaying or activating it, then restore both inline values.
 el.style.visibility='hidden';el.style.display='flex';
 q.id=gm.L.id;q.width=innerWidth;q.height=innerHeight;q.w=el.offsetWidth;
 // The stars row appears after delivery. Reserve it before Send, so no status,
 // queue or button change can move a running board beneath the pointer.
 q.h=el.offsetHeight+(gm.mode==='running'?100:32);
 el.style.display=display;el.style.visibility=visibility;
}
function parcelPaperLoad(index){
 if(index<0||index>=LEVELS.length||!/^7-/.test(LEVELS[index].id))return;
 parcelPaperLoadingLevel=true;try{parcelPaperNative.gLoad(index);}finally{parcelPaperLoadingLevel=false;}
 if(parcelPaper.ready){parcelPaperReserveControls(true);parcelPaperGeometry();parcelPaperFit(parcelPaper.view);}
}
function parcelPaperFrameFor(mode){
 const f=parcelPaperFrame,d=devDpr,chapter=cpActive()&&CP.mode!=='enter',side=devW>devH&&innerHeight<=520||innerWidth>=1180&&innerHeight>=650;
 const panel=chapter?CP.card:hud.root,header=document.getElementById('paperHeader').offsetHeight;
 parcelPaperReserveControls();
 const pw=side?(chapter?panel.offsetWidth:parcelPaperReserve.w)+18:0,ph=side?0:(chapter?panel.offsetHeight:parcelPaperReserve.h)+20;
 f.x=12*d;f.y=(header+12)*d;f.w=Math.max(80*d,devW-(24+pw)*d);f.h=Math.max(80*d,devH-(header+24+ph)*d);
 if(mode==='room'||mode==='court'){const b=mode==='court'?ParcelExteriorLayout.bounds:ParcelLayout.bounds;f.cx=OX+((b.left+b.right)/2-760)*parcelPaperScale;f.cy=OY+80+((b.top+b.bottom)/2-465)*parcelPaperScale;f.width=(b.right-b.left)*parcelPaperScale;f.height=(b.bottom-b.top)*parcelPaperScale;}
 else{const B=CP.mode==='demo'?CP.src:gm.L.board;f.cx=OX+(B.w-B.h)*8;f.cy=OY+80+(B.w+B.h)*4-3;f.width=(B.w+B.h)*16+36;f.height=(B.w+B.h)*8+60;}
 return f;
}
function parcelPaperCameraMode(){return cpActive()&&CP.mode!=='enter'?(CP.mode==='demo'?'board':'room'):parcelPaper.view;}
function parcelPaperCamera(atScale=scale){
 if(!parcelPaperEligible())return parcelPaperNative.gCamera(atScale);
 const f=parcelPaperFrameFor(parcelPaperCameraMode());WS.camX=f.cx-VIEW_CX-(f.x+f.w/2-devW/2)/atScale;WS.camY=f.cy-VIEW_CY-(f.y+f.h/2-devH/2)/atScale;
}
function parcelPaperDefault(w,h,d){
 if(!parcelPaperEligible())return parcelPaperNative.defaultScale(w,h,d);
 const f=parcelPaperFrameFor(parcelPaperCameraMode());return Math.min(f.w/f.width,f.h/f.height);
}
function parcelPaperEligible(){return parcelPaper.ready&&ACTIVE_ROOM.id==='parcel'&&/^7-/.test(gm.L.id);}
function parcelPaperGeometry(){
 const b=ParcelLayout.bounds,x=OX+80-80,y=OY+80;
 ROOM_FIT_W=(b.right-b.left)*parcelPaperScale+20;ROOM_FIT_H=(b.bottom-b.top)*parcelPaperScale+46;
 PAN_MAX_X=Math.ceil(ROOM_FIT_W/2);PAN_MAX_Y=Math.ceil(ROOM_FIT_H/2);
 VIEW_CX=x+((b.left+b.right)/2-760)*parcelPaperScale;
 VIEW_CY=y+((b.top+b.bottom)/2-465)*parcelPaperScale-8;
}
function parcelPaperFit(mode){
 parcelPaper.view=mode;parcelPaperGeometry();view.panX=view.panY=0;
 view.zoom=parcelPaperDefault(devW,devH,devDpr);
 applyScale();gCamera();dirty=true;
 document.getElementById('paperView').textContent=mode==='board'?'Explore the room':'Closer to the board';
 for(const id of ['paperCourt','paperCourtSmall'])document.getElementById(id).textContent=mode==='court'?'Back to the room':'Explore courtyard';
}
function parcelStudySelect(id){
 const index=LEVELS.findIndex(l=>l.id===id);if(index<0||!/^7-/.test(id))throw RangeError('Parcel study owns 7-1–7-15');
 cpCancel();parcelPaperLoad(index);parcelPaperFit(parcelPaper.view);gSyncSrc();gHud();
}
function parcelPaperHud(){
 parcelPaperNative.gHud();if(!parcelPaper.ready)return;
 const pick=document.getElementById('paperPick');pick.value=gm.L.id;
 document.getElementById('paperSize').textContent=gm.L.board.w+' × '+gm.L.board.h+' · '+gm.L.id;
 if(CP.parcel&&CP.mode==='exit')CP.primary.textContent='Return to this collection';
}
function parcelPaperDraw(){
 const r=parcelPaper.renderer,c=parcelPaper.canvas,S=Math.max(2,paperRenderScale()),w=LW*S,h=LH*S;
 if(c.width!==w||c.height!==h){c.width=w;c.height=h;}
 parcelPaperDensity=S;r.dpr=S;r.config.width=LW;r.config.height=LH;
 r.cam.scale=parcelPaperScale;r.cam.tx=OX-760*parcelPaperScale-viewX;r.cam.ty=OY+80-465*parcelPaperScale-viewY;
 r.gray=grayscale;r.motion=parcelPaper.motion&&!parcelPaperMedia.matches;r.ambient=loopFrame/60;
 parcelPaperSelection.x=gm.cx;parcelPaperSelection.y=gm.cy;r.selected=gm.cursor&&gm.mode!=='running'&&!cpActive()?parcelPaperSelection:null;
 gPrepareSrc();r.draw(gBoardSource());parcelPaperOutput=true;paperReady=false;
}
function parcelPaperExit(){if(CP.parcel&&CP.mode==='exit'){cpCancel();parcelPaperFit('room');return true;}return false;}
async function parcelPaperBoot(){
 try{
  __so.pause(true);cpCancel();parcelPaperNative.gLoad(LEVELS.findIndex(l=>l.id==='7-1'));
  const c=parcelPaper.canvas=document.createElement('canvas'),r=parcelPaper.renderer=new ParcelRenderer(c,ParcelPaperAssets);await r.load();
  parcelPaper.ready=true;
  gCamera=parcelPaperCamera;defaultScale=parcelPaperDefault;
  scaleLimits=function(w,h){const lim=parcelPaperNative.scaleLimits(w,h);if(parcelPaperEligible())lim[0]=.3*devDpr;return lim;};
  cpWorld=function(){
   if(!parcelPaperEligible()||!CP.parcel)return parcelPaperNative.cpWorld();
   pcWorld();
   if(CP.mode==='enter'){const u=Math.min(1,CP.frame/72),v=u*u*(3-2*u);CP.visualScale=CP.enterScale+(CP.targetScale-CP.enterScale)*v;parcelPaperCamera(CP.targetScale);WS.camX=CP.enterX+(WS.camX-CP.enterX)*v;WS.camY=CP.enterY+(WS.camY-CP.enterY)*v;}
   else{const s=parcelPaperDefault(devW,devH,devDpr);if(Math.abs(s-scale)>.001){view.zoom=s;applyScale();}parcelPaperCamera();}
   if(CP.mode==='exit')CP.primary.textContent='Return to this collection';dirty=true;
  };
  resolve=function(){parcelPaperOutput=false;if(parcelPaperEligible())return parcelPaperDraw();return parcelPaperNative.resolve();};
  paperOutputCanvas=function(){return parcelPaperOutput?parcelPaper.canvas:parcelPaperNative.paperOutputCanvas();};
  paperOutputScale=function(){return parcelPaperOutput?parcelPaperDensity:parcelPaperNative.paperOutputScale();};
  renderFrame=function(){
   if(!parcelPaperEligible())return parcelPaperNative.renderFrame();
   viewX=Math.round(VIEW_CX+WS.camX+view.panX-LW/2);viewY=Math.round(VIEW_CY+WS.camY+view.panY-LH/2);
   parcelPaperDraw();
  };
  present=function(){
   if(!parcelPaperOutput)return parcelPaperNative.present();
   const zoom=CP.mode==='enter'?CP.visualScale:scale,w=LW*zoom,h=LH*zoom;
   sctx.fillStyle=grayscale?'#e8e8e8':'#d8dbd0';sctx.fillRect(0,0,screen.width,screen.height);
   sctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;sctx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';
   sctx.drawImage(parcelPaper.canvas,0,0,LW*parcelPaperDensity,LH*parcelPaperDensity,CP.mode==='enter'?(devW-w)/2:dstX,CP.mode==='enter'?(devH-h)/2:dstY,w,h);
  };
  gLoad=parcelPaperLoad;
  gHud=parcelPaperHud;
  cpBegin=function(){if(!parcelPaperExit())return parcelPaperNative.cpBegin();};
  CP.primary.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(parcelPaperExit()){e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();}},true);
  const pick=document.getElementById('paperPick');for(const l of LEVELS.filter(l=>/^7-/.test(l.id))){const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=l.id;o.textContent=l.id+' · '+l.title;pick.append(o);}pick.onchange=()=>parcelStudySelect(pick.value);
  document.getElementById('paperView').onclick=()=>parcelPaperFit(parcelPaper.view==='board'?'room':'board');
  document.getElementById('paperCourtSmall').onclick=document.getElementById('paperCourt').onclick=()=>parcelPaperFit(parcelPaper.view==='court'?'room':'court');
  document.getElementById('paperMotion').onclick=e=>{parcelPaper.motion=!parcelPaper.motion;e.currentTarget.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(parcelPaper.motion));e.currentTarget.textContent=parcelPaper.motion?'Motion on':'Motion off';dirty=true;};
  document.getElementById('paperStory').onclick=()=>{pcIntro();};
  document.getElementById('paperSolve').onclick=()=>{if(gm.mode==='running'||cpActive())return;gm.chutes=gm.L.solution.map(p=>({...p}));gm.tray={...gm.L.tray};for(const p of gm.chutes)gm.tray[p.type]--;gSyncSrc();gHud();dirty=true;};
  document.getElementById('paperGray').onclick=()=>{grayscale=!grayscale;dirty=true;};
  parcelPaper.state=()=>({ready:parcelPaper.ready,error:parcelPaper.error,level:gm.L.id,mode:gm.mode,chapter:CP.mode,frame:CP.frame,size:[gBoardSource().w,gBoardSource().h],motion:r.motion,bakes:r.architecture.bakes});
  parcelPaper.select=parcelStudySelect;parcelPaper.fit=parcelPaperFit;
  if(innerWidth<700||innerHeight<520)parcelPaper.view='board';
  const query=new URLSearchParams(location.search);if(['room','board','court'].includes(query.get('view')))parcelPaper.view=query.get('view');
  parcelStudySelect(query.get('level')||'7-1');__so.seek(600);__so.pause(false);
  window.addEventListener('resize',()=>{if(parcelPaperEligible()&&!cpActive())parcelPaperFit(parcelPaper.view);});
  document.body.classList.remove('paper-loading');document.getElementById('paperLoading').remove();
 }catch(e){parcelPaper.error=e.stack||String(e);document.getElementById('paperLoading').textContent='The study could not load. '+e.message;console.error(e);}
}

boot();parcelPaperBoot();
